        public static readonly HullDef[] Hulls =
        {
            // Crew roles are flexible; every hull keeps enough offers for each available berth.
            new() { Name = "Rowboat", Model = "Rowboat", Width = 3, Height = 2, Capacity = 20, CrewCap = 2,
                TravelMultiplier = 1f, Hold = new(1, 1), HoldAccess = new(1, 0), Seat = new(1, 0), Rest = new(0, 1),
                Blocked = new[] { new Vector2Int(1, 1) }, RodTiles = new[] { new Vector2Int(0, 0), new Vector2Int(2, 0) },
                RodBases = new[] { new Vector3(0f, .44f, -.615f), new Vector3(2f, .44f, -.615f) } },
            new() { Name = "Skiff", Model = "Skiff", Width = 5, Height = 3, Capacity = 18, CrewCap = 3,
                TravelMultiplier = .5f, Hold = new(1, 1), HoldAccess = new(1, 0), Seat = new(3, 2), Rest = new(0, 2),
                Blocked = new[] { new Vector2Int(1, 1), new Vector2Int(2, 1), new Vector2Int(4, 2), new Vector2Int(4, 1) },
                RodTiles = new[] { new Vector2Int(0, 0), new Vector2Int(2, 0), new Vector2Int(4, 0) },
                RodBases = new[] { new Vector3(.25f, .45f, -.68f), new Vector3(2f, .45f, -.68f), new Vector3(3.75f, .45f, -.68f) } },
            // Additive saved ID. Tier/economy labels can change without migrating old boats.
            new() { Name = "Compact fisher", Model = "CompactFisherV1", Width = 43, Height = 23, CellSize = .1f,
                Capacity = 100, CrewCap = 3, TravelMultiplier = .5f,
                // Unity FBX import reflects source X; these are verified native socket/deck coordinates.
                Hold = new(29, 17), HoldAccess = new(29, 11), Seat = new(13, 12), Rest = new(34, 11), NetAccess = new(39, 6),
                CrewRest = new[] { new Vector2Int(34, 11), new Vector2Int(21, 18), new Vector2Int(21, 3) },
                Blocked = System.Array.Empty<Vector2Int>(),
                RodTiles = new[] { new Vector2Int(21, 18), new Vector2Int(21, 3), new Vector2Int(38, 18) },
                RodBases = new[] { new Vector3(2f, .47f, 2.38f), new Vector3(2f, .47f, -.18f), new Vector3(3.75f, .47f, 2.33f) } },
            // Tier4 approved BOAT032 hull. Existing saved IDs0-2 stay unchanged.
            // First integration retains tier3 cargo/net capacity; economy unlock is still untuned.
            new() { Name = "Prep fisher", Model = "PrepFisherV1", Width = 57, Height = 29, CellSize = .1f,
                Capacity = 100, CrewCap = 4, TravelMultiplier = .5f,
                Hold = new(29, 14), HoldAccess = new(29, 24), Seat = new(15, 15), Rest = new(42, 18), NetAccess = new(53, 4),
                CrewRest = new[] { new Vector2Int(42, 18), new Vector2Int(42, 10), new Vector2Int(28, 24), new Vector2Int(28, 4) },
                Blocked = System.Array.Empty<Vector2Int>(),
                RodTiles = new[] { new Vector2Int(28, 24), new Vector2Int(28, 4), new Vector2Int(50, 24) },
                RodBases = new[] { new Vector3(2.8f, .47f, 3f), new Vector3(2.8f, .47f, -.2f), new Vector3(5.0037f, .47f, 2.9375f) } },
        };

        // Legacy pack constants retained for fixtures. Timed tanks use GameState.FuelFillCost.
        public const int FuelPackSize = 10, FuelPackCost = 10;
        public static int TripFuel(int spot) => Mathf.CeilToInt(Spots[spot].RowSeconds / 5f);

        // Hull goal
        public const int SkiffFishGoal = 16, SkiffCost = 1500;

        // Crew contracts: experienced costs 2x for 1.5x the voyages; veterans are rare.
        public static float Speed(Grade g) => g switch { Grade.Hand => 1f, Grade.Experienced => 1.5f, _ => 2f };
        public static int ContractVoyages(Grade g) => g switch { Grade.Hand => 2, Grade.Experienced => 3, _ => 5 };
        public static int Fee(Grade g) => g switch { Grade.Hand => 30, Grade.Experienced => 60, _ => 150 };
        public static float StrikeChance(Grade g) => g switch { Grade.Hand => .80f, Grade.Experienced => .85f, _ => .90f };
        public const float NetSoakMin = 60f, NetSoakMax = 90f, NetHaulTime = 5f;
        // Provisional compact-deck handling: one novice hand is busy serving three rods.
        public const float CompactPickupTime = 1.4f, CompactStowTime = 3f;
        public static string GradeName(Grade g) => g switch { Grade.Hand => "Deckhand", Grade.Experienced => "Experienced", _ => "Veteran" };

        public static readonly string[] Names =
        {
            "Sam", "Jo", "Ana", "Bram", "Kofi", "Lise", "Mats", "Noor", "Pim", "Rosa", "Tess", "Yusuf", "Ines", "Dirk", "Mei", "Olaf",
        };

        public static readonly Color[] Suits =
        {
            Palette.Oilskin, Palette.Hex("4E8F5A"), Palette.Hex("7A5AA0"), Palette.Hex("3F6FB0"), Palette.Hex("B85A3C"),
        };
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/GameState.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    [System.Serializable]
    public class CaughtFish
    {
        public Species Species;
        public float Kg;
        public float StowedAt;
        public bool Prepared;
        public float PreparedQuality;
    }

    [System.Serializable]
    public class CrewMember
    {
        public string Name;
        public Grade Grade;
        public CrewRole Role;
        public DeckhandWork WorkPreference; // Missing in older saves means Fish.
        public int VoyagesLeft;
        public Color Suit;
        public int Fee => GameData.Fee(Grade) * (Role == CrewRole.Fisherman ? 2 : 1);
        public string RoleName => Role == CrewRole.Fisherman ? "Fisherman" : "Deckhand";
    }

    [System.Serializable]
    public class Contract
    {
        public Species Species;
        public int Count, Delivered, VoyagesLeft, Reward;
        public string Title => $"{Count} {GameData.Get(Species).Name.ToLower()}";
    }

    public class SaleReport
    {
        public int FishSold, Coins, ToContracts, ContractCoins;
        public float Minutes;
        public int Caught;
        public readonly List<string> Lines = new();
    }

    // Everything that survives between voyages: money, supplies, upgrades, crew, contracts,
    // spot stocks and market prices. No Unity objects in here.
    public partial class GameState
    {
        public int Coins = GameData.StartCoins;
        public int Bait = GameData.StartBait;
        public int Squid;
        [System.NonSerialized] public int Chum; // Voyage stock: never saved or bought.
        public PrepOrderSettings PrepOrders = new();
        public bool UseSquid = true;
        public readonly HashSet<int> Charts = new();
        // Legacy save/fixture field only. Early boats have no food mechanic.
        public float FoodMinutes = 4f;
        public int Voyage = 1;
        public int BestVoyageFish;
        public int Hull;
        public float Fuel;
        // Includes the fitted net. Successful cycles reuse it; a tear removes one.
        public int NetInventory = 3;
        public HullDef HullDef => GameData.Hulls[Mathf.Clamp(Hull, 0, GameData.Hulls.Length - 1)];
        public bool CanRefit => Hull == 0 && BestVoyageFish >= GameData.SkiffFishGoal && Coins >= GameData.SkiffCost;
        public readonly HashSet<UpgradeId> Owned = new();
        public readonly float[] Richness;
        public readonly float[] Market;
        // Fish log: how many of each species you have landed, and the biggest one.
        public readonly int[] LogCount;
        public readonly float[] LogBest;
        public bool UnseenSale;
        public CrewMember Deckhand;
        public readonly List<CrewMember> ExtraCrew = new();
        public IEnumerable<CrewMember> Crew
        {
            get { if (Deckhand != null) yield return Deckhand; foreach (var c in ExtraCrew) yield return c; }
        }
        public int CrewCount => ExtraCrew.Count + (Deckhand != null ? 1 : 0);
        public readonly List<CrewMember> Board = new();
        public readonly List<Contract> Offers = new();
        public readonly List<Contract> Active = new();
        public SaleReport LastSale;
        // Unsold fish are saved at port with StowedAt = negative age on arrival.
        // Harbour storage freezes freshness; it never silently sells or discards a haul.
        public readonly List<CaughtFish> UnsoldCatch = new();
        public float UnsoldMinutes;
        [System.Serializable]
        public class CoinCredit { public string Id; public int Amount; }
        public readonly List<CoinCredit> CoinCredits = new();
        public System.Random Rng;

        public GameState(int seed)
        {
            Rng = new System.Random(seed);
            Richness = new float[GameData.Spots.Length];
            Market = new float[GameData.Fish.Length];
            LogCount = new int[GameData.Fish.Length];
            LogBest = new float[GameData.Fish.Length];
            for (int i = 0; i < Richness.Length; i++) Richness[i] = 1f;
            for (int i = 0; i < Market.Length; i++) Market[i] = 1f;
            RollBoard();
            while (Offers.Count < 3) Offers.Add(NewContract());
        }

        public bool Has(UpgradeId id) => Owned.Contains(id);
        public float CoolerCapacity => Hull == 0 ? (Has(UpgradeId.BigCooler) ? 35f : 20f) : HullDef.Capacity * (Has(UpgradeId.BigCooler) ? 1.5f : 1f);
        public float FuelCapacity => Has(UpgradeId.LunchTin) ? 20f : 10f;
        public float FuelFraction => Mathf.Clamp01(Fuel / FuelCapacity);
        public int FuelFillCost => Mathf.CeilToInt(Mathf.Max(0, FuelCapacity - Fuel) - .00001f);
        public float FoodCapacity => (Has(UpgradeId.LunchTin) ? 12f : 6f) * (Hull > 0 ? 4f : 1f);
        public bool Known(int spot) => GameData.Spots[spot].ChartCost == 0 || Charts.Contains(spot);
        public float BiteWindow => Has(UpgradeId.RodCarbon) ? 1.4f : Has(UpgradeId.RodFibreglass) ? 1.15f : GameData.BiteWindow;
        public float ReelSpeed => Has(UpgradeId.RodCarbon) ? 1.5f : Has(UpgradeId.RodFibreglass) ? 1.25f : 1f;
        public float SelfHookChance => Has(UpgradeId.RodCarbon) ? 0.45f : GameData.SelfHookChance;
        public int FoodFillCost => Mathf.CeilToInt(FoodCapacity - FoodMinutes) * GameData.FoodCostPerMinute;

        public float PricePerKg(Species s) => GameData.Get(s).PricePerKg * Market[(int)s];

        public float Freshness(CaughtFish f, float now)
        {
            if (f.Prepared) return f.PreparedQuality > 0 ? f.PreparedQuality : 1f;
            if (Has(UpgradeId.IceBox)) return 1f;
            return Mathf.Max(GameData.MinFreshness, 1f - GameData.FreshnessLossPerMinute * Mathf.Max(0f, now - f.StowedAt - GameData.FreshnessGraceSeconds) / 60f);
        }

        public float SpecimenPricePerKg(Species species, float kg)
        {
            var d = GameData.Get(species);
            float size = Mathf.InverseLerp(d.MinKg, d.MaxKg, kg);
            return PricePerKg(species) * (1f + .5f * size);
        }
        public int ValueOf(CaughtFish f, float now) => Mathf.Max(1, Mathf.RoundToInt(f.Kg * SpecimenPricePerKg(f.Species, f.Kg) * Freshness(f, now) * (f.Prepared ? 1.25f : 1f)));

        float Rand() => (float)Rng.NextDouble();

        // ---- harbour actions ----------------------------------------------------------

        public bool RefitSkiff()
        {
            if (!CanRefit) return false;
            Coins -= GameData.SkiffCost;
            Hull = 1;
            // The shipyard supplies the first tank, avoiding a stranded first refit.
            Fuel = FuelCapacity;
            RollBoard();
            return true;
        }

        public bool BuyFuel()
        {
            int cost = FuelFillCost;
            if (Hull == 0 || cost <= 0 || Coins < cost) return false;
            Coins -= cost;
            Fuel = FuelCapacity;
            return true;
        }

        public bool TryBuy(UpgradeId id)
        {
            var u = GameData.Upgrade(id);
            int cost = UpgradeCost(id);
            if (!UpgradeAvailable(id) || Has(id) || Coins < cost || u.Requires.HasValue && !Has(u.Requires.Value)) return false;
            Coins -= cost;
            Owned.Add(id);
            return true;
        }

        public bool BuyBait(bool bulk = false)
        {
            int cost = bulk ? GameData.BaitBulkCost : GameData.BaitPackCost;
            if (Coins < cost) return false;
            Coins -= cost;
            Bait += bulk ? GameData.BaitBulkSize : GameData.BaitPackSize;
            return true;
        }

        public bool BuyNet()
        {
            if (NetInventory < 0 || NetInventory >= GameData.NetInventoryCapacity || Coins < GameData.NetReplacementCost) return false;
            Coins -= GameData.NetReplacementCost;
            NetInventory++;
            return true;
        }

        public bool BuySquid(bool bulk = false)
        {
            int cost = bulk ? GameData.SquidBulkCost : GameData.SquidPackCost;
            if (Coins < cost) return false;
            Coins -= cost;
            Squid += bulk ? GameData.SquidBulkSize : GameData.SquidPackSize;
            return true;
        }

        public bool BuyChart(int spot)
        {
            int cost = GameData.Spots[spot].ChartCost;
            if (Known(spot) || Coins < cost) return false;
            Coins -= cost;
            Charts.Add(spot);
            return true;
        }

        public bool HasPaidBait => Chum > 0 || Bait > 0 || Squid > 0;
        public bool WillUseSquid => Chum <= 0 && Squid > 0 && (UseSquid || Bait <= 0);
        public bool HasAnyBait => true; // Unlimited harbour scraps prevent a bait soft lock.

        // Takes paid bait if available, otherwise free scraps. Always succeeds; out flag identifies squid.
        public bool TakeBait(out bool squid) => TakeBait(out squid, out _);
        public bool TakeBait(out bool squid, out bool chum)
        {
            chum = Chum > 0;
            if (chum) { Chum--; squid = false; return true; }
            squid = WillUseSquid;
            if (squid) { Squid--; return true; }
            if (Bait > 0) { Bait--; return true; }
            return true; // Free scraps: the rod records this bait type before taking it.
        }

        public bool FillFood() => false; // Retired; cannot charge for inactive food.

        public bool Hire(CrewMember c)
        {
            if (!Board.Contains(c) || CrewCount >= HullDef.CrewCap || Coins < c.Fee) return false;
            Coins -= c.Fee;
            Board.Remove(c);
            if (c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand && Deckhand == null) Deckhand = c;
            else ExtraCrew.Add(c);
            EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
            return true;
        }

        public bool TakeContract(Contract c)
        {
            if (Active.Count >= GameData.MaxActiveContracts) return false;
            Offers.Remove(c);
            Active.Add(c);
            return true;
        }

        // ---- end of a voyage --------------------------------------------------------------

        public SaleReport QuoteSale(List<CaughtFish> catchList, float now)
        {
            var quote = new GameState(0) { Hull = Hull };
            System.Array.Copy(Market, quote.Market, Market.Length);
            quote.Owned.UnionWith(Owned);
            foreach (var c in Active)
                quote.Active.Add(new Contract { Species = c.Species, Count = c.Count,
                    Delivered = c.Delivered, VoyagesLeft = c.VoyagesLeft, Reward = c.Reward });
            return quote.Sell(catchList, now);
        }

        public SaleReport Sell(List<CaughtFish> catchList, float now)
        {
            var r = new SaleReport { Caught = catchList.Count };
            foreach (var f in catchList)
            {
                var c = Active.Find(k => k.Species == f.Species && k.Delivered < k.Count);
                if (c != null)
                {
                    c.Delivered++;
                    r.ToContracts++;
                    // Contract progress is a bonus objective, never an unpaid fish sink.
                    // Pay normal market value below even when this contract is unfinished.
                }
                int v = ValueOf(f, now);
                r.Coins += v;
                r.FishSold++;
                Coins += v;
                Market[(int)f.Species] = Mathf.Max(0.5f, Market[(int)f.Species] * Mathf.Pow(GameData.MarketDropPerFish, f.Kg / (Hull >= 2 ? 5f : .5f)));
            }
            for (int i = Active.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                var c = Active[i];
                if (c.Delivered < c.Count) continue;
                Coins += c.Reward;
                r.ContractCoins += c.Reward;
                r.Lines.Add($"Contract done: {c.Title}  +{c.Reward}");
                Active.RemoveAt(i);
            }
            BestVoyageFish = Mathf.Max(BestVoyageFish, catchList.Count);
            foreach (var f in catchList)
            {
                int i = (int)f.Species;
                if (LogCount[i] == 0) r.Lines.Insert(0, $"New in your fish log: {GameData.Get(f.Species).Name}!");
                LogCount[i]++;
                LogBest[i] = Mathf.Max(LogBest[i], f.Kg);
            }
            UnseenSale = true;
            LastSale = r;
            return r;
        }

        public void EndVoyage(SaleReport r)
        {
            Voyage++;
            for (int i = Active.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                if (--Active[i].VoyagesLeft > 0) continue;
                r.Lines.Add($"Contract missed: {Active[i].Title}");
                Active.RemoveAt(i);
            }
            if (Deckhand != null && --Deckhand.VoyagesLeft <= 0)
            {
                r.Lines.Add($"{Deckhand.Name}'s contract ended. Hire someone new on the crew board.");
                Deckhand = null;
            }
            for (int i = ExtraCrew.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                if (--ExtraCrew[i].VoyagesLeft <= 0)
                {
                    r.Lines.Add($"{ExtraCrew[i].Name}'s {ExtraCrew[i].RoleName.ToLower()} contract ended.");
                    ExtraCrew.RemoveAt(i);
                }
            if (Deckhand == null)
            {
                int i = ExtraCrew.FindIndex(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand);
                if (i >= 0) { Deckhand = ExtraCrew[i]; ExtraCrew.RemoveAt(i); }
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < Richness.Length; i++) Richness[i] = Mathf.Min(1f, Richness[i] + GameData.RichnessRecovery);
            for (int i = 0; i < Market.Length; i++) Market[i] += (1f - Market[i]) * GameData.MarketRecovery;
            RollBoard();
            if (Offers.Count > 0) Offers.RemoveAt(0);
            while (Offers.Count < 3) Offers.Add(NewContract());
        }

        // Grades vary, but each role always has enough candidates to fill every berth.
        void RollBoard()
        {
            Board.Clear();
            if (Rand() < 0.75f) Board.Add(NewCrew(Grade.Hand));
            if (Rand() < 0.4f) Board.Add(NewCrew(Grade.Experienced));
            if (Rand() < 0.06f) Board.Add(NewCrew(Grade.Veteran));
            if (Hull > 0)
            {
                if (Rand() < .8f) Board.Add(NewCrew(Grade.Hand, CrewRole.Fisherman));
                if (Rand() < .5f) Board.Add(NewCrew(Grade.Experienced, CrewRole.Fisherman));
                if (Rand() < .08f) Board.Add(NewCrew(Grade.Veteran, CrewRole.Fisherman));
            }
            EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
        }

        public void EnsureCrewRoleOffers()
        {
            int required = HullDef.CrewCap;
            foreach (CrewRole role in new[] { CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman })
            {
                int available = Board.FindAll(c => c.Role == role).Count;
                while (available < required)
                {
                    Board.Add(NewCrew(Grade.Hand, role));
                    available++;
                }
            }
            var deckhands = Board.FindAll(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand);
            var fishermen = Board.FindAll(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman);
            deckhands.Sort((left, right) => left.Grade.CompareTo(right.Grade));
            fishermen.Sort((left, right) => left.Grade.CompareTo(right.Grade));
            Board.Clear();
            for (int i = 0; i < Mathf.Max(deckhands.Count, fishermen.Count); i++)
            {
                if (i < deckhands.Count) Board.Add(deckhands[i]);
                if (i < fishermen.Count) Board.Add(fishermen[i]);
            }
        }

        CrewMember NewCrew(Grade g, CrewRole role = CrewRole.Deckhand)
        {
            string name;
            do name = GameData.Names[Rng.Next(GameData.Names.Length)];
            while (Board.Exists(b => b.Name == name) || System.Linq.Enumerable.Any(Crew, c => c.Name == name));
            return new CrewMember
            {
                Name = name, Grade = g, Role = role, VoyagesLeft = GameData.ContractVoyages(g),
                Suit = GameData.Suits[Rng.Next(GameData.Suits.Length)],
            };
        }

        Contract NewContract()
        {
            // Pick a species that lives in one of the spots, weighted by how common it is.
            var pool = new List<(Species, float)>();
            for (int i = 0; i < GameData.Spots.Length; i++)
                if (Known(i))
                    foreach (var e in GameData.Spots[i].Table)
                        pool.Add(e);
            float total = 0f;
            foreach (var e in pool) total += e.Item2;
            float roll = Rand() * total;
            var species = pool[0].Item1;
            foreach (var e in pool)
            {
                roll -= e.Item2;
                if (roll <= 0f) { species = e.Item1; break; }
            }

            var def = GameData.Get(species);
            int count = species switch
            {
                Species.Herring or Species.Mackerel => 5 + Rng.Next(5),
                Species.Pollock or Species.Whiting => 3 + Rng.Next(3),
                Species.SeaBass or Species.RedMullet => 2 + Rng.Next(2),
                _ => 1,
            };
            int reward = Mathf.Max(20, Mathf.RoundToInt(count * def.AvgValue * 1.8f / 5f) * 5);
            return new Contract { Species = species, Count = count, VoyagesLeft = 2 + Rng.Next(2), Reward = reward };
        }

        // ---- fishing rolls ----------------------------------------------------------------

        // Longer casts shift the odds toward heavier fish and bigger specimens.
        public (Species, float) RollFish(int spot, float power, bool scraps = false, bool squid = false, bool chum = false)
        {
            // Poor bait is unlimited, but cannot roll a large species or specimen.
            if (scraps)
            {
                var small = GameData.Get(Rng.Next(2) == 0 ? Species.Herring : Species.Mackerel);
                return (small.Species, Mathf.Lerp(small.MinKg, small.MinKg + (small.MaxKg - small.MinKg) * .2f, Rand()));
            }
            power = Mathf.Clamp(power, 0f, 1.4f);
            var table = GameData.Spots[spot].Table;
            float total = 0f;
            var weights = new float[table.Length];
            for (int i = 0; i < table.Length; i++)
            {
                float h = GameData.Get(table[i].species).Heaviness;
                weights[i] = table[i].weight * Mathf.Lerp(1f - 0.7f * h, 1f + 1.6f * h, power);
                // Premium bait targets substantial fish; small schools remain possible, not dominant.
                if (chum) weights[i] *= .01f + h * h * 8f;
                if (squid) weights[i] *= h < .35f ? .04f : .65f + h * 1.6f;
                total += weights[i];
            }
            float roll = Rand() * total;
            int pick = table.Length - 1;
            for (int i = 0; i < table.Length; i++)
            {
                roll -= weights[i];
                if (roll <= 0f) { pick = i; break; }
            }
            var def = GameData.Get(table[pick].species);
            float specimen = Mathf.Pow(Rand(), 1.5f - power);
            if (squid) specimen = Mathf.Lerp(.35f, 1f, specimen);
            if (chum) specimen = Mathf.Lerp(.75f, 1f, specimen);
            float kg = Mathf.Lerp(def.MinKg, def.MaxKg, specimen);
            return (def.Species, kg);
        }

        public float RollBiteWait(int spot, float power)
            => (5f + Rand() * 4f + power * 5f) * GameData.Spots[spot].BiteWait / Mathf.Max(0.3f, Richness[spot]);

        public void OnCatch(int spot, float kg) => Richness[spot] = Mathf.Max(0.3f, Richness[spot] - (Hull >= 2 ? .0035f * kg : GameData.RichnessPerCatch));

        // ---- save / load ----------------------------------------------------------------
        // Saved in the harbour (after selling, after every purchase). A voyage in progress is not saved.

        [System.Serializable]
        class SaveData
        {
            public int Version = 1;
            public int Coins, Bait, Squid, Voyage, BestVoyageFish, Hull;
            public float Fuel;
            public bool HasTimedFuel;
            public int NetInventory;
            // Presence bit distinguishes legacy saves from an explicitly exhausted rack.
            public bool HasNetInventory;
            public int SelectedNet;
            public List<int> DeepNetUses = new();
            public bool UseSquid;
            public PrepOrderSettings PrepOrders;
            public List<CaughtFish> UnsoldCatch = new();
            public List<CoinCredit> CoinCredits = new();
            public float UnsoldMinutes;
            public float FoodMinutes;
            public List<int> Owned = new(), Charts = new();
            public float[] Richness, Market, LogBest;
            public int[] LogCount;
            public bool HasDeckhand;
            public CrewMember Deckhand;
            public List<CrewMember> ExtraCrew = new();
            public List<CrewMember> Board = new();
            public List<Contract> Offers = new(), Active = new();
        }

        public static string SavePath
        {
            get
            {
                // Standalone verification can never target the real save through this probe.
                var args = System.Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
                int flag = System.Array.IndexOf(args, "-save-check-root");
                if (Application.isBatchMode && flag >= 0 && flag + 1 < args.Length)
                {
                    string root = System.IO.Path.GetFullPath(args[flag + 1]);
                    System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(root);
                    return System.IO.Path.Combine(root, "catchline-save.json");
                }
                return System.IO.Path.Combine(Application.persistentDataPath, "catchline-save.json");
            }
        }

        public void Save(string path = null)
        {
            // Preserve an authorized external one-time credit if a live, older in-memory
            // state saves afterwards. NewGame deletes the save, so credits do not respawn.
            path ??= SavePath;
            try
            {
                if (System.IO.File.Exists(path))
                {
                    var previous = JsonUtility.FromJson<SaveData>(System.IO.File.ReadAllText(path));
                    if (previous?.CoinCredits != null)
                        foreach (var credit in previous.CoinCredits)
                            if (!CoinCredits.Exists(c => c.Id == credit.Id))
                            {
                                Coins += credit.Amount;
                                CoinCredits.Add(credit);
                            }
                }
            }
            catch (System.Exception e) { Debug.LogWarning("[Catchline] Credit reconciliation failed: " + e.Message); }
            var d = new SaveData
            {
                Coins = Coins, Bait = Bait, Squid = Squid, Voyage = Voyage, BestVoyageFish = BestVoyageFish, UseSquid = UseSquid, PrepOrders = PrepOrders,
                Hull = Hull, Fuel = Fuel, HasTimedFuel = true, NetInventory = NetInventory, HasNetInventory = true,
                SelectedNet = (int)SelectedNet, DeepNetUses = new List<int>(DeepNetUses),
                FoodMinutes = FoodMinutes, Richness = Richness, Market = Market, LogCount = LogCount, LogBest = LogBest, HasDeckhand = Deckhand != null, Deckhand = Deckhand,
            };
            foreach (var u in Owned) d.Owned.Add((int)u);
            d.Charts.AddRange(Charts);
            d.UnsoldCatch.AddRange(UnsoldCatch); d.UnsoldMinutes = UnsoldMinutes;
            d.CoinCredits.AddRange(CoinCredits);
            d.Board.AddRange(Board);
            d.ExtraCrew.AddRange(ExtraCrew);
            d.Offers.AddRange(Offers);
            d.Active.AddRange(Active);
            try { System.IO.File.WriteAllText(path ?? SavePath, JsonUtility.ToJson(d, true)); }
            catch (System.Exception e) { Debug.LogWarning("[Catchline] Save failed: " + e.Message); }
        }

        public static GameState Load(string path = null)
        {
            try
            {
                path ??= SavePath;
                if (!System.IO.File.Exists(path)) return null;
                var d = JsonUtility.FromJson<SaveData>(System.IO.File.ReadAllText(path));
                if (d == null || d.Version != 1) return null;
                var s = new GameState(System.Environment.TickCount);
                s.Coins = d.Coins; s.Bait = d.Bait; s.Squid = d.Squid; s.Voyage = d.Voyage; s.BestVoyageFish = d.BestVoyageFish;
                s.PrepOrders = d.PrepOrders ?? new PrepOrderSettings();
                s.UseSquid = d.UseSquid; s.FoodMinutes = d.FoodMinutes;
                s.Hull = Mathf.Clamp(d.Hull, 0, GameData.Hulls.Length - 1); s.Fuel = d.Fuel;
                s.NetInventory = d.HasNetInventory ? Mathf.Clamp(d.NetInventory, 0, GameData.NetInventoryCapacity) : 3;
                s.SelectedNet = d.SelectedNet == 1 ? NetKind.Deep : NetKind.Shallow;
                if (d.DeepNetUses != null)
                    foreach (int uses in d.DeepNetUses)
                        if (uses > 0 && s.DeepNetUses.Count < GameData.DeepNetRackCapacity)
                            s.DeepNetUses.Add(Mathf.Clamp(uses, 1, GameData.DeepNetUses));
                foreach (var u in d.Owned) s.Owned.Add((UpgradeId)u);
                s.Fuel = Mathf.Clamp(float.IsNaN(s.Fuel) ? 0 : s.Fuel, 0, s.FuelCapacity);
                foreach (var c in d.Charts) s.Charts.Add(c);
                // Arrays can grow between versions (new seas, new fish): copy what fits.
                if (d.Richness != null) System.Array.Copy(d.Richness, s.Richness, Mathf.Min(d.Richness.Length, s.Richness.Length));
                if (d.Market != null) System.Array.Copy(d.Market, s.Market, Mathf.Min(d.Market.Length, s.Market.Length));
                if (d.LogCount != null) System.Array.Copy(d.LogCount, s.LogCount, Mathf.Min(d.LogCount.Length, s.LogCount.Length));
                if (d.LogBest != null) System.Array.Copy(d.LogBest, s.LogBest, Mathf.Min(d.LogBest.Length, s.LogBest.Length));
                s.Deckhand = d.HasDeckhand ? d.Deckhand : null;
                if (d.ExtraCrew != null) s.ExtraCrew.AddRange(d.ExtraCrew);
                s.Board.Clear(); s.Board.AddRange(d.Board);
                s.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
                if (d.UnsoldCatch != null) s.UnsoldCatch.AddRange(d.UnsoldCatch);
                s.UnsoldMinutes = d.UnsoldMinutes;
                if (d.CoinCredits != null) s.CoinCredits.AddRange(d.CoinCredits);
                s.Offers.Clear(); s.Offers.AddRange(d.Offers);
                s.Active.Clear(); s.Active.AddRange(d.Active);
                return s;
            }
            catch (System.Exception e)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning("[Catchline] Could not load the save: " + e.Message);
                return null;
            }
        }

        public static void DeleteSave()
        {
            try { if (System.IO.File.Exists(SavePath)) System.IO.File.Delete(SavePath); } catch { }
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/GameStateNetLoadout.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public enum NetKind { Shallow, Deep }
    public partial class GameState
    {
        public NetKind SelectedNet;
        // First entry is fitted, subsequent entries are reserves. No default free deep nets.
        public readonly List<int> DeepNetUses = new();
        public int ActiveNetStock => SelectedNet == NetKind.Deep ? DeepNetUses.Count : NetInventory;
        public string NetLoadoutLabel => SelectedNet == NetKind.Deep
            ? $"Deep net · {(DeepNetUses.Count > 0 ? DeepNetUses[0] : 0)}/3 uses · {DeepNetUses.Count} aboard · {NetCapacityKg:0}kg"
            : $"Shallow net · {NetInventory} aboard · {NetCapacityKg:0}kg";
        public bool BuyDeepNet()
        {
            if (DeepNetUses.Count >= GameData.DeepNetRackCapacity || Coins < GameData.DeepNetCost) return false;
            Coins -= GameData.DeepNetCost;
            DeepNetUses.Add(GameData.DeepNetUses);
            return true;
        }
        public void SpendDeepNetUse()
        {
            if (DeepNetUses.Count == 0) return;
            if (--DeepNetUses[0] <= 0) DeepNetUses.RemoveAt(0);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/GameStateProgression.cs
========================================================================
namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class GameState
    {
        public float NetCapacityKg => Has(UpgradeId.NetSystem) ? 15f : 10f;
        public bool UpgradeAvailable(UpgradeId id) => (id != UpgradeId.NetSystem || Hull >= 2) && (id != UpgradeId.RodHolder || Hull == 0);
        public const int CompactFisherCost = 4000;
        public bool CanRefitCompact => Hull == 1 && Coins >= CompactFisherCost;
        public bool RefitCompact()
        {
            if (!CanRefitCompact) return false;
            Coins -= CompactFisherCost; Hull = 2; Fuel = FuelCapacity;
            EnsureCrewRoleOffers(); return true;
        }
        public int UpgradeCost(UpgradeId id)
        {
            if (id == UpgradeId.NetSystem) return 5000;
            if (Hull < 2) return GameData.Upgrade(id).Cost;
            return id switch
            {
                UpgradeId.RodHolder => 1500,
                UpgradeId.LunchTin => 1500,
                UpgradeId.RodFibreglass => 1800,
                UpgradeId.IceBox => 2500,
                UpgradeId.BigCooler => 3000,
                UpgradeId.RodCarbon => 3500,
                _ => GameData.Upgrade(id).Cost
            };
        }
        public string UpgradeDescription(UpgradeId id) => id == UpgradeId.BigCooler && Hull >= 2
            ? "Expand the fish hold from100kg to150kg. More room for rods and repeated net hauls."
            : GameData.Upgrade(id).Desc;
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/PtsWater.cs
========================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // Supplies the original PTS shading without its open-ocean managers/passes.
    public static class PtsWater
    {
        public const float MotionSpeed = .65f;
        public static float Clock(float simulationTime) => simulationTime * MotionSpeed;

        // Same five Gerstner height terms as the GPU; hull-sized sampling filters
        // short ripples rather than making the whole boat follow each small crest.
        public static float Height(Vector3 position, float simulationTime)
        {
            float height = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
            {
                float scale = Mathf.Lerp(.65f, 1.45f, i / 4f);
                float direction = (35f + (i - 2) * 23f) * Mathf.Deg2Rad;
                float frequency = 2 * Mathf.PI / (1.5f * scale);
                float phase = (Mathf.Sin(direction) * position.x + Mathf.Cos(direction) * position.z) * frequency
                    - Clock(simulationTime) * .55f * Mathf.Sqrt(9.8f * frequency);
                height += Mathf.Sin(phase) * .035f * scale / 5;
            }
            return height;
        }

        public static void HullPose(Vector3 center, float heading, bool skiff, float time, out float heave, out Quaternion tilt)
        {
            var yaw = Quaternion.Euler(0, heading, 0);
            Vector3 along = yaw * Vector3.right * (skiff ? 2.2f : 1.4f);
            Vector3 across = yaw * Vector3.forward * (skiff ? 1f : .65f);
            float bow = Height(center + along, time), stern = Height(center - along, time);
            float port = Height(center + across, time), starboard = Height(center - across, time);
            heave = (Height(center, time) * 2 + bow + stern + port + starboard) / 6;
            float pitch = -Mathf.Atan2(port - starboard, across.magnitude * 2) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;
            float roll = Mathf.Atan2(bow - stern, along.magnitude * 2) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;
            tilt = Quaternion.Euler(Mathf.Clamp(pitch, -.8f, .8f), 0, Mathf.Clamp(roll, -.8f, .8f));
        }

        static Texture2D neutral;
        static Cubemap sky;

        public static void Configure(Material material, Texture2D packedNormals, Texture2D dither)
        {
            if (!material.HasProperty("_AbsorptionColor")) return;
            material.SetTexture("_SurfaceNormals", packedNormals);
            material.SetTexture("_DitherPattern", dither);
            if (neutral == null)
            {
                neutral = new Texture2D(1, 1, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false, true) { name = "Neutral PTS interaction buffer" };
                neutral.SetPixel(0, 0, new Color(0, .5f, .5f, .5f));
                neutral.Apply(false, true);
            }
            material.SetTexture("_WaterBufferA", neutral);
            material.SetTexture("_WaterBufferB", neutral);
            if (sky == null)
            {
                const int size = 32;
                sky = new Cubemap(size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, true) { name = "Coastal daylight reflection" };
                for (int face = 0; face < 6; face++)
                {
                    var pixels = new Color[size * size];
                    for (int y = 0; y < size; y++)
                        for (int x = 0; x < size; x++)
                        {
                            float u = 2f * (x + .5f) / size - 1f, v = 2f * (y + .5f) / size - 1f;
                            Vector3 direction = face switch
                            {
                                0 => new Vector3(1, -v, -u), 1 => new Vector3(-1, -v, u),
                                2 => new Vector3(u, 1, v), 3 => new Vector3(u, -1, -v),
                                4 => new Vector3(u, -v, 1), _ => new Vector3(-u, -v, -1)
                            };
                            float elevation = direction.normalized.y;
                            pixels[y * size + x] = Color.Lerp(new Color(.80f, .88f, .87f), new Color(.28f, .57f, .76f), Mathf.Pow(Mathf.Max(0, elevation), .45f));
                        }
                    sky.SetPixels(pixels, (CubemapFace)face);
                }
                sky.Apply(true, true);
            }
            material.SetTexture("_CubemapTexture", sky);
            material.EnableKeyword("_REFLECTION_CUBEMAP");
            material.DisableKeyword("USE_STRUCTURED_BUFFER");
            material.SetFloat("_BoatAttack_Water_DistanceBlend", 256);
            material.SetFloat("_BoatAttack_water_FoamIntensity", 0);
            material.SetFloat("_DetailScale", .8f);
            material.SetFloat("_WaterSmoothness", .76f);
            material.SetFloat("_SpecularStrength", .085f);
            material.SetFloat("_WaveSteepness", .18f);
            SetBiome(material, 0);
        }

        public static void SetBiome(Material material, int kind)
        {
            // PTS optical model, calibrated to each existing boat-sized location.
            material.SetVector("_AbsorptionColor", new Vector4(.06f, .66f, .84f, 1));
            material.SetVector("_ScatteringColor", kind == 4 ? new Vector4(.018f, .26f, .43f, 1)
                : kind == 1 ? new Vector4(.025f, .32f, .43f, 1)
                : kind == 2 ? new Vector4(.025f, .34f, .48f, 1) : new Vector4(.025f, .31f, .43f, 1));
            material.SetFloat("_MaxDepth", kind == 2 ? 3.5f : kind == 4 ? 1.3f : 3f);
            material.SetFloat("_BoatAttack_Water_MicroWaveIntensity", .22f);
            const int count = 5;
            var waves = new Vector4[20];
            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                float scale = Mathf.Lerp(.65f, 1.45f, i / 4f);
                waves[i] = new Vector4(.035f * scale, 35f + (i - 2) * 23f, 1.5f * scale, 0);
            }
            material.SetInt("_WaveCount", count);
            material.SetVectorArray("waveData", waves);
            material.SetFloat("_MaxWaveHeight", .055f);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/SceneryLife.cs
========================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // Small ambient behaviours on scenery models. Purely visual: they read real time, not the voyage clock.

    // A perched seagull: turns its head about, flaps now and then, and sometimes flies off and comes back later.
    public class Gull : MonoBehaviour
    {
        Transform wingL, wingR;
        Quaternion baseL, baseR;
        Vector3 perch;
        Quaternion perchRot;
        float nextFlap, flapUntil, nextTurn, flightT = -1f, awayUntil;
        Vector3 flightDir;
        System.Random rng;

        float R(float a, float b) => a + (float)rng.NextDouble() * (b - a);

        void Start()
        {
            rng = new System.Random(GetInstanceID());
            wingL = ArtLibrary.Find(gameObject, "Wing_L");
            wingR = ArtLibrary.Find(gameObject, "Wing_R");
            if (wingL != null) baseL = wingL.localRotation;
            if (wingR != null) baseR = wingR.localRotation;
            perch = transform.localPosition;
            perchRot = transform.localRotation;
            nextFlap = Time.time + R(2f, 8f);
            nextTurn = Time.time + R(1f, 4f);
        }

        void Update()
        {
            float t = Time.time;
            if (flightT >= 0f)
            {
                // Flying away: climb and leave, then reappear on the perch after a while.
                flightT += Time.deltaTime;
                transform.localPosition = perch + flightDir * (flightT * 3.5f) + Vector3.up * (flightT * flightT * 0.9f + flightT * 0.8f);
                Flap(t, 14f, 55f);
                if (flightT > 6f)
                {
                    flightT = -1f;
                    awayUntil = t + R(10f, 25f);
                    SetVisible(false);
                }
                return;
            }
            if (awayUntil > 0f)
            {
                if (t < awayUntil) return;
                awayUntil = 0f;
                transform.localPosition = perch;
                transform.localRotation = perchRot;
                SetVisible(true);
                flapUntil = t + 0.8f;
            }
            if (t > nextTurn)
            {
                transform.localRotation = perchRot * Quaternion.Euler(0f, R(-50f, 50f), 0f);
                nextTurn = t + R(1.5f, 5f);
            }
            if (t > nextFlap)
            {
                flapUntil = t + R(0.5f, 1.2f);
                nextFlap = t + R(4f, 12f);
                if (rng.NextDouble() < 0.12) FlyOff();
            }
            if (t < flapUntil) Flap(t, 12f, 45f);
            else Flap(0f, 0f, 0f);
        }

        public void FlyOff()
        {
            if (flightT >= 0f || awayUntil > 0f) return;
            flightT = 0f;
            float a = R(0f, Mathf.PI * 2f);
            flightDir = new Vector3(Mathf.Cos(a), 0f, Mathf.Sin(a));
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(flightDir);
        }

        void Flap(float t, float speed, float amp)
        {
            float a = Mathf.Sin(t * speed) * amp;
            if (wingL != null) wingL.localRotation = baseL * Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, a);
            if (wingR != null) wingR.localRotation = baseR * Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, -a);
        }

        void SetVisible(bool on)
        {
            foreach (var r in GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>()) r.enabled = on;
        }
    }

    // Bobs and rocks a floating object (buoys, moored boats).
    public class Floater : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public float Amp = 0.04f, Rock = 3f, Speed = 1f;
        Vector3 basePos;
        Quaternion baseRot;
        float phase;

        void Start()
        {
            basePos = transform.localPosition;
            baseRot = transform.localRotation;
            phase = (GetInstanceID() % 100) * 0.37f;
        }

        void Update()
        {
            float t = Time.time * Speed + phase;
            transform.localPosition = basePos + Vector3.up * (Mathf.Sin(t * 1.3f) * Amp);
            transform.localRotation = baseRot * Quaternion.Euler(Mathf.Sin(t) * Rock, 0f, Mathf.Sin(t * 0.8f + 1f) * Rock);
        }
    }

    // Slowly rises and sinks a part (the whale tail, later), or spins one (propellers, winch drums).
    public class Spinner : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public Vector3 Axis = Vector3.forward;
        public float DegreesPerSecond;
        void Update() => transform.Rotate(Axis, DegreesPerSecond * Time.deltaTime, Space.Self);
    }

    // A whale surfacing now and then far off: the tail rises out of the water, hangs, and slides back down.
    public class WhaleTail : MonoBehaviour
    {
        Vector3 basePos;
        float next, t = -1f;

        void Start()
        {
            basePos = transform.localPosition;
            transform.localPosition = basePos + Vector3.down * 2f;
            next = Time.time + 4f;
        }

        void Update()
        {
            if (t < 0f)
            {
                if (Time.time < next) return;
                t = 0f;
            }
            t += Time.deltaTime;
            // 0-1.5 s rise, 1.5-3.5 s hold, 3.5-5 s sink.
            float up = t < 1.5f ? t / 1.5f : t < 3.5f ? 1f : Mathf.Max(0f, 1f - (t - 3.5f) / 1.5f);
            up = Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, up);
            transform.localPosition = basePos + Vector3.down * (2f * (1f - up));
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, transform.localEulerAngles.y, (1f - up) * 25f);
            if (t > 5f)
            {
                t = -1f;
                next = Time.time + Random.Range(25f, 60f);
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/Ui.cs

using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // IMGUI styles in the Catchline palette: cream cards, navy text, orange buttons.
    // The supplied sliced artwork and Fredoka font are shared by HUD, shops and menus.
    public static class Ui
    {
        public static readonly Color Ink = Palette.HullNavy;
        public static readonly Color Muted = Palette.Hex("52747C");

        public static GUIStyle Panel, Card, CardOn, Title, H2, Body, Small, Mutedish, Center, Btn, BtnAlt, BtnOff, Tab, TabOn,
            Action, ActionHot, ActionOff, Chip, ToastStyle, BarBg, BarFill, BarWarn, BarGood,
            WhiteCenter, WhiteSmall, WhiteCenterSmall, Bubble, BubbleHot, DotWhite, DotRed, DotGold, Ring, FishShape, Water, MiniTitle, MiniHot,
            LogoStyle, TitleCenter, MutedCenter, Reward, ScrollTrack, ScrollThumb, Slider, SliderThumb;

        static bool ready;
        static System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<GUIStyle, int> typeSizes;
        static float typeScale;

        public static void SetTypeScale(float scale)
        {
            if (typeSizes == null)
            {
                typeSizes = new();
                foreach (var style in new[] { Title, H2, Body, Small, Mutedish, Center, Btn, BtnAlt, BtnOff, Tab, TabOn, WhiteCenter, WhiteSmall, WhiteCenterSmall, Bubble, BubbleHot, MiniTitle, MiniHot, LogoStyle, TitleCenter, MutedCenter })
                    typeSizes[style] = style.fontSize;
            }
            if (Mathf.Approximately(typeScale, scale)) return;
            typeScale = scale;
            foreach (var pair in typeSizes) pair.Key.fontSize = Mathf.RoundToInt(pair.Value * scale);
        }

        public static void Init()
        {
            if (ready && Panel.normal.background != null) return;
            ready = true;
            typeSizes = null;
            typeScale = 0f;

            BtnAlt = Button(Palette.Sea, Color.white, 20);
            BtnOff = Button(Palette.Hex("D6D2C4"), Palette.Hex("5F6B68"), 20);
            Tab = Button(new Color(0.88f, 0.84f, 0.76f), Ink, 17);
            TabOn = Button(Palette.HullNavy, Color.white, 17);
            Action = Button(Palette.Accent, Color.white, 26);
            ActionHot = Button(Palette.Oilskin, Palette.HullNavy, 30);
            ActionOff = Button(new Color(0.55f, 0.6f, 0.62f, 0.9f), Color.white, 20);
            Chip = Box(Round(new Color(0.09f, 0.25f, 0.34f, 0.88f), 12), 12, 12);
            Chip.normal.textColor = Color.white;
            Chip.fontSize = 15;
            Chip.wordWrap = true;
            ToastStyle = Box(Round(new Color(0.09f, 0.25f, 0.34f, 0.94f), 14), 16, 14);
            ToastStyle.normal.textColor = Color.white;
            ToastStyle.fontSize = 17;
            ToastStyle.alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;
            ToastStyle.wordWrap = true;
            BarBg = Box(Round(new Color(0.09f, 0.25f, 0.34f, 0.15f), 6), 0, 6);
            BarFill = Box(Round(Palette.Sea, 6), 0, 6);
            BarWarn = Box(Round(Palette.Bad, 6), 0, 6);
            BarGood = Box(Round(Palette.Good, 6), 0, 6);

            WhiteCenter = Text(20, Color.white, FontStyle.Normal, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            WhiteSmall = Text(16, Palette.Cream);
            WhiteCenterSmall = Text(17, Palette.Cream, FontStyle.Normal, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            MiniTitle = Text(22, Color.white, FontStyle.Bold, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            MiniHot = Text(30, Palette.Oilskin, FontStyle.Bold, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            Bubble = Button(Color.white, Ink, 18);
            Bubble.normal.background = Round(Color.white, 14, Palette.Accent, 3);
            BubbleHot = Button(Palette.Oilskin, Ink, 24);
            DotWhite = Box(Round(Color.white, 13), 0, 13);
            DotRed = Box(Round(Palette.Bad, 13), 0, 13);
            DotGold = Box(Round(Palette.Oilskin, 13), 0, 13);
            Ring = Box(Round(new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.35f), 20), 0, 20);
            FishShape = Box(Round(new Color(0.04f, 0.2f, 0.25f, 0.85f), 10), 0, 10);
            Water = Box(Round(new Color(0.08f, 0.5f, 0.58f, 0.93f), 18, new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.5f), 3), 0, 18);
            LogoStyle = Text(64, Palette.HullNavy, FontStyle.Bold, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            TitleCenter = Text(28, Ink, FontStyle.Bold, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            MutedCenter = Text(16, Muted, FontStyle.Normal, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            Reward = new GUIStyle(Panel);
            ScrollTrack = Box(Round(Palette.Hex("DFDED0"), 5), 0, 5);
            ScrollTrack.fixedWidth = 10f;
            ScrollThumb = Box(Round(Palette.Sea, 5), 0, 5);
            ScrollThumb.fixedWidth = 10f;
            ScrollThumb.stretchHeight = true;

            ApplyKit();
            Slider = new GUIStyle(BarBg) { fixedHeight = 12, margin = new RectOffset(0, 0, 10, 0) };
            SliderThumb = Box(Round(Palette.HullNavy, 10), 0, 10);
            SliderThumb.fixedWidth = 26;
            SliderThumb.fixedHeight = 30;
            SliderThumb.margin = new RectOffset(0, 0, -9, 0);
        }

        // ---- Codex's UI kit (Assets/Art/UI), imported at 1x. Borders are the slices.json values halved. ----

        public static Font KitFont;
        static ArtLibrary lib;

        public static Texture2D Tex(string name) => lib != null ? lib.UiTex(name) : null;

        static void ApplyKit()
        {
            lib = ArtLibrary.Get();
            if (lib == null) return;
            KitFont = lib.UiFont;
            if (KitFont != null)
                foreach (var s in new[] { Title, H2, Body, Small, Mutedish, Center, Btn, BtnAlt, BtnOff, Tab, TabOn, Chip, ToastStyle, WhiteCenter, WhiteSmall, WhiteCenterSmall, Bubble, BubbleHot, MiniTitle, MiniHot, LogoStyle, TitleCenter, MutedCenter })
                {
                    s.font = KitFont;
                    if (s.fontStyle == FontStyle.Bold) s.fontStyle = FontStyle.Normal; // Fredoka Medium is already heavy
                }

            Skin(Panel, "panel-cream", 28, 28, 28, 32);
            Skin(Reward, "catch-card", 32, 32, 32, 54);
            Skin(Card, "card-white", 28, 28, 28, 32);
            Skin(CardOn, "card-selected", 28, 28, 28, 32);
            Skin(Chip, "chip-dark", 28, 28, 28, 32);
            Skin(ToastStyle, "toast-dark", 28, 28, 28, 32);
            Button(Btn, "button-primary");
            Button(BtnAlt, "button-secondary");
            Button(BtnOff, "button-disabled");
            Button(Tab, "button-tab-off");
            Button(TabOn, "button-tab-on");
            Skin(Bubble, "bubble-white", 24, 24, 20, 20);
            Skin(BubbleHot, "bubble-hot", 24, 24, 20, 20);
            Skin(BarBg, "bar-background", 12, 12, 9, 9);
            Skin(BarFill, "bar-teal", 12, 12, 9, 9);
            Skin(BarGood, "bar-green", 12, 12, 9, 9);
            Skin(BarWarn, "bar-red", 12, 12, 9, 9);
            // Kit panels carry a drop shadow at the bottom: keep content clear of it.
            foreach (var s in new[] { Card, CardOn })
                s.padding = new RectOffset(16, 16, 12, 16);
        }

        static void Skin(GUIStyle s, string tex, int left, int right, int top, int bottom)
        {
            var t = Tex(tex);
            if (t == null) return;
            s.normal.background = t;
            s.hover.background = t;
            s.border = new RectOffset(left, right, top, bottom);
        }

        static void Button(GUIStyle s, string prefix)
        {
            var normal = Tex(prefix + "-normal");
            var pressed = Tex(prefix + "-pressed");
            if (normal == null) return;
            s.normal.background = s.hover.background = normal;
            s.active.background = pressed != null ? pressed : normal;
            s.border = new RectOffset(22, 22, 20, 20);
            s.padding = new RectOffset(14, 14, 6, 10);
        }

        // Draws a kit sprite scaled to a rect; returns false when the kit is missing so callers can fall back.
        public static bool Sprite(Rect r, string name, float alpha = 1f)
        {
            var t = Tex(name);
            if (t == null) return false;
            var prev = GUI.color;
            GUI.color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, alpha);
            GUI.DrawTexture(r, t, ScaleMode.StretchToFill, true);
            GUI.color = prev;
            return true;
        }

        public static void Bar(Rect r, float t, GUIStyle fill = null)
        {
            if (Tex("bar-background") != null) r.height = Mathf.Max(r.height, 18f);
            GUI.Box(r, GUIContent.none, BarBg);
            t = Mathf.Clamp01(t);
            if (t > 0.01f) GUI.Box(new Rect(r.x, r.y, Mathf.Max(24f, r.width * t), r.height), GUIContent.none, fill ?? BarFill);
        }

        static GUIStyle Box(Texture2D bg, int pad, int radius)
        {
            var s = new GUIStyle
            {
                border = new RectOffset(radius, radius, radius, radius),
                padding = new RectOffset(pad, pad, pad, pad),
            };
            s.normal.background = bg;
            return s;
        }

        static GUIStyle Text(int size, Color c, FontStyle fs = FontStyle.Normal, TextAnchor a = TextAnchor.UpperLeft)
        {
            var s = new GUIStyle { fontSize = size, fontStyle = fs, alignment = a, wordWrap = true, richText = true };
            // IMGUI's font pass converts these colours to linear without the matching
            // output conversion in a linear project. Compensate so kit ink stays navy.
            s.normal.textColor = QualitySettings.activeColorSpace == ColorSpace.Linear ? c.gamma : c;
            return s;
        }

        static GUIStyle Button(Color fill, Color text, int size)
        {
            var s = Box(Round(fill, 12), 10, 12);
            s.hover.background = Round(Color.Lerp(fill, Color.white, 0.12f), 12);
            s.active.background = Round(Color.Lerp(fill, Color.black, 0.12f), 12);
            var ink = QualitySettings.activeColorSpace == ColorSpace.Linear ? text.gamma : text;
            s.normal.textColor = s.hover.textColor = s.active.textColor = ink;
            s.fontSize = size;
            s.fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold;
            s.alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;
            s.wordWrap = true;
            s.richText = true;
            return s;
        }

        // Anti-aliased rounded rectangle, optionally with a border, for 9-slicing.
        static Texture2D Round(Color fill, int r, Color? border = null, int borderWidth = 2)
        {
            int size = r * 2 + 4;
            var tex = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false) { wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp, hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave };
            var px = new Color[size * size];
            for (int y = 0; y < size; y++)
                for (int x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    float cx = Mathf.Clamp(x + 0.5f, r, size - r);
                    float cy = Mathf.Clamp(y + 0.5f, r, size - r);
                    float d = Mathf.Sqrt((x + 0.5f - cx) * (x + 0.5f - cx) + (y + 0.5f - cy) * (y + 0.5f - cy));
                    float alpha = Mathf.Clamp01(r - d + 0.5f);
                    var c = fill;
                    if (border.HasValue && d > r - borderWidth) c = Color.Lerp(fill, border.Value, Mathf.Clamp01(d - (r - borderWidth) + 0.5f) * border.Value.a / Mathf.Max(0.01f, border.Value.a));
                    c.a *= alpha;
                    px[y * size + x] = c;
                }
            tex.SetPixels(px);
            tex.Apply();
            return tex;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageAudio.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // Original, generated foley. No downloaded recordings or per-frame audio allocations.
    public sealed class VoyageAudio : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public enum Cue { Cast, Splash, Bite, Reel, Land, Stow, Moor, Sale, Select, Purchase }
        readonly Dictionary<Cue, AudioClip> clips = new();
        AudioSource ambience, foley;
        AudioClip sea;
        float nextOar;
        bool preview;
        float previewVolume = .65f;
        public float Volume
        {
            get => preview ? previewVolume : PlayerPrefs.GetFloat("catchline.sound", .65f);
            set { if (preview) previewVolume = value; else { PlayerPrefs.SetFloat("catchline.sound", value); PlayerPrefs.Save(); } }
        }

        public void Initialize(bool sessionOnly)
        {
            preview = sessionOnly;
            ambience = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
            foley = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
            ambience.spatialBlend = foley.spatialBlend = 0;
            ambience.loop = true;
            sea = MakeSea();
            ambience.clip = sea;
            foreach (Cue cue in Enum.GetValues(typeof(Cue))) clips[cue] = MakeCue(cue);
            ambience.volume = Volume * .23f;
            ambience.Play();
        }

        public void Play(Cue cue, float gain = 1f)
        {
            if (foley != null && Volume > .001f) foley.PlayOneShot(clips[cue], Volume * gain);
        }

        public void Mix(bool paused, bool harbour, bool rowing)
        {
            if (ambience == null) return;
            ambience.volume = Volume * (paused ? .06f : harbour ? .16f : .23f);
            foley.volume = paused ? 0 : 1;
            if (rowing && !paused && Time.time >= nextOar)
            {
                nextOar = Time.time + 1.96f;
                Play(Cue.Splash, .28f);
                Play(Cue.Moor, .12f);
            }
        }

        static AudioClip MakeSea()
        {
            const int rate = 22050, seconds = 16;
            var samples = new float[rate * seconds * 2];
            var random = new System.Random(4106);
            float lowL = 0, lowR = 0, smoothL = 0, smoothR = 0;
            for (int n = 0; n < rate * seconds; n++)
            {
                float t = n / (float)rate;
                lowL = Mathf.Lerp(lowL, (float)random.NextDouble() * 2 - 1, .055f);
                lowR = Mathf.Lerp(lowR, (float)random.NextDouble() * 2 - 1, .055f);
                smoothL = Mathf.Lerp(smoothL, lowL, .1f);
                smoothR = Mathf.Lerp(smoothR, lowR, .1f);
                float lap = .35f + .65f * Mathf.Pow(.5f + .5f * Mathf.Sin(t * Mathf.PI * .5f), 2);
                // Bring both ends to silence for a click-free looping bed.
                float seam = Mathf.Min(1f, Mathf.Min(t * 2, (seconds - t) * 2));
                samples[n * 2] = smoothL * lap * seam * 1.8f;
                samples[n * 2 + 1] = smoothR * lap * seam * 1.8f;
            }
            var clip = AudioClip.Create("Catchline coastal water", rate * seconds, 2, rate, false);
            clip.SetData(samples, 0);
            return clip;
        }

        static AudioClip MakeCue(Cue cue)
        {
            const int rate = 22050;
            float duration = cue == Cue.Reel ? 1.2f : cue == Cue.Splash ? .65f : cue == Cue.Sale ? .65f : .32f;
            var samples = new float[(int)(rate * duration)];
            var random = new System.Random(730 + (int)cue);
            float low = 0;
            for (int n = 0; n < samples.Length; n++)
            {
                float t = n / (float)rate, noise = (float)random.NextDouble() * 2 - 1;
                low = Mathf.Lerp(low, noise, .16f);
                float envelope = Mathf.Min(1, t * 180) * Mathf.Exp(-t * 10);
                float sample;
                switch (cue)
                {
                    case Cue.Select:
                        sample = (Mathf.Sin(6.283f * 220 * t) * .12f + low * .2f) * envelope; break;
                    case Cue.Purchase:
                        // Paper rustle and a short coin touch, not a musical reward jingle.
                        sample = low * Mathf.Exp(-t * 22) * .32f + Mathf.Sin(6.283f * 1740 * t) * envelope * .055f; break;
                    case Cue.Cast: sample = low * Mathf.Sin(Mathf.PI * t / duration) * .65f; break;
                    case Cue.Splash:
                        sample = low * Mathf.Exp(-t * 5) * .9f + Mathf.Sin(6.283f * (320 * t - 110 * t * t)) * envelope * .07f; break;
                    case Cue.Bite:
                        sample = (Mathf.Sin(6.283f * (600 * t - 260 * t * t)) * .19f + low * .35f) * envelope; break;
                    case Cue.Reel:
                        float tooth = t % .065f;
                        sample = (Mathf.Sin(6.283f * 850 * tooth) * .15f + low * .5f) * Mathf.Exp(-tooth * 180) * Mathf.Min(1, (duration - t) * 8); break;
                    case Cue.Sale:
                        float coin = t % .14f;
                        sample = (Mathf.Sin(6.283f * 2110 * coin) + .4f * Mathf.Sin(6.283f * 3263 * coin)) * Mathf.Exp(-coin * 75) * .11f * (1 - t / duration); break;
                    default:
                        float pitch = cue == Cue.Moor ? 135 : cue == Cue.Land ? 280 : 190;
                        sample = (Mathf.Sin(6.283f * pitch * t) * .21f + Mathf.Sin(6.283f * pitch * 2.73f * t) * .08f + low * .3f) * envelope; break;
                }
                samples[n] = sample * Mathf.Clamp01((duration - t) * 100);
            }
            var clip = AudioClip.Create("Catchline " + cue, samples.Length, 1, rate, false);
            clip.SetData(samples, 0);
            return clip;
        }

        void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (sea != null) Destroy(sea);
            foreach (var clip in clips.Values) Destroy(clip);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageCapture.cs
