using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        bool captureFreeze;

        // Opt-in presentation checks run only in the save-isolated preview scene.
        // These are real player frames including OnGUI, not camera-only mockups.
        void Start()
        {
            var args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Launch mode: {(SessionOnly ? "TEST / no save reads or writes" : "REAL / persistent progress")}; save={GameState.SavePath}");
            if (!SessionOnly && Application.isBatchMode && Array.IndexOf(args, "-persistence-probe") >= 0)
            {
                int probe = Array.IndexOf(args, "-save-check-root");
                if (probe < 0 || probe + 1 >= args.Length || !System.IO.Path.GetFileName(System.IO.Path.GetFullPath(args[probe + 1]).TrimEnd(System.IO.Path.DirectorySeparatorChar)).StartsWith("net023-probe-")) { Application.Quit(3); return; }
                if (Array.IndexOf(args, "-probe-write") >= 0)
                {
                    State.Coins = 4321; State.Hull = 2; State.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.NetSystem);
                    State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
                    State.UnsoldCatch.Clear(); State.UnsoldCatch.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Cod, Kg = 6 });
                    State.Save();
                    Debug.Log("[Catchline] PERSISTENCE PROBE WRITE PASSED");
                }
                else if (State.Coins == 4321 && State.Hull == 2 && State.Has(UpgradeId.NetSystem) && State.UnsoldCatch.Count == 1 && State.UnsoldCatch[0].Kg == 6
                    && State.Board.FindAll(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman).Count >= State.HullDef.CrewCap
                    && State.Board.FindAll(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand).Count >= State.HullDef.CrewCap)
                    Debug.Log("[Catchline] PERSISTENCE PROBE RELOAD PASSED");
                else { Debug.LogError("[Catchline] Persistence probe failed"); Application.Quit(4); return; }
                Application.Quit(0); return;
            }
            if (SessionOnly && Array.IndexOf(args, "-prepboat-trial") >= 0)
            {
                if (!StartPrepBoatTrial()) Debug.LogError("[Catchline] Could not start Tier4 prep boat trial");
                else Debug.Log("[Catchline] TIER033 trial: approved6.8x3.5m hull, four temporary crew, no save reads or writes; prep/chum automation active");
                if (Array.IndexOf(args, "-prepboat-ice") >= 0) { State.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.IceBox); SyncUpgrades(); }
            }
            else if (SessionOnly && Array.IndexOf(args, "-crew-mix-trial") >= 0)
            {
                if (!StartCrewMixTrial()) Debug.LogError("[Catchline] Could not start the crew mix trial");
                else Debug.Log("[Catchline] Crew mix trial: compact fisher at harbour, empty berths,1000coins,temporary contracts,save isolated");
            }
            else if (SessionOnly && Array.IndexOf(args, "-netboat-trial") >= 0)
            {
                StartNetBoatTrial();
                Debug.Log("[Catchline] Net boat trial: hull2,200coins,2fishers+1deckhand,60bait,full fuel; save isolated");
            }
            else if (SessionOnly && Array.IndexOf(args, "-deep-net-trial") >= 0)
            {
                if (Array.IndexOf(args, "-netboat-trial") < 0) StartNetBoatTrial();
                State.SelectedNet = NetKind.Deep; State.DeepNetUses.Clear(); State.DeepNetUses.AddRange(new[] { 3, 3 });
                Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Weight020 deep net trial: {State.CoolerCapacity:0.#}kg hold, deep3uses+reserve,200coins; save isolated");
            }
            int orderCapture = Array.IndexOf(args, "-capture-prep-orders");
            if (SessionOnly && orderCapture >= 0 && orderCapture + 1 < args.Length)
            {
                if (!IsPrepFisher) StartPrepBoatTrial();
                StartCoroutine(CapturePrepOrders(Path.GetFullPath(args[orderCapture + 1])));
            }
            int prepCapture = Array.IndexOf(args, "-capture-tier4");
            if (SessionOnly && prepCapture >= 0 && prepCapture + 1 < args.Length)
            {
                if (!IsPrepFisher) StartPrepBoatTrial();
                StartCoroutine(CapturePrepBoat(Path.GetFullPath(args[prepCapture + 1])));
            }
            if (Array.IndexOf(args, "-native-display") >= 0)
            {
                int width = Display.main.systemWidth, height = Display.main.systemHeight;
                Screen.SetResolution(width, height, FullScreenMode.FullScreenWindow);
                Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Native borderless display requested: {width}x{height}; render scale1");
            }
            if (SessionOnly && Array.IndexOf(args, "-chart-review") >= 0)
            {
                if (Array.IndexOf(args, "-netboat-trial") < 0) StartNetBoatTrial();
                Showcase(-1, 0); hudTab = HudTab.HarbourMaster; panelOpen = true; toastUntil = 0;
                RefreshInterface();
                var plate = Resources.Load<Texture2D>("Charts/gull-coast");
                if (plate == null) Debug.LogError("[Catchline] Illustrated chart resource missing");
                else Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Illustrated chart ready: {plate.width}x{plate.height}; live native markers, routes and navigation; save isolated");
            }
            int flag = Array.IndexOf(args, "-capture-voyage");
            if (SessionOnly && flag >= 0 && Application.isBatchMode)
            {
                Debug.LogError("[Catchline] Native HUD capture needs a rendered player window; use scratch RenderMaps for background camera-only checks.");
                Application.Quit(2);
                return;
            }
            if (SessionOnly && flag >= 0 && flag + 1 < args.Length)
                StartCoroutine(Array.IndexOf(args, "-capture-polish") >= 0 ? CapturePolish(Path.GetFullPath(args[flag + 1]))
                    : Array.IndexOf(args, "-capture-urgent") >= 0 ? CaptureUrgent(Path.GetFullPath(args[flag + 1])) : CaptureVoyage(Path.GetFullPath(args[flag + 1])));
        }

        IEnumerator CapturePolish(string directory)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            Application.runInBackground = true;
            captureFreeze = true;
            AutoPlay = showTitle = false;
            State.FoodMinutes = 20;
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++) State.Charts.Add(spot);
            // Real route actions, frozen at readable manoeuvre stages for framing review.
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                SelectedSpot = spot;
                SetSail();
                float duration = GameData.Spots[spot].RowSeconds;
                Tick(duration * .17f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"route-{spot}-01-clearance");
                Tick(duration * .27f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"route-{spot}-02-bearing");
                Tick(duration * .28f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"route-{spot}-03-channel-approach");
                CaptureUntil(() => CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing, "route fishing");
                Tick(4f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"map-{spot}-03-fishing");
                if (spot == 0)
                {
                    // Native moving-water sequence; no camera-only rendering or compositing.
                    for (int n = 0; n < 12; n++)
                    {
                        Tick(.12f);
                        yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"water-motion-{n:00}");
                    }
                    captureFreeze = false;
                    yield return null;
                    double total = 0;
                    float worst = 0;
                    for (int frame = 0; frame < 180; frame++)
                    {
                        yield return null;
                        total += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                        worst = Mathf.Max(worst, Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
                    }
                    captureFreeze = true;
                    Debug.Log($"[Catchline] NATIVE FRAME SAMPLE: {Screen.width}x{Screen.height}, {180 / total:0.0} fps mean, worst {worst * 1000:0.0}ms, GPU {SystemInfo.graphicsDeviceName}; desktop sample, not mobile hardware validation");
                    Debug.Log($"[Catchline] AUDIO: listeners={FindObjectsByType<AudioListener>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Length}, sampleRate={AudioSettings.outputSampleRate}, coastalBed={GetComponent<AudioSource>()?.isPlaying}");
                }
                HeadHome();
                Tick(duration * .84f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"route-{spot}-04-align");
                Tick(duration * .10f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"route-{spot}-05-sideways-berth");
                CaptureUntil(() => CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour, "route home");
                Tick(3.5f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, $"route-{spot}-06-moored");
            }
            // Preserve the existing full fishing/market/settings walkthrough too.
            SelectedSpot = 0;
            yield return CaptureVoyage(directory);
        }

        void CaptureUntil(Func<bool> done, string state)
        {
            for (int n = 0; n < 1200 && !done(); n++) Tick(.05f);
            if (!done()) throw new InvalidOperationException("Capture did not reach " + state);
        }

        IEnumerator CaptureFrame(string directory, string name)
        {
            yield return new WaitForEndOfFrame();
            var frame = ScreenCapture.CaptureScreenshotAsTexture();
            if (frame == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Native capture has no render target.");
            File.WriteAllBytes(Path.Combine(directory, name + ".png"), frame.EncodeToPNG());
            Destroy(frame);
        }

        IEnumerator CaptureUrgent(string directory)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            Application.runInBackground = true;
            captureFreeze = true;
            AutoPlay = false;
            showTitle = false;
            Ui.Init();
            // Save-isolated fixture: fund the ordinary hire actions, then test being broke.
            State.Coins = 1000;
            OpenShop(HudTab.CrewOffice);
            yield return null;
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "01-guaranteed-crew");
            State.Hire(State.Board.Find(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand));
            State.Hire(State.Board.Find(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman));
            SyncCrew(); SyncUpgrades();
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "02-two-crew-hired");
            State.Coins = 0; State.Bait = 0; State.Squid = 0; State.FoodMinutes = 20;
            SetSail();
            CaptureUntil(() => CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing, "fishing");
            Tick(4f);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "03-free-bait-two-rods");
            var rod = Rods[0];
            CommandRod(rod);
            CaptureUntil(() => rod.State == RodState.Bite, "bite");
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "04-bite");
            StrikeTap();
            CaptureUntil(() => AwaitingCast, "throw again choice");
            Tick(2f);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "05-throw-or-leave");
            ThrowAgain();
            CaptureUntil(() => rod.State == RodState.Bite, "second bite");
            StrikeTap();
            CaptureUntil(() => AwaitingCast, "second catch");
            Tick(5f);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "06-haul-value");
            LeaveFishing(); HeadHome();
            CaptureUntil(() => CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour, "port");
            Tick(4f);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "07-sell-preview");
            SellHaul();
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "08-paid-receipt");
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] URGENT PRESENTATION COMPLETE: {Screen.width}x{Screen.height}, {directory}");
            Application.Quit(0);
        }

        IEnumerator CaptureVoyage(string directory)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            Application.runInBackground = true;
            captureFreeze = true;
            AutoPlay = false;
            // Consistent typography for review; the user's stored preference is untouched.
            Ui.Init();
            showTitle = true;
            yield return null;
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "01-menu");
            showTitle = false;
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "02-harbour");
            SetSail();
            CaptureUntil(() => CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing, "fishing");
            Tick(4f); // settle the actual camera and dismiss the arrival toast
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "03-fishing");
            var rod = Rods[0];
            CommandRod(rod);
            CaptureUntil(() => rod.State == RodState.Waiting, "waiting");
            Tick(.6f);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "04-waiting");
            CaptureUntil(() => rod.State == RodState.Bite, "bite");
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "05-bite");
            StrikeTap();
            CaptureUntil(() => OnDeck.Count > 0 && CanCarry(OnDeck[0]), "landed fish");
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "06-catch");
            CommandCarry(OnDeck[0]);
            CaptureUntil(() => Cooler.Count == 1, "stowed fish");
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "07-stowed");
            HeadHome();
            CaptureUntil(() => CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour, "sale");
            Tick(3.5f);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "08-unsold");
            SellHaul();
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "08-sale");
            OpenShop(HudTab.Chandlery);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "09-chandlery");
            OpenShop(HudTab.HarbourMaster);
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "10-chart");
            paused = true;
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "11-pause");
            showSettings = true;
            yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "12-settings");
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] PRESENTATION CAPTURE COMPLETE: {Screen.width}x{Screen.height}, {directory}");
            Application.Quit(0);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageCatchRewards.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public NetCatchReceipt LastNetReceipt { get; private set; }
        float netReceiptUntil;
        const float CatchDisplaySeconds = 6, NetReceiptSeconds = 11;
        static readonly Color RewardWhite = Palette.Hex("F4F1E8");
        static readonly Color RewardMuted = Palette.Hex("B3CAD2");

        void RecordStoredNet(IEnumerable<CaughtFish> stored)
        {
            var receipt = new NetCatchReceipt(stored, State, SimTime);
            if (receipt.Count == 0) return;
            LastNetReceipt = receipt;
            netReceiptUntil = SimTime + NetReceiptSeconds;
        }
        void ResetCatchReceipts() { LastNetReceipt = null; netReceiptUntil = 0; }
        void RewardSurface(string id, Rect r, Color accent, bool halo = false, float alpha = 1)
        {
            var rect = coastalUi.Box(id, r, "", Color.clear, blocks: false);
            // UGUI permits only one Graphic on each object. Keep the retained
            // element as its lifecycle/position owner and draw in a fill child.
            var surface = rect.Find("Reward surface") as RectTransform;
            if (surface == null)
            {
                surface = new GameObject("Reward surface", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(CatchRewardGraphic)).GetComponent<RectTransform>();
                surface.gameObject.layer = 5;
                surface.SetParent(rect, false);
                surface.anchorMin = Vector2.zero; surface.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
                surface.offsetMin = surface.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
            }
            var graphic = surface.GetComponent<CatchRewardGraphic>();
            graphic.Style(accent, halo, alpha);
        }
        void RewardText(string id, Rect r, string text, int size, Color color, TextAnchor anchor = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft)
            => coastalUi.Label(id, r, text, size, color, anchor);
        void InterfaceCatchRewards()
        {
            if (SimTime < catchUntil) InterfaceCatchSpecimen();
            if (LastNetReceipt == null) return;
            if (SimTime < netReceiptUntil) InterfaceNetReceipt();
            else
            {
                var review = new Rect(16, ih - 62, 238, 44);
                IButton("catch/net/review", review, $"Last net · {LastNetReceipt.Count} fish", () => netReceiptUntil = SimTime + NetReceiptSeconds, false);
            }
        }
        void InterfaceCatchSpecimen()
        {
            var tier = CatchPresentation.Tier(lastCatchSpecies, lastCatchKg);
            var accent = CatchPresentation.Accent(tier);
            float elapsed = CatchDisplaySeconds - (catchUntil - SimTime);
            float enter = 1 - Mathf.Pow(1 - Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / .28f), 3);
            float width = compactInterface ? 258 : 244, height = compactInterface ? 94 : 210;
            var r = new Rect(iw - width - 16, ih - height - 12 + (1 - enter) * 12, width, height);
            RewardSurface("catch", r, accent); Block(r);
            string title = catchNew ? "New species!" : catchRecord ? "Personal best!" : "Nice catch!";
            int value = State.ValueOf(new CaughtFish { Species = lastCatchSpecies, Kg = lastCatchKg, StowedAt = SimTime }, SimTime);
            if (compactInterface)
            {
                RewardSurface("catch/glow", new Rect(r.x + 1, r.y + 7, 102, 82), accent, true);
                coastalUi.Art("catch/portrait", new Rect(r.x + 5, r.y + 20, 93, 59), FishPortrait(lastCatchSpecies));
                RewardText("catch/tier", new Rect(r.x + 104, r.y + 4, 145, 19), CatchPresentation.Label(tier), 12, accent);
                RewardText("catch/name", new Rect(r.x + 104, r.y + 23, 145, 28), GameData.Get(lastCatchSpecies).Name, 19, RewardWhite);
                RewardText("catch/value", new Rect(r.x + 104, r.y + 51, 145, 20), $"{lastCatchKg:0.00} kg · ~{value} coins", 13, RewardMuted);
                RewardText("catch/achievement", new Rect(r.x + 104, r.y + 70, 145, 18), title, 12, accent);
            }
            else
            {
                RewardText("catch/achievement", new Rect(r.x + 16, r.y + 9, 212, 26), title, 18, RewardWhite);
                RewardSurface("catch/glow", new Rect(r.x + 10, r.y + 29, 224, 112), accent, true);
                coastalUi.Art("catch/portrait", new Rect(r.x + 18, r.y + 43, 208, 85), FishPortrait(lastCatchSpecies));
                RewardText("catch/name", new Rect(r.x + 16, r.y + 126, 212, 32), GameData.Get(lastCatchSpecies).Name, 23, RewardWhite);
                RewardText("catch/tier", new Rect(r.x + 16, r.y + 157, 212, 20), CatchPresentation.Label(tier), 13, accent);
                RewardText("catch/weight", new Rect(r.x + 16, r.y + 180, 98, 23), $"{lastCatchKg:0.00} kg", 17, RewardMuted);
                coastalUi.Art("catch/coin", new Rect(r.x + 124, r.y + 184, 16, 16), "icon-coin");
                RewardText("catch/value", new Rect(r.x + 145, r.y + 180, 83, 23), $"~{value}", 19, RewardWhite, TextAnchor.MiddleRight);
            }
        }
        void InterfaceNetReceipt()
        {
            var receipt = LastNetReceipt;
            var groups = receipt.Items.GroupBy(f => (f.Species, f.Tier)).OrderByDescending(g => g.Key.Tier).ThenBy(g => g.Key.Species).ToList();
            bool small = compactInterface;
            float row = small ? 30 : 40, header = small ? 42 : 54, footer = small ? 32 : 42;
            // A future five-species batch still clears the left instruments on phones.
            float available = ih - 26 - ReferenceRect(20, 416, 260, 72).yMax;
            if (small && groups.Count > 0) row = Mathf.Min(row, (available - header - footer) / groups.Count);
            float height = header + groups.Count * row + footer;
            float width = small ? 266 : 288;
            float elapsed = NetReceiptSeconds - (netReceiptUntil - SimTime);
            float enter = 1 - Mathf.Pow(1 - Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / .28f), 3);
            var r = new Rect(16, ih - height - 14 + (1 - enter) * 12, width, height);
            RewardSurface("catch/net", r, Palette.Hex("78C8BB")); Block(r);
            coastalUi.Art("catch/net/icon", new Rect(r.x + 13, r.y + (small ? 9 : 14), 27, 27), "icon-net");
            RewardText("catch/net/title", new Rect(r.x + 49, r.y + 7, width - 65, small ? 27 : 29), "Net catch stowed", small ? 20 : 22, RewardWhite);
            if (!small) RewardText("catch/net/subtitle", new Rect(r.x + 49, r.y + 33, width - 65, 18), "Safely in your hold", 12, RewardMuted);
            for (int i = 0; i < groups.Count; i++)
            {
                var group = groups[i]; var tier = group.Key.Tier;
                float y = r.y + header + i * row;
                var accent = CatchPresentation.Accent(tier);
                RewardSurface($"catch/net/{i}/glow", new Rect(r.x + 8, y, 61, row), accent, true);
                coastalUi.Art($"catch/net/{i}/portrait", new Rect(r.x + 12, y + 3, 54, row - 6), FishPortrait(group.Key.Species));
                string label = GameData.Get(group.Key.Species).Name + (group.Count() > 1 ? $" ×{group.Count()}" : "");
                if (small)
                {
                    RewardText($"catch/net/{i}/name", new Rect(r.x + 76, y, width - 140, row), label, 15, RewardWhite);
                    RewardText($"catch/net/{i}/weight", new Rect(r.xMax - 62, y, 46, row), $"{group.Sum(f => f.Kg):0.00} kg", 12, RewardMuted, TextAnchor.MiddleRight);
                }
                else
                {
                    RewardText($"catch/net/{i}/name", new Rect(r.x + 76, y, width - 148, row * .6f), label, 17, RewardWhite);
                    RewardText($"catch/net/{i}/weight", new Rect(r.x + 76, y + row * .55f, width - 148, row * .45f), $"{group.Sum(f => f.Kg):0.00} kg", 12, RewardMuted);
                    RewardText($"catch/net/{i}/value", new Rect(r.xMax - 66, y, 50, row), $"~{group.Sum(f => f.Value)}", 18, accent, TextAnchor.MiddleRight);
                }
            }
            float fy = r.yMax - footer;
            coastalUi.Box("catch/net/divider", new Rect(r.x + 16, fy + 1, width - 32, 1), "", new Color(.7f, .85f, .87f, .16f), blocks: false);
            RewardText("catch/net/total", new Rect(r.x + 16, fy + 5, width - 103, footer - 9), $"{receipt.Count} fish · {receipt.Kg:0.00} kg", small ? 13 : 15, RewardMuted);
            coastalUi.Art("catch/net/coin", new Rect(r.xMax - 88, fy + (footer - 18) * .5f, 18, 18), "icon-coin");
            RewardText("catch/net/value", new Rect(r.xMax - 65, fy + 3, 49, footer - 6), $"~{receipt.Value}", small ? 18 : 21, RewardWhite, TextAnchor.MiddleRight);
        }
    }
}

            1 => new(.216f, .278f), 2 => new(.815f, .414f),
            3 => new(.159f, .583f), 4 => new(.607f, .08f), _ => new(.447f, .445f) };

        }

        {

            coastalUi.Button("chart/contracts/close", new Rect(panel.xMax - 58, panel.y + 8, 44, 44), "×", () => chartContractsOpen = false, "button-secondary-normal");

            catalog = coastalUi.Scroll("catalog/contracts", new Rect(panel.x + 16, top, panel.width - 32, height), 110 + State.Active.Count * 80 + State.Offers.Count * 88);
            catalogWidth = panel.width - 48; catalogY = 0;

            ((RectTransform)catalog).sizeDelta = new Vector2(catalogWidth, Mathf.Max(height, catalogY));

        void DrawNauticalContracts()
        {
            CatalogHeading("contracts/title", $"Harbour contracts · {State.Active.Count}/{GameData.MaxActiveContracts}", "Sell matching catches to make progress. Every fish is paid; completion earns a bonus.", 82);
            foreach (var c in State.Active)
            {
                coastalUi.Art("active/" + c.Title + "/icon", new Rect(8, catalogY + 8, 40, 40), "icon-contract-scroll", catalog);
                coastalUi.Label("active/" + c.Title, new Rect(64, catalogY, catalogWidth - 70, 70), $"{c.Title} · {c.Delivered}/{c.Count}\n{c.Reward} bonus coins · {c.VoyagesLeft} voyages left", 18, null, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
                catalogY += 78;
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < State.Offers.Count; i++)
            {
                var c = State.Offers[i];
                coastalUi.Label("offer/" + i, new Rect(4, catalogY, catalogWidth - 170, 78), $"Deliver {c.Title}\n{c.Reward} bonus coins · {c.VoyagesLeft} voyages", 18, null, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
                coastalUi.Button("offer/" + i + "/take", new Rect(catalogWidth - 158, catalogY + 12, 150, 48), "Take contract", () => { if (State.Offers.Contains(c) && State.TakeContract(c)) SaveGame(); }, "button-secondary-normal", State.Active.Count < GameData.MaxActiveContracts, catalog, 16);
                catalogY += 88;
            }
        }
    }

========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageCompactFisher.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Prototype;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public bool IsNetFisher => State != null && State.Hull >= 2;
        Transform netModelRoot;
        Quaternion compactLidRest;
        public int CompactWalkableTiles { get; private set; }

        void FitCompactFisher(GameObject model, GameObject prefab)
        {
            // Keep the importer's source-Z-up conversion exactly once.
            model.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, -90, 0) * prefab.transform.localRotation;
            model.transform.localScale = prefab.transform.localScale;
            netModelRoot = model.transform;
            foreach (var t in model.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>())
                if (t.name.StartsWith("RodVisual_")) t.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            compactLidRest = ArtLibrary.Find(model, "HoldLid")?.localRotation ?? Quaternion.identity;
            if (IsPrepFisher) ConfigurePrepFisher(model);
            FitCompactDeck(model);
        }

        // Rasterize the delivered mesh at the artist's three body-clearance heights.
        // This deliberately includes the hatch even while its lid is animated open:
        // crew route around the real opening, never across it.
        void FitCompactDeck(GameObject model)
        {
            var segments = new List<(Vector2 a, Vector2 b)>();
            foreach (var mf in model.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
            {
                var mesh = mf.sharedMesh;
                if (mesh == null || !mf.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) continue;
                var matrix = Boat.worldToLocalMatrix * mf.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                var vertices = mesh.vertices;
                for (int i = 0; i < vertices.Length; i++) vertices[i] = matrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(vertices[i]);
                var tris = mesh.triangles;
                foreach (float height in new[] { .20f, .55f, .95f })
                    for (int i = 0; i < tris.Length; i += 3)
                    {
                        var points = new List<Vector2>(3);
                        for (int edge = 0; edge < 3; edge++)
                        {
                            var a = vertices[tris[i + edge]];
                            var b = vertices[tris[i + (edge + 1) % 3]];
                            if ((a.y < height) == (b.y < height) || Mathf.Abs(a.y - b.y) < .00001f) continue;
                            var p = Vector3.Lerp(a, b, (height - a.y) / (b.y - a.y));
                            points.Add(new Vector2(p.x, p.z));
                        }
                        if (points.Count >= 2) segments.Add((points[0], points[1]));
                    }
            }
            CompactWalkableTiles = 0;
            for (int y = 0; y < Grid.Height; y++)
                for (int x = 0; x < Grid.Width; x++)
                {
                    var tile = new Vector2Int(x, y);
                    var local = Grid.GetLocal(tile);
                    var point = new Vector2(local.x, local.z);
                    bool blocked = segments.Any(s => SegmentDistance(point, s.a, s.b) < .28f);
                    // Hold opening below the first body-height slice.
                    var native = new Vector2(State.HullDef.Center.z - local.z, State.HullDef.Center.x - local.x);
                    if (!IsPrepFisher && native.x > -.97f - .28f && native.x < -.33f + .28f && native.y > -1.13f - .28f && native.y < -.37f + .28f) blocked = true;
                    // The low net basket sits below the body slices too.
                    var basket = NetBasketLocal;
                    var nearBasket = new Vector2(Mathf.Clamp(local.x, basket.x - .18f, basket.x + .18f), Mathf.Clamp(local.z, basket.z - .24f, basket.z + .24f));
                    if (Vector2.Distance(point, nearBasket) < .28f) blocked = true;
                    if (blocked) Grid.Block(new RectInt(x, y, 1, 1));
                    else CompactWalkableTiles++;
                }
        }

        static float SegmentDistance(Vector2 p, Vector2 a, Vector2 b)
        {
            var d = b - a;
            return Vector2.Distance(p, a + d * Mathf.Clamp01(Vector2.Dot(p - a, d) / Mathf.Max(.000001f, d.sqrMagnitude)));
        }

        public bool SetDeckhandWork(CrewMember crew, DeckhandWork preference)
        {
            if (!IsNetFisher || crew == null || crew.Role != CrewRole.Deckhand || !State.Crew.Contains(crew)) return false;
            if (preference == DeckhandWork.Prep && (!IsPrepFisher || crew.WorkPreference != DeckhandWork.Prep
                && State.Crew.Count(c => c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep && c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand) >= 2)) return false;
            crew.WorkPreference = preference;
            // Complete any fish already in hand. Release a walking/timed job safely;
            // Person cancellation also releases fish, bait and winch claims.
            var person = People.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Contract == crew);
            person?.Stop();
            if (person != null && preference != DeckhandWork.Prep) prepStations.Remove(person);
            SaveGame();
            return true;
        }

        public bool CanCaptainNet => CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing && Captain.Idle && Captain.Carrying == null
            && (Manning == null || Manning.State is not (RodState.Reeling or RodState.Flying))
            && !NetUnloadActive && (Net == NetState.Ready || Net == NetState.Stowed && (NetBasketBlocked || State.ActiveNetStock > 0) && !HoldFull);

        public bool CaptainNetAction()
        {
            if (!CanCaptainNet || Net is not (NetState.Stowed or NetState.Ready)) return false;
            // A baited/waiting rod stays in its holder; returning to Watch can resume it.
            var oldRod = Manning;
            bool oldCastChoice = AwaitingCast;
            Manning = null;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            if (Net == NetState.Stowed && NetBasketBlocked) { BeginNetUnload(Captain); return true; }
            bool started = Net == NetState.Ready ? HaulNet() : SetNet();
            if (!started) { Manning = oldRod; AwaitingCast = oldCastChoice; }
            return started;
        }

        public bool TrialCompactFisher()
        {
            if (!SessionOnly || CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour || State.Hull == 2) return false;
            State.Hull = 2;
            // A layout-trial downgrade releases the extra temporary trial berth.
            while (State.CrewCount > State.HullDef.CrewCap && State.ExtraCrew.Count > 0)
                State.ExtraCrew.RemoveAt(State.ExtraCrew.Count - 1);
            State.Fuel = State.FuelCapacity;
            State.FoodMinutes = State.FoodCapacity;
            State.Coins = Mathf.Max(State.Coins, 200); // Explicit isolated trial budget, not progression economy.
            State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
            RebuildBoat();
            Toast("Compact fisher trial. Hire any three crew at the Crew Office; choose Fish or Nets for each deckhand.");
            return true;
        }

        // Explicit opt-in launch fixture. Never reads or writes the player's save.
        public bool StartNetBoatTrial()
        {
            if (!SessionOnly) return false;
            NewGame();
            TrialCompactFisher();
            State.Coins = 1000;
            foreach (var role in new[] { CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Fisherman })
            {
                State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
                State.Hire(State.Board.First(c => c.Role == role));
            }
            State.Coins = 200;
            State.Bait = 60;
            State.FoodMinutes = State.FoodCapacity;
            SyncCrew();
            showTitle = paused = showSettings = panelOpen = captureFreeze = netOrdersOpen = false;
            ActiveSpot = SelectedSpot = 0;
            voyageStart = SimTime;
            Showcase(0, 0);
            toastUntil = 0;
            return true;
        }

        public bool StartCrewMixTrial()
        {
            if (!SessionOnly) return false;
            NewGame();
            if (!TrialCompactFisher()) return false;
            State.Coins = 1000;
            State.Bait = 60;
            State.DeepNetUses.Clear();
            State.DeepNetUses.AddRange(new[] { 3, 3 });
            State.SelectedNet = NetKind.Deep;
            State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
            SyncCrew();
            showTitle = paused = showSettings = captureFreeze = netOrdersOpen = false;
            ActiveSpot = SelectedSpot = 0;
            departureReady = false;
            toastUntil = 0f;
            OpenShop(HudTab.CrewOffice);
            Toast("Hire any three crew. Contracts expire, but both roles remain available.");
            RefreshInterface();
            return true;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageCrewLife.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        // Cosmetic routes use no catch/economy RNG and never claim a work station.
        // HandThink runs during these walks, so real jobs interrupt on the next step.
        void IdlePatrol(Person hand)
        {
            if (!hand.PatrolReady) return;
            int phase = (hand.PatrolLeg + (hand == Hand ? 0 : ExtraPeople.IndexOf(hand) + 1)) % 4;
            bool port = phase < 2;
            float targetX = (Grid.Width - 1) * Grid.CellSize * (phase % 2 == 0 ? .25f : .75f);
            float targetZ = port ? 0 : (Grid.Height - 1) * Grid.CellSize;
            var target = new Vector3(targetX, 0, targetZ);
            var candidates = new List<Vector2Int>();
            for (int y = 0; y < Grid.Height; y++)
                for (int x = 0; x < Grid.Width; x++)
                {
                    var tile = new Vector2Int(x, y);
                    if (Grid.IsBlocked(tile) || tile == CoolerAccess || tile == NetAccess
                        || Rods.Any(r => r.Unlocked && r.Tile == tile)) continue;
                    var local = Grid.GetLocal(tile);
                    if (Vector3.Distance(local, Grid.GetLocal(hand.Tile)) < .6f) continue;
                    if (People.Any(p => p != hand && Vector3.Distance(Grid.GetLocal(p.Tile), local) < .5f)) continue;
                    candidates.Add(tile);
                }
            foreach (var tile in candidates.OrderBy(t => (Grid.GetLocal(t) - target).sqrMagnitude))
            {
                if (Grid.FindPath(hand.Tile, tile) == null) continue;
                hand.Stroll(Grid, tile, port ? Vector3.back : Vector3.forward);
                return;
            }
        }

        void FaceHold(Person person) => person.FacePoint(Grid.GetLocal(CoolerTile));
        void FaceNet(Person person) => person.Face(IsNetFisher ? Vector3.right : new Vector3(1, 0, -.5f));
    }
}

========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageFeedback.cs
========================================================================
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // First value comes from the real voyage: cast, catch, carry, sell, improve the boat.
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        Species lastCatchSpecies;
        float lastCatchKg, catchUntil, stowedAt = -100f;
        bool catchNew, catchRecord;

        public bool FirstVoyage => State.Voyage == 1 && State.Hull == 0 && State.LogCount.All(n => n == 0);
        public bool LearningFishing => State.Hull == 0 && State.LogCount.Sum() + Stats.Landed < 3;
        public float StrikeWindow => LearningFishing ? Mathf.Max(1.5f, State.BiteWindow) : State.BiteWindow;

        void ResetFeedback() { catchUntil = 0f; stowedAt = -100f; ResetCatchReceipts(); }

        void ShowCatch(Species species, float kg)
        {
            toast = null;
            lastCatchSpecies = species;
            lastCatchKg = kg;
            // The just-landed fish is already in OnDeck. Compare against earlier catches this trip too.
            var earlier = OnDeck.Where(f => f.Species == species).Select(f => f.Kg)
                .Concat(Cooler.Where(f => f.Species == species).Select(f => f.Kg))
                .Concat(People.Where(p => p.Carrying?.Species == species).Select(p => p.Carrying.Kg));
            catchNew = State.LogCount[(int)species] == 0 && earlier.Count() == 1;
            catchRecord = !catchNew && kg > State.LogBest[(int)species] && earlier.All(k => k <= kg);
            catchUntil = SimTime + CatchDisplaySeconds;
        }

        static string FishPortrait(Species species) => "fish-" + species.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();

        public string VoyageHint()
        {
            if (HoldFull) return "Cooler full! Head home to sell.";
            if (Captain.Carrying != null || Captain.Busy || Captain.Walking)
                return Captain.Carrying != null ? "Taking your fish to the cooler…" : "The captain is on it…";
            if (OnDeck.Any(CanCarry)) return "Tap Carry. The captain takes it to the cooler.";
            var rod = MinigameRod;
            if (rod != null) return rod.State switch
            {
                RodState.Bite => "Bite! Tap the water or press Space now!",
                RodState.Waiting => rod.Nibbling ? "Just a nibble. Wait until the bobber goes under." : "Watch the bobber. Wait for it to go under.",
                RodState.Reeling => "Hooked! The captain reels it in for you.",
                _ => "Casting… get ready to watch the bobber.",
            };
            if (FirstVoyage && Cooler.Count >= 3) return "Three safely aboard! Head home to sell, or fill the cooler.";
            if (!State.HasPaidBait && Rods.All(r => r.State == RodState.Unbaited)) return "Free scraps: small fish, no cost. Tap Fish.";
            if (Rods.Any(r => CanCommandRod(r) && r.InWater)) return "Tap Watch to follow that bobber.";
            return Rods.Any(CanCommandRod) ? "Tap Fish. The captain baits and casts for you." : null;
        }

        void DrawVoyageGuide()
        {
            if (!ShowTips || MinigameRod != null || AwaitingCast) return;
            var hint = VoyageHint();
            if (hint == null) return;
            float width = Mathf.Min(520f, VW - 660f);
            if (width < 180f) return; // Action bubbles still guide unusually narrow windows.
            float height = VH < 600f ? 78f : 58f;
            var r = Panel(new Rect((VW - width) / 2f, VH - height - 20f, width, height), Ui.ToastStyle);
            GUI.Label(new Rect(r.x + 12f, r.y + 6f, r.width - 24f, height - 12f), hint, Ui.WhiteCenterSmall);
        }

        void DrawNextUpgrade()
        {
            if (State.Hull != 0) return;
            var id = !State.Has(UpgradeId.BigCooler) ? UpgradeId.BigCooler : UpgradeId.RodHolder;
            if (State.Has(id)) return;
            var upgrade = GameData.Upgrade(id);
            GUILayout.Space(12f);
            GUILayout.Label("Make the next trip better", Ui.H2);
            GUILayout.Label(id == UpgradeId.BigCooler ? "A bigger cooler carries six more fish and makes the skiff's 16-fish goal possible." : "A second rod lets you keep another line fishing while you work. Then hire a deckhand to handle bait and carrying.", Ui.Body);
            int shortfall = Mathf.Max(0, upgrade.Cost - State.Coins);
            GUILayout.Label(shortfall > 0 ? $"{upgrade.Name}: {upgrade.Cost} coins · {shortfall} more to go" : $"{upgrade.Name}: {upgrade.Cost} coins · you can afford it", Ui.Small);
            if (GUILayout.Button("Visit Chandlery", Ui.BtnAlt, GUILayout.Height(44f))) OpenShop(HudTab.Chandlery);
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageGame.cs

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Prototype;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering.Universal;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // Build 1: tier 1-2 rowboat. Harbour -> row out -> fish with the rod -> row home -> sell.
    // Runtime stage uses the authored art library; the HUD lives in VoyageHud.cs.
    public partial class VoyageGame : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public enum Mode { Harbour, SailingOut, Fishing, SailingHome }

        public class VoyageStats { public int Landed, Spooked, Missed, Lost; }

        public GameState State { get; private set; }
        public Mode CurrentMode { get; private set; } = Mode.Harbour;
        public int SelectedSpot;
        public int ActiveSpot { get; private set; }
        public Camera Cam { get; private set; }
        public Transform Boat { get; private set; }
        public DeckGrid Grid { get; private set; }
        public readonly List<Rod> Rods = new();
        public Person Captain { get; private set; }
        public Person Hand { get; private set; }
        public readonly List<Person> ExtraPeople = new();
        IEnumerable<Person> People => new[] { Captain, Hand }.Where(p => p != null).Concat(ExtraPeople);
        public readonly List<DeckFish> OnDeck = new();
        public readonly List<CaughtFish> Cooler = new();
        public VoyageStats Stats = new();
        public float SimTime;
        public bool AutoPlay;
        // Preview builds and checks can play from scratch without touching the captain's save.
        [HideInInspector] public bool SessionOnly;
        public int Seed = 7;
        public readonly List<string> Log = new();

        Vector2Int CoolerTile => State.HullDef.Hold;
        Vector2Int CoolerAccess => State.HullDef.HoldAccess;
        Vector2Int SeatTile => State.HullDef.Seat;
        Vector2Int HandRest => State.HullDef.Rest;
        Vector2Int RestFor(Person p) => IsNetFisher ? State.HullDef.CrewRest[Mathf.Clamp(p == Hand ? 0 : ExtraPeople.IndexOf(p) + (Hand != null ? 1 : 0), 0, State.HullDef.CrewRest.Length - 1)] : p == Hand ? HandRest : ExtraPeople.IndexOf(p) == 0 ? new Vector2Int(2, State.Hull == 0 ? 1 : 2) : new Vector2Int(0, 1);

        Transform world, scenery, oldScenery, coolerLid;
        TextMesh coolerLabel;
        Material seaMat;
        VoyageAudio audio;
        Color seaFrom, seaTo;
        GameObject oarPort, oarStarboard, coolerModel, iceBoxModel;
        Quaternion oarPortBase = Quaternion.identity, oarStarboardBase = Quaternion.identity;
        bool modelOars;
        Transform propeller;
        int boatVisualSignature;
        int BoatVisualSignature => (State.Has(UpgradeId.BigCooler) ? 1 : 0) | (State.Has(UpgradeId.RodFibreglass) ? 2 : 0) | (State.Has(UpgradeId.RodCarbon) ? 4 : 0) | (State.Has(UpgradeId.RodHolder) ? 8 : 0) | (IsPrepFisher && State.Has(UpgradeId.IceBox) ? 16 : 0);
        float bowSign = 1f; // +1: bow toward +X (primitive hull), -1: toward -X (model)

        // Harbour buildings you tap to open a shop.
        public readonly List<(Transform anchor, HudTab tab)> Shops = new();
        readonly List<Transform> waves = new();
        readonly List<(Transform t, float age)> splashes = new();
        float modeTimer, modeDuration, voyageStart, lidPop;
        bool built, fullWarned;
        public static readonly Color ShadowColor = new(0.03f, 0.22f, 0.27f);
        readonly List<(Transform t, float speed, float radius, float phase)> school = new();
        string toast;
        float toastUntil;

        void Awake() => Build();

        void Update()
        {
            if (!built) return;
            Tick(paused || captureFreeze ? 0f : Time.deltaTime); // the harbour keeps bobbing behind the title screen
            HandleInput();
        }

        // ---- build ------------------------------------------------------------------------

        public void Build()
        {
            if (built) return;
            built = true;
            var loaded = AutoPlay || SessionOnly ? null : GameState.Load();
            hadSave = loaded != null;
            showTitle = !AutoPlay;
            State = loaded ?? new GameState(Seed);
            BuildGrid();
            BuildWorld();
            if (Application.isPlaying && !AutoPlay)
            {
                audio = gameObject.AddComponent<VoyageAudio>();
                audio.Initialize(SessionOnly);
            }
            BuildBoat();
            Captain = new Person("Captain", true, IsNetFisher ? Vector3.zero : new Vector3(0f, 0f, -0.05f)) { Grid = Grid };
            Captain.Build(Boat, SeatTile, Palette.HullNavy, Palette.Rail);
            scenery = BuildScenery(-1);
            SetWaterBiome(-1);
            SyncCrew();
            SyncUpgrades();
            EnterHarbour(false);
        }

        void BuildWorld()
        {
            var camGo = new GameObject("Camera") { tag = "MainCamera" };
            camGo.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            Cam = camGo.AddComponent<Camera>();
            camGo.AddComponent<AudioListener>();
            var waterCamera = camGo.AddComponent<UnityEngine.Rendering.Universal.UniversalAdditionalCameraData>();
            waterCamera.requiresDepthTexture = true;
            waterCamera.requiresColorTexture = true;
            Cam.orthographic = true;
            Cam.orthographicSize = 3.2f;
            Cam.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
            Cam.backgroundColor = Palette.Sea;
            Cam.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
            Cam.farClipPlane = 120f;
            // Same angle as the art previews (45 down, 35 yaw): every hull is placed bow toward -X, which reads as 8 o'clock on screen.
            camGo.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(45f, 35f, 0f);
            camGo.transform.position = new Vector3(1.1f, 0f, -1.3f) - camGo.transform.forward * 40f;

            var sun = new GameObject("Sun").AddComponent<Light>();
            sun.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            sun.type = LightType.Directional;
            sun.intensity = 1.3f;
            sun.color = new Color(1f, 0.96f, 0.88f);
            sun.shadows = LightShadows.Soft;
            sun.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(55f, 195f, 0f);
            RenderSettings.ambientMode = UnityEngine.Rendering.AmbientMode.Flat;
            RenderSettings.ambientLight = new Color(0.58f, 0.64f, 0.7f);

            world = new GameObject("World").transform;
            world.SetParent(transform, false);
            var waterArt = ArtLibrary.Get();
            seaMat = waterArt?.WaterShader != null ? new Material(waterArt.WaterShader) : new Material(Visuals.Mat(Palette.Sea));
            if (waterArt?.WaterTex("water-tile") != null) seaMat.SetTexture("_WaterTile", waterArt.WaterTex("water-tile"));
            seaMat.color = Palette.Sea;
            seaMat.SetTexture("_Ripples", waterArt?.WaterRipples);
            seaMat.SetTexture("_Crossing", waterArt?.WaterCrossing);
            PtsWater.Configure(seaMat, waterArt?.PtsWaterNormals, waterArt?.PtsWaterDither);
            var cameraData = Cam.GetUniversalAdditionalCameraData();
            cameraData.requiresColorTexture = cameraData.requiresDepthTexture = true;
            cameraData.renderPostProcessing = true;
            cameraData.antialiasing = AntialiasingMode.SubpixelMorphologicalAntiAliasing;
            var sea = CoastalWater.Build(world, seaMat);
            sea.GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial = seaMat;
            seaFrom = seaTo = Palette.Sea;

            var rng = new System.Random(3);
            for (int i = 0; i < (waterArt?.WaterShader != null ? 0 : 34); i++)
            {
                var w = Visuals.Box("Wave", world, Vector3.zero, new Vector3(0.5f + (float)rng.NextDouble() * 0.6f, 0.01f, 0.05f), new Color(0.85f, 0.95f, 0.97f));
                w.transform.position = new Vector3(-14f + (float)rng.NextDouble() * 30f, -0.315f, -9f + (float)rng.NextDouble() * 14f);
                waves.Add(w.transform);
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
            {
                float len = 0.25f + (float)rng.NextDouble() * 0.25f;
                var f = Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "SchoolFish", world, Vector3.zero, new Vector3(len * 0.35f, 0.01f, len), ShadowColor);
                school.Add((f.transform, (rng.Next(2) == 0 ? -1f : 1f) * (0.15f + (float)rng.NextDouble() * 0.2f), 2.4f + (float)rng.NextDouble() * 2.5f, (float)rng.NextDouble() * 6.28f));
            }
        }

        void BuildBoat()
        {
            var hull = State.HullDef;
            boatVisualSignature = BoatVisualSignature;
            Boat = new GameObject(hull.Name).transform;
            Boat.SetParent(world, false);

            var lib = ArtLibrary.Get();
            if (State.Hull > 0)
            {
                var prefab = lib?.M(hull.Model);
                if (prefab != null)
                {
                    var skiff = ArtLibrary.Spawn(prefab, Boat, hull.Center, Quaternion.Euler(0f, -90f, 0f));
                    if (IsNetFisher) FitCompactFisher(skiff, prefab);
                    coolerLid = ArtLibrary.Find(skiff, "HoldLid");
                    propeller = ArtLibrary.Find(skiff, "Propeller");
                }
                else BuildPrimitiveSkiff();
                bowSign = -1f;
            }
            else if (lib != null && lib.Rowboat != null)
            {
                // Model: deck centred on its origin, bow +Z. Turned so the port gunwale (rods) faces the camera;
                // the bow then points to -X, so the boat rows toward -X.
                var rb = ArtLibrary.Spawn(lib.Rowboat, Boat, new Vector3(1f, 0f, 0.5f), Quaternion.Euler(0f, -90f, 0f));
                bowSign = -1f;
                oarPort = ArtLibrary.Find(rb, "Oar_Port")?.gameObject;
                oarStarboard = ArtLibrary.Find(rb, "Oar_Starboard")?.gameObject;
                modelOars = oarPort != null && oarStarboard != null;
                if (modelOars)
                {
                    oarPortBase = oarPort.transform.localRotation;
                    oarStarboardBase = oarStarboard.transform.localRotation;
                }
            }
            else BuildPrimitiveHull();

            var floor = new GameObject("DeckFloor");
            floor.transform.SetParent(Boat, false);
            var fc = floor.AddComponent<BoxCollider>();
            fc.center = hull.Center + Vector3.down * .02f;
            fc.size = new Vector3((hull.Width - 1) * hull.CellSize + hull.CellSize, 0.06f, (hull.Height - 1) * hull.CellSize + hull.CellSize);
            floor.AddComponent<Tappable>().Deck = true;

            // Cooler on the starboard middle tile.
            var cooler = new GameObject("Cooler");
            cooler.transform.SetParent(Boat, false);
            cooler.transform.localPosition = Grid.GetLocal(CoolerTile);
            if (State.Hull > 0)
            {
                // The skiff FBX already contains FishHold and its hinged lid.
            }
            else if (lib != null && lib.Cooler != null && lib.IceBox != null)
            {
                var plain = State.Has(UpgradeId.BigCooler) ? lib.M("BigCooler") ?? lib.Cooler : lib.Cooler;
                var iced = State.Has(UpgradeId.BigCooler) ? lib.M("BigIceBox") ?? lib.IceBox : lib.IceBox;
                coolerModel = ArtLibrary.Spawn(plain, cooler.transform, Vector3.zero, Quaternion.Euler(0f, 180f, 0f));
                iceBoxModel = ArtLibrary.Spawn(iced, cooler.transform, Vector3.zero, Quaternion.Euler(0f, 180f, 0f));
                if (lib.BaitBucket != null) ArtLibrary.Spawn(lib.BaitBucket, Boat, new Vector3(0.25f, 0f, 1.25f), Quaternion.identity);
                DressDeck(lib);
            }
            else
            {
                Visuals.Box("Body", cooler.transform, new Vector3(0f, 0.22f, 0f), new Vector3(0.8f, 0.42f, 0.55f), Palette.Rail);
                Visuals.Box("Handle", cooler.transform, new Vector3(0f, 0.3f, -0.29f), new Vector3(0.3f, 0.05f, 0.04f), Palette.Rubber);
                coolerLid = new GameObject("LidHinge").transform;
                coolerLid.SetParent(cooler.transform, false);
                coolerLid.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, 0.43f, 0.28f);
                Visuals.Box("Lid", coolerLid, new Vector3(0f, 0.04f, -0.28f), new Vector3(0.84f, 0.08f, 0.59f), Palette.Accent);
            }
            var cc = cooler.AddComponent<BoxCollider>();
            cc.center = new Vector3(0f, 0.3f, 0f);
            cc.size = new Vector3(0.9f, 0.6f, 0.7f);
            cooler.AddComponent<Tappable>().Cooler = true;
            coolerLabel = Visuals.Label("Count", cooler.transform, new Vector3(0f, 0.75f, 0f), 0.045f);
            coolerLabel.gameObject.SetActive(false); // storage is shown by the HUD, leaving the boat clear
            coolerLabel.color = Palette.Rail;

            for (int i = 0; i < hull.RodTiles.Length; i++)
            {
                var rod = new Rod(i, hull.RodTiles[i]);
                Rods.Add(rod);
                var rodModel = State.Has(UpgradeId.RodCarbon) ? lib?.M("FishingRod_Carbon") : State.Has(UpgradeId.RodFibreglass) ? lib?.M("FishingRod_Fibreglass") : null;
                rod.Build(Boat, world, hull.RodBases[i], rodModel, IsNetFisher && hull.RodBases[i].z > hull.Center.z ? Vector3.forward : Vector3.back);
                if (!IsNetFisher && i > 0 && (State.Hull > 0 || State.Has(UpgradeId.RodHolder) || State.Crew.Any(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman)))
                    ArtLibrary.Spawn(lib?.M("RodHolderClamp"), Boat, hull.RodBases[i], Quaternion.identity);
            }
            BuildNetKit();
        }

        void BuildGrid()
        {
            var hull = State.HullDef;
            Grid = new DeckGrid(hull.Width, hull.Height, hull.CellSize);
            foreach (var tile in hull.Blocked) Grid.Block(new RectInt(tile.x, tile.y, 1, 1));
        }

        void BuildPrimitiveSkiff()
        {
            var h = State.HullDef;
            Visuals.Box("Hull", Boat, h.Center + Vector3.down * .3f, new Vector3(6.5f, .6f, 3.5f), Palette.HullNavy);
            Visuals.Box("Deck", Boat, h.Center + Vector3.down * .025f, new Vector3(5f, .05f, 3f), Palette.DeckWood);
            Visuals.Box("Console", Boat, new Vector3(4f, .32f, 2f), new Vector3(.65f, .64f, .72f), Palette.Rail);
            Visuals.Box("Motor", Boat, new Vector3(5.25f, .15f, 1f), new Vector3(.45f, .55f, .45f), Palette.HullNavy);
            coolerLid = Visuals.Box("HoldLid", Boat, new Vector3(1f, .04f, 1f), new Vector3(1f, .08f, .8f), Palette.Steel).transform;
        }

        void RebuildBoat(bool preserveBait = true)
        {
            ClearPrepWork();
            var baited = Rods.Select(r => (ready: preserveBait && r.State == RodState.Ready, squid: r.Squid, scraps: r.Scraps, chum: r.Chum)).ToArray();
            Captain?.Stop();
            Hand?.Stop();
            foreach (var p in ExtraPeople) p.Stop();
            foreach (var rod in Rods) rod.Destroy();
            Rods.Clear();
            if (Boat != null) { Boat.gameObject.SetActive(false); Visuals.Kill(Boat.gameObject); }
            Hand = null;
            ExtraPeople.Clear();
            ResetNet();
            if (netFloats != null) Visuals.Kill(netFloats.gameObject);
            netFloats = winchCrank = null;
            Manning = null;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            coolerLid = propeller = null;
            netModelRoot = null;
            rawBuckets = rawIceCoolers = null;
            PrepWorkerTiles = System.Array.Empty<Vector2Int>();
            coolerModel = iceBoxModel = oarPort = oarStarboard = null;
            modelOars = false;
            BuildGrid();
            BuildBoat();
            for (int i = 0; i < baited.Length && i < Rods.Count; i++)
                if (baited[i].ready) { Rods[i].State = RodState.Ready; Rods[i].Squid = baited[i].squid; Rods[i].Scraps = baited[i].scraps; Rods[i].Chum = baited[i].chum; }
            Captain = new Person("Captain", true, IsNetFisher ? Vector3.zero : new Vector3(0f, 0f, -.05f)) { Grid = Grid };
            Captain.Build(Boat, SeatTile, Palette.HullNavy, Palette.Rail);
            SyncCrew();
            SyncUpgrades();
        }

        public bool RefitSkiff()
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour || !State.RefitSkiff()) return false;
            RebuildBoat();
            refitUntil = SimTime + 1.2f;
            Toast("Skiff refitted! A bigger hold, three rod sockets and an outboard. Your first tank is included.");
            SaveGame();
            return true;
        }

        public bool RefitCompact()
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour || !State.RefitCompact()) return false;
            RebuildBoat(); refitUntil = SimTime + 1.2f;
            Toast("Compact fisher ready ·100kg hold, three rods and stern net. First tank included.");
            SaveGame(); return true;
        }

        float refitUntil;

        void BuildPrimitiveHull()
        {
            // Hull with a pointed bow (cube turned 45 degrees) and a flat transom.
            Visuals.Box("Hull", Boat, new Vector3(0.9f, -0.33f, 0.5f), new Vector3(3.0f, 0.6f, 2.3f), Palette.HullNavy);
            Visuals.Box("Stripe", Boat, new Vector3(0.9f, -0.12f, 0.5f), new Vector3(3.04f, 0.07f, 2.34f), Palette.Accent);
            float bow = 2.3f / Mathf.Sqrt(2f);
            var bowGo = Visuals.Box("Bow", Boat, new Vector3(2.4f, -0.33f, 0.5f), new Vector3(bow, 0.6f, bow), Palette.HullNavy);
            bowGo.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 45f, 0f);
            var bowStripe = Visuals.Box("BowStripe", Boat, new Vector3(2.4f, -0.12f, 0.5f), new Vector3(bow + 0.03f, 0.07f, bow + 0.03f), Palette.Accent);
            bowStripe.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 45f, 0f);
            var bowDeck = Visuals.Box("BowDeck", Boat, new Vector3(2.4f, -0.02f, 0.5f), new Vector3(bow - 0.25f, 0.06f, bow - 0.25f), Palette.DeckWoodDark);
            bowDeck.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 45f, 0f);

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                Visuals.Box("Plank", Boat, new Vector3(1f, -0.02f, -0.375f + i * 0.5f), new Vector3(3.0f, 0.06f, 0.47f), i % 2 == 0 ? Palette.DeckWood : Palette.DeckWoodDark);

            Visuals.Box("GunwalePort", Boat, new Vector3(0.95f, 0.08f, -0.6f), new Vector3(3.1f, 0.14f, 0.1f), Palette.Rail);
            Visuals.Box("GunwaleStarboard", Boat, new Vector3(0.95f, 0.08f, 1.6f), new Vector3(3.1f, 0.14f, 0.1f), Palette.Rail);
            Visuals.Box("Transom", Boat, new Vector3(-0.6f, 0.08f, 0.5f), new Vector3(0.1f, 0.14f, 2.3f), Palette.Rail);

            oarPort = new GameObject("OarPort");
            oarPort.transform.SetParent(Boat, false);
            oarPort.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(1f, 0.15f, -0.62f);
            Visuals.Box("Shaft", oarPort.transform, new Vector3(0f, 0f, -0.8f), new Vector3(0.06f, 0.06f, 1.9f), Palette.DeckWoodDark);
            Visuals.Box("Blade", oarPort.transform, new Vector3(0f, 0f, -1.8f), new Vector3(0.2f, 0.03f, 0.45f), Palette.Accent);
            oarStarboard = new GameObject("OarStarboard");
            oarStarboard.transform.SetParent(Boat, false);
            oarStarboard.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(1f, 0.15f, 1.62f);
            Visuals.Box("Shaft", oarStarboard.transform, new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0.8f), new Vector3(0.06f, 0.06f, 1.9f), Palette.DeckWoodDark);
            Visuals.Box("Blade", oarStarboard.transform, new Vector3(0f, 0f, 1.8f), new Vector3(0.2f, 0.03f, 0.45f), Palette.Accent);

        }

        // -1 = Gull Harbour quay with the shops, otherwise the fishing spot index.
        Transform BuildScenery(int kind)
        {
            var root = new GameObject(kind < 0 ? "Scenery Harbour" : $"Scenery {GameData.Spots[kind].Name}").transform;
            root.SetParent(world, false);
            var rng = new System.Random(100 + kind);
            float R(float a, float b) => a + (float)rng.NextDouble() * (b - a);
            var lib = ArtLibrary.Get();
            var stone = Palette.Hex("8E8A80");
            var rockColor = Palette.Hex("6F7270");

            void Rock(Vector3 p, float s)
            {
                var rot = Quaternion.Euler(0f, R(0f, 360f), 0f);
                var models = lib != null ? new[] { lib.RockA, lib.RockB, lib.RockC } : null;
                var prefab = models?[rng.Next(3)];
                if (prefab != null) ArtLibrary.Spawn(prefab, root, new Vector3(p.x, -0.45f, p.z), rot, s * 0.9f);
                else
                {
                    var g = Visuals.Box("Rock", root, p, new Vector3(s, s * 0.7f, s * 0.9f), rockColor);
                    g.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(R(-15f, 15f), R(0f, 90f), R(-10f, 10f));
                }
            }

            GameObject Place(GameObject prefab, Vector3 p, float yaw = 0f, float scale = 1f)
                => ArtLibrary.Spawn(prefab, root, p, Quaternion.Euler(0f, yaw, 0f), scale);

            if (kind < 0)
            {
                Shops.Clear();
                const float quayTop = 0.35f;
                if (lib != null && lib.Quay != null)
                    for (float x = -22f; x <= 26f; x += 4f) Place(x == 2f && lib.QuayLadder != null ? lib.QuayLadder : lib.Quay, new Vector3(x, quayTop, 3.3f)); // ladder down to our berth
                else Visuals.Box("QuayEdge", root, new Vector3(1f, quayTop - 0.45f, 3.3f), new Vector3(50f, 0.9f, 2f), stone);
                Visuals.Box("QuayBack", root, new Vector3(1f, quayTop - 0.47f, 8.5f), new Vector3(50f, 0.9f, 8.5f), stone);

                foreach (float x in new[] { -4.5f, -0.6f, 3.4f, 7.5f })
                {
                    if (lib != null && lib.Bollard != null) Place(lib.Bollard, new Vector3(x, quayTop, 2.6f));
                    else Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "Bollard", root, new Vector3(x, quayTop + 0.2f, 2.6f), new Vector3(0.25f, 0.2f, 0.25f), Palette.Rubber);
                }
                foreach (float x in new[] { -3.8f, 3.4f, 10.6f })
                {
                    if (lib != null && lib.LampPost != null) Place(lib.LampPost, new Vector3(x, quayTop, 4.0f));
                    else Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "Lamp", root, new Vector3(x, quayTop + 0.75f, 4.0f), new Vector3(0.07f, 0.75f, 0.07f), Palette.Rubber);
                }

                // The shops along the quay. Each gets a sign and a tap bubble.
                var shops = new[]
                {
                    (HudTab.Market, "FISH MARKET", -5.6f),
                    (HudTab.HarbourMaster, "HARBOUR MASTER", -2.0f),
                    (HudTab.CrewOffice, "CREW OFFICE", 1.6f),
                    (HudTab.Chandlery, "CHANDLERY", 5.2f),
                    (HudTab.Supplies, "SUPPLIES", 8.8f),
                };
                Color[] walls = { Palette.Hex("E9D8B4"), Palette.Hex("C9DDE0"), Palette.Hex("E7B9A0"), Palette.Hex("D9E3C5"), Palette.Rail };
                for (int i = 0; i < shops.Length; i++)
                {
                    var (tab, sign, x) = shops[i];
                    var house = i % 2 == 0 ? lib?.HouseA : lib?.HouseB;
                    float height = 3.8f;
                    if (house != null) Place(house, new Vector3(x, quayTop, 6.6f), 180f, 0.95f);
                    else
                    {
                        height = 2.4f + i % 2 * 0.4f;
                        Visuals.Box("House", root, new Vector3(x, quayTop + height / 2f, 6.6f), new Vector3(3f, height, 2.4f), walls[i % walls.Length]);
                        var roof = Visuals.Box("Roof", root, new Vector3(x, quayTop + height, 6.6f), new Vector3(3.1f, 1.2f, 1.2f), i % 2 == 0 ? Palette.Accent : Palette.HullNavy);
                        roof.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(45f, 0f, 0f);
                        Visuals.Box("Door", root, new Vector3(x, quayTop + 0.45f, 5.38f), new Vector3(0.5f, 0.9f, 0.05f), Palette.HullNavy);
                    }
                    // Codex's shop front (awning, displays, door frame) on the water-facing wall; the sign sits above it.
                    var front = lib?.M(ShopFronts[i]);
                    float signY = 1.55f;
                    if (front != null)
                    {
                        Place(front, new Vector3(x, quayTop, 4.95f));
                        signY = Mathf.Max(signY, ModelHeight(front) + 0.35f);
                    }
                    var board = Visuals.Box("Sign", root, new Vector3(x, quayTop + signY, 5.05f), new Vector3(2.2f, 0.42f, 0.06f), Palette.HullNavy);
                    var label = Visuals.Label("SignText", board.transform.parent, new Vector3(x, quayTop + signY - 0.13f, 5.0f), 0.032f);
                    label.text = sign;
                    label.color = Palette.Cream;
                    label.fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold;
                    label.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
                    var anchor = new GameObject($"{sign} bubble").transform;
                    anchor.SetParent(root, false);
                    anchor.localPosition = new Vector3(x, quayTop + height + 0.6f, 6.0f);
                    Shops.Add((anchor, tab));
                }
                // A neighbour's boat moored further along, crates on the quay.
                var neighbour = lib?.M("Rowboat_VariantRed") ?? lib?.Rowboat;
                if (neighbour != null) Place(neighbour, new Vector3(-6.5f, 0f, 0.75f), -90f).AddComponent<Floater>();
                else Visuals.Box("OtherBoat", root, new Vector3(-6.5f, -0.25f, 1.2f), new Vector3(3.6f, 0.55f, 1.6f), Palette.Hex("B8473A"));
                DressHarbour(root, lib, quayTop, R);
            }
            else BuildSea(kind, root, lib, R, Rock);
            return root;
        }

        // ---- flow -----------------------------------------------------------------------

        public void SyncCrew()
        {
            for (int i = 1; i < Rods.Count; i++)
                Rods[i].SetUnlocked(State.Hull > 0 || State.Has(UpgradeId.RodHolder) || State.Crew.Any(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman));
            if (Hand != null && Hand.Contract != State.Deckhand)
            {
                Visuals.Kill(Hand.Root.gameObject);
                Hand = null;
            }
            if (State.Deckhand != null && Hand == null)
            {
                var c = State.Deckhand;
                Hand = new Person(c.Name, false, IsNetFisher ? Vector3.zero : new Vector3(0.12f, 0f, 0.08f)) { Speed = GameData.Speed(c.Grade), Contract = c, Grid = Grid };
                Hand.Build(Boat, HandRest, c.Suit, c.Grade == Grade.Veteran ? Palette.HullNavy : Palette.Accent);
            }
            for (int i = ExtraPeople.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                if (!State.ExtraCrew.Contains(ExtraPeople[i].Contract))
                {
                    var p = ExtraPeople[i];
                    p.Stop();
                    foreach (var rod in Rods) if (rod.Fisher == p) rod.Fisher = null;
                    Visuals.Kill(p.Root.gameObject);
                    ExtraPeople.RemoveAt(i);
                }
            foreach (var c in State.ExtraCrew)
                if (!ExtraPeople.Any(p => p.Contract == c))
                {
                    var p = new Person(c.Name, false, IsNetFisher ? Vector3.zero : new Vector3(.12f, 0f, .08f)) { Speed = GameData.Speed(c.Grade), Contract = c, Grid = Grid };
                    ExtraPeople.Add(p);
                    p.Build(Boat, RestFor(p), c.Suit, c.Grade == Grade.Veteran ? Palette.HullNavy : Palette.Accent);
                }
        }

        public void SyncUpgrades()
        {
            if (CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour && boatVisualSignature != BoatVisualSignature) { RebuildBoat(); return; }
            for (int i = 1; i < Rods.Count; i++) Rods[i].SetUnlocked(State.Hull > 0 || State.Has(UpgradeId.RodHolder) || State.Crew.Any(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman));
            bool ice = State.Has(UpgradeId.IceBox);
            if (IsPrepFisher) SyncPrepStorage();
            if (coolerModel != null)
            {
                coolerModel.SetActive(!ice);
                iceBoxModel.SetActive(ice);
                coolerLid = ArtLibrary.Find(ice ? iceBoxModel : coolerModel, "HingedLid");
            }
            else if (State.Hull == 0 && coolerLid != null) coolerLid.GetComponentInChildren<Renderer>().sharedMaterial = Visuals.Mat(ice ? Palette.Hex("4FB3C4") : Palette.Accent);
        }

        void SetRowing(bool on)
        {
            if (State.Hull > 0) return;
            if (modelOars)
            {
                oarPort.transform.localRotation = oarPortBase;
                oarStarboard.transform.localRotation = oarStarboardBase;
            }
            else
            {
                oarPort.SetActive(on);
                oarStarboard.SetActive(on);
            }
        }

        // Saves after selling and after every harbour purchase. The bot never touches the save file.
        public void SaveGame()
        {
            if (!AutoPlay && !SessionOnly && Application.isPlaying) State.Save();
        }

        public void NewGame()
        {
            if (!SessionOnly) GameState.DeleteSave();
            OnDeck.Clear();
            Cooler.Clear();
            State = new GameState(System.Environment.TickCount);
            SelectedSpot = 0;
            RebuildBoat(false);
            departureReady = false;
            Stats = new VoyageStats();
            refitUntil = 0f;
            ResetFeedback();
            if (oldScenery != null) Visuals.Kill(oldScenery.gameObject);
            oldScenery = null;
            if (scenery != null) Visuals.Kill(scenery.gameObject);
            scenery = BuildScenery(-1);
            EnterHarbour(false);
            hudTab = HudTab.Market;
            Toast("New game started.");
        }

        void EnterHarbour(bool arrived)
        {
            ClearPrepWork();
            State.Chum = 0;
            CurrentMode = Mode.Harbour;
            if (arrived)
            {
                State.UnsoldCatch.Clear();
                foreach (var fish in Cooler)
                    State.UnsoldCatch.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = fish.Species, Kg = fish.Kg,
                        StowedAt = -Mathf.Max(0f, SimTime - fish.StowedAt), Prepared = fish.Prepared, PreparedQuality = fish.PreparedQuality });
                State.UnsoldMinutes = (SimTime - voyageStart) / 60f;
                Cooler.Clear();
                if (State.UnsoldCatch.Count == 0) State.EndVoyage(new SaleReport());
                Toast(State.UnsoldCatch.Count > 0 ? "Haul safely ashore! Tap Sell haul to collect your coins." : "Back in Gull Harbour.");
                hudTab = HudTab.Market;
                panelOpen = true;
            }
            SyncCrew();
            Manning = null;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            Captain.Rowing = false;
            Captain.Place(SeatTile);
            Captain.Face(Vector3.forward);
            Hand?.Place(HandRest);
            foreach (var p in ExtraPeople) p.Place(RestFor(p));
            foreach (var r in Rods) r.ResetForTrip();
            SetRowing(false);
            if (!State.Known(SelectedSpot)) SelectedSpot = 0;
            SaveGame();
            botHarbourAt = SimTime + 0.5f;
        }

        public bool SellHaul()
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour || State.UnsoldCatch.Count == 0) return false;
            var sale = State.Sell(State.UnsoldCatch, 0f);
            sale.Minutes = State.UnsoldMinutes;
            State.UnsoldCatch.Clear(); // Consume once, including across save/reload.
            State.EndVoyage(sale);
            int earned = sale.Coins + sale.ContractCoins;
            audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Sale);
            Toast($"Haul sold! +{earned} coins");
            Log.Add($"Voyage {State.Voyage - 1}: {sale.Caught} fish, +{earned} coins, {sale.Minutes:0.0} min");
            SyncCrew();
            SyncUpgrades();
            SaveGame();
            return true;
        }

        public string SailBlocker()
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour) return "Not in the harbour";
            if (State.UnsoldCatch.Count > 0) return "Sell your haul first";
            if (State.Hull > 0 && State.FuelFraction <= .1f) return "Buy fuel at Supplies";
            return null;
        }

        public void SetSail()
        {
            if (SailBlocker() != null) return;
            SyncUpgrades();
            ActiveSpot = SelectedSpot;
            departureReady = false;
            voyageStart = SimTime;
            Stats = new VoyageStats();
            ResetFeedback();
            fullWarned = false;
            BeginTransition(Mode.SailingOut, ActiveSpot);
        }

        public void HeadHome()
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing) return;
            ClearNetUnload();
            netOrdersOpen = false;
            PrepOrdersOpen = false;
            // Grab what's on deck if there's room; the rest goes back in the sea.
            foreach (var p in People)
            {
                if (p == null) continue;
                p.Stop();
                if (p.Carrying != null)
                {
                    TryStore(new CaughtFish { Species = p.Carrying.Species, Kg = p.Carrying.Kg, StowedAt = p.Carrying.CaughtAt });
                    Visuals.Kill(p.Carrying.Root.gameObject);
                    p.Carrying = null;
                }
            }
            ResetNet();
            foreach (var f in OnDeck)
            {
                TryStore(new CaughtFish { Species = f.Species, Kg = f.Kg, StowedAt = f.CaughtAt });
                Visuals.Kill(f.Root.gameObject);
            }
            OnDeck.Clear();
            Manning = null;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            foreach (var r in Rods) r.ResetForTrip();
            BeginTransition(Mode.SailingHome, -1);
        }

        void BeginTransition(Mode mode, int sceneryKind)
        {
            CurrentMode = mode;
            modeTimer = 0f;
            modeDuration = sceneryKind < 0 ? GameData.Spots[ActiveSpot].RowSeconds : GameData.Spots[sceneryKind].RowSeconds;
            modeDuration *= State.HullDef.TravelMultiplier;
            if (oldScenery != null) Visuals.Kill(oldScenery.gameObject);
            oldScenery = scenery;
            scenery = BuildScenery(sceneryKind);
            scenery.localPosition = new Vector3(.573576f, 0, .819152f) * 30f;
            seaFrom = seaMat.color;
            seaTo = sceneryKind < 0 ? Palette.Sea : GameData.Spots[sceneryKind].Water;
            Captain.Stop();
            Captain.Place(SeatTile);
            Captain.Face(new Vector3(-bowSign, 0f, 0f)); // rowers face the stern
            Captain.Rowing = State.Hull == 0;
            Hand?.Stop();
            Hand?.Place(HandRest);
            foreach (var p in ExtraPeople) { p.Stop(); p.Place(RestFor(p)); }
            SetRowing(true);
            audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Moor, .55f);
            UpdateTravel();
        }

        void FinishTransition()
        {
            if (oldScenery != null) Visuals.Kill(oldScenery.gameObject);
            oldScenery = null;
            scenery.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
            travelOffset = Vector3.zero;
            travelYaw = travelWake = 0f;
            seaMat.color = seaTo;
            SetWaterBiome(CurrentMode == Mode.SailingHome ? -1 : ActiveSpot);
            Captain.Rowing = false;
            SetRowing(false);
            if (CurrentMode == Mode.SailingOut)
            {
                CurrentMode = Mode.Fishing;
                Toast($"Arrived at {GameData.Spots[ActiveSpot].Name}.");
            }
            else { EnterHarbour(true); audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Moor, .7f); }
        }

        // ---- simulation -------------------------------------------------------------------

        public float WorkMult => GameData.CrewWorkSpeed;
        int CrewCount => State.CrewCount + 1;

        public void Tick(float dt)
        {
            if (!built) return;
            float total = dt;
            while (dt > 0f)
            {
                float step = Mathf.Min(dt, 0.05f);
                dt -= step;
                Step(step);
            }
            Visual(total);
        }

        void Step(float dt)
        {
            SimTime += dt;
            var camRot = Cam.transform.rotation;
            switch (CurrentMode)
            {
                case Mode.Harbour:
                    if (AutoPlay && SimTime >= botHarbourAt) BotHarbour();
                    break;

                case Mode.SailingOut:
                case Mode.SailingHome:
                    BurnFuel(dt);
                    modeTimer += dt * (State.Hull > 0 ? 1f : WorkMult);
                    UpdateTravel();
                    float stroke = Mathf.Sin(SimTime * 3.2f);
                    if (oarPort != null) oarPort.transform.localRotation = oarPortBase * Quaternion.Euler(stroke * 8f, stroke * 25f, 0f);
                    if (oarStarboard != null) oarStarboard.transform.localRotation = oarStarboardBase * Quaternion.Euler(-stroke * 8f, -stroke * 25f, 0f);
                    if (propeller != null) propeller.Rotate(IsNetFisher ? Vector3.up : Vector3.forward, 1000f * dt, Space.Self);
                    if (modeTimer >= modeDuration) FinishTransition();
                    break;

                case Mode.Fishing:
                    BurnFuel(dt);
                    if (CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing) break;
                    float mult = WorkMult;
                    foreach (var r in Rods)
                        if (r.Unlocked) r.Tick(this, dt, mult, Attending(r) || FisherAt(r));
                    ManRod();
                    if (Hand != null && (Hand.Idle || Hand.Loitering)) HandThink(Hand);
                    foreach (var employee in ExtraPeople)
                        if (employee.Contract.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman) FisherThink(employee);
                        else if (employee.Idle || employee.Loitering) HandThink(employee);
                    TickNet(dt);
                    TickNetUnload(dt);
                    if (AutoPlay) BotFish();
                    TickDeckFish(dt);
                    if (!fullWarned && HoldFull)
