                    {
                        fullWarned = true;
                        Toast("The cooler is full. Head home to sell.");
                    }
                    break;
            }
            Captain.Pose = Manning?.State switch { RodState.Flying => "Cast", RodState.Waiting or RodState.Bite => "Wait", RodState.Reeling => "Reel", _ => "Idle" };
            Captain.Tick(dt, WorkMult, camRot);
            Hand?.Tick(dt, WorkMult, camRot);
            foreach (var p in ExtraPeople) p.Tick(dt, WorkMult, camRot);
        }

        void BurnFuel(float dt)
        {
            if (State.Hull == 0) return;
            State.Fuel = Mathf.Max(0, State.Fuel - dt / 60f);
            if (CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing && State.FuelFraction <= .1f)
            {
                confirmHeadHome = false;
                HeadHome();
                Toast("Fuel reserve reached · returning to Gull Harbour.");
            }
        }

        void TickDeckFish(float dt)
        {
            for (int i = OnDeck.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                var f = OnDeck[i];
                f.Age += dt;
                if (f.FromNetBasket && f.Root.parent != Boat) continue;
                if (f.FromNetBasket && f.Age >= .4f)
                {
                    f.Root.localPosition = f.RestPos;
                    f.Root.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
                    continue;
                }
                if (f.Age < 0.4f)
                {
                    // Tossed from the rod tip onto the deck.
                    float a = f.Age / 0.4f;
                    f.Root.localPosition = Vector3.Lerp(f.SpawnFrom, f.RestPos, a) + Vector3.up * Mathf.Sin(a * Mathf.PI) * 0.5f;
                    f.Root.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(a * 360f, 0f, 0f);
                    continue;
                }
                if (f.Tail != null) f.Tail.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, Mathf.Sin(f.Age * 14f) * 35f, 0f);
                float flop = Mathf.Max(0f, Mathf.Sin(f.Age * 7f + i)) * 0.1f;
                f.Root.localPosition = f.RestPos + Vector3.up * flop;
                f.Root.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, f.Age * 40f % 360f, Mathf.Sin(f.Age * 9f) * 25f);
                if (f.ClaimedBy == null && f.Age > GameData.DeckFishLifetime)
                {
                    Visuals.Kill(f.Root.gameObject);
                    OnDeck.RemoveAt(i);
                    Stats.Lost++;
                    if (!f.CaughtByCrew) Toast("A fish flopped back overboard.");
                }
            }
        }

        Vector3 camCenter = new(1.0f, 0f, -0.7f);
        public float ZoomOffset;
        bool snapCamera = true;

        void Visual(float frameDt)
        {
            bool inTransit = CurrentMode is Mode.SailingOut or Mode.SailingHome;
            audio?.Mix(MenuOpen || captureFreeze || !Application.isFocused, CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour, inTransit && State.Hull == 0 && travelWake > .1f);
            PtsWater.HullPose(world.TransformPoint(travelOffset + State.HullDef.Center), travelYaw, State.Hull > 0,
                SimTime, out float heave, out Quaternion tilt);
            Boat.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, travelYaw, 0) * tilt;
            // Rotate about the hull centre, rather than the corner of the deck grid.
            var pivotCorrection = State.HullDef.Center - Boat.localRotation * State.HullDef.Center;
            Boat.localPosition = travelOffset + pivotCorrection + Vector3.up * heave
                + (IsPrepFisher ? Vector3.back * .65f : Vector3.zero);
            if (seaMat.HasProperty("_Clock"))
            {
                seaMat.SetFloat("_Clock", PtsWater.Clock(SimTime));
                seaMat.SetVector("_BoatCenter", Boat.TransformPoint(State.HullDef.Center));
                seaMat.SetVector("_BoatShape", new Vector4(IsPrepFisher ? 3.85f : State.Hull > 0 ? 3.25f : 2.1f, IsPrepFisher ? 2f : State.Hull > 0 ? 1.75f : 1.2f,
                    inTransit ? travelWake : 0f, travelYaw * Mathf.Deg2Rad));
            }
            // Camera: wide on the harbour (shifted left of the shop panel), close on the water.
            bool harbour = CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour;
            float harbourBlend = harbour ? 1f : CurrentMode == Mode.SailingOut ? 1f - Segment(TravelProgress, .18f, .65f)
                : CurrentMode == Mode.SailingHome ? Segment(TravelProgress, .55f, 1f) : 0f;
            var center = Vector3.Lerp(Boat.TransformPoint(State.HullDef.Center) + new Vector3(.35f, 0f, -.30f), new Vector3(.3f, 0, 3.4f), harbourBlend);
            if (harbour && panelOpen) center += Cam.transform.right * 3.7f;
            float k = snapCamera ? 1f : 1f - Mathf.Exp(-frameDt * 3f);
            snapCamera = false;
            camCenter = Vector3.Lerp(camCenter, center, k);
            Cam.orthographicSize = Mathf.Lerp(Cam.orthographicSize, Mathf.Lerp(IsPrepFisher ? 5.35f : State.Hull > 0 ? 4.8f : 3.2f, 5.6f, harbourBlend) + ZoomOffset, k);
            Cam.transform.position = camCenter - Cam.transform.forward * 40f;
            RefreshInterface();

            float waveSpeed = CurrentMode is Mode.SailingOut or Mode.SailingHome ? 3.5f : 0.25f;
            foreach (var w in waves)
            {
                var p = w.position;
                p.x -= waveSpeed * frameDt * bowSign;
                if (p.x > 15f) p.x -= 30f;
                if (p.x < -15f) p.x += 30f;
                w.position = p;
            }
            for (int i = splashes.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                var (t, age) = splashes[i];
                age += frameDt;
                float s = 0.2f + age * 1.6f;
                t.localScale = new Vector3(s, 0.005f, s);
                if (age > 0.45f)
                {
                    Visuals.Kill(t.gameObject);
                    splashes.RemoveAt(i);
                }
                else splashes[i] = (t, age);
            }
            // Fish swimming around the boat; they keep their distance while sailing.
            bool sailing = CurrentMode is Mode.SailingOut or Mode.SailingHome;
            for (int i = 0; i < school.Count; i++)
            {
                var (t, speed, radius, phase) = school[i];
                t.gameObject.SetActive(!sailing && CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour);
                float ang = phase + SimTime * speed;
                var c = new Vector3(1f, 0f, 0.5f);
                var pos = c + new Vector3(Mathf.Cos(ang) * radius * 1.4f, 0f, Mathf.Sin(ang) * radius);
                var next = c + new Vector3(Mathf.Cos(ang + 0.05f * Mathf.Sign(speed)) * radius * 1.4f, 0f, Mathf.Sin(ang + 0.05f * Mathf.Sign(speed)) * radius);
                t.position = new Vector3(pos.x, WaterY - .60f, pos.z);
                t.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(next - pos);
            }

            lidPop = Mathf.Max(0f, lidPop - frameDt * 3f);
            if (coolerLid != null) coolerLid.localRotation = IsNetFisher ? compactLidRest * Quaternion.Euler(lidPop * 90f, 0f, 0f) : Quaternion.Euler(-lidPop * 70f, 0f, 0f);
            coolerLabel.text = $"{HoldKg:0.00}/{State.CoolerCapacity:0.#} kg";
            coolerLabel.color = HoldFull ? Palette.Oilskin : Palette.Rail;
            coolerLabel.transform.rotation = Cam.transform.rotation;
        }

        public void Splash(Vector3 pos, float size, bool sound = true)
        {
            if (sound) audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Splash, .38f);
            if (seaMat.HasProperty("_Impact")) seaMat.SetVector("_Impact", new Vector4(pos.x, pos.z, SimTime, Mathf.Clamp01(size * 2f)));
            var s = Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "Splash", world, pos + Vector3.up * 0.01f, new Vector3(size, 0.005f, size), new Color(0.92f, 0.97f, 1f));
            splashes.Add((s.transform, 0f));
        }

        public void Toast(string msg)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(msg)) return;
            toast = msg;
            toastUntil = SimTime + 3.2f;
        }

        // ---- deck actions (one tap each) -------------------------------------------------------
        //
        // Tap a rod's bubble: the captain walks there, baits if needed and casts by himself.
        // While he mans that rod the strike minigame is on screen; one tap strikes.
        // The fish lands next to him. Solo you tap its bubble to carry it to the cooler;
        // with a deckhand they fetch it and the rod's bubble comes back for the next cast.

        public Rod Manning { get; private set; }
        public bool AwaitingCast { get; private set; }
        public bool HasDeckhand => State.Crew.Any(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand);

        public bool ThrowAgain()
        {
            if (!AwaitingCast || Manning == null || CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing) return false;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            return true;
        }

        public void LeaveFishing()
        {
            // Only leave the between-casts panel; an active cast must resolve first.
            if (!AwaitingCast) return;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            Manning = null;
        }

        public bool Attending(Rod r) => !Captain.Walking && Captain.Tile == r.Tile;

        // The rod whose strike minigame is on screen, if any.
        public Rod MinigameRod => Manning != null && Attending(Manning) && Manning.State is RodState.Flying or RodState.Waiting or RodState.Bite or RodState.Reeling ? Manning : null;

        public bool CanCommandRod(Rod r) =>
            CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing && r.Unlocked && Manning != r
            && r.Fisher == null
            && (r.State is RodState.Waiting or RodState.Bite || r.State == RodState.Ready || r.State == RodState.Unbaited && (State.HasAnyBait || r.BaitingBy != null));

        public bool CanCarry(DeckFish f) => CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing && f.ClaimedBy == null && f.Age > 0.4f
            && (!IsNetFisher || Captain.Idle && (Manning == null || Manning.State is not (RodState.Reeling or RodState.Flying)));

        public void CommandRod(Rod r)
        {
            if (!CanCommandRod(r)) return;
            if (Captain.Carrying != null) { Toast("Stow your fish first."); return; }
            Manning = r;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            if (!Attending(r)) Captain.WalkTo(Grid, r.Tile, () => Captain.Face(r.Outward));
        }

        public void CommandCarry(DeckFish f)
        {
            if (!CanCarry(f)) return;
            if (Captain.Carrying != null) { Toast("Your hands are full."); return; }
            Manning = null;
            AwaitingCast = false;
            Fetch(Captain, f);
        }

        // One tap during the minigame strikes. Too early on a nibble or too late both cost the bait.
        public void StrikeTap()
        {
            var r = MinigameRod;
            if (r != null && r.State is RodState.Waiting or RodState.Bite)
            {
                Captain.PlayOnce("Strike");
                if (r.State == RodState.Bite) audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Reel, .6f);
                Toast(r.Strike(this));
            }
        }

        // The captain runs the rod he was sent to: bait, then cast.
        void ManRod()
        {
            var r = Manning;
            if (r == null || !Captain.Idle || Captain.Carrying != null || !Attending(r)) return;
            Captain.Face(r.Outward);
            if (AwaitingCast) return;
            switch (r.State)
            {
                case RodState.Unbaited:
                    if (r.BaitingBy != null) return; // the deckhand is on it
                    Bait(Captain, r);
                    break;
                case RodState.Ready:
                    Captain.Face(r.Outward);
                    Captain.Do("Casting", GameData.CastTime, () => { r.Cast(this, 0.35f + (float)State.Rng.NextDouble() * 0.4f); audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Cast); });
                    break;
            }
        }

        public void LandFish(Rod rod, Species s, float kg)
        {
            State.OnCatch(ActiveSpot, kg);
            Stats.Landed++;
            var f = new DeckFish { Species = s, Kg = kg, Tile = rod.Tile, CaughtAt = SimTime, CaughtByCrew = rod.Fisher != null };
            f.Root = DeckFish.BuildModel(s, kg, Boat);
            int n = OnDeck.Count(o => o.Tile == rod.Tile);
            f.RestPos = Grid.GetLocal(rod.Tile) + (IsNetFisher ? new Vector3(-.12f + n * .08f, .07f, .08f) : new Vector3(-0.22f + n * 0.18f, 0.07f, 0.3f));
            f.SpawnFrom = rod.TipLocal;
            foreach (var t in f.Root.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>())
                if (t.name.EndsWith("Tail")) f.Tail = t;
            f.Root.localPosition = f.SpawnFrom;
            OnDeck.Add(f);
            if (rod == Manning)
            {
                if (HasDeckhand) AwaitingCast = true;
                else Manning = null;
                Captain.PlayOnce("Cheer");
            }
            if (rod.Fisher == null) { ShowCatch(s, kg); audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Land, .6f); }
        }

        public static float FishHandlingSeconds(Person person, float seconds)
            => seconds / (person?.Contract?.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand ? GameData.DeckhandHandlingSpeed : 1f);

        void Fetch(Person p, DeckFish f)
        {
            f.ClaimedBy = p;
            void Release() { if (f.ClaimedBy == p) f.ClaimedBy = null; }
            if (!p.WalkTo(Grid, f.Tile, () =>
            {
                p.FacePoint(f.RestPos);
                p.Do("Picking up", FishHandlingSeconds(p, IsNetFisher ? GameData.CompactPickupTime : GameData.PickupTime), () => PickUp(p, f), Release);
            }, Release)) Release();
        }

        void PickUp(Person p, DeckFish f)
        {
            if (!OnDeck.Contains(f)) return;
            OnDeck.Remove(f);
            f.Root.SetParent(p.Root, false);
            p.Carrying = f;
            if (p == Captain) Stow(p);
        }

        void Stow(Person p)
        {
            // Actors may overlap for playability. Fish claims/capacity remain atomic;
            // one actor's stow animation never locks out another delivery.
            p.WalkTo(Grid, CoolerAccess, () =>
            {
                FaceHold(p);
                p.Do("Stowing", FishHandlingSeconds(p, IsNetFisher ? GameData.CompactStowTime : GameData.StowTime), () => TryStow(p));
            });
        }

        bool TryStow(Person p)
        {
            if (p.Carrying == null) return false;
            if (HoldFull)
            {
                if (p == Captain) Toast("The cooler is full! Head home to sell.");
                return false;
            }
            var secured = new CaughtFish { Species = p.Carrying.Species, Kg = p.Carrying.Kg, StowedAt = p.Carrying.CaughtAt };
            if (!TryStore(secured)) return false;
            if (p.Carrying.FromNetBasket) RecordStoredNet(new[] { secured });
            bool captainCatch = !p.Carrying.CaughtByCrew;
            Visuals.Kill(p.Carrying.Root.gameObject);
            p.Carrying = null;
            lidPop = 1f;
            if (captainCatch) { stowedAt = SimTime; audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Stow, .55f); }
            return true;
        }

        void Bait(Person p, Rod rod)
        {
            if (!State.HasAnyBait) return;
            p.Face(rod.Outward);
            rod.BaitingBy = p;
            p.Do("Baiting", GameData.BaitTime, () =>
            {
                rod.BaitingBy = null;
                bool scraps = !State.HasPaidBait;
                if (rod.State == RodState.Unbaited && State.TakeBait(out bool squid, out bool chum))
                {
                    rod.Scraps = scraps;
                    rod.Squid = squid;
                    rod.Chum = chum;
                    rod.State = RodState.Ready;
                }
            }, () => rod.BaitingBy = null);
        }

        // Deckhand: carry fish to the cooler first, then bait empty hooks, then rest.
        void HandThink(Person h)
        {
            if (IsNetUnloading(h)) return;
            var rest = RestFor(h);
            if (h.Carrying != null)
            {
                if (!HoldFull) Stow(h);
                else if (h.Tile != rest) h.WalkTo(Grid, rest, null);
                else h.Do("Waiting", 1f, null);
                return;
            }
            if (IsPrepFisher && h.Contract.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep)
            {
                TryPrepWork(h); // Stay at the table even between jobs / while waiting for inputs.
                return;
            }
            if (IsNetFisher && h.Contract.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Nets)
            {
                var basket = OnDeck.FirstOrDefault(x => x.FromNetBasket && x.ClaimedBy == null && x.Age > .4f);
                if (basket != null && !HoldFull) { Fetch(h, basket); return; }
                if (netWorker == null && !NetBasketBlocked && !HoldFull
                    && (Net == NetState.Ready || Net == NetState.Stowed && State.ActiveNetStock > 0))
                {
                    if (h.Loitering) h.Stop();
                    if (TryNetWork(h)) return;
                }
                // Net duty is a real allocation: this hand leaves rod catches to captain/other hands.
                IdlePatrol(h);
                return;
            }
            var f = OnDeck.FirstOrDefault(x => !x.FromNetBasket && x.ClaimedBy == null && x.Age > 0.4f);
            if (f != null)
            {
                Fetch(h, f);
                return;
            }
            var rod = Rods.FirstOrDefault(r => r.Unlocked && r.State == RodState.Unbaited && r.BaitingBy == null);
            if (rod != null && State.HasAnyBait)
            {
                rod.BaitingBy = h;
                h.WalkTo(Grid, rod.Tile, () => Bait(h, rod), () => rod.BaitingBy = null);
                return;
            }
            IdlePatrol(h);
        }

        bool FisherAt(Rod rod) => rod.Fisher != null && !rod.Fisher.Walking && rod.Fisher.Tile == rod.Tile;

        void FisherThink(Person fisher)
        {
            var rod = Rods.FirstOrDefault(r => r.Fisher == fisher);
            if (rod == null)
            {
                rod = Rods.LastOrDefault(r => r.Unlocked && r != Manning && r.Fisher == null);
                if (rod == null) return;
                rod.Fisher = fisher;
            }
            fisher.Pose = rod.State switch { RodState.Flying => "Cast", RodState.Waiting or RodState.Bite => "Wait", RodState.Reeling => "Reel", _ => "Idle" };
            if (!fisher.Idle) return;
            if (!FisherAt(rod)) { fisher.WalkTo(Grid, rod.Tile, () => fisher.Face(rod.Outward)); return; }
            fisher.Face(rod.Outward);
            if (rod.State == RodState.Unbaited && rod.BaitingBy == null && State.HasAnyBait) Bait(fisher, rod);
            else if (rod.State == RodState.Ready)
                fisher.Do("Casting", GameData.CastTime, () => rod.Cast(this, .55f));
            else if (rod.State == RodState.Bite && !rod.FisherAttempted)
            {
                rod.FisherAttempted = true;
                if (State.Rng.NextDouble() < GameData.StrikeChance(fisher.Contract.Grade))
                {
                    fisher.PlayOnce("Strike");
                    rod.Strike(this);
                }
            }
        }

        // ---- events from the rods (the bot listens here too) --------------------------------

        Rod botStrikeRod;
        float botStrikeAt, botHarbourAt;

        public void OnNibble(Rod r)
        {
            if (AutoPlay && r == MinigameRod && State.Rng.NextDouble() < 0.05) StrikeTap();
        }

        public void OnBite(Rod r)
        {
            if (r == MinigameRod && r.Fisher == null) audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Bite, .7f);
            if (!AutoPlay || r != MinigameRod) return;
            botStrikeRod = r;
            botStrikeAt = SimTime + (State.Rng.NextDouble() < 0.12 ? 1.0f : 0.25f + (float)State.Rng.NextDouble() * 0.5f);
        }

        // ---- autoplay (headless balance checks): only uses the same taps a player has --------

        void BotFish()
        {
            if (HoldFull) { HeadHome(); return; }
            if (State.Hull > 0 && Captain.Idle && Captain.Carrying == null && MinigameRod == null && Manning == null)
            {
                if (Net == NetState.Ready) { HaulNet(); return; }
                if (Net == NetState.Stowed && HoldKg + OnDeck.Sum(f => f.Kg) < State.CoolerCapacity) { SetNet(); return; }
            }
            if (botStrikeRod != null && SimTime >= botStrikeAt)
            {
                if (botStrikeRod == MinigameRod) StrikeTap();
                botStrikeRod = null;
            }
            if (AwaitingCast) ThrowAgain();
            var c = Captain;
            if (!c.Idle || c.Carrying != null || MinigameRod != null) return;

            bool handBusy = Hand != null && (Hand.Carrying != null || !Hand.Idle && Hand.BusyLabel == "Picking up");
            bool full = HoldFull;
            bool noBait = !State.HasAnyBait && Rods.All(r => !r.Unlocked || r.State == RodState.Unbaited);
            if (full || State.Hull == 0 && noBait && OnDeck.Count == 0 && !handBusy && Rods.All(r => r.State is not RodState.Reeling))
            {
                HeadHome();
                return;
            }
            var f = OnDeck.FirstOrDefault(CanCarry);
            if (f != null) { CommandCarry(f); return; }
            if (Manning != null || Hand == null && OnDeck.Any(x => x.ClaimedBy == null)) return;
            var rod = Rods.Where(CanCommandRod).OrderByDescending(r => r.State == RodState.Ready).FirstOrDefault();
            if (rod != null) CommandRod(rod);
        }

        void BotHarbour()
        {
            botHarbourAt = float.MaxValue;
            SellHaul();
            while (State.Active.Count < GameData.MaxActiveContracts && State.Offers.Count > 0) State.TakeContract(State.Offers[0]);
            // Buy in a fixed order, saving up for the next item (negative numbers are sea charts).
            foreach (int step in new[] { (int)UpgradeId.RodHolder, -2, (int)UpgradeId.IceBox, -3, (int)UpgradeId.RodFibreglass, (int)UpgradeId.BigCooler, (int)UpgradeId.LunchTin, (int)UpgradeId.RodCarbon, -4 })
            {
                if (step < 0)
                {
                    int spot = -step;
                    if (State.Known(spot)) continue;
                    if (State.Coins < GameData.Spots[spot].ChartCost + 30) break;
                    State.BuyChart(spot);
                    continue;
                }
                var id = (UpgradeId)step;
                if (State.Has(id)) continue;
                if (State.Coins < State.UpgradeCost(id) + 30) break;
                State.TryBuy(id);
            }
            if (State.Coins > 150 && State.Squid < 5) State.BuySquid();
            if (State.CanRefit && State.Coins >= GameData.SkiffCost + 100) RefitSkiff();
            SyncUpgrades();
            if (State.Deckhand == null && State.Has(UpgradeId.RodHolder))
            {
                var pick = State.Board.Where(b => b.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand && State.Coins >= b.Fee + 80).OrderByDescending(b => b.Grade).FirstOrDefault();
                if (pick != null) State.Hire(pick);
                SyncCrew();
            }
            if (State.Hull > 0)
            {
                foreach (var candidate in State.Board.OrderByDescending(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman).ThenByDescending(c => c.Grade).ToList())
                    if (State.Coins >= candidate.Fee + 70 && (candidate.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand || !State.Crew.Any(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman))) State.Hire(candidate);
                SyncCrew();
            }
            while (State.Bait < (State.Hull > 0 ? 60 : State.Has(UpgradeId.RodHolder) ? 40 : 25) && State.BuyBait()) { }
            if (State.Hull > 0) State.BuyFuel();

            float best = -1f;
            for (int i = 0; i < GameData.Spots.Length; i++)
            {
                if (!State.Known(i)) continue;
                var s = GameData.Spots[i];
                float total = 0f, value = 0f;
                foreach (var e in s.Table)
                {
                    total += e.weight;
                    value += e.weight * GameData.Get(e.species).AvgValue;
                }
                float score = value / total * State.Richness[i] / (1f + s.RowSeconds / 30f);
                if (score > best) { best = score; SelectedSpot = i; }
            }
            if (State.Hull > 0)
                while (State.FuelFraction <= .1f && State.BuyFuel()) { }
            SetSail();
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageHaul.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public List<CaughtFish> CurrentHaul()
        {
            var haul = new List<CaughtFish>(Cooler);
            haul.AddRange(UnstowedHaul());
            return haul;
        }

        // A picked-up fish leaves OnDeck, so these two groups do not overlap.
        public List<CaughtFish> UnstowedHaul() => OnDeck
            .Concat(People.Where(p => p != null && p.Carrying != null).Select(p => p.Carrying))
            .Select(f => new CaughtFish { Species = f.Species, Kg = f.Kg, StowedAt = f.CaughtAt }).ToList();

        public int DepartureOverflow()
        {
            // Match HeadHome exactly: actors' carried fish first, then deck order.
            var fish = People.Where(p => p != null && p.Carrying != null).Select(p => p.Carrying)
                .Concat(OnDeck).ToList();
            double kg = Cooler.Sum(f => (double)f.Kg); int left = 0;
            foreach (var f in fish)
                if (kg < State.CoolerCapacity) kg += f.Kg; else left++;
            return left;
        }

        bool confirmHeadHome;

        // Player input only; automation and HeadHome's capacity/payout rules stay unchanged.
        void RequestHeadHome()
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing) return;
            if (DepartureOverflow() == 0 && (!IsNetFisher || Net == NetState.Stowed)) { HeadHome(); return; }
            confirmHeadHome = true;
            paused = true; // Freeze catches, freshness and crew while the exact loss is displayed.
        }

        void CancelHeadHome()
        {
            confirmHeadHome = false;
            paused = false;
        }

        void ConfirmHeadHome()
        {
            confirmHeadHome = false;
            paused = false;
            HeadHome();
        }

        void DrawHeadHomeConfirmation()
        {
            Track(new Rect(0f, 0f, VW, VH));
            Dim(1f);
            var p = Panel(new Rect(VW / 2f - Mathf.Min(520f, VW - 32f) / 2f, VH / 2f - 170f, Mathf.Min(520f, VW - 32f), 340f));
            int overflow = DepartureOverflow();
            GUI.Label(new Rect(p.x + 20f, p.y + 16f, p.width - 40f, 40f), "Leave fish behind?", Ui.H2);
            GUI.Label(new Rect(p.x + 20f, p.y + 64f, p.width - 40f, 90f),
                (overflow > 0 ? $"{overflow} fish cannot fit in the hold and will go back to sea. " : "") +
                (IsNetFisher && Net != NetState.Stowed ? Net == NetState.Ready ? $"Your net still has {NetCatch} fish to haul. Leaving forfeits them. " : "Your net is still deployed; leaving ends this soak. " : "") +
                "The trip is paused while you choose.", Ui.Body);
            if (GUI.Button(new Rect(p.x + 20f, p.y + 174f, p.width - 40f, 60f), "Keep fishing · Esc", Ui.BtnAlt))
            { CancelHeadHome(); return; }
            if (GUI.Button(new Rect(p.x + 20f, p.y + 250f, p.width - 40f, 60f), overflow > 0 ? $"Head home · leave {overflow} fish" : "Head home · abandon net catch", Ui.Btn)) ConfirmHeadHome();
        }

        public string FreshnessStatus(List<CaughtFish> haul, float now)
        {
            if (haul.Count == 0) return "No catch yet";
            if (State.Has(UpgradeId.IceBox)) return "Iced · full value";
            if (haul.All(f => f.Prepared)) return "Prepared / never spoils";
            haul = haul.Where(f => !f.Prepared).ToList();
            float oldest = haul.Max(f => Mathf.Max(0f, now - f.StowedAt));
            float grace = GameData.FreshnessGraceSeconds - oldest;
            if (grace > 0f) return $"Value drops in {Mathf.CeilToInt(grace)}s";
            float freshness = haul.Min(f => State.Freshness(f, now));
            return freshness <= GameData.MinFreshness ? "Oldest: 60% value · minimum" :
                $"Oldest: {freshness * 100f:0}% · loses 7%/min";
        }

        void DrawCastChoice()
        {
            if (!AwaitingCast || Manning == null) return;
            var p = Panel(new Rect(VW / 2f - 200f, VH - 146f, 400f, 128f));
            GUI.Label(new Rect(p.x + 12f, p.y + 10f, p.width - 24f, 28f), "Deckhand takes care of your catch", Ui.Center);
            var cast = new Rect(p.x + 14f, p.y + 50f, 244f, 60f);
            var leave = new Rect(p.x + 268f, p.y + 50f, 118f, 60f);
            Track(cast); Track(leave);
            bool full = HoldFull;
            if (GUI.Button(cast, full ? "Cooler full" : "Throw again", full ? Ui.BtnOff : Ui.Btn) && !full) ThrowAgain();
            if (GUI.Button(leave, "Leave", Ui.BtnAlt)) LeaveFishing();
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageHud.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // Runtime presentation uses the supplied kit and one shared landscape layout space.
    // On the water everything is one tap: bubbles above rods and fish, and a strike minigame.
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public enum HudTab { Market, HarbourMaster, CrewOffice, Chandlery, Supplies }

        static string TabName(HudTab t) => t switch { HudTab.Market => "Market", HudTab.HarbourMaster => "Harbour Master", HudTab.CrewOffice => "Crew Office", HudTab.Chandlery => "Chandlery", _ => "Supplies" };
        bool panelOpen = true;
        // Layout uses the same safe-area space as menus; no device-specific scaling.

        HudTab hudTab = HudTab.Market;

        readonly List<Rect> hudRects = new();

        Rect HudViewport => Screen.safeArea.width > 0 ? Screen.safeArea : new Rect(0, 0, Screen.width, Screen.height);
        float VH => HudViewport.height < 600 ? 480f : 720f;
        float GuiScale => HudViewport.height / VH;
        float VW => HudViewport.width / GuiScale;

        Vector2 ToGui(Vector2 screen) => new((screen.x - HudViewport.x) / GuiScale, (HudViewport.yMax - screen.y) / GuiScale);
        bool OverHud(Vector2 gui) => hudRects.Any(r => r.Contains(gui));

        // ---- input --------------------------------------------------------------------------

        void HandleInput()
        {
            var kb = Keyboard.current;
            if (PrepOrdersOpen)
            {
                if (kb != null && kb.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame) PrepOrdersOpen = false;
                return;
            }
            if (departureReady)
            {
                if (kb != null && kb.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame) departureReady = false;
                return;
            }
            if (NetGameActive && !MenuOpen && !confirmHeadHome)
            {
                if (kb != null && kb.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame) CancelNetHaulGame();
                else if (!netOrdersOpen && Application.isFocused)
                {
                    var netPointer = Pointer.current;
                    bool cancel = netPointer != null && NetGameCancelRect.Contains(ToGui(netPointer.position.ReadValue()));
                    if ((kb != null && kb.spaceKey.wasPressedThisFrame) || (netPointer != null && netPointer.press.wasPressedThisFrame && !cancel)) TapNetGame();
                }
                return;
            }
            if (confirmHeadHome)
            {
                if (kb != null && kb.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame) CancelHeadHome();
                else if (kb != null && (kb.enterKey.wasPressedThisFrame || kb.numpadEnterKey.wasPressedThisFrame)) ConfirmHeadHome();
                return; // H/Space and pointer strikes cannot bypass the protected choice.
            }
            if (kb != null && kb.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame && !showTitle)
            {
                if (showSettings) showSettings = false;
                else paused = !paused;
            }
            if (MenuOpen) return;
            if (kb != null && CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing)
            {
                if (kb.hKey.wasPressedThisFrame) { RequestHeadHome(); return; }
                if (kb.spaceKey.wasPressedThisFrame) StrikeTap();
            }

            var mouse = Mouse.current;
            if (mouse != null)
            {
                float s = mouse.scroll.ReadValue().y;
                if (Mathf.Abs(s) > 0.01f && !OverHud(ToGui(mouse.position.ReadValue()))) ZoomOffset = Mathf.Clamp(ZoomOffset - Mathf.Sign(s) * 0.3f, -1.5f, 3f);
            }

            // While the minigame is up, a tap anywhere that isn't a button strikes (on press, so it feels instant).
            var pointer = Pointer.current;
            if (pointer == null || !pointer.press.wasPressedThisFrame) return;
            if (CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing && MinigameRod != null && !OverHud(ToGui(pointer.position.ReadValue()))) StrikeTap();
        }

        // ---- drawing ------------------------------------------------------------------------

        void OnGUI()
        {
            if (!built) return;
            if (coastalUi != null && !MenuOpen && !confirmHeadHome && SimTime >= refitUntil) return;
            Ui.Init();
            Ui.SetTypeScale(UiScales[UiScaleIndex]);
            GUI.skin.verticalScrollbar = Ui.ScrollTrack;
            GUI.skin.verticalScrollbarThumb = Ui.ScrollThumb;
            GUI.skin.verticalScrollbarUpButton = GUIStyle.none;
            GUI.skin.verticalScrollbarDownButton = GUIStyle.none;
            GUI.matrix = Matrix4x4.TRS(new Vector3(HudViewport.x, Screen.height - HudViewport.yMax, 0), Quaternion.identity, new Vector3(GuiScale, GuiScale, 1f));
            if (Event.current.type == EventType.Layout) hudRects.Clear();

            // Menus replace the HUD so nothing underneath can be clicked.
            if (showTitle) { DrawTitle(); return; }
            if (confirmHeadHome) { DrawHeadHomeConfirmation(); return; }
            if (paused) { DrawPause(); return; }
            if (SimTime < refitUntil)
            {
                Dim(Mathf.Clamp01((refitUntil - SimTime) / 1.2f));
                GUI.Label(new Rect(VW / 2f - 200f, 310f, 400f, 80f), "Your skiff is ready", Ui.TitleCenter);
                return;
            }

            RefreshInterface();
        }

        Rect Panel(Rect r, GUIStyle style = null)
        {
            Track(r);
            GUI.Box(r, GUIContent.none, style ?? Ui.Panel);
            return r;
        }

        void Track(Rect r)
        {
            if (Event.current.type == EventType.Layout) hudRects.Add(r);
        }

        static void Icon(Rect r, string name)
        {
            var t = Ui.Tex(name);
            if (t != null) GUI.DrawTexture(r, t, ScaleMode.ScaleToFit, true);
        }

        // The strike minigame: a close-up of the bobber with the fish circling in.

        // ---- harbour ------------------------------------------------------------------------
        //
        // The quay has five shops. Tap a building's bubble (or a tab) to open its panel.

        static string ShopIcon(HudTab t) => t switch { HudTab.Market => "icon-coin", HudTab.HarbourMaster => "icon-map-pin", HudTab.CrewOffice => "icon-crew", HudTab.Chandlery => "icon-home-anchor", _ => "icon-fuel" };
        static string ShortTab(HudTab t) => t switch { HudTab.HarbourMaster => "Chart", HudTab.CrewOffice => "Crew", HudTab.Chandlery => "Gear", _ => TabName(t) };
        static string ShopHeader(HudTab t) => t switch { HudTab.HarbourMaster => "header-harbourmaster", HudTab.CrewOffice => "header-crewoffice", HudTab.Chandlery => "header-chandlery", HudTab.Supplies => "header-supplies", _ => "header-market" };

        void OpenShop(HudTab tab)
        {
            audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Select, .35f);
            hudTab = tab;
            panelOpen = true;

        }

        string CoinShortfall(int cost) => State.Coins < cost ? $"Need {cost - State.Coins} more coins" : null;

        int chartFocus;

    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageIllustratedChart.cs
========================================================================
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        static T ChartGraphic<T>(RectTransform parent) where T : Graphic
        {
            var graphic = parent.GetComponentInChildren<T>(true);
            if (graphic != null) return graphic;
            var go = new GameObject(typeof(T).Name, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer));
            go.layer = 5; go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform; rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero; rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            rect.offsetMin = rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
            return go.AddComponent<T>();
        }
    }
    public abstract class CoastGraphic : MaskableGraphic
    {
        protected VertexHelper mesh;
        protected Vector2 Pixel(Vector2 p) => new(rectTransform.rect.xMin + p.x, rectTransform.rect.yMax - p.y);
        protected void Tri(Vector2 a, Vector2 b, Vector2 c, Color color)
        {
            int i = mesh.currentVertCount;
            mesh.AddVert(Pixel(a), color, Vector2.zero); mesh.AddVert(Pixel(b), color, Vector2.zero); mesh.AddVert(Pixel(c), color, Vector2.zero);
            mesh.AddTriangle(i, i + 1, i + 2);
        }
        protected void Line(Vector2 a, Vector2 b, float width, Color color)
        {
            var n = new Vector2(-(b - a).y, (b - a).x).normalized * width * .5f;
            Tri(a + n, b + n, b - n, color); Tri(a + n, b - n, a - n, color);
        }
        protected void Circle(Vector2 center, float radius, Color color, int sides = 36)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < sides; i++)
            {
                float a = i * Mathf.PI * 2 / sides, b = (i + 1) * Mathf.PI * 2 / sides;
                Tri(center, center + new Vector2(Mathf.Cos(a), Mathf.Sin(a)) * radius,
                    center + new Vector2(Mathf.Cos(b), Mathf.Sin(b)) * radius, color);
            }
        }
    }
    public sealed class IllustratedRoutesGraphic : CoastGraphic
    {
        protected override void OnPopulateMesh(VertexHelper vh)
        {
            vh.Clear(); mesh = vh;
            var size = rectTransform.rect.size;
            var start = Vector2.Scale(VoyageGame.ChartHarbour + new Vector2(0, -.035f), size);
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                var end = Vector2.Scale(VoyageGame.NauticalChartPoint(spot), size);
                end.y += 27;
                var delta = end - start;
                var c1 = start + new Vector2(delta.x * .25f, delta.y * .43f);
                var c2 = end - new Vector2(delta.x * .43f, delta.y * .22f);
                Vector2 Curve(float u) => Mathf.Pow(1 - u, 3) * start + 3 * Mathf.Pow(1 - u, 2) * u * c1 + 3 * (1 - u) * u * u * c2 + u * u * u * end;
                float length = delta.magnitude; int steps = Mathf.Max(10, Mathf.CeilToInt(length / 13));
                for (int n = 0; n < steps; n++)
                {
                    float a = n / (float)steps, b = (n + .58f) / steps;
                    Line(Curve(a), Curve(b), 2.6f, new Color(1, 1, .96f, .95f));
                }
                var tangent = (end - Curve(.97f)).normalized; var normal = new Vector2(-tangent.y, tangent.x);
                Tri(end, end - tangent * 13 + normal * 5, end - tangent * 13 - normal * 5, Color.white);
            }
        }
    }
    public sealed class ChartPinGraphic : CoastGraphic
    {
        int glyph; bool selected, pin;
        public void Configure(int kind, bool chosen, bool isPin)
        { if (glyph == kind && selected == chosen && pin == isPin) return; glyph = kind; selected = chosen; pin = isPin; SetVerticesDirty(); }
        protected override void OnPopulateMesh(VertexHelper vh)
        {
            vh.Clear(); mesh = vh;
            float w = rectTransform.rect.width, h = rectTransform.rect.height;
            var c = new Vector2(w / 2, pin ? w / 2 : h / 2);
            float r = w * .46f;
            Color ink = FishReferenceStyle.Ink, symbol = pin ? Color.white : ink;
            if (pin)
            {
                Color rim = selected ? Palette.Hex("E8B444") : Palette.Hex("FFF9E9");
                Tri(c + new Vector2(-r * .72f, r * .42f), c + new Vector2(r * .72f, r * .42f), new Vector2(w / 2, h), rim);
                Circle(c, r, rim); Circle(c, r * .78f, ink); r *= .63f;
            }
            Vector2 P(float x, float y) => c + new Vector2(x, y) * r;
            void L(float x, float y, float a, float b, float width = .14f) => Line(P(x, y), P(a, b), r * width, symbol);
            void C(float x, float y, float radius) => Circle(P(x, y), r * radius, symbol);
            switch (glyph)
            {
                case 0: // wave
                    for (int row = 0; row < 2; row++)
                    {
                        var previous = P(-.85f, row * .52f - .2f);
                        for (int i = 1; i <= 16; i++) { float x = -.85f + i * 1.7f / 16; var next = P(x, Mathf.Sin(x * 5) * .19f + row * .52f - .2f); Line(previous, next, r * .13f, symbol); previous = next; }
                    }
                    break;
                case 1: // kelp fronds
                    for (int stem = -1; stem <= 1; stem++)
                    {
                        L(0, .8f, stem * .55f, -.75f);
                        for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++) { float y = .45f - j * .42f, x = stem * (.8f - y) * .34f; L(x, y, x - .28f, y - .3f); L(x, y, x + .28f, y - .3f); }
                    }
                    break;
                case 2: // scallop shell
                    for (int i = 0; i <= 7; i++) { float angle = Mathf.PI + i * Mathf.PI / 7; var edge = P(Mathf.Cos(angle) * .85f, Mathf.Sin(angle) * .85f); Line(P(0, .7f), edge, r * .13f, symbol); Circle(edge, r * .10f, symbol); }
                    L(-.25f, .74f, .25f, .74f, .22f); break;
                case 3: // rock
                    Tri(P(-.85f, .65f), P(-.6f, -.5f), P(.15f, -.8f), symbol);
                    Tri(P(-.85f, .65f), P(.15f, -.8f), P(.75f, .62f), symbol); break;
                case 4: // fish
                    Circle(P(-.05f, 0), r * .58f, symbol);
                    Tri(P(.2f, 0), P(.95f, -.52f), P(.95f, .52f), symbol); Circle(P(-.33f, -.14f), r * .09f, ink); break;
                case 5: // home
                    Tri(P(-.95f, -.05f), P(0, -.85f), P(.95f, -.05f), symbol);
                    L(-.6f, -.05f, -.6f, .8f, .25f); L(.6f, -.05f, .6f, .8f, .25f); L(-.6f, .7f, .6f, .7f, .25f); L(0, .8f, 0, .1f, .28f); break;
                case 6: // boat
                    Tri(P(-.95f, .2f), P(.95f, .2f), P(.55f, .8f), symbol); Tri(P(-.95f, .2f), P(.55f, .8f), P(-.55f, .8f), symbol);
                    L(-.45f, -.55f, -.45f, .15f, .16f); L(-.45f, -.55f, .45f, -.55f, .16f); L(.45f, -.55f, .45f, .15f, .16f); L(0, -.95f, 0, -.55f, .13f); break;
                case 7: // crew
                    C(0, -.48f, .31f); C(-.65f, -.28f, .25f); C(.65f, -.28f, .25f);
                    C(0, .48f, .51f); C(-.65f, .55f, .33f); C(.65f, .55f, .33f); break;
                case 8: // map
                    Circle(P(0, -.3f), r * .58f, symbol); Tri(P(-.46f, 0), P(.46f, 0), P(0, .87f), symbol); Circle(P(0, -.3f), r * .22f, Palette.Hex("AEE5EE")); break;
                case 9: // gear
                    C(0, 0, .65f);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) { float a = i * Mathf.PI / 4; var v = new Vector2(Mathf.Cos(a), Mathf.Sin(a)); Line(c + v * r * .55f, c + v * r, r * .3f, symbol); }
                    Circle(c, r * .25f, Palette.Hex("FFF9E9")); break;
                default: // port office
                    L(-.75f, .85f, -.75f, -.2f, .2f); L(.75f, .85f, .75f, -.2f, .2f); L(-.8f, -.2f, .8f, -.2f, .2f);
                    L(-.3f, -.3f, -.3f, -.75f, .2f); L(.3f, -.3f, .3f, -.75f, .2f); L(-.4f, -.75f, .4f, -.75f, .2f);
                    for (int i = -1; i <= 1; i++) { C(i * .45f, .15f, .1f); C(i * .45f, .55f, .1f); } break;
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        CoastalCanvas coastalUi;
        int interfaceWidth, interfaceHeight;
        float interfaceTextScale;
        float iw, ih;
        bool compactInterface => ih < 600;
        Color SeaInk => Palette.Hex("3E626D");
        public void PrepareInterface(int width, int height, float textScale = 0)
        {
            interfaceWidth = width; interfaceHeight = height;
            interfaceTextScale = textScale;
            RefreshInterface();
        }
        public bool InvokeInterface(string control) => coastalUi != null && coastalUi.Invoke(control);
        void RefreshInterface()
        {
            if (coastalUi == null) coastalUi = CoastalCanvas.Create(transform, Cam);
            if (MenuOpen || confirmHeadHome || SimTime < refitUntil) { coastalUi.Hide(); return; }
            int width = interfaceWidth > 0 ? interfaceWidth : Screen.width;
            int height = interfaceHeight > 0 ? interfaceHeight : Screen.height;
            coastalUi.Begin(width, height, interfaceTextScale > 0 ? interfaceTextScale : UiScales[UiScaleIndex], interfaceWidth > 0 ? new Rect(0, 0, width, height) : Screen.safeArea);
            iw = coastalUi.Width; ih = coastalUi.Height;
            hudRects.Clear();
            if (departureReady && CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour) InterfaceDeparture();
            else if (CurrentMode == Mode.Harbour) InterfaceHarbour();
            else if (CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing) InterfaceFishing();
            else InterfaceTravel();
            if (!departureReady && toast != null && SimTime < toastUntil)
            {
                var r = new Rect((iw - 430) / 2, CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing ? 76 : 18, 430, 64);
                coastalUi.Box("toast", r, "toast-dark");
                coastalUi.Label("toast/text", new Rect(r.x + 14, r.y + 4, r.width - 28, 54), toast, 16, Palette.Cream, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            }
            coastalUi.End();
        }
        void Block(Rect rect) => hudRects.Add(rect);
        void IButton(string id, Rect r, string label, Action action, bool primary = true, bool enabled = true)
        {
            if (compactInterface) r.height = Mathf.Max(r.height, 54);
            Block(r);
            coastalUi.Button(id, r, label, () => { action(); audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Select, .45f); },
                primary ? "button-primary-normal" : "button-secondary-normal", enabled);
        }
        void InterfaceCoins()
        {
            var c = new Rect(iw - 230, 18, 150, 46);
            coastalUi.Box("wallet", c, "chip-dark"); Block(c);
            coastalUi.Art("wallet/icon", new Rect(c.x + 12, c.y + 10, 24, 24), "icon-coin");
            coastalUi.Label("wallet/coins", new Rect(c.x + 44, c.y, 92, 42), State.Coins.ToString("N0"), 21, Palette.Cream);
            IButton("pause", new Rect(iw - 70, 18, 54, 46), "II", () => paused = true, false);
        }
        void InterfaceFishing()
        {
            coastalUi.ReferenceScale = ih / FishReferenceStyle.Height;
            InterfaceReferenceGoals();
            InterfaceReferenceCrew();
            InterfaceReferenceSupplies();
            float homeY = ReferenceCrewBottom + 12 * coastalUi.ReferenceScale;
            IButton("home", new Rect(iw - 208, homeY, 192, 48), "Head home", RequestHeadHome, false);
            foreach (var rod in Rods.Where(r => r.Unlocked && CanCommandRod(r)))
                InterfaceBubble("rod/" + Rods.IndexOf(rod), Boat.TransformPoint(rod.TipLocal + Vector3.up * .25f),
                    rod.State == RodState.Bite ? "Bite!" : rod.InWater ? "Watch" : "Fish", "icon-rod", () => CommandRod(rod), rod.State == RodState.Bite);
            for (int i = 0; i < OnDeck.Count; i++)
            {
                var fish = OnDeck[i];
                if (!fish.FromNetBasket && CanCarry(fish)) InterfaceBubble("carry/" + i, fish.Root.position + Vector3.up * .35f, "Carry", "icon-fish", () => CommandCarry(fish));
            }
            InterfaceFishingAction();
            InterfaceCatchRewards();
            // Windows owns net/orders implementation; draw last so its modal stays on top.
            InterfaceNetOrders();
        }
        void InterfaceBubble(string id, Vector3 worldPosition, string caption, string icon, Action action, bool hot = false)
        {
            Vector3 view = Cam.WorldToViewportPoint(worldPosition);
            if (view.z <= 0) return;
            float x = view.x * iw, y = (1 - view.y) * ih;
            var r = new Rect(Mathf.Clamp(x - 56, 14, iw - 126), Mathf.Clamp(y - 52, 18, ih - 58), 112, 44);
            Block(r);
            coastalUi.Art(id + "/tail", new Rect(r.center.x - 10, r.yMax - 4, 20, 16), hot ? "bubble-hot-tail" : "bubble-white-tail");
            coastalUi.Button(id, r, "", action, hot ? "bubble-hot" : "bubble-white");
            coastalUi.Art(id + "/icon", new Rect(r.x + 11, r.y + 9, 24, 24), icon);
            coastalUi.Label(id + "/label", new Rect(r.x + 39, r.y, 64, 39), caption, 18);
        }
        void InterfaceFishingAction()
        {
            float width = Mathf.Min(390, iw - 530);
            if (width < 270) width = 270;
            float x = (iw - width) / 2;
            var rod = MinigameRod;
            if (AwaitingCast)
            {
                var r = new Rect(x, ih - 122, width, 104);
                coastalUi.Box("cast/choice", r); Block(r);
                coastalUi.Label("cast/title", new Rect(x + 14, r.y + 7, width - 28, 28), "Ready for another?", 21, null, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
                IButton("cast/again", new Rect(x + 12, r.y + 43, width * .56f - 16, 48), HoldFull ? "Cooler full" : "Throw again", () => ThrowAgain(), true, !HoldFull);
                IButton("cast/leave", new Rect(x + width * .56f + 4, r.y + 43, width * .44f - 16, 48), "Leave rod", LeaveFishing, false);
                return;
            }
            if (rod == null)
            {
                string hint = ShowTips ? VoyageHint() : null;
                if (hint != null)
                {
                    var r = new Rect(x, ih - 76, width, 58); coastalUi.Box("guide", r, "toast-dark");
                    coastalUi.Label("guide/text", new Rect(x + 14, r.y + 6, width - 28, 44), hint, 16, Palette.Cream, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
                }
                return;
            }
            float height = compactInterface ? 146 : 172;
            var box = new Rect(x, ih - height - 18, width, height);
            coastalUi.Box("strike", box); Block(box);
            coastalUi.Art("strike/sea", new Rect(x + 8, box.y + 43, width - 16, height - 52), "strike-water");
            string title = rod.State == RodState.Bite ? "The bobber is under!" : rod.State == RodState.Waiting ? rod.Nibbling ? "A nibble… wait for it" : "Watch the bobber" : rod.State == RodState.Reeling ? "You've hooked one!" : "Casting your line…";
            coastalUi.Label("strike/title", new Rect(x + 12, box.y + 6, width - 24, 32), title, 21, null, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            float cy = box.y + height * .60f;
            coastalUi.Art("strike/fish", new Rect(x + width * .25f + Mathf.Sin(SimTime) * 18, cy, 76, 32), "fish-shadow");
            coastalUi.Art("strike/bobber", new Rect(x + width / 2 - 25, cy - 24, 50, 50), rod.State == RodState.Bite ? "bobber-underwater" : rod.Nibbling ? "bobber-dipped" : "bobber-floating");
            if (rod.State == RodState.Bite)
            {
                IButton("strike/tap", new Rect(x + width - 104, cy - 24, 88, 48), "Strike!", StrikeTap);
                coastalUi.Bar("strike/time", new Rect(x + 22, box.yMax - 23, width - 44, 9), 1 - rod.BiteProgress, true);
            }
            else if (rod.State == RodState.Reeling) coastalUi.Bar("strike/progress", new Rect(x + 22, box.yMax - 23, width - 44, 9), rod.ReelProgress);
            else coastalUi.Label("strike/help", new Rect(x + 12, box.yMax - 30, width - 24, 24), "Wait for the dip · then tap or Space", 14, Palette.Cream, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
        }
        void InterfaceTravel()
        {
            InterfaceCoins();
            var r = new Rect((iw - 440) / 2, 22, 440, 110);
            coastalUi.Box("journey", r); Block(r);
            coastalUi.Art("journey/icon", new Rect(r.x + 18, r.y + 18, 52, 52), "icon-home-anchor");
            coastalUi.Label("journey/title", new Rect(r.x + 86, r.y + 10, 334, 34), CurrentMode == Mode.SailingHome ? "Home to Gull Harbour" : GameData.Spots[ActiveSpot].Name, 24);
            coastalUi.Label("journey/stage", new Rect(r.x + 86, r.y + 47, 334, 25), TravelStage, 16, SeaInk);
            coastalUi.Bar("journey/progress", new Rect(r.x + 20, r.y + 84, 400, 10), TravelProgress);
        }
    }
}
