using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        Rect ReferenceRect(float x, float y, float width, float height)
        {
            float scale = ih / FishReferenceStyle.Height;
            return new Rect(x * scale, y * scale, width * scale, height * scale);
        }
        Rect ReferenceRightRect(float x, float y, float width, float height)
        {
            var r = ReferenceRect(x, y, width, height);
            r.x += iw - FishReferenceStyle.Width * ih / FishReferenceStyle.Height;
            return r;
        }
        static float ReferenceRowTop(int i) => i switch { 0 => 130, 1 => 215, 2 => 301, 3 => 387, 4 => 475, _ => 562 + (i - 5) * 86 };
        float ReferenceCrewBottom => (ReferenceRowTop(Mathf.Max(1, People.Count()) - 1) + 91) * ih / FishReferenceStyle.Height;
        void InterfaceReferenceCrew()
        {
            var people = People.ToList();
            var toolbar = ReferenceRightRect(1407, 25, 248, 49);
            coastalUi.ReferencePanel("reference/crew/toolbar", toolbar, FishReferenceStyle.PanelKind.Toolbar);
            Block(toolbar);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/crew/badge", ReferenceRightRect(1418, 31, 40, 38), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Badge);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/crew/group", ReferenceRightRect(1470, 39, 28, 26), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Crew);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/crew/title", ReferenceRightRect(1501, 38, 58, 28), "Crew", 18, true);
            // Reference includes captain in its total. Gameplay CrewCap excludes captain.
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/crew/count", ReferenceRightRect(1560, 38, 43, 27),
                $"{people.Count}/{State.HullDef.CrewCap + 1}", 18, true, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/crew/menu/icon", ReferenceRightRect(1620, 39, 22, 24), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Menu);
            var menu = ReferenceRightRect(1609, 25, 46, 49);

            var panel = ReferenceRightRect(1365, 85, 290, ReferenceRowTop(Mathf.Max(1, people.Count) - 1) + 91 - 85);
            coastalUi.ReferencePanel("reference/crew/panel", panel, FishReferenceStyle.PanelKind.Crew); Block(panel);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/crew/dot", ReferenceRightRect(1377, 100, 11, 12), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Bullet);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/crew/heading", ReferenceRightRect(1395, 96, 245, 23), "Crew Roles", 20, true);
            for (int i = 0; i < people.Count; i++)
            {
                var person = people[i];
                float y = ReferenceRowTop(i);
                bool captain = person == Captain;
                bool fisher = !captain && person.Contract.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman;
                string role = captain ? "Captain" : fisher ? "Fisher" : "Deckhand";
                string portrait = captain ? "captain" : fisher ? "fisherman" : "deckhand";
                var row = ReferenceRightRect(1377, y, 268, i == 0 ? 77 : 78);
                coastalUi.ReferencePanel($"reference/crew/{i}/row", row, FishReferenceStyle.PanelKind.Row);
                coastalUi.ReferencePortrait($"reference/crew/{i}/portrait", ReferenceRightRect(1387, y + 8, 57, 60), portrait);
                coastalUi.ReferenceLabel($"reference/crew/{i}/role", ReferenceRightRect(1454, y + (role == "Deckhand" ? 8 : 7), 145, 23), role, 16, true);
                string grade = captain ? "You" : person.Name;
                coastalUi.ReferenceLabel($"reference/crew/{i}/grade", ReferenceRightRect(1599, y + 6, 37, 23), grade, 13, false, TextAnchor.MiddleRight);
                coastalUi.ReferenceIcon($"reference/crew/{i}/icon", ReferenceRightRect(captain ? 1608 : 1606, y + (captain ? 26 : fisher ? 26 : 25), captain ? 28 : fisher ? 30 : 31, captain ? 27 : fisher ? 27 : 29),
                    captain ? FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Wheel : fisher ? FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Fish : FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Net);

                // These are existing resources, NOT invented experience/stamina statistics.
                // Caption explicitly names the quantity represented by the reference-style bar.
                float remaining = captain ? State.Hull > 0 ? State.FuelFraction : 1f
                    : person.Contract.VoyagesLeft / (float)Mathf.Max(1, GameData.ContractVoyages(person.Contract.Grade));
                var rod = Rods.FirstOrDefault(r => r.Fisher == person);
                string activity = captain && Manning != null ? AwaitingCast ? "Ready to cast" : Manning.StateText : rod?.StateText ?? person.State;
                string detail = captain ? State.Hull > 0 ? $"Fuel {State.FuelFraction:P0} · {activity}" : activity : $"{person.Contract.VoyagesLeft} trips · {activity}";
                coastalUi.ReferenceLabel($"reference/crew/{i}/detail", ReferenceRightRect(1455, y + 35, 142, 18), detail, 12);
                coastalUi.ReferenceBar($"reference/crew/{i}/progress", ReferenceRightRect(1455, y + 55, 143, 14), remaining);
                coastalUi.ReferenceLabel($"reference/crew/{i}/percent", ReferenceRightRect(1605, y + 54, 32, 20),
                    $"{Mathf.FloorToInt(Mathf.Clamp01(remaining) * 100)}%", 13, false, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            }
            // Hit area is last so the neighbouring noninteractive header cannot swallow touch.
            Block(coastalUi.ReferenceButton("pause", menu, () => paused = true));
        }
        void InterfaceReferenceSupplies()
        {
            // Retain vital existing haul/bait/fuel information below the requested goal panel.
            var r = ReferenceRect(20, 252, 260, 152);
            coastalUi.ReferencePanel("instruments", r, FishReferenceStyle.PanelKind.Goal); Block(r);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("instruments/place", ReferenceRect(32, 260, 236, 26), GameData.Spots[ActiveSpot].Name, 17, true);
            var secured = State.QuoteSale(Cooler, SimTime);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("hold/amount", ReferenceRect(32, 292, 235, 22), $"Hold {HoldKg:0.00}/{State.CoolerCapacity:0.#} kg", 15, true);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("hold/value", ReferenceRect(150, 292, 117, 22), $"~{secured.Coins + secured.ContractCoins} coins", 14, false, TextAnchor.MiddleRight);
            coastalUi.ReferenceBar("hold/bar", ReferenceRect(32, 321, 235, 10), HoldKg / Mathf.Max(.01f, State.CoolerCapacity));
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("fuel/text", ReferenceRect(32, 339, 236, 22), State.Hull > 0 ? $"Fuel · {State.FuelFraction:P0} · return at10%" : "Oars ready · no fuel needed", 14);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("bait/text", ReferenceRect(32, 367, 236, 22), State.Chum > 0 ? $"Chum {State.Chum} / premium hooks" : State.WillUseSquid ? $"Bait · {State.Squid} squid" : State.Bait > 0 ? $"Bait · {State.Bait} worms" : "Bait · unlimited scraps", 14);
            var pending = UnstowedHaul();
            var haul = ReferenceRect(20, 416, 260, pending.Count > 0 ? 100 : 72);
            coastalUi.ReferencePanel("hold/freshness", haul, FishReferenceStyle.PanelKind.Goal); Block(haul);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("hold/freshness/title", ReferenceRect(32, 423, 236, 24), pending.Count > 0 ? $"{pending.Count} fish to stow" : "Haul secured", 16, true);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("hold/freshness/time", ReferenceRect(32, 451, 236, 30), FreshnessStatus(CurrentHaul(), SimTime), 13);
            if (pending.Count > 0)
                coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("hold/pending", ReferenceRect(32, 482, 236, 24), "On deck / carried · needs space", 12);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageInterfaceGoals.cs
========================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        // Rectangles are measured in the 1672 x 941 reference, then mapped by
        // the shared left-anchored transform. Style and typography stay shared.
        void InterfaceReferenceGoals()
        {
            var status = ReferenceRect(20, 25, 320, 65);
            coastalUi.ReferencePanel("reference/status", status, FishReferenceStyle.PanelKind.Status);
            Block(status);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/status/sun", ReferenceRect(31, 36, 44, 44), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Sun);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/status/day", ReferenceRect(91, 35, 80, 23), $"Day {State.Voyage}", 18);

            // This is elapsed voyage time, not an invented time of day. SetSail
            // resets voyageStart; simulation pause also pauses this display.
            int seconds = Mathf.FloorToInt(Mathf.Max(0, SimTime - voyageStart));
            // Keep extended sessions inside the same clock slot: minutes below
            // 100 minutes, hours/minutes below 100 hours, then days/hours.
            string elapsed = seconds < 6000 ? $"{seconds / 60:00}:{seconds % 60:00}"
                : seconds < 360000 ? $"{seconds / 3600}h{seconds % 3600 / 60:00}"
                : $"{seconds / 86400}d{seconds % 86400 / 3600:00}";
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/status/time", ReferenceRect(91, 58, 80, 24), elapsed, 20, true);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/status/coin", ReferenceRect(194, 37, 38, 41), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Coin);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/status/coins", ReferenceRect(238, 40, 95, 38), ReferenceCoinBalance(), 30, true);

            string objective;
            string count;
            float progress;
            if (State.Active.Count > 0)
            {
                // Contracts advance when fish are sold. Fish still in the hold
                // are not delivered and cannot claim this goal as complete.
                var contract = State.Active[0];
                int target = Mathf.Max(1, contract.Count);
                objective = $"Sell {contract.Title}";
                count = $"{contract.Delivered}/{contract.Count}";
                progress = contract.Delivered / (float)target;
            }
            else if (State.Hull == 0 && State.BestVoyageFish < GameData.SkiffFishGoal)
            {
                // BestVoyageFish is the actual largest sold haul, not catches
                // lying on deck or a projected record before returning home.
                objective = $"Sell {GameData.SkiffFishGoal} in one haul";
                count = $"{State.BestVoyageFish}/{GameData.SkiffFishGoal}";
                progress = State.BestVoyageFish / (float)Mathf.Max(1, GameData.SkiffFishGoal);
            }
            else if (State.Hull == 0)
            {
                progress = State.Coins / (float)Mathf.Max(1, GameData.SkiffCost);
                objective = State.CanRefit ? "Skiff refit ready" : $"Save {GameData.SkiffCost:N0} coins";
                // A percentage fits the reference counter without squeezing a
                // four-digit coin fraction into the fish-sized counter slot.
                count = $"{Mathf.FloorToInt(Mathf.Clamp01(progress) * 100)}%";
            }
            else
            {
                progress = HoldKg / Mathf.Max(.01f, State.CoolerCapacity);
                objective = $"Hold {HoldKg:0.0}/{State.CoolerCapacity:0.#} kg";
                count = $"{Mathf.Clamp01(progress) * 100:0}%";
            }

            var goal = ReferenceRect(20, 130, 220, 104);
            coastalUi.ReferencePanel("reference/goal", goal, FishReferenceStyle.PanelKind.Goal);
            Block(goal);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/goal/target", ReferenceRect(31, 137, 24, 25), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Target);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/goal/title", ReferenceRect(64, 138, 166, 23), "Current Goal", 16, true);
            coastalUi.ReferenceIcon("reference/goal/crate", ReferenceRect(30, 178, 39, 43), FishReferenceStyle.Icon.Crate);
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/goal/objective", ReferenceRect(75, 172, 155, 20), objective, 13);
            coastalUi.ReferenceBar("reference/goal/progress", ReferenceRect(75, 200, 114, 13), Mathf.Clamp01(progress));
            coastalUi.ReferenceLabel("reference/goal/count", ReferenceRect(197, 198, 34, 20), count, 13, true, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
        }

        string ReferenceCoinBalance()
        {
            // Preserve the reference's full balance at ordinary values. Large
            // balances use conventional units rather than overflowing the card.
            // Decimal also handles int.MinValue without an Abs(int) overflow.
            decimal value = State.Coins;
            decimal magnitude = System.Math.Abs(value);
            if (magnitude >= 1000000000m) return (value / 1000000000m).ToString("0.##") + "B";
            if (magnitude >= 100000000m) return (value / 1000000m).ToString("0") + "M";
            if (magnitude >= 1000000m) return (value / 1000000m).ToString("0.#") + "M";
            if (magnitude >= 10000m) return (value / 1000m).ToString("0") + "k";
            return State.Coins.ToString("N0");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageInterfaceShop.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        float catalogWidth;
        Transform catalog;
        int catalogItem;
        float catalogY;
        void InterfaceHarbour()
        {
            if (panelOpen && hudTab == HudTab.HarbourMaster) { InterfaceNauticalChart(); return; }
            float left = Mathf.Clamp(iw * .24f, 220, 280);
            coastalUi.Box("port/title", new Rect(18, 18, left - 34, 90), "chip-dark"); Block(new Rect(18, 18, left - 34, 90));
            coastalUi.Label("port/name", new Rect(34, 26, left - 66, 34), "Gull Harbour", 25, Palette.Cream);
            coastalUi.Label("port/voyage", new Rect(34, 65, left - 66, 24), $"Voyage {State.Voyage} · {State.HullDef.Name}", 15, Palette.Hex("A9D7D4"));
            IButton("port/pause", new Rect(18, 120, 54, 48), "II", () => paused = true, false);
            float footerY = ih - 156;
            coastalUi.Box("port/departure", new Rect(18, footerY, left - 34, 138)); Block(new Rect(18, footerY, left - 34, 138));
            coastalUi.Label("port/destination", new Rect(32, footerY + 7, left - 62, 32), GameData.Spots[SelectedSpot].Name, 19);
            coastalUi.Label("port/readiness", new Rect(32, footerY + 39, left - 62, 24), SailBlocker() ?? "Boat ready · supplies aboard", 14, SeaInk);
            IButton("port/sail", new Rect(30, footerY + 71, left - 58, 54), "Set sail", RequestDeparture, true, State.UnsoldCatch.Count == 0);
            if (!panelOpen)
            {
                InterfaceCoins();
                IButton("port/open", new Rect(iw - 238, 86, 220, 52), "Visit harbour shops", () => panelOpen = true);
                foreach (var (anchor, tab) in Shops)
                {
                    HudTab chosen = tab;
                    InterfaceBubble("building/" + tab, anchor.position, ShortTab(tab), ShopIcon(tab), () => OpenShop(chosen));
                }
                return;
            }
            var shell = new Rect(left, 18, iw - left - 18, ih - 36);
            coastalUi.Box("shop/shell", shell); Block(shell);
            float nav = compactInterface ? 126 : 142;
            coastalUi.Label("shop/brand", new Rect(left + 18, 32, nav - 24, 36), "Shops", 25);
            var tabs = (HudTab[])Enum.GetValues(typeof(HudTab));
            for (int i = 0; i < tabs.Length; i++)
            {
                HudTab tab = tabs[i]; float y = 91 + i * (compactInterface ? 59 : 72);
                coastalUi.Button("shop/tab/" + tab, new Rect(left + 12, y, nav - 16, compactInterface ? 56 : 64), "", () => OpenShop(tab), hudTab == tab ? "card-selected" : "button-tab-off-normal");
                coastalUi.Art("shop/tab/" + tab + "/icon", new Rect(left + 20, y + 12, 26, 28), ShopIcon(tab));
                coastalUi.Label("shop/tab/" + tab + "/name", new Rect(left + 51, y + 4, nav - 60, compactInterface ? 43 : 54), ShortTab(tab), 16, Palette.HullNavy);
            }
            float contentX = left + nav + 18, contentW = iw - contentX - 38;
            coastalUi.Art("shop/header", new Rect(contentX, 31, contentW - 176, compactInterface ? 64 : 88), ShopHeader(hudTab));
            coastalUi.Art("shop/coin", new Rect(iw - 186, 42, 26, 26), "icon-coin");
            coastalUi.Label("shop/balance", new Rect(iw - 151, 35, 73, 40), State.Coins.ToString("N0"), 23);
            IButton("shop/close", new Rect(iw - 74, 32, 38, 44), "X", () => panelOpen = false, false);
            float headerBottom = compactInterface ? 112 : 142;
            string caption = hudTab switch
            {
                HudTab.Market => State.UnsoldCatch.Count > 0 ? "Your catch, ready for the morning market." : "Fresh fish, fair prices. Every catch counts.",
                HudTab.Chandlery => "Good gear. Longer trips. A boat that grows with you.",
                HudTab.Supplies => "Stock the boat before you cast off.",
                HudTab.CrewOffice => "A helping hand for your next few voyages.",
                _ => "Find your next fishing ground and take a harbour contract."
            };
            coastalUi.Label("shop/intro", new Rect(contentX + 2, headerBottom - 14, contentW - 4, 35), caption, 17, SeaInk);
            float top = headerBottom + 34, height = ih - top - 36;
            int count = hudTab switch { HudTab.Supplies => 4 + GameData.Upgrades.Count(u => u.Shop == "Supplies") + (State.Hull > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (IsNetFisher ? 2 : 0), HudTab.Chandlery => 7, HudTab.CrewOffice => State.CrewCount + State.Board.Count + 1, _ => 14 };
            float estimatedHeight = hudTab == HudTab.Market ? 340 + GameData.Fish.Length * 88 : hudTab == HudTab.HarbourMaster ? 740 + State.Active.Count * 80 + State.Offers.Count * 100 : Mathf.Ceil(count / 2f) * 264 + (hudTab == HudTab.Supplies ? 120 + (IsNetFisher ? 100 : 0) : 0);
            catalog = coastalUi.Scroll("catalog/" + hudTab, new Rect(contentX, top, contentW, height), estimatedHeight);
            catalogWidth = contentW - 12; catalogItem = 0; catalogY = 0;
            switch (hudTab)
            {
                case HudTab.Market: InterfaceMarket(); break;
                case HudTab.HarbourMaster: InterfaceChart(); break;
                case HudTab.Chandlery: InterfaceGear(); break;
                case HudTab.Supplies: InterfaceSupplies(); break;
                case HudTab.CrewOffice: InterfaceCrew(); break;
            }
            // Fit scrolling to actual content, rather than a catalogue's estimated row count.
            var contentRect = (RectTransform)catalog;
            contentRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(catalogWidth, Mathf.Max(height, catalogY + Mathf.Ceil(catalogItem / 2f) * 264));
        }
        void CatalogHeading(string id, string title, string detail, float height = 76)
        {
            coastalUi.Label(id + "/title", new Rect(4, catalogY, catalogWidth - 8, 34), title, 26, null, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
            coastalUi.Label(id + "/detail", new Rect(4, catalogY + 35, catalogWidth - 8, height - 36), detail, 16, SeaInk, TextAnchor.UpperLeft, catalog);
            catalogY += height;
        }
        void Product(string id, string name, string description, string icon, string action, bool enabled, Action onBuy, string reason = null)
        {
            float w = (catalogWidth - 16) / 2;
            float x = catalogItem % 2 * (w + 16), y = catalogY + catalogItem / 2 * 264;
            coastalUi.Box(id, new Rect(x, y, w, 248), "card-white", null, catalog);
            coastalUi.Art(id + "/art", new Rect(x + 16, y + 12, w - 32, 68), icon, catalog);
            coastalUi.Label(id + "/title", new Rect(x + 18, y + 83, w - 36, 30), name, 22, null, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
            coastalUi.Label(id + "/description", new Rect(x + 18, y + 115, w - 36, 64), reason ?? description, 15, SeaInk, TextAnchor.UpperLeft, catalog);
            coastalUi.Button(id + "/buy", new Rect(x + 14, y + 184, w - 28, 52), action, () => { onBuy(); audio?.Play(VoyageAudio.Cue.Purchase, .6f); },
                "button-primary-normal", enabled, catalog, 17);
            catalogItem++;
        }
        void FinishProducts() { catalogY += Mathf.Ceil(catalogItem / 2f) * 264; catalogItem = 0; }
        void InterfaceGear()
        {
            foreach (var u in GameData.Upgrades.Where(u => u.Shop == "Chandlery")) InterfaceUpgrade(u);
            Product("refit", State.Hull > 0 ? State.HullDef.Name : "Skiff refit", State.Hull > 0 ? $"{State.CoolerCapacity:0.#} kg hold · three rods · outboard" : $"Bring home {GameData.SkiffFishGoal} fish in one trip. Best: {State.BestVoyageFish}.", "icon-skiff",
                State.Hull > 0 ? "Refitted" : $"Refit · {GameData.SkiffCost} coins", State.CanRefit, () => RefitSkiff(),
                State.Hull == 0 && State.BestVoyageFish >= GameData.SkiffFishGoal && State.Coins < GameData.SkiffCost ? CoinShortfall(GameData.SkiffCost) : null);
            if (State.Hull == 1) Product("refit/compact", "Compact fisher", "Tier3 ·100kg hold · three rods · stern net system. Your crew and owned upgrades transfer.", "icon-skiff",
                $"Refit · {GameState.CompactFisherCost:N0} coins", State.CanRefitCompact, () => RefitCompact(), State.CanRefitCompact ? null : CoinShortfall(GameState.CompactFisherCost));
            if (SessionOnly) Product("trial/netboat", "Compact fisher · trial", "Three crew · three rods · stern net. Trial includes fuel and a200coin crew budget. Prices are still being tuned.", "icon-skiff",
                State.Hull == 2 ? "Trial boat aboard" : "Try net boat", State.Hull != 2, () => TrialCompactFisher());
            if (SessionOnly) Product("trial/prepboat", "Prep fisher / Tier 4 trial", "Four crew berths, three rods and a stern net. Two-person prep table and front storage. Processing orders coming next.", "icon-skiff",
                IsPrepFisher ? "Trial boat aboard" : "Try prep boat", !IsPrepFisher, () => TrialPrepFisher());
        }
        void InterfaceUpgrade(UpgradeDef u)
        {
            if (!State.UpgradeAvailable(u.Id)) return;
            int cost = State.UpgradeCost(u.Id);
            bool owned = State.Has(u.Id), prerequisite = !u.Requires.HasValue || State.Has(u.Requires.Value);
            string reason = !owned && !prerequisite ? "Needs " + GameData.Upgrade(u.Requires.Value).Name : !owned && State.Coins < cost ? CoinShortfall(cost) : null;
            string icon = u.Id switch { UpgradeId.NetSystem => "icon-net", UpgradeId.LunchTin => "icon-fuel", UpgradeId.RodFibreglass => "icon-rod-fibreglass", UpgradeId.RodCarbon => "icon-rod-carbon", _ => "icon-" + u.Id.ToString().ToLowerInvariant() };
            Product("upgrade/" + u.Id, u.Name, State.UpgradeDescription(u.Id), icon, owned ? "Owned" : $"Buy · {cost:N0} coins", !owned && prerequisite && State.Coins >= cost,
                () => { if (State.TryBuy(u.Id)) { SyncUpgrades(); Toast("Bought: " + u.Name); SaveGame(); } }, reason);
        }
        void InterfaceSupplies()
        {
            if (IsNetFisher)
            {
                coastalUi.Label("supplies/net-loadout", new Rect(4, catalogY, catalogWidth - 8, 34), State.NetLoadoutLabel, 18, SeaInk, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
                coastalUi.Button("supplies/loadout/shallow", new Rect(0, catalogY + 38, catalogWidth * .48f, 48), "Shallow · 20s", () => SelectNetLoadout(NetKind.Shallow), State.SelectedNet == NetKind.Shallow ? "button-primary-normal" : "button-secondary-normal", true, catalog);
                coastalUi.Button("supplies/loadout/deep", new Rect(catalogWidth * .51f, catalogY + 38, catalogWidth * .48f, 48), "Deep · 30s · 3 uses", () => SelectNetLoadout(NetKind.Deep), State.SelectedNet == NetKind.Deep ? "button-primary-normal" : "button-secondary-normal", State.DeepNetUses.Count > 0, catalog);
                catalogY += 100;
            }
            Product("supplies/worms", "Worms", $"{GameData.BaitPackSize} everyday hooks · {State.Bait} aboard", "icon-bait-worm", $"Buy · {GameData.BaitPackCost} coins", State.Coins >= GameData.BaitPackCost, () => { if (State.BuyBait()) SaveGame(); });
            Product("supplies/squid", "Squid bait", $"{GameData.SquidPackSize} hooks for larger fish · {State.Squid} aboard", "icon-squid", $"Buy · {GameData.SquidPackCost} coins", State.Coins >= GameData.SquidPackCost, () => { if (State.BuySquid()) SaveGame(); });
            Product("supplies/worms-bulk", "Worms · bulk box", $"{GameData.BaitBulkSize} hooks · better value for longer trips", "icon-bait-worm", $"Buy · {GameData.BaitBulkCost} coins", State.Coins >= GameData.BaitBulkCost, () => { if (State.BuyBait(true)) SaveGame(); });
            Product("supplies/squid-bulk", "Squid · bulk box", $"{GameData.SquidBulkSize} hooks · favour bigger specimens", "icon-squid", $"Buy · {GameData.SquidBulkCost} coins", State.Coins >= GameData.SquidBulkCost, () => { if (State.BuySquid(true)) SaveGame(); });
            int cost = State.FuelFillCost;
            foreach (var upgrade in GameData.Upgrades.Where(u => u.Shop == "Supplies")) InterfaceUpgrade(upgrade);
            if (State.Hull > 0) Product("supplies/fuel", "Outboard fuel", $"{State.FuelFraction:P0} aboard · {State.FuelCapacity:0} minute tank · return at10%", "icon-fuel", cost > 0 ? $"Fill · {cost} coins" : "Tank full", cost > 0 && State.Coins >= cost, () => { if (State.BuyFuel()) SaveGame(); });
            if (IsNetFisher)
            {
                bool rackFull = State.NetInventory >= GameData.NetInventoryCapacity;
                string stock = $"{State.NetInventory}/{GameData.NetInventoryCapacity} usable nets aboard.";
                string reason = rackFull ? stock + " Net rack full." : State.Coins < GameData.NetReplacementCost ? stock + " " + CoinShortfall(GameData.NetReplacementCost) + "." : null;
                Product("supplies/net", "Replacement net", $"{State.NetCapacityKg:0}kg capacity. " + stock + " Successful cycles reuse a net. A tear uses one.", "icon-net",
                    rackFull ? "Net rack full" : $"Buy net · {GameData.NetReplacementCost} coins", !rackFull && State.Coins >= GameData.NetReplacementCost,
                    () => { if (State.BuyNet()) { Toast("Replacement net aboard."); SaveGame(); } }, reason);
            }
            if (IsNetFisher)
            {
                Product("supplies/deep-net", "Deep net · 3 uses", $"{State.DeepNetUses.Count}/{GameData.DeepNetRackCapacity} aboard. {State.NetCapacityKg:0}kg capacity. Fewer, larger local fish. Failure spends one use.", "icon-net",
                    $"Buy · {GameData.DeepNetCost} coins", State.Coins >= GameData.DeepNetCost && State.DeepNetUses.Count < GameData.DeepNetRackCapacity,
                    () => { if (State.BuyDeepNet()) { Toast("Deep net aboard — choose it below before sailing."); SaveGame(); } });
            }
            FinishProducts();
            coastalUi.Label("supplies/free", new Rect(4, catalogY + 4, catalogWidth - 8, 50), "Free scraps are always aboard. No purchase needed.", 17, SeaInk, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
            coastalUi.Button("supplies/preference", new Rect(0, catalogY + 58, catalogWidth, 46), State.UseSquid ? "Squid first · On" : "Squid first · Off", () => { State.UseSquid = !State.UseSquid; SaveGame(); }, "button-secondary-normal", true, catalog);
            catalogY += 120;
        }
        void InterfaceCrew()
        {
            CatalogHeading("crew/heading", $"Your crew · {State.CrewCount}/{State.HullDef.CrewCap}", "Deckhands bait and carry. Fishermen work their own rods. Choose the mix; both roles stay available.");
            foreach (var c in State.Crew)
                Product("employee/" + c.Name, c.Name, $"{c.RoleName} · {c.Grade}\n{c.VoyagesLeft} voyages remaining" + (IsNetFisher && c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand ? " · " + c.WorkPreference + " duty" : ""), c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman ? "portrait-fisherman" : "portrait-deckhand",
                    IsNetFisher && c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand ? "Duty: " + c.WorkPreference + " · change" : "Aboard", IsNetFisher && c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand,
                    () => SetDeckhandWork(c, c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Fish ? DeckhandWork.Nets : c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Nets && IsPrepFisher ? DeckhandWork.Prep : DeckhandWork.Fish));
            FinishProducts();
            CatalogHeading("crew/board", "Looking for work", "Temporary contracts · both roles stay available after hiring.");
            for (int i = 0; i < State.Board.Count; i++)
            {
                var c = State.Board[i];
                bool room = State.CrewCount < State.HullDef.CrewCap;
                Product("hire/" + i, c.Name, $"{c.RoleName} · {c.Grade}\n{GameData.Speed(c.Grade):0.0}× speed · {c.VoyagesLeft} voyages", c.Grade == Grade.Veteran ? "portrait-veteran" : c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman ? "portrait-fisherman" : "portrait-deckhand",
                    $"Hire · {c.Fee} coins", room && State.Coins >= c.Fee, () => { if (State.Hire(c)) { SyncCrew(); SaveGame(); } }, !room ? "All crew berths occupied" : State.Coins < c.Fee ? CoinShortfall(c.Fee) : null);
            }
        }
        void InterfaceMarket()
        {
            State.UnseenSale = false;
            if (State.UnsoldCatch.Count > 0)
            {
                var quote = State.QuoteSale(State.UnsoldCatch, 0);
                CatalogHeading("market/haul", $"{State.UnsoldCatch.Sum(f => f.Kg):0.00} kg safely ashore", "Every fish earns coins. Matching catches also advance your contracts.", 85);
                coastalUi.Box("market/summary", new Rect(0, catalogY, catalogWidth, 90), "card-white", null, catalog);
                coastalUi.Art("market/crate", new Rect(12, catalogY + 9, 80, 70), "icon-fish", catalog);
                coastalUi.Label("market/value", new Rect(104, catalogY + 8, catalogWidth * .4f, 66), $"{quote.Coins + quote.ContractCoins} coins\nReady to collect", 22, null, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
                coastalUi.Button("market/sell", new Rect(catalogWidth - 180, catalogY + 20, 164, 48), "Sell haul", () => SellHaul(), "button-primary-normal", true, catalog);
                catalogY += 108;
            }
            else CatalogHeading("market/welcome", State.LastSale == null ? "Your first good day's fishing" : "Catch sold. Boat ready.", State.LastSale == null ? "Bring home three fish, sell them here, then choose your first improvement." : $"Last haul: {State.LastSale.FishSold} fish · {State.LastSale.Coins + State.LastSale.ContractCoins} coins earned.", 93);
            CatalogHeading("market/log", $"Fish log · {State.LogCount.Count(n => n > 0)}/{GameData.Fish.Length}", "Your records and today's market prices. Prices recover between voyages.", 82);
            for (int i = 0; i < GameData.Fish.Length; i++)
            {
                var f = GameData.Fish[i]; bool found = State.LogCount[(int)f.Species] > 0;
                coastalUi.Art("log/" + i + "/fish", new Rect(6, catalogY + 4, 114, 68), found ? FishPortrait(f.Species) : "fish-unknown", catalog);
                coastalUi.Label("log/" + i + "/name", new Rect(138, catalogY + 5, catalogWidth - 270, 29), found ? f.Name : "Undiscovered fish", 21, null, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
                coastalUi.Label("log/" + i + "/record", new Rect(138, catalogY + 36, catalogWidth - 270, 34), found ? $"{State.LogCount[(int)f.Species]} caught · best {State.LogBest[(int)f.Species]:0.00} kg" : "Explore the fishing grounds", 15, SeaInk, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, catalog);
                coastalUi.Label("log/" + i + "/price", new Rect(catalogWidth - 122, catalogY + 10, 116, 56), $"{State.PricePerKg(f.Species):0.0} base/kg\n+ size bonus", 17, SeaInk, TextAnchor.MiddleRight, catalog);
                catalogY += 84;
            }
        }
        void InterfaceChart() => InterfaceNauticalChart();
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageMenus.cs
========================================================================
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // Title screen, pause menu, settings, and the "something new here" badges on the shop bubbles.
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        bool showTitle = true, paused, showSettings, hadSave, confirmReset;
        int titleBackFrame = -1;

        // Settings live in PlayerPrefs (per device), not in the save.
        static readonly float[] UiScales = { 0.85f, 1f, 1.15f };
        static readonly string[] UiScaleNames = { "Small", "Normal", "Large" };
        int UiScaleIndex
        {
            get => Mathf.Clamp(PlayerPrefs.GetInt("catchline.uiScale", 1), 0, 2);
            set { PlayerPrefs.SetInt("catchline.uiScale", value); PlayerPrefs.Save(); }
        }
        bool ShowTips
        {
            get => PlayerPrefs.GetInt("catchline.tips", 1) == 1;
            set { PlayerPrefs.SetInt("catchline.tips", value ? 1 : 0); PlayerPrefs.Save(); }
        }

        public bool MenuOpen => showTitle || paused;

        void Quit()
        {
#if UNITY_EDITOR
            UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying = false;
#else
            Application.Quit();
#endif
        }

        void Dim(float alpha)
        {
            var prev = GUI.color;
            GUI.color = new Color(0.05f, 0.14f, 0.2f, alpha);
            GUI.DrawTexture(new Rect(0, 0, VW, VH), Texture2D.whiteTexture);
            GUI.color = prev;
        }

        bool MenuButton(Rect r, string label, GUIStyle style = null) => GUI.Button(r, label, style ?? Ui.Btn);

        // ---- title ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

        void DrawTitle()
        {
            // HandleInput owns Escape while paused; title settings need their own Back.
            // OnGUI runs several times per frame, so consume the press only once.
            var keyboard = UnityEngine.InputSystem.Keyboard.current;
            if (keyboard != null && keyboard.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame && titleBackFrame != Time.frameCount)
            {
                titleBackFrame = Time.frameCount;
                if (showSettings) showSettings = false;
                else confirmReset = false;
            }
            var backdrop = Ui.Tex("title-backdrop");
            if (backdrop != null)
            {
                float drift = Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * .075f) * 8f;
                GUI.DrawTexture(new Rect(-10f + drift, -6f, VW + 28f, VH + 16f), backdrop, ScaleMode.ScaleAndCrop);
            }
            if (showSettings) { Dim(.35f); DrawSettings(); return; }
            bool compact = VH < 600f;
            float x = compact ? 34f : 52f, w = Mathf.Min(compact ? 354f : 380f, VW - x - 16f);
            var logo = Ui.Tex("logo-catchline");
            var logoRect = new Rect(x - 6f, compact ? 12f : 42f, w + 12f, compact ? 90f : 136f);
            if (logo != null) GUI.DrawTexture(logoRect, logo, ScaleMode.ScaleToFit);
            else GUI.Label(logoRect, "Catchline", Ui.LogoStyle);
            var card = new Rect(x, compact ? 110f : 210f, w, 354f);
            GUI.Box(card, GUIContent.none, Ui.Panel);
            float inset = 26f, bx = card.x + inset, bw = w - inset * 2f;
            GUI.Label(new Rect(bx, card.y + 16f, bw, 38f), confirmReset ? "Start over?" : "Gull Harbour", Ui.TitleCenter);
            if (confirmReset)
            {
                GUI.Label(new Rect(bx, card.y + 64f, bw, 96f),
                    "Your coins, crew and catch will be erased. This cannot be undone. Settings stay unchanged.", Ui.MutedCenter);
                // Separate, explicit actions: opening this confirmation never deletes data.
                if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, card.y + 180f, bw, 52f), "Cancel · keep progress", Ui.BtnAlt))
                { confirmReset = false; return; }
                if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, card.y + 246f, bw, 52f), "Erase & start over", Ui.Tab))
                {
                    NewGame();
                    hadSave = false;
                    confirmReset = showSettings = paused = showTitle = false;
                    return;
                }
                return;
            }
            string welcome = SessionOnly || AutoPlay ? "Test session · progress is not saved."
                : CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour ? "Voyage paused. Quit loses fish aboard."
                : hadSave ? $"Voyage {State.Voyage} · ready in harbour." : "A little boat. A sea of possibilities.";
            GUI.Label(new Rect(bx, card.y + 56f, bw, 48f), welcome, Ui.MutedCenter);
            float y = card.y + 112f;
            string start = CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour ? "Resume voyage" : hadSave ? "Continue in harbour" : "Start in harbour";
            if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 52f), start))
            { confirmReset = showSettings = paused = showTitle = false; return; }
            y += 60f;
            if (hadSave)
            {
                if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 48f), "New game", Ui.BtnAlt))
                { confirmReset = true; return; }
                y += 56f;
            }
            if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 44f), "Settings", Ui.Tab))
            { confirmReset = false; showSettings = true; return; }
            y += 52f;
            if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 44f), CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour ? "Quit (lose trip)" : "Quit", Ui.Tab)) Quit();
        }
        // ---- pause ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

        void DrawPause()
        {
            Dim(0.5f);
            if (showSettings) { DrawSettings(); return; }
            float w = Mathf.Min(420f, VW - 32f), x = VW / 2f - w / 2f;
            var card = new Rect(x, (VH - 448f) / 2f, w, 448f);
            GUI.Box(card, GUIContent.none, Ui.Panel);
            GUI.Label(new Rect(card.x, card.y + 24, w, 40), "Paused", Ui.TitleCenter);
            float y = card.y + 80, bw = w - 56, bx = card.x + 28;
            if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 52), "Resume")) { paused = false; return; }
            y += 62;
            if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 48), "Settings", Ui.Tab)) { showSettings = true; return; }
            y += 58;
            if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 48), "Main menu", Ui.Tab))
            {
                // Keep the in-memory trip frozen behind the title, including freshness/food.
                paused = true;
                showTitle = true;
                hadSave = true;
                confirmReset = false;
                SaveGame();
                return;
            }
            y += 58;
            if (MenuButton(new Rect(bx, y, bw, 48), CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour ? "Quit (lose trip)" : "Quit", Ui.Tab))
            {
                SaveGame();
                Quit();
            }
            string saveNote = SessionOnly || AutoPlay ? "Test session · progress is not saved."
                : CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour ? "Quitting loses this trip and fish aboard. Return to harbour to save your haul."
                : "Returning to harbour saves your haul. Sell it there to collect your coins.";
            GUI.Label(new Rect(card.x + 28, card.y + 330, w - 56, 96), saveNote, Ui.MutedCenter);
        }

        // ---- settings ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

        void DrawSettings()
        {
            float w = Mathf.Min(460f, VW - 32f), x = VW / 2f - w / 2f;
            var card = new Rect(x, (VH - 448f) / 2f, w, 448f);
            GUI.Box(card, GUIContent.none, Ui.Panel);
            GUI.Label(new Rect(card.x, card.y + 22, w, 40), "Settings", Ui.TitleCenter);

            float y = card.y + 74, ix = card.x + 30, iw = w - 60;
            GUI.Label(new Rect(ix, y, iw, 32), "Text size", Ui.H2);
            y += 32;
            float bw = (iw - 16) / 3f;
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                if (GUI.Button(new Rect(ix + i * (bw + 8), y, bw, 44), UiScaleNames[i], UiScaleIndex == i ? Ui.TabOn : Ui.Tab))
                    UiScaleIndex = i;
            y += 56;
            GUI.Label(new Rect(ix, y, iw, 32), "Hints on the water", Ui.H2);
            y += 32;
            if (GUI.Button(new Rect(ix, y, iw, 44), ShowTips ? "Hints: On" : "Hints: Off", ShowTips ? Ui.TabOn : Ui.Tab)) ShowTips = !ShowTips;
            y += 56;
            GUI.Label(new Rect(ix, y, iw, 32), "Coastal sounds", Ui.H2);
            y += 34f;
            if (audio != null)
            {
                float volume = GUI.HorizontalSlider(new Rect(ix, y, iw - 68f, 38f), audio.Volume, 0, 1, Ui.Slider, Ui.SliderThumb);
                if (Mathf.Abs(volume - audio.Volume) > .005f) audio.Volume = volume;
                GUI.Label(new Rect(ix + iw - 62f, y, 62f, 28f), audio.Volume < .01f ? "Off" : $"{audio.Volume * 100:0}%", Ui.Small);
            }
            else GUI.Label(new Rect(ix, y, iw, 38f), "Sound unavailable in this session", Ui.Mutedish);
            if (GUI.Button(new Rect(ix, card.yMax - 66, iw, 48), showTitle ? "Back to main menu" : "Back to pause", Ui.BtnAlt)) showSettings = false;
        }

        // ---- badges -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        // True when a shop has something worth a visit: shown as a "!" on its bubble.
        bool ShopHasNews(HudTab tab)
        {
            var s = State;
            switch (tab)
            {
                case HudTab.Market: return s.UnsoldCatch.Count > 0 || s.UnseenSale;
                case HudTab.HarbourMaster:
                    return Enumerable.Range(0, GameData.Spots.Length).Any(i => !s.Known(i) && s.Coins >= GameData.Spots[i].ChartCost)
                           || s.Active.Count < GameData.MaxActiveContracts && s.Offers.Count > 0;
                case HudTab.CrewOffice: return s.CrewCount < s.HullDef.CrewCap && s.Board.Any(c => s.Coins >= c.Fee);
                case HudTab.Chandlery:
                    return s.CanRefit || s.CanRefitCompact || GameData.Upgrades.Any(u => u.Shop == "Chandlery" && s.UpgradeAvailable(u.Id) && !s.Has(u.Id) && s.Coins >= s.UpgradeCost(u.Id) && (!u.Requires.HasValue || s.Has(u.Requires.Value)));
                case HudTab.Supplies:
                    // Free scraps always permit fishing. Flag actionable purchases, not a
                    // permanent bait emergency when the player cannot afford supplies.
                    return s.Bait + s.Squid < 8 && (s.Coins >= GameData.BaitPackCost || s.Coins >= GameData.SquidPackCost)
                           || s.Hull > 0 && s.FuelFraction < .5f && s.FuelFillCost > 0 && s.Coins >= s.FuelFillCost
                           || s.Hull > 0 && s.FuelFraction <= .1f && s.Coins >= GameData.FuelPackCost
                           || GameData.Upgrades.Any(u => u.Shop == "Supplies" && s.UpgradeAvailable(u.Id) && !s.Has(u.Id) && s.Coins >= s.UpgradeCost(u.Id) && (!u.Requires.HasValue || s.Has(u.Requires.Value)));
            }
            return false;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageNet.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Catchline.Prototype;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public enum NetState { Stowed, Setting, Soaking, Ready, Hauling }
        public NetState Net { get; private set; }
        public int NetCatch => netFish.Count;
        public float NetRemaining => Mathf.Max(0f, netSoak - netTime);
        readonly List<(Species species, float kg)> netFish = new();
        float netSoak, netTime, netNextCatch;
        Transform netFloats, winchCrank, netDrum;
        LineRenderer[] netTowLines;
        float netTowStern, netTowFront, netTowHalfWidth;
        Person netWorker;
        int netClaim;
        public int NetBasketCount => OnDeck.FindAll(f => f.FromNetBasket).Count;
        public bool NetBasketBlocked => NetBasketCount > 0;
        Vector2Int NetAccess => IsNetFisher ? State.HullDef.NetAccess : new(3, 1);
        Vector3 NetBasketLocal => IsPrepFisher ? new(5.65f, .14f, 1.6f) : new(4.2f, .14f, 1.15f);

        void BuildNetKit()
        {
            if (State.Hull == 0) return;
            winchCrank = netDrum = null;
            var lib = ArtLibrary.Get();
            if (IsNetFisher)
            {
                winchCrank = netModelRoot == null ? null : ArtLibrary.Find(netModelRoot.gameObject, "NetCrank");
                netDrum = netModelRoot == null ? null : ArtLibrary.Find(netModelRoot.gameObject, "NetDrum");
                var basket = new GameObject("Net catch basket").transform;
                basket.SetParent(Boat, false);
                basket.localPosition = new Vector3(NetBasketLocal.x, .015f, NetBasketLocal.z);
                Visuals.Box("Basket floor", basket, Vector3.zero, new Vector3(.36f, .025f, .48f), Palette.DeckWoodDark);
                for (int side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2)
                {
                    Visuals.Box("Basket rim", basket, new Vector3(side * .18f, .08f, 0), new Vector3(.025f, .16f, .48f), Palette.DeckWood);
                    Visuals.Box("Basket rim", basket, new Vector3(0, .08f, side * .24f), new Vector3(.36f, .16f, .025f), Palette.DeckWood);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                var winch = ArtLibrary.Spawn(lib?.M("HandWinch"), Boat, new Vector3(4f, 0f, 1f), Quaternion.Euler(0f, 90f, 0f));
                if (winch != null) winchCrank = ArtLibrary.Find(winch, "Crank");
                else Visuals.Box("HandWinch", Boat, new Vector3(4f, .25f, 1f), new Vector3(.6f, .5f, .6f), Palette.Steel);
                ArtLibrary.Spawn(lib?.M("GillNetCoil"), Boat, new Vector3(3f, 0f, 2.32f), Quaternion.identity);
            }
            netFloats = new GameObject("Gill net in water").transform;
            netTowLines = null;
            netFloats.SetParent(world, false);
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
            {
                var position = IsNetFisher ? Grid.GetLocal(NetAccess) + new Vector3(.8f + i * .9f, -.30f, -.9f) : new Vector3(4.5f + i * 2f, -.30f, -.9f);
                var floats = ArtLibrary.Spawn(lib?.M("GillNetFloatLine"), netFloats, position, Quaternion.identity);
                ArtLibrary.Spawn(lib?.M("GillNetSection"), netFloats, position, Quaternion.identity);
                if (floats == null)
                    for (int f = 0; f < 4; f++) Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "Net float", netFloats, position + Vector3.right * (f * .5f - .75f), Vector3.one * .15f, Palette.Accent);
            }
            if (IsNetFisher) PositionNetBehindStern();
            netFloats.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }

        static Bounds NetMeshBounds(Transform source, Transform frame)
        {
            Bounds result = new(); bool first = true;
            foreach (var mf in source.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
            {
                if (mf.sharedMesh == null) continue;
                var b = mf.sharedMesh.bounds; var m = frame.worldToLocalMatrix * mf.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
                {
                    var p = m.MultiplyPoint3x4(b.center + Vector3.Scale(b.extents,
                        new Vector3((i & 1) == 0 ? -1 : 1, (i & 2) == 0 ? -1 : 1, (i & 4) == 0 ? -1 : 1)));
                    if (first) { result = new Bounds(p, Vector3.zero); first = false; } else result.Encapsulate(p);
                }
            }
            return result;
        }
        void PositionNetBehindStern()
        {
            // Bow is -X. Centre the floating gear behind +X, not beside the rail.
            var hull = NetMeshBounds(netModelRoot != null ? netModelRoot : Boat, Boat);
            var gear = NetMeshBounds(netFloats, world);
            // The delivered panels are large enough to cover the whole boat.
            // Keep this small stationary net readable behind the compact fisher.
            float scale = Mathf.Min(1f, 2.2f / Mathf.Max(.01f, gear.size.x), 1.35f / Mathf.Max(.01f, gear.size.z));
            foreach (Transform child in netFloats)
            {
                child.localPosition = gear.center + (child.localPosition - gear.center) * scale;
                child.localScale *= scale;
            }
            gear = NetMeshBounds(netFloats, world);
            netTowStern = hull.max.x;
            var shift = new Vector3(netTowStern + .65f - gear.min.x, 0, State.HullDef.Center.z - gear.center.z);
            foreach (Transform child in netFloats) child.localPosition += shift;
            gear = NetMeshBounds(netFloats, world);
            netTowFront = gear.min.x; netTowHalfWidth = gear.extents.z * .7f;
            netTowLines = new LineRenderer[2];
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
            {
                var line = new GameObject("Stern net rope " + i).AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
                line.transform.SetParent(netFloats, false); line.useWorldSpace = true;
                line.positionCount = 2; line.startWidth = line.endWidth = .018f;
                line.sharedMaterial = Visuals.Mat(Palette.Hex("BBA47A"));
                netTowLines[i] = line;
            }
            UpdateNetTowLines();
        }
        void UpdateNetTowLines()
        {
            if (netTowLines == null) return;
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
            {
                float side = i == 0 ? -1 : 1;
                netTowLines[i].SetPosition(0, Boat.TransformPoint(new Vector3(netTowStern, .02f, State.HullDef.Center.z + side * .3f)));
                netTowLines[i].SetPosition(1, netFloats.TransformPoint(new Vector3(netTowFront, WaterY + .06f, State.HullDef.Center.z + side * netTowHalfWidth)));
            }
        }

        public bool CanWorkNet => CurrentMode == Mode.Fishing && State.Hull > 0 && Captain.Idle && Captain.Carrying == null && Manning == null
            && (Net == NetState.Ready || !NetBasketBlocked && !HoldFull);

        public bool SetNet() => CanWorkNet && Net == NetState.Stowed && StartCompactNet(Captain, false);
        public bool HaulNet() => CanWorkNet && Net == NetState.Ready && StartCompactNet(Captain, true);

        void TickNet(float dt)
        {
            if (State.Hull == 0) return;
            bool settingGame = NetGameActive && NetGameSetting;
            TickNetHaulGame(dt);
            if (settingGame && Net == NetState.Soaking) return;
            float winchDrive = NetGameActive ? NetGameHeld && NetGameTension < NetHaulRedTension ? 1f : 0f : 1f;
            if (netFloats != null && netFloats.gameObject.activeSelf)
            {
                netFloats.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, Mathf.Sin(SimTime * 1.8f) * .025f, 0f);
                UpdateNetTowLines();
            }
            if (Net == NetState.Hauling && winchCrank != null) winchCrank.Rotate(Vector3.right, dt * WorkMult * winchDrive * 300f, Space.Self);
            if (IsNetFisher && Net == NetState.Hauling && netDrum != null) netDrum.Rotate(Vector3.right, dt * WorkMult * winchDrive * 100f, Space.Self);
            if (Net != NetState.Soaking) return;
            netTime = Mathf.Min(netTime + dt, netSoak);
            if (netTime >= netSoak)
            {
                RollCompactCatch(); Net = NetState.Ready;
                Toast($"Net ready: {NetCatchKg:0.0} kg. Haul it yourself or assign a deckhand.");
            }
        }

        // Only the deckhand scheduler's Nets preference calls this; soak never owns a worker.
        public bool TryNetWork(Person person)
        {
            if (!IsNetFisher || person == null || person.IsCaptain || person.Contract?.Role != CrewRole.Deckhand)
                return false;
            if (Net == NetState.Stowed) return StartCompactNet(person, false);
            return Net == NetState.Ready && StartCompactNet(person, true);
        }

        bool StartCompactNet(Person person, bool haul)
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing || !person.Idle || person.Carrying != null || netWorker != null)
                return false;
            if (Net != (haul ? NetState.Ready : NetState.Stowed)) return false;
            if (!haul && State.ActiveNetStock <= 0) return false;
            if (NetBasketBlocked || (!haul && HoldFull)) return false;
            netWorker = person;
            int claim = ++netClaim;
            Net = haul ? NetState.Hauling : NetState.Setting;
            bool Owns() => netClaim == claim && netWorker == person;
            void Cancel()
            {
                if (!Owns()) return;
                netWorker = null;
                netClaim++;
                Net = haul ? NetState.Ready : NetState.Stowed;
            }
            void Complete()
            {
                if (!Owns()) return;
                // One roll per full worker recovery, never one roll per stage.
                if (haul && !person.IsCaptain && State.Rng.NextDouble() >= .8) { Tear(); return; }
                netWorker = null;
                netClaim++;
                if (haul) { if (DeepNetSelected) State.SpendDeepNetUse(); FinishHaul(); BeginNetUnload(person); return; }
                Net = NetState.Soaking;
                netTime = 0f;
                netSoak = DeepNetSelected ? 30f : 20f;
                netFish.Clear();
                if (netFloats != null) netFloats.gameObject.SetActive(true);
                Toast($"{(DeepNetSelected ? "Deep" : "Shallow")} net set. {netSoak:0}s soak while the crew works.");
            }
            void Tear()
            {
                if (!Owns()) return;
                bool deep = DeepNetSelected;
                if (deep) State.SpendDeepNetUse();
                else State.NetInventory = Mathf.Max(0, State.NetInventory - 1);
                ResetNet();
                Toast(deep ? $"Deep haul lost — one use spent. {State.NetLoadoutLabel}." : $"Net torn — catch lost. {State.NetInventory} shallow nets left; replacements {GameData.NetReplacementCost} coins.");
            }
            bool walking = person.WalkTo(Grid, NetAccess, () =>
            {
                if (!Owns()) return;
                FaceNet(person);
                if (person.IsCaptain) BeginNetGame(person, !haul, Complete, Cancel, Tear);
                else person.Do(haul ? "Hauling net" : "Setting net", haul ? 5f : 2f, Complete, Cancel);
            }, Cancel);
            if (!walking) Cancel();
            return walking;
        }

        void RollCompactCatch()
        {
            netFish.Clear();
            bool large = DeepNetSelected;
            float target = NetWeightRange(ActiveSpot, large).y;
            float mass = 0;
            var table = GameData.Spots[ActiveSpot].Table;
            // Every added specimen has positive mass. The loop ends on remaining kg,
            // never a fish-count limit; a fish cannot cross the net's hard weight limit.
            while (true)
            {
                float remaining = target - mass;
                float total = 0;
                foreach (var entry in table)
                {
                    var d = GameData.Get(entry.species);
                    if (!large && !SmallSchool(entry.species)) continue;
                    float min = Mathf.Lerp(d.MinKg, d.MaxKg, large ? .65f : .05f);
                    if (min > remaining) continue;
                    total += entry.weight * (large ? .08f + 6f * d.Heaviness * d.Heaviness : 1);
                }
                if (total <= 0)
                {
                    if (large) { large = false; continue; }
                    break; // Residual smaller than any local school specimen stays empty.
                }
                float roll = (float)State.Rng.NextDouble() * total;
                Species species = table[0].species;
                foreach (var entry in table)
                {
                    var d = GameData.Get(entry.species);
                    if (!large && !SmallSchool(entry.species)) continue;
                    float min = Mathf.Lerp(d.MinKg, d.MaxKg, large ? .65f : .05f);
                    if (min > remaining) continue;
                    species = entry.species;
                    roll -= entry.weight * (large ? .08f + 6f * d.Heaviness * d.Heaviness : 1);
                    if (roll <= 0) break;
                }
                var fish = GameData.Get(species);
                float low = Mathf.Lerp(fish.MinKg, fish.MaxKg, large ? .65f : .05f);
                float high = Mathf.Min(remaining, Mathf.Lerp(fish.MinKg, fish.MaxKg, large ? 1f : .25f));
                float kg = Mathf.Lerp(low, high, (float)State.Rng.NextDouble());
                if (kg <= 0 || mass + kg > target + .00001f) break;
                netFish.Add((species, kg)); mass += kg; State.OnCatch(ActiveSpot, kg);
            }
        }

        static bool SmallSchool(Species species) => species == Species.Herring || species == Species.Mackerel || species == Species.Whiting || species == Species.Pollock;

        void FinishHaul()
        {
            int count = netFish.Count;
            foreach (var (species, kg) in netFish)
            {
                var fish = new DeckFish { Species = species, Kg = kg, Tile = NetAccess, CaughtAt = SimTime, FromNetBasket = true };
                fish.Root = DeckFish.BuildModel(species, kg, Boat);
                int i = IsNetFisher ? NetBasketCount : OnDeck.Count;
                fish.RestPos = IsNetFisher ? NetBasketLocal + new Vector3((i % 2 - .5f) * .1f, (i / 2) * .012f, (i % 3 - 1) * .08f)
                    : DeckGrid.ToLocal(NetAccess) + new Vector3((i % 4 - 1.5f) * .12f, .07f, (i / 4) * .08f);
                fish.SpawnFrom = IsNetFisher ? Grid.GetLocal(NetAccess) + new Vector3(.3f, .5f, 0f) : new Vector3(4.4f, .5f, .2f);
                fish.Root.localPosition = fish.SpawnFrom;
                OnDeck.Add(fish);
                Stats.Landed++;
            }
            ResetNet();
            Toast($"Hauled {count} fish. Bringing the catch into storage.");
        }

        void ResetNet()
        {
            ClearNetUnload();
            netClaim++;
            ResetNetHaulGame();
            netWorker = null;
            Net = NetState.Stowed;
            netFish.Clear();
            netTime = 0f;
            netCycleMistakes = 0;
            if (netFloats != null) netFloats.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageNetGame.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public const float NetHaulRedTension = .85f;
        public bool NetGameActive => netGameComplete != null;
        public bool NetGameSetting { get; private set; }
        public float NetGamePhase => Mathf.PingPong(netGameAge * .8f, 1f);
        public float NetGameProgress { get; private set; }
        public float NetGameTension { get; private set; }
        public int NetGameDamage => netCycleMistakes;
        public bool NetGameHeld => netPullFlash > 0; // Visual pulse only, never held input.
        float netGameAge, netTapCooldown, netPullFlash;
        int netCycleMistakes;
        bool netTapPending;
        Person netGamePerson;
        Action netGameComplete, netGameCancel, netGameFail;

        // A press queues one pull/cast. Holding generates no repeat input.
        public bool TapNetGame()
        {
            if (!NetGameActive || netTapPending || netTapCooldown > 0) return false;
            netTapPending = true;
            return true;
        }

        void BeginNetGame(Person person, bool setting, Action complete, Action cancel, Action fail)
        {
            netGamePerson = person; NetGameSetting = setting;
            netGameComplete = complete; netGameCancel = cancel; netGameFail = fail;
            NetGameProgress = 0; NetGameTension = .22f;
            netGameAge = netTapCooldown = netPullFlash = 0; netTapPending = false;
            person.Do(setting ? "Setting net" : "Winching net", float.MaxValue, null, () =>
            { if (NetGameActive && netGamePerson == person) CancelNetHaulGame(); });
        }

        public void CancelNetHaulGame()
        {
            if (!NetGameActive)
            {
                if ((Net is NetState.Hauling or NetState.Setting) && netWorker == Captain) Captain.Stop();
                return;
            }
            var person = netGamePerson; var cancel = netGameCancel;
            ClearNetHaulGame(); person?.Stop(); cancel?.Invoke();
        }

        void ClearNetHaulGame()
        {
            netGameComplete = netGameCancel = netGameFail = null; netGamePerson = null;
            netTapPending = false; NetGameSetting = false;
            NetGameProgress = NetGameTension = netGameAge = netTapCooldown = netPullFlash = 0;
        }

        void ResetNetHaulGame()
        {
            var person = netGamePerson; ClearNetHaulGame(); person?.Stop();
        }

        void FinishNetGame(bool torn)
        {
            var person = netGamePerson; var callback = torn ? netGameFail : netGameComplete;
            ClearNetHaulGame(); person?.Stop(); callback?.Invoke();
        }

        void TickNetHaulGame(float dt)
        {
            if (!NetGameActive) return;
            if (State.Hull == 0 || CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing || netWorker != netGamePerson ||
                Net != (NetGameSetting ? NetState.Setting : NetState.Hauling))
            { CancelNetHaulGame(); return; }
            while (dt > 0 && NetGameActive)
            {
                float step = Mathf.Min(.05f, dt); dt -= step;
                netGameAge += step;
                netTapCooldown = Mathf.Max(0, netTapCooldown - step);
                netPullFlash = Mathf.Max(0, netPullFlash - step);
                if (!NetGameSetting)
                {
                    NetGameTension = Mathf.Max(0, NetGameTension - .24f * step);
                    // Keep the line working: low-tension pecking cannot win.
                    float slip = NetGameTension >= NetHaulRedTension ? .36f :
                        Mathf.Lerp(.12f, .012f, Mathf.InverseLerp(.25f, .68f, NetGameTension));
                    NetGameProgress = Mathf.Max(0, NetGameProgress - slip * step);
                }
                if (!netTapPending) continue;
                netTapPending = false;
                netTapCooldown = NetGameSetting ? .45f : .12f;
                netPullFlash = .2f;
                if (NetGameSetting)
                {
                    if (NetGamePhase >= .35f && NetGamePhase <= .65f) NetGameProgress += 1f / 3;
                    else { netCycleMistakes++; NetGameProgress = Mathf.Max(0, NetGameProgress - 1f / 3); }
                }
                else
                {
                    NetGameTension = Mathf.Clamp01(NetGameTension + .18f);
                    if (NetGameTension >= NetHaulRedTension)
                    {
                        netCycleMistakes++;
                        NetGameProgress = Mathf.Max(0, NetGameProgress - .18f);
                    }
                    else NetGameProgress += Mathf.Lerp(.025f, .14f, Mathf.InverseLerp(.25f, .76f, NetGameTension));
                }
                if (netCycleMistakes >= 2) { FinishNetGame(true); continue; }
                if (NetGameProgress >= .999f) FinishNetGame(false);
            }
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageNetInterface.cs
========================================================================
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        bool netOrdersOpen;

        // Kept separate from Codex2's reference HUD. Its lead chooses the hook/placement.
        void InterfaceNetOrders()
        {
            if (State.Hull == 0) return;
            var access = IsNetFisher ? State.HullDef.NetAccess : new Vector2Int(3, 1);
            var anchor = Boat.TransformPoint(Grid.GetLocal(access) + new Vector3(.5f, .8f, 0));
            var view = Cam.WorldToViewportPoint(anchor);
            var icon = new Rect(Mathf.Clamp(view.x * iw - 34, 14, iw - 82), Mathf.Clamp((1 - view.y) * ih - 64, 16, ih - 114), 68, 64);
            Block(icon);
            bool enabled = CanCaptainNet && (Net == NetState.Ready || Net == NetState.Stowed && (NetBasketBlocked || State.ActiveNetStock > 0));
            coastalUi.Button("net", icon, "", () => CaptainNetAction(), Net == NetState.Ready ? "bubble-hot" : "bubble-white", enabled);
            coastalUi.Art("net/icon", new Rect(icon.x + 16, icon.y + 12, 36, 36), "icon-net");
            string status = NetUnloadActive ? "Unloading catch" : Net switch {
                NetState.Soaking => $"{NetRemaining:0}s", NetState.Ready => $"Haul {NetCatchKg:0.0} kg", NetState.Setting => "Setting…",
                NetState.Hauling => "Winching…", _ => HoldFull ? "Hold full" : NetBasketBlocked ? "Unload catch" : State.ActiveNetStock == 0 ? "No nets · buy supplies" : DeepNetSelected ? $"Deep {State.DeepNetUses[0]}/3 · {State.ActiveNetStock - 1} spare" : $"Shallow · {State.ActiveNetStock} aboard" };
            var caption = new Rect(icon.center.x - 76, icon.yMax + 2, 152, 29);
            coastalUi.Box("net/status/back", caption, "chip-dark");
            coastalUi.Label("net/status", caption, status, 15, Palette.Cream, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            if (IsNetFisher)
            {
                var hands = State.Crew.Where(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand).ToList();
                IButton("net/orders", new Rect(iw - 208, ReferenceCrewBottom + 72, 192, 44), "Crew duties", () => { netOrdersOpen = true; PrepOrdersOpen = false; toastUntil = 0; }, false, hands.Count > 0);
            }
            if (NetGameActive) InterfaceNetHaulGame();
            InterfacePrepOrders();
            if (!netOrdersOpen || !IsNetFisher) return;
            Block(new Rect(0, 0, iw, ih));
            coastalUi.Box("orders/shade", new Rect(0, 0, iw, ih), "chip-dark", new Color(1, 1, 1, .7f));
            var crew = State.Crew.Where(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand).ToList();
            float height = 174 + crew.Count * 70;
            var panel = new Rect((iw - (IsPrepFisher ? 550 : 450)) / 2, (ih - height) / 2, IsPrepFisher ? 550 : 450, height);
            coastalUi.Box("orders/panel", panel);
            coastalUi.Label("orders/title", new Rect(panel.x + 20, panel.y + 8, 335, 40), "Deckhand duties", 25);
            IButton("net/orders/close", new Rect(panel.xMax - 70, panel.y + 9, 54, 42), "X", () => netOrdersOpen = false, false);
            coastalUi.Label("orders/help", new Rect(panel.x + 20, panel.y + 53, 410, 48), "Fish: carry rod catches and bait.\nNets: set, haul and empty the basket.", 16, SeaInk);
            for (int i = 0; i < crew.Count; i++)
            {
                var c = crew[i]; float y = panel.y + 108 + i * 70;
                coastalUi.Label("orders/" + i + "/name", new Rect(panel.x + 20, y, 166, 52), c.Name, 21);
                IButton("net/orders/" + i + "/fish", new Rect(panel.x + 190, y, 108, 50), "Fish", () => SetDeckhandWork(c, DeckhandWork.Fish), c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Fish);
                IButton("net/orders/" + i + "/nets", new Rect(panel.x + 308, y, 122, 50), "Nets", () => SetDeckhandWork(c, DeckhandWork.Nets), c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Nets);
                if (IsPrepFisher) IButton("net/orders/" + i + "/prep", new Rect(panel.x + 440, y, 90, 50), "Prep", () => SetDeckhandWork(c, DeckhandWork.Prep), c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep, c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep || crew.Count(h => h.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep) < 2);
            }
            coastalUi.Label("orders/footer", new Rect(panel.x + 20, panel.yMax - 60, 410, 50), "80% safe. Deep failure: catch + one use lost.\nShallow tear: net lost. Captain: timing decides.", 15, SeaInk);
        }

        Rect NetGamePanel => new Rect((iw - 450) / 2, ih - 234, 450, 216);
        Rect NetGameCancelRect => new Rect(NetGamePanel.x + 312, NetGamePanel.y + 164, 122, 40);

        void InterfaceNetHaulGame()
        {
            var panel = NetGamePanel;
            float width = panel.width;
            Block(panel); coastalUi.Box("net/haul/panel", panel);
            coastalUi.Label("net/haul/title", new Rect(panel.x + 18, panel.y + 8, width - 36, 30), NetGameSetting ? "Spread the net" : "Work the net", 24);
            string help = NetGameSetting ? "Tap in the green · 3 clean casts" : NetGameTension >= NetHaulRedTension ? "Too tight! Wait — red loses progress." : "Tap to pull · stay close to red, then ease";
            coastalUi.Label("net/haul/help", new Rect(panel.x + 18, panel.y + 42, width - 36, 28), help, 16, SeaInk);
            var meter = new Rect(panel.x + 18, panel.y + 80, width - 36, 16);
            coastalUi.Bar("net/haul/tension", meter, NetGameSetting ? NetGamePhase : NetGameTension, !NetGameSetting && NetGameTension >= NetHaulRedTension);
            float lo = NetGameSetting ? .35f : .65f, hi = NetGameSetting ? .65f : .84f;
            coastalUi.Box("net/haul/sweet", new Rect(meter.x + meter.width * lo, meter.y, meter.width * (hi - lo), meter.height), "", new Color(.3f, .75f, .49f, .8f), blocks: false);
            if (!NetGameSetting) coastalUi.Box("net/haul/red", new Rect(meter.x + meter.width * .85f, meter.y, meter.width * .15f, meter.height), "", new Color(.88f, .28f, .2f, .9f), blocks: false);
            float cursor = NetGameSetting ? NetGamePhase : NetGameTension;
            coastalUi.Box("net/haul/cursor", new Rect(meter.x + meter.width * cursor - 2, meter.y - 3, 4, meter.height + 6), "", SeaInk, blocks: false);
            coastalUi.Label("net/haul/progresslabel", new Rect(panel.x + 18, panel.y + 102, 145, 22), NetGameSetting ? $"Spread {NetGameProgress * 3:0}/3" : $"Net aboard {NetGameProgress * 100:0}%", 14, SeaInk);
            coastalUi.Bar("net/haul/progress", new Rect(panel.x + 160, panel.y + 108, width - 178, 8), NetGameProgress);
            coastalUi.Label("net/haul/risk", new Rect(panel.x + 18, panel.y + 127, width - 36, 28), NetGameDamage > 0 ? "Fraying — one more mistake loses this haul!" : DeepNetSelected ? "2 mistakes lose catch + one use · other uses kept" : $"2 mistakes tear it · {State.NetInventory} shallow nets", 15, NetGameDamage > 0 ? Palette.Accent : SeaInk);
            // Raw press handling gives exactly one pull. UI release never repeats it.
            IButton("net/haul/tap", new Rect(panel.x + 16, panel.y + 164, 280, 40), "Tap screen or press Space", () => { }, true);
            IButton("net/haul/cancel", NetGameCancelRect, "Leave net", CancelNetHaulGame, false);
        }
    }
}
