using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public bool SelectNetLoadout(NetKind kind)
        {
            if (CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour || State.Hull == 0 || Net != NetState.Stowed || NetUnloadActive || NetBasketBlocked) return false;
            State.SelectedNet = kind; SaveGame(); return true;
        }
        public bool DeepNetSelected => State.SelectedNet == NetKind.Deep;
        public float NetCatchKg
        {
            get { float kg = 0; foreach (var f in netFish) kg += f.kg; return kg; }
        }
        // Both net types share one hard mass limit. Richness affects the catch,
        // not equipment capacity; no species is imported from another ground.
        public Vector2 NetWeightRange(int spot, bool deep)
        {
            float target = State.NetCapacityKg * Mathf.Lerp(.85f, 1f, State.Richness[spot]);
            return new Vector2(target * .85f, target);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageNetUnload.cs
========================================================================
using System.Linq;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using Catchline.Prototype;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        Person netUnloader;
        readonly List<DeckFish> netUnloadBatch = new();
        public bool NetUnloadActive => netUnloader != null;
        bool IsNetUnloading(Person person) => netUnloader == person;
        void BeginNetUnload(Person person)
        {
            netUnloader = person;
            Toast($"Bringing {NetBasketCount} net fish into storage.");
        }
        void ClearNetUnload()
        {
            foreach (var fish in netUnloadBatch)
            {
                if (fish.Root == null || !OnDeck.Contains(fish)) continue;
                fish.Root.SetParent(Boat, false); fish.Root.localPosition = fish.RestPos;
                fish.ClaimedBy = null;
            }
            if (netUnloader != null) netUnloader.CarryingBatch = false;
            netUnloadBatch.Clear(); netUnloader = null;
        }

        void TickNetUnload(float dt)
        {
            if (dt <= 0 || CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing || netUnloader == null) return;
            var person = netUnloader;
            if (person.Root == null) { ClearNetUnload(); return; }
            if (!person.Idle) return;
            if (HoldFull) { ClearNetUnload(); return; }
            var ready = OnDeck.Where(f => f.FromNetBasket && f.ClaimedBy == null && f.Age >= .4f)
                .ToList();
            if (ready.Count > 0)
            {
                foreach (var fish in ready) { fish.ClaimedBy = person; netUnloadBatch.Add(fish); }
                person.FacePoint(ready[0].RestPos);
                person.Do("Gathering net catch", FishHandlingSeconds(person, 1.2f), () =>
                {
                    for (int i = 0; i < netUnloadBatch.Count; i++)
                    {
                        var f = netUnloadBatch[i]; f.Root.SetParent(person.Root, false);
                        f.Root.localPosition = new Vector3((i % 2 - .5f) * .13f, .55f + (i / 4) * .018f, .21f + (i % 4 / 2) * .10f);
                    }
                    person.CarryingBatch = true;
                    if (!person.WalkTo(Grid, CoolerAccess, () => { FaceHold(person); person.Do("Stowing net catch", FishHandlingSeconds(person, 1.8f), () =>
                    {
                        var stored = new List<CaughtFish>();
                        foreach (var f in netUnloadBatch.ToArray())
                        {
                            if (HoldFull || !OnDeck.Contains(f)) continue;
                            var secured = new CaughtFish { Species = f.Species, Kg = f.Kg, StowedAt = f.CaughtAt };
                            if (!TryStore(secured)) continue; stored.Add(secured);
                            OnDeck.Remove(f); Visuals.Kill(f.Root.gameObject);
                        }
                        lidPop = 1f;
                        RecordStoredNet(stored);
                        ClearNetUnload();
                    }, ClearNetUnload); }, ClearNetUnload)) ClearNetUnload();
                }, ClearNetUnload);
                return;
            }
            // A just-landed batch needs the brief drop animation before pickup.
            if (!OnDeck.Any(f => f.FromNetBasket)) ClearNetUnload();
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyagePrepFisher.cs
========================================================================
using System.Linq;
using System.Collections;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        public bool IsPrepFisher => State != null && State.Hull == 3;
        public Vector2Int[] PrepWorkerTiles { get; private set; } = System.Array.Empty<Vector2Int>();
        Transform rawBuckets, rawIceCoolers;

        void ConfigurePrepFisher(GameObject model)
        {
            rawBuckets = ArtLibrary.Find(model, "RawBuckets");
            rawIceCoolers = ArtLibrary.Find(model, "RawIceCoolers");
            SyncPrepStorage(); // Route rasterization must see only the active variant.
            PrepWorkerTiles = new[] { "PrepWorker_1", "PrepWorker_2" }
                .Select(n => Grid.GetTile(Boat.InverseTransformPoint(ArtLibrary.Find(model, n).position))).ToArray();
        }

        void SyncPrepStorage()
        {
            bool ice = State.Has(UpgradeId.IceBox);
            if (rawBuckets != null) rawBuckets.gameObject.SetActive(!ice);
            if (rawIceCoolers != null) rawIceCoolers.gameObject.SetActive(ice);
        }

        IEnumerator CapturePrepBoat(string directory)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            Application.runInBackground = true;
            captureFreeze = true;
            State.Owned.Remove(UpgradeId.IceBox); SyncUpgrades();
            foreach (var size in new[] { new Vector2Int(1440, 900), new Vector2Int(844, 390) })
            {
                Screen.SetResolution(size.x, size.y, FullScreenMode.Windowed);
                // Let the native swapchain, shaders and canvas settle after resize.
                for (int frame = 0; frame < 16; frame++) yield return null;
                Showcase(0, 0); captureFreeze = true; toastUntil = 0; Tick(.01f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "tier4-buckets-" + size.x);
            }
            Showcase(-1, 0); State.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.IceBox); SyncUpgrades();
            foreach (var size in new[] { new Vector2Int(1440, 900), new Vector2Int(844, 390) })
            {
                Screen.SetResolution(size.x, size.y, FullScreenMode.Windowed);
                // Let the native swapchain, shaders and canvas settle after resize.
                for (int frame = 0; frame < 16; frame++) yield return null;
                Showcase(0, 0); captureFreeze = true; toastUntil = 0; Tick(.01f);
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "tier4-ice-coolers-" + size.x);
            }
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] TIER033 NATIVE CAPTURES COMPLETE " + directory);
            Application.Quit(0);
        }

        // First integration: shipyard trial, not an unbalanced paid progression step.
        // Changing hulls is restricted to port and never touches a real save.
        public bool TrialPrepFisher()
        {
            if (!SessionOnly || CurrentMode != Mode.Harbour || IsPrepFisher) return false;
            State.Hull = 3;
            State.Fuel = State.FuelCapacity;
            State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
            RebuildBoat();
            Toast("Prep fisher trial · four crew berths. Assign up to two deckhands to the prep table.");
            return true;
        }

        public bool StartPrepBoatTrial()
        {
            if (!SessionOnly) return false;
            NewGame();
            if (!TrialPrepFisher()) return false;
            State.Coins = 1000;
            foreach (var role in new[] { CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Fisherman })
            {
                State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
                State.Hire(State.Board.First(c => c.Role == role));
            }
            State.ExtraCrew.First(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand).WorkPreference = DeckhandWork.Prep;
            State.Coins = 200;
            State.Bait = 60;
            SyncCrew();
            showTitle = paused = showSettings = panelOpen = captureFreeze = netOrdersOpen = false;
            ActiveSpot = SelectedSpot = 0;
            voyageStart = SimTime;
            Showcase(0, 0);
            toastUntil = 0;
            return true;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyagePrepOrders.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections;
using System.IO;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Prototype;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    [System.Serializable]
    public class PrepOrderSettings
    {
        public bool ChumFirst = true;
        public int ChumTarget = 10;
        public float ChumMaxKg = 1;
        public int ChumMaxValue = 10;
        public float PrepareMinKg;
    }

    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        sealed class PrepJob
        {
            public Person Worker;
            public CaughtFish Fish;
            public bool Chum;
            public int Slot;
            public Transform Visual;
        }
        readonly List<PrepJob> prepJobs = new();
        // A station belongs to a hand across jobs and empty-input periods.
        // Job completion releases the fish, never the worker's standing position.
        readonly Dictionary<Person, int> prepStations = new();
        public int PrepWorkersActive => prepJobs.Count;
        public int ChumBeingMade => prepJobs.Count(j => j.Chum);
        public bool PrepOrdersOpen;

        // Reserved fish stay in cargo: processing never creates spare hold capacity.
        public bool TryPrepWork(Person hand)
        {
            if (!IsPrepFisher || CurrentMode != Mode.Fishing || hand == null || hand.Contract == null
                || hand.Contract.Role != CrewRole.Deckhand || hand.Contract.WorkPreference != DeckhandWork.Prep
                || hand.Carrying != null || (!hand.Idle && !hand.Loitering) || prepJobs.Count >= 2
                || prepJobs.Any(j => j.Worker == hand) || PrepWorkerTiles.Length != 2) return false;
            if (!prepStations.TryGetValue(hand, out int slot))
            {
                if (prepStations.Count >= 2) return false;
                slot = Enumerable.Range(0, 2).First(i => !prepStations.Values.Contains(i));
                prepStations.Add(hand, slot);
            }
            var order = State.PrepOrders;
            CaughtFish Pick(bool chum) => Cooler.FirstOrDefault(f => !f.Prepared && !prepJobs.Any(j => j.Fish == f)
                && (chum ? State.Chum + ChumBeingMade < Mathf.Clamp(order.ChumTarget, 0, 99)
                    && f.Kg <= order.ChumMaxKg && State.ValueOf(f, SimTime) <= order.ChumMaxValue
                    : f.Kg >= order.PrepareMinKg));
            bool isChum = order.ChumFirst;
            var fish = Pick(isChum);
            if (fish == null) { isChum = !isChum; fish = Pick(isChum); }
            if (fish == null)
            {
                // Go to the table once, then wait there instead of wandering.
                if (hand.Tile != PrepWorkerTiles[slot])
                    hand.WalkTo(Grid, PrepWorkerTiles[slot], () => hand.FacePoint(new Vector3(3.6f, .6f, 1.4f)));
                else { hand.Stop(); hand.FacePoint(new Vector3(3.6f, .6f, 1.4f)); }
                return false;
            }
            // Stop a prior patrol before claiming; no path is queued for table work.
            hand.Stop();
            var job = new PrepJob { Worker = hand, Fish = fish, Chum = isChum,
                Slot = slot };
            prepJobs.Add(job);
            void Release()
            {
                prepJobs.Remove(job);
                if (job.Visual != null) Visuals.Kill(job.Visual.gameObject);
            }
            void Finish()
            {
                // A return/duty change cancels reservations; never consume twice.
                if (prepJobs.Contains(job) && Cooler.Contains(fish) && !fish.Prepared)
                {
                    if (job.Chum)
                    {
                        if (State.Chum < Mathf.Clamp(State.PrepOrders.ChumTarget, 0, 99))
                        { Cooler.Remove(fish); State.Chum++; }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        fish.PreparedQuality = State.Freshness(fish, SimTime);
                        fish.Prepared = true;
                    }
                }
                Release();
                hand.FacePoint(new Vector3(3.6f, .6f, 1.4f));
            }
            void Begin()
            {
                float side = slot == 0 ? 1.76f : 1.04f;
                hand.FacePoint(new Vector3(3.6f, .6f, 1.4f));
                job.Visual = DeckFish.BuildModel(fish.Species, fish.Kg, Boat);
                job.Visual.localScale *= .65f;
                job.Visual.localPosition = new Vector3(2.94f, .5f, side);
                hand.Do("Taking fish", .15f, () =>
                {
                    job.Visual.localPosition = new Vector3(3.6f, .8f, side);
                    hand.Do(job.Chum ? "Making chum" : "Preparing fish", job.Chum ? .9f : 1.1f, () =>
                    {
                        // Turn/reach to the box behind; feet stay on the same tile.
                        hand.FacePoint(new Vector3(4.84f, .5f, side));
                        job.Visual.localPosition = new Vector3(4.84f, .5f, side);
                        hand.Do("Putting fish away", .15f, Finish, Release);
                    }, Release);
                }, Release);
            }
            if (hand.Tile == PrepWorkerTiles[slot]) Begin();
            else if (!hand.WalkTo(Grid, PrepWorkerTiles[slot], Begin, Release)) { Release(); return false; }
            return true;
        }

        void ClearPrepWork()
        {
            foreach (var hand in prepStations.Keys.ToArray()) hand.Stop();
            prepJobs.Clear();
            prepStations.Clear();
            PrepOrdersOpen = false;
        }

        public void ChangePrepPriority()
        {
            State.PrepOrders.ChumFirst = !State.PrepOrders.ChumFirst;
            SaveGame(); // Current jobs finish; new orders choose the new priority.
        }

        IEnumerator CapturePrepOrders(string directory)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            Application.runInBackground = true;
            captureFreeze = true;
            Cooler.Clear();
            State.Chum = 9;
            for (int i = 0; i < 14; i++) Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .2f, StowedAt = SimTime });
            Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Cod, Kg = 5, StowedAt = SimTime });
            var hands = People.Where(p => p.Contract?.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand).ToArray();
            foreach (var h in hands) SetDeckhandWork(h.Contract, DeckhandWork.Prep);
            foreach (var h in hands) TryPrepWork(h);
            // Drive real walks until both hands reach their actual table anchors.
            for (int i = 0; i < 1600 && hands.Any(h => h.BusyLabel is not ("Making chum" or "Preparing fish")); i++)
                foreach (var h in hands) if (h.BusyLabel is not ("Making chum" or "Preparing fish")) h.Tick(.025f, 1, Cam.transform.rotation);
            foreach (var size in new[] { new Vector2Int(1440,900), new Vector2Int(844,390) })
            {
                Screen.SetResolution(size.x, size.y, FullScreenMode.Windowed);
                for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) yield return null;
                PrepOrdersOpen = netOrdersOpen = false; toastUntil = 0;
                yield return null;
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "prep-working-" + size.x);
                PrepOrdersOpen = true;
                yield return null;
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "prep-orders-" + size.x);
                PrepOrdersOpen = false; netOrdersOpen = true;
                yield return null;
                yield return CaptureFrame(directory, "prep-duties-" + size.x);
                netOrdersOpen = false;
            }
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PREP035 NATIVE CAPTURES COMPLETE");
            Application.Quit(0);
        }

        void InterfacePrepOrders()
        {
            if (!IsPrepFisher) return;
            IButton("prep/open", new Rect(18, ih - 72, 232, 54), $"Prep table / chum {State.Chum}", () => { PrepOrdersOpen = true; netOrdersOpen = false; toastUntil = 0; }, false);
            if (!PrepOrdersOpen) return;
            Block(new Rect(0, 0, iw, ih));
            coastalUi.Box("prep/shade", new Rect(0, 0, iw, ih), "chip-dark", new Color(1, 1, 1, .7f));
            var p = new Rect((iw - 550) / 2, (ih - 454) / 2, 550, 454);
            coastalUi.Box("prep/panel", p);
            coastalUi.Label("prep/title", new Rect(p.x + 20, p.y + 8, 430, 36), "Prep table", 26);
            IButton("prep/close", new Rect(p.xMax - 74, p.y + 8, 54, 48), "X", () => PrepOrdersOpen = false, false);
            coastalUi.Label("prep/status", new Rect(p.x + 20, p.y + 49, 510, 28), $"{PrepWorkersActive}/2 working / {Cooler.Count(f => f.Prepared)} prepared / {State.Chum} chum", 17, SeaInk);
            var o = State.PrepOrders;
            IButton("prep/priority", new Rect(p.x + 20, p.y + 84, 510, 54), o.ChumFirst ? "1. Chum to target   /   2. Prepare   - swap" : "1. Prepare   /   2. Chum to target   - swap", ChangePrepPriority, false);
            coastalUi.Label("prep/help", new Rect(p.x + 20, p.y + 144, 510, 40), "Full chum stock? Prepare fish. Use chum? Refill it.\nOne fish makes one chum. Prepared fish never spoils.", 15, SeaInk);
            IButton("prep/target", new Rect(p.x + 20, p.y + 190, 248, 54), $"Chum target: {o.ChumTarget}", () => { o.ChumTarget = o.ChumTarget == 10 ? 20 : o.ChumTarget == 20 ? 0 : 10; SaveGame(); }, false);
            IButton("prep/input", new Rect(p.x + 282, p.y + 190, 248, 54), $"Input <= {o.ChumMaxKg:0.#}kg / {o.ChumMaxValue}c", () => { o.ChumMaxKg = o.ChumMaxKg == 1 ? 2 : o.ChumMaxKg == 2 ? .5f : 1; SaveGame(); }, false);
            IButton("prep/minimum", new Rect(p.x + 20, p.y + 252, 510, 54), $"Prepare fish >= {o.PrepareMinKg:0.#}kg / +25% sale value", () => { o.PrepareMinKg = o.PrepareMinKg == 0 ? 1 : o.PrepareMinKg == 1 ? 3 : 0; SaveGame(); }, false);
            var hands = State.Crew.Where(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand).ToList();
            for (int i = 0; i < hands.Count; i++)
            {
                var c = hands[i];
                // Two columns accommodate every berth without growing the modal.
                IButton("prep/worker/" + i, new Rect(p.x + 20 + i % 2 * 262, p.y + 316 + i / 2 * 60, 248, 54),
                    $"{c.Name}: {c.WorkPreference}", () => SetDeckhandWork(c, c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep ? DeckhandWork.Fish : DeckhandWork.Prep), c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep,
                    c.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep || hands.Count(h => h.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Prep) < 2);
            }
            if (hands.Count == 0) coastalUi.Label("prep/nohands", new Rect(p.x + 20, p.y + 328, 510, 54), "Hire deckhands at the Crew Office to run this table.", 18);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageScenery.cs
========================================================================
using Catchline.Prototype;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    // World dressing from Codex's kits: quay clutter, gulls, deck props and the landmarks of each sea.
    // Every model is optional: a missing one is simply skipped, so art can land in any order.
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        const float WaterY = -0.32f;

        static readonly string[] ShopFronts = { "ShopFront_FishMarket", "ShopFront_HarbourMaster", "ShopFront_CrewOffice", "ShopFront_Chandlery", "ShopFront_Supplies" };

        public static float ModelHeight(GameObject prefab)
        {
            float top = 0f;
            foreach (var mf in prefab.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
            {
                if (mf.sharedMesh == null) continue;
                var b = mf.sharedMesh.bounds;
                var m = mf.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                top = Mathf.Max(top, m.MultiplyPoint3x4(b.center + new Vector3(0f, b.extents.y, 0f)).y - prefab.transform.position.y);
            }
            return top;
        }

        static GameObject Put(Transform root, GameObject prefab, Vector3 p, float yaw = 0f, float scale = 1f)
            => prefab == null ? null : ArtLibrary.Spawn(prefab, root, p, Quaternion.Euler(0f, yaw, 0f), scale);

        void DressHarbour(Transform root, ArtLibrary lib, float quayTop, System.Func<float, float, float> R)
        {
            if (lib == null) return;
            float q = quayTop;

            // Behind the shops: the town on the hill and the shipyard (task 07, when it lands).
            var hill = lib.M("HillBackdrop");
            if (hill != null)
                for (float x = -38f; x <= 42f; x += 40f) Put(root, hill, new Vector3(x, q, 13.5f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("Shed_Shipyard"), new Vector3(15.5f, q, 7.5f), 180f);

            // Fish market corner: the stall with its awning, fish crates and barrels.
            Put(root, lib.M("MarketStall") ?? lib.M("MarketStallAwning"), new Vector3(-9.2f, q, 4.6f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("FishCrate"), new Vector3(-7.6f, q, 3.7f), 12f);
            Put(root, lib.M("FishCrate"), new Vector3(-7.4f, q + 0.335f, 3.75f), -8f);
            Put(root, lib.M("FishCrate"), new Vector3(-0.6f, q, 3.9f), 80f);
            Put(root, lib.M("Barrel"), new Vector3(-0.4f, q, 4.6f));
            Put(root, lib.M("Barrel"), new Vector3(0.15f, q, 4.75f), 40f);

            // Along the quay.
            Put(root, lib.M("CrateStack"), new Vector3(7.0f, q, 4.5f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("Lifebuoy"), new Vector3(-4.3f, q, 4.4f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("LifebuoyStand"), new Vector3(10.7f, q, 3.0f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("Bench"), new Vector3(3.4f, q, 4.65f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("CrabPotStack"), new Vector3(11.4f, q, 4.2f), 200f);
            Put(root, lib.M("NetRack"), new Vector3(12.6f, q, 4.9f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("Barrel"), new Vector3(11.1f, q, 4.8f), 10f);
            Put(root, lib.M("Skiff_VariantBlue"), new Vector3(10f, 0f, 0.9f), -90f)?.AddComponent<Floater>();
            Put(root, lib.M("Rowboat_VariantGreen"), new Vector3(-12f, 0f, 1.3f), -90f)?.AddComponent<Floater>();

            // People about the harbour (task 07).
            Put(root, lib.M("NPC_Fishmonger"), new Vector3(-9.0f, q, 3.8f), 180f);
            Put(root, lib.M("NPC_HarbourMaster"), new Vector3(-2.6f, q, 4.3f), 160f);
            Put(root, lib.M("NPC_Sitting"), new Vector3(3.4f, q + .2235f, 4.5f), 180f);

            // Gulls on bollards, a lamp, roofs and crates.
            var gull = lib.M("Seagull");
            if (gull != null)
            {
                var perches = new[]
                {
                    (new Vector3(-4.5f, q + 0.47f, 2.6f), 200f), (new Vector3(7.5f, q + 0.47f, 2.6f), 150f),
                    (new Vector3(3.4f, q + 2.8f, 4.0f), 220f), (new Vector3(-2.0f, q + 3.65f, 6.6f), 170f),
                    (new Vector3(5.2f, q + 3.65f, 6.8f), 190f), (new Vector3(7.0f, q + 0.68f, 4.5f), 230f),
                };
                foreach (var (p, yaw) in perches) Put(root, gull, p, yaw)?.AddComponent<Gull>();
            }
        }

        void DressDeck(ArtLibrary lib)
        {
            if (lib == null) return;
            // Bow is -X; port (rods) is -Z. Keep the walking tiles clear: props hug the rails and corners.
            Put(Boat, lib.M("RopeCoil"), new Vector3(-0.38f, 0.01f, 0.45f), 30f, 0.8f);
            Put(Boat, lib.M("SmallAnchor"), new Vector3(-0.42f, 0.02f, 0.95f), 90f, 0.6f);
            Put(Boat, lib.M("TackleBox"), new Vector3(2.32f, 0f, 1.32f), 90f, 0.8f);
            Put(Boat, lib.M("Lantern"), new Vector3(2.4f, 0f, 0.55f), 0f, 0.8f);
        }

        // The fishing camera looks at (1, 0, -0.7) with ortho size 3.2 at 16:9, so it sees about 11 x 6.4 screen units.
        // Sea landmarks are placed in screen units (sx right, sy up, measured on the water) so they actually show up
        // around the boat. The boat itself covers about |sx| < 2.6, |sy| < 1.6.
        static readonly Vector3 SeaCenter = new(1f, 0f, -0.7f);
        public const float ScreenYaw = 35f; // a model's +X lines up with screen-right at this yaw

        public static Vector3 AtScreen(float sx, float sy, float y = WaterY)
        {
            // Inverse of the camera's ground projection (right = (cos35, 0, -sin35), up = (sin35 cos45, ., cos35 cos45) / cos45 on the ground).
            const float c = 0.81915f, s = 0.57358f, k = 0.70711f;
            // Solve [c, -s; s*k, c*k] [dx, dz] = [sx, sy]
            float det = c * c * k + s * s * k;
            float dx = (c * k * sx + s * sy) / det;
            float dz = (-s * k * sx + c * sy) / det;
            return new Vector3(SeaCenter.x + dx, y, SeaCenter.z + dz);
        }

        void BuildSea(int kind, Transform root, ArtLibrary lib, System.Func<float, float, float> R, System.Action<Vector3, float> rock)
        {
            BuildSeaDressing(kind, root, lib, R, rock);
            ReserveFishingWater(root);
        }

        void BuildSeaDressing(int kind, Transform root, ArtLibrary lib, System.Func<float, float, float> R, System.Action<Vector3, float> rock)
        {
            string[] environments = { "GullHarbourMouth", "GullKelpBank", "GullSandbar", "GullRockyPoint", "GullDeepChannel" };
            if (kind >= 0 && kind < environments.Length && lib?.M(environments[kind]) != null)
            {
                var environment = Put(root, lib.M(environments[kind]), new Vector3(0, WaterY, 0));
                OrientFishingCoast(environment, root);
                if (kind == 2)
                {
                    // Let actual water depth make sand wet. A per-quad colour
                    // boundary otherwise creates a square staircase at the shore.
                    var drySand = Visuals.Mat(Palette.Hex("D9C393"));
                    var seabedSand = Visuals.Mat(Palette.Hex("BEA77D"));
                    foreach (var renderer in environment.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
                    {
                        if (!renderer.name.Contains("Terrain_Connected")) continue;
                        var materials = renderer.sharedMaterials;
                        for (int i = 0; i < materials.Length; i++)
                            if (materials[i] == seabedSand) materials[i] = drySand;
                        renderer.sharedMaterials = materials;
                    }
                }
                return;
            }
            GameObject M(string n) => lib?.M(n);
            GameObject At(string n, float sx, float sy, float yaw = ScreenYaw, float scale = 1f, float y = WaterY)
                => Put(root, M(n), AtScreen(sx, sy, y), yaw, scale);
            void Rocks(params (float sx, float sy, float s)[] list)
            {
                foreach (var (sx, sy, s) in list) rock(AtScreen(sx, sy), s);
            }
            void Floating(GameObject go, float amp = 0.04f) { if (go != null) go.AddComponent<Floater>().Amp = amp; }

            switch (kind)
            {
                case 0: // Harbour mouth: the breakwater behind, the lighthouse at its end, marker buoys.
                    if (M("GullHarbourMouth") != null)
                    {
                        Put(root, M("GullHarbourMouth"), new Vector3(0f, WaterY, 0f));
                        break;
                    }
                    for (float sx = -8f; sx <= 2.5f; sx += 1.15f) Rocks((sx, R(3.2f, 3.7f), R(.65f, .95f)));
                    Rocks((3.4f, 3.6f, .9f), (6.6f, 3.4f, .9f));
                    if (lib?.Lighthouse != null)
                    {
                        Put(root, lib.Lighthouse, AtScreen(6.8f, 2.6f, .15f), ScreenYaw);
                        rock(AtScreen(6.8f, 2.6f, -.44f), .95f);
                    }
                    Floating(At("MarkerBuoy_Red", -4.6f, -1.15f) ?? At("ChannelBuoy", -4.6f, -1.15f, ScreenYaw, 0.6f));
                    Floating(At("MarkerBuoy_Green", 4.9f, -1.2f));
                    break;

                case 1: // Kelp bank: floating kelp all round, a couple of big mats, one rock.
                    for (int i = 0; i < 26; i++)
                    {
                        float sx = R(-6.5f, 6.5f), sy = R(-3.3f, 3.2f);
                        if (Mathf.Abs(sx) < 3f && Mathf.Abs(sy) < 1.9f) continue;
                        if (lib?.Kelp != null) Put(root, lib.Kelp, AtScreen(sx, sy, WaterY + 0.01f), R(0f, 360f), R(1.2f, 2.4f));
                    }
                    foreach (var (sx, sy) in new[] { (-5.2f, 2.3f), (5.0f, -2.5f), (-4.8f, -2.6f), (5.4f, 2.1f) })
                        At("KelpForest", sx, sy, R(0f, 360f), 1f, WaterY + 0.012f);
                    Rocks((4.6f, 2.8f, 1.3f));
                    break;

                case 2: // Sandbar: pale banks breaking the surface, a bird rock.
                    var bank = M("SandBank_Large") ?? M("SandBank");
                    foreach (var (sx, sy, s) in new[] { (-5.8f, 2.7f, 1.0f), (1.2f, 3.3f, 0.8f), (6.0f, 2.2f, 1.0f), (-6.0f, -2.9f, 0.8f), (5.0f, -3.1f, 0.9f) })
                    {
                        if (bank != null) Put(root, bank, AtScreen(sx, sy, WaterY + 0.015f), ScreenYaw + R(-20f, 20f), s);
                        else Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "SandBank", root, AtScreen(sx, sy, -0.33f), new Vector3(4f * s, 0.12f, 2f * s), Palette.Hex("E3CF9C"));
                    }
                    if (At("BirdIsland", -3.6f, 2.8f) == null) Rocks((-3.6f, 2.8f, 0.8f));
                    Rocks((3.2f, 3.0f, 0.6f), (-6.2f, 0.4f, 0.7f));
                    break;

                case 3: // Rocky point: a cliff wall behind, rock clusters, the arch.
                    var cliff = M("CliffSegment8m");
                    for (float sx = -8f; sx <= 8f; sx += 8f)
                    {
                        if (cliff != null) Put(root, cliff, AtScreen(sx, 3.1f), ScreenYaw);
                        else Visuals.Box("Cliff", root, AtScreen(sx, 3.1f, 0.8f), new Vector3(8f, 2.4f, 2.4f), Palette.Hex("7C7A6E"))
                            .transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, ScreenYaw, 0f);
                    }
                    foreach (var (sx, sy, s) in new[] { (-5.6f, 2.0f, 0.8f), (5.6f, -2.7f, 0.7f), (-5.2f, -3.0f, 0.7f), (6.0f, 1.7f, 0.9f) })
                        if (At("SeaRockCluster", sx, sy, R(0f, 360f), s) == null) Rocks((sx, sy, s * 1.4f));
                    if (At("RockArch", 3.6f, 2.6f) == null) Rocks((3.6f, 2.6f, 1.6f));
                    Rocks((-2.5f, 3.2f, 0.9f), (1.0f, 3.3f, 0.8f));
                    break;

                default: // Deep channel: open water, a channel buoy, a ship crossing far off, a wreck.
                    var buoy = At("ChannelBuoy", -4.6f, -2.2f, ScreenYaw, 0.7f);
                    if (buoy == null)
                    {
                        Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "ChannelBuoy", root, AtScreen(-4.4f, -2f, 0.1f), new Vector3(0.45f, 0.45f, 0.45f), Palette.Bad);
                        Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "BuoyTop", root, AtScreen(-4.4f, -2f, 0.65f), Vector3.one * 0.3f, Palette.Oilskin);
                    }
                    else Floating(buoy, 0.06f);
                    var ship = At("DistantShip", 3.8f, 2.9f, ScreenYaw + 90f, 0.45f);
                    if (ship == null) Visuals.Box("FarShip", root, AtScreen(3.8f, 2.9f, 0.2f), new Vector3(6f, 1.2f, 1.4f), Palette.Hex("5A6670"))
                        .transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, ScreenYaw, 0f);
                    else Floating(ship, 0.02f);
                    At("WreckMast", -5.3f, 2.4f, 20f);
                    var tail = At("WhaleTail", 5.0f, -2.6f, ScreenYaw - 30f);
                    if (tail != null) tail.AddComponent<WhaleTail>();
                    break;
            }
        }

        void SetWaterBiome(int kind)
        {
            if (seaMat.HasProperty("_AbsorptionColor")) { PtsWater.SetBiome(seaMat, kind); return; }
            if (!seaMat.HasProperty("_ShallowColor")) return;
            string[] shallow = { "21B9C3", "31AA9E", "62D2CB", "2AAABB", "208B9E" };
            seaMat.SetColor("_ShallowColor", Palette.Hex(kind < 0 ? "54C7BE" : shallow[Mathf.Clamp(kind, 0, 4)]));
            seaMat.SetFloat("_DepthRange", kind == 2 ? 2.7f : kind == 4 ? 5f : 3.5f);
            seaMat.SetFloat("_WaveAmplitude", kind == 4 ? .032f : .018f);
            seaMat.SetFloat("_NormalStrength", kind == 4 ? .48f : .35f);
        }

        // Editor screenshots: puts the boat at a sea (kind >= 0), the harbour (-1) or keeps the scene for a deck close-up (-2).
        public void Showcase(int kind, float zoom)
        {
            if (kind >= -1)
            {
                if (scenery != null) Visuals.Kill(scenery.gameObject);
                if (oldScenery != null) Visuals.Kill(oldScenery.gameObject);
                oldScenery = null;
                scenery = BuildScenery(kind);
                CurrentMode = kind < 0 ? Mode.Harbour : Mode.Fishing;
                seaMat.color = kind < 0 ? Palette.Sea : GameData.Spots[kind].Water;
                SetWaterBiome(kind);
            }
            else CurrentMode = Mode.Fishing;
            ZoomOffset = zoom;
            snapCamera = true;
            Visual(0f);
        }

        // Which primitive stand-ins a sea can drop because a model replaces them.
        bool HasModel(string name) => ArtLibrary.Get()?.M(name) != null;
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageSeaLayout.cs
========================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        // Permanent open water along the entire fore/aft axis. Reserve for future
        // boats as well as today's hull; never decorate the centre or either end.
        public const float FishingWaterHalfWidth = 4.8f;

        void OrientFishingCoast(GameObject environment, Transform parent)
        {
            var t = environment.transform;
            var pivot = State.HullDef.Center;
            var turn = Quaternion.Euler(0, 90, 0);
            t.localPosition = pivot + turn * (t.localPosition - pivot);
            t.localRotation = turn * t.localRotation;
        }

        void ReserveFishingWater(Transform root)
        {
            // Include piers, piles, props, rocks and shallow obstructions. The old
            // terrain-name filter missed the pier projecting into the boat.
            float nearest = float.PositiveInfinity;
            float surface = WaterY - .2f;
            foreach (var mf in root.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
            {
                if (mf.sharedMesh == null) continue;
                if (!mf.sharedMesh.isReadable)
                {
                    // Future art must still fail safe in a standalone player.
                    // Bounds are conservative; delivered maps retain readable meshes
                    // for the more precise clipped-surface check below.
                    var bounds = mf.sharedMesh.bounds;
                    float low = float.PositiveInfinity, high = float.NegativeInfinity;
                    var transform = root.worldToLocalMatrix * mf.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                    for (int corner = 0; corner < 8; corner++)
                    {
                        var point = transform.MultiplyPoint3x4(bounds.center + Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents,
                            new Vector3((corner & 1) == 0 ? -1 : 1, (corner & 2) == 0 ? -1 : 1, (corner & 4) == 0 ? -1 : 1)));
                        low = Mathf.Min(low, point.z); high = Mathf.Max(high, point.y);
                    }
                    if (high >= surface) nearest = Mathf.Min(nearest, low);
                    continue;
                }
                var vertices = mf.sharedMesh.vertices;
                var points = new Vector3[vertices.Length];
                var matrix = root.worldToLocalMatrix * mf.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                for (int i = 0; i < vertices.Length; i++)
                {
                    points[i] = matrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(vertices[i]);
                    if (points[i].y >= surface) nearest = Mathf.Min(nearest, points[i].z);
                }
                // A shore/pile triangle can cross the surface between vertices.
                // Clip its edges too, so submerged vertices cannot hide an intrusion.
                var triangles = mf.sharedMesh.triangles;
                for (int i = 0; i < triangles.Length; i += 3)
                    for (int edge = 0; edge < 3; edge++)
                    {
                        var a = points[triangles[i + edge]];
                        var b = points[triangles[i + (edge + 1) % 3]];
                        if ((a.y < surface) == (b.y < surface)) continue;
                        float z = Mathf.Lerp(a.z, b.z, (surface - a.y) / (b.y - a.y));
                        nearest = Mathf.Min(nearest, z);
                    }
            }
            if (float.IsInfinity(nearest)) return;
            float halfWidth = Mathf.Max(FishingWaterHalfWidth, State.HullDef.Height * State.HullDef.CellSize * .5f + 2f);
            float shift = Mathf.Max(0, State.HullDef.Center.z + halfWidth - nearest);
            // Move the complete diorama together: dock furniture stays on its pier.
            foreach (Transform child in root) child.localPosition += Vector3.forward * shift;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageStorage.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Linq;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        // Sum actual cargo, never a rounded HUD value or a separately cached counter.
        public float HoldKg => (float)Cooler.Sum(f => (double)f.Kg);
        public bool HoldFull => Cooler.Sum(f => (double)f.Kg) >= State.CoolerCapacity;
        public bool TryStore(CaughtFish fish)
        {
            if (fish == null || float.IsNaN(fish.Kg) || float.IsInfinity(fish.Kg) || fish.Kg <= 0 || HoldFull) return false;
            // Unity callbacks execute sequentially. Recheck on every individual insert,
            // including two workers finishing their animations on the same frame.
            Cooler.Add(fish);
            return true;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Game/VoyageTravel.cs
========================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Game
{
    public partial class VoyageGame
    {
        // Boat stays in the camera's small stage; scenery travels along the chart bearing.
        public static Vector3 RouteDirection(int spot) => (spot switch
        {
            1 => new Vector3(-.72f, 0, -.70f),
            2 => new Vector3(.72f, 0, -.70f),
            3 => new Vector3(-.98f, 0, -.20f),
            4 => new Vector3(.20f, 0, -.98f),
            _ => new Vector3(-.25f, 0, -.97f)
        }).normalized;

        public float TravelProgress => Mathf.Clamp01(modeTimer / Mathf.Max(.1f, modeDuration));
        public string TravelStage => CurrentMode == Mode.SailingOut
            ? TravelProgress < .18f ? "Casting off · clearing the quay" : TravelProgress < .34f ? "Turning toward the channel" : TravelProgress < .82f ? "Following the coast" : "Settling at the fishing ground"
            : TravelProgress < .15f ? "Turning for Gull Harbour" : TravelProgress < .72f ? "Following the channel home" : TravelProgress < .86f ? "Lining up alongside" : "Sliding gently into the berth";

        Vector3 travelOffset;
        float travelYaw, travelWake;
        static float Segment(float u, float start, float end) => Mathf.SmoothStep(0, 1, Mathf.InverseLerp(start, end, u));

        void UpdateTravel()
        {
            float u = TravelProgress;
            bool home = CurrentMode == Mode.SailingHome;
            var direction = RouteDirection(ActiveSpot) * (home ? -1f : 1f);
            float heading = Mathf.Atan2(direction.z, -direction.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;
            float cruise = home ? Segment(u, .15f, .72f) : Segment(u, .34f, .82f);
            // Both sets flow past the stern. A fixed camera-up arrival vector
            // made the world move toward the bow and read as travelling backwards.
            if (oldScenery != null) oldScenery.localPosition = -direction * (30f * cruise);
            scenery.localPosition = direction * (30f * (1f - cruise));
            float clear = home ? 1f - Segment(u, .86f, 1f) : Segment(u, 0, .18f) * (1f - Segment(u, .62f, .82f));
            travelOffset = Vector3.back * (1.35f * clear);
            float turn = home ? Segment(u, 0, .15f) * (1f - Segment(u, .72f, .86f))
                              : Segment(u, .18f, .34f) * (1f - Segment(u, .82f, 1f));
            travelYaw = Mathf.LerpAngle(0, heading, turn);
            travelWake = Mathf.Sin(cruise * Mathf.PI);
            seaMat.color = Color.Lerp(seaFrom, seaTo, cruise);
        }
    }
}

========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Prototype/DeckGrid.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Prototype
{
    // Walkable tile grid of the deck. Tile (x, y) sits at boat-local (x, 0, y).
    public class DeckGrid
    {
        public readonly int Width, Height;
        public readonly float CellSize;
        readonly bool[] blocked;

        static readonly Vector2Int[] Dirs =
        {
            new(1, 0), new(-1, 0), new(0, 1), new(0, -1),
            new(1, 1), new(1, -1), new(-1, 1), new(-1, -1),
        };

        public DeckGrid(int width, int height, float cellSize = 1f)
        {
            Width = width;
            Height = height;
            CellSize = cellSize;
            blocked = new bool[width * height];
        }

        public bool InBounds(Vector2Int t) => t.x >= 0 && t.y >= 0 && t.x < Width && t.y < Height;
        public bool IsBlocked(Vector2Int t) => !InBounds(t) || blocked[t.y * Width + t.x];

        public void Block(RectInt r)
        {
            foreach (var t in r.allPositionsWithin) blocked[t.y * Width + t.x] = true;
        }

        public static Vector3 ToLocal(Vector2Int t) => new(t.x, 0f, t.y);
        public static Vector2Int ToTile(Vector3 local) => new(Mathf.RoundToInt(local.x), Mathf.RoundToInt(local.z));
        public Vector3 GetLocal(Vector2Int t) => ToLocal(t) * CellSize;
        public Vector2Int GetTile(Vector3 local) => ToTile(local / CellSize);

        // Breadth-first search with diagonal moves that never cut a blocked corner.
        // Returns the tiles to walk (excluding the start), or null when unreachable.
        public List<Vector2Int> FindPath(Vector2Int from, Vector2Int to)
        {
            var result = new List<Vector2Int>();
            if (from == to) return result;
            if (IsBlocked(to)) return null;

            var came = new Dictionary<Vector2Int, Vector2Int> { [from] = from };
            var queue = new Queue<Vector2Int>();
            queue.Enqueue(from);
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                var c = queue.Dequeue();
                if (c == to) break;
                foreach (var d in Dirs)
                {
                    var n = c + d;
                    if (IsBlocked(n) || came.ContainsKey(n)) continue;
                    if (d.x != 0 && d.y != 0 && (IsBlocked(new Vector2Int(c.x + d.x, c.y)) || IsBlocked(new Vector2Int(c.x, c.y + d.y)))) continue;
                    came[n] = c;
                    queue.Enqueue(n);
                }
            }
            if (!came.ContainsKey(to)) return null;
            for (var c = to; c != from; c = came[c]) result.Add(c);
            result.Reverse();
            return result;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Prototype/DeckPrototype.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;

namespace Catchline.Prototype
{
    // Blocky deck prototype: a 10x5 day-boat deck, five Crab Coast stations, a captain
    // and two deckhands. Everything is built from primitives at startup, so the scene
    // only needs this one component.
    //
    // Controls: tap a person to select them, tap a station to assign them to it,
    // tap the bare deck to make them a free deckhand (carries and fills gaps).
    public class DeckPrototype : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const int TotalPots = 10;
        public const int CrabPrice = 6;
        public const int HireCost = 150;
        public const int MaxCrew = 5;

        public DeckGrid Grid { get; private set; }
        public Transform Boat { get; private set; }
        public Camera Cam { get; private set; }
        public readonly List<Station> Stations = new();
        public readonly List<Worker> Workers = new();

        public int PotsInWater = TotalPots;
        public int PotsBeingHauled;
        public int Money = 100;
        public int CrabsDelivered;
        public float SimTime;
        public float Speed = 1f;

        readonly System.Random rng = new(42);
        readonly Queue<float> deliveryTimes = new();
        readonly List<GameObject> buoys = new();
        Station hauler, sorter, bander, tank, rack;
        Transform tankWater;
        Worker selected;
        string toast;
        float toastUntil;
        bool built;

        static readonly Vector2Int[] RestTiles = { new(4, 3), new(3, 3), new(7, 3), new(7, 1), new(2, 2) };
        static readonly string[] HireNames = { "Ana", "Bram", "Kofi" };
        static readonly Color[] HireSuits = { new(0.25f, 0.6f, 0.35f), new(0.55f, 0.3f, 0.6f), new(0.3f, 0.45f, 0.8f) };

        void Awake() => Build();

        void Update()
        {
            if (!built) return;
            Tick(Time.deltaTime * Speed);
            HandleInput();
        }

        // ---- world ----------------------------------------------------------------

        public void Build()
        {
            if (built) return;
            built = true;
            Grid = new DeckGrid(10, 5);
            BuildWorld();
            BuildStations();
            BuildCrew();
        }

        void BuildWorld()
        {
            var sea = new Color(0.13f, 0.56f, 0.72f);
            var deckCenter = new Vector3(4.5f, 0f, 2f);

            var camGo = new GameObject("Camera") { tag = "MainCamera" };
            Cam = camGo.AddComponent<Camera>();
            Cam.orthographic = true;
            Cam.orthographicSize = 4.4f;
            Cam.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
            Cam.backgroundColor = sea;
            Cam.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
            Cam.farClipPlane = 100f;
            camGo.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(48f, 28f, 0f);
            camGo.transform.position = deckCenter + new Vector3(0.6f, 0f, 0.4f) - camGo.transform.forward * 30f;

            var sun = new GameObject("Sun").AddComponent<Light>();
            sun.type = LightType.Directional;
            sun.intensity = 1.25f;
            sun.color = new Color(1f, 0.96f, 0.88f);
            sun.shadows = LightShadows.Soft;
            sun.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(55f, -35f, 0f);
            RenderSettings.ambientMode = UnityEngine.Rendering.AmbientMode.Flat;
            RenderSettings.ambientLight = new Color(0.55f, 0.62f, 0.7f);

            var world = new GameObject("World").transform;
            Visuals.Box("Sea", world, new Vector3(5f, -0.6f, 2f), new Vector3(120f, 0.1f, 120f), sea);
            for (int i = 0; i < TotalPots; i++)
            {
                var b = Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "Buoy", world,
                    new Vector3(0.6f + i * 0.9f, -0.5f, -2.0f - (i % 2) * 0.7f), Vector3.one * 0.24f, new Color(1f, 0.45f, 0.15f));
                buoys.Add(b);
            }

            Boat = new GameObject("Boat").transform;
            var navy = new Color(0.12f, 0.24f, 0.48f);
            var woodA = new Color(0.74f, 0.56f, 0.36f);
            var woodB = new Color(0.69f, 0.51f, 0.32f);
            var rail = new Color(0.93f, 0.93f, 0.9f);

            // Hull, stripe and a pointed bow made from a cube turned 45 degrees.
            Visuals.Box("Hull", Boat, new Vector3(4.5f, -0.42f, 2f), new Vector3(10.6f, 0.75f, 5.6f), navy);
            Visuals.Box("Stripe", Boat, new Vector3(4.5f, -0.24f, 2f), new Vector3(10.64f, 0.08f, 5.64f), new Color(0.88f, 0.36f, 0.2f));
            float bowSize = 5.6f / Mathf.Sqrt(2f);
            var bow = Visuals.Box("Bow", Boat, new Vector3(9.8f, -0.42f, 2f), new Vector3(bowSize, 0.75f, bowSize), navy);
            bow.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 45f, 0f);
            var bowDeck = Visuals.Box("BowDeck", Boat, new Vector3(9.8f, -0.05f, 2f), new Vector3(bowSize - 0.3f, 0.1f, bowSize - 0.3f), woodB);
            bowDeck.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 45f, 0f);

            for (int x = 0; x < Grid.Width; x++)
                for (int y = 0; y < Grid.Height; y++)
                    Visuals.Box($"Tile {x},{y}", Boat, new Vector3(x, -0.05f, y), new Vector3(0.98f, 0.1f, 0.98f), (x + y) % 2 == 0 ? woodA : woodB);

            var floor = new GameObject("DeckFloor");
            floor.transform.SetParent(Boat, false);
            var fc = floor.AddComponent<BoxCollider>();
            fc.center = new Vector3(4.5f, -0.05f, 2f);
            fc.size = new Vector3(10f, 0.1f, 5f);
            floor.AddComponent<Clickable>().Deck = true;

            // Rails, with a gap at the pot hauler on the port side.
            Visuals.Box("RailPortAft", Boat, new Vector3(0f, 0.15f, -0.46f), new Vector3(1f, 0.3f, 0.08f), rail);
            Visuals.Box("RailPortFwd", Boat, new Vector3(5.65f, 0.15f, -0.46f), new Vector3(8.3f, 0.3f, 0.08f), rail);
            Visuals.Box("RailStarboard", Boat, new Vector3(4.65f, 0.15f, 4.46f), new Vector3(10.3f, 0.3f, 0.08f), rail);
            Visuals.Box("RailStern", Boat, new Vector3(-0.46f, 0.15f, 2f), new Vector3(0.08f, 0.3f, 5f), rail);

            // Wheelhouse at the bow, 2x3 tiles.
            var house = new RectInt(8, 1, 2, 3);
            Grid.Block(house);
            Visuals.Box("Wheelhouse", Boat, new Vector3(8.5f, 0.8f, 2f), new Vector3(1.9f, 1.6f, 2.9f), new Color(0.95f, 0.95f, 0.93f));
            Visuals.Box("Roof", Boat, new Vector3(8.5f, 1.66f, 2f), new Vector3(2.1f, 0.12f, 3.1f), new Color(0.9f, 0.35f, 0.22f));
            Visuals.Box("Windows", Boat, new Vector3(7.54f, 1.1f, 2f), new Vector3(0.04f, 0.35f, 2.4f), new Color(0.2f, 0.3f, 0.4f));
            Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "Mast", Boat, new Vector3(8.8f, 2.4f, 2f), new Vector3(0.08f, 0.7f, 0.08f), rail);
        }

        Station AddStation(string name, StationKind kind, RectInt footprint, Vector2Int access, float workTime, float height, Color color)
        {
            var s = new Station(name, kind, footprint, access, workTime, height);
            Grid.Block(footprint);
            s.BuildVisual(Boat, color);
            Stations.Add(s);
            return s;
        }

        void BuildStations()
        {
            hauler = AddStation("Pot hauler", StationKind.Hauler, new RectInt(1, 0, 1, 1), new Vector2Int(1, 1), 3.5f, 0.45f, new Color(0.95f, 0.55f, 0.15f));
            hauler.AddOutput(ItemType.FullPot, 4, new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0.05f));
            Visuals.Box("Davit", hauler.Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.9f, -0.35f), new Vector3(0.12f, 0.9f, 0.12f), new Color(0.95f, 0.55f, 0.15f));

            sorter = AddStation("Sorting table", StationKind.SortTable, new RectInt(3, 2, 2, 1), new Vector2Int(3, 1), 2.5f, 0.55f, new Color(0.62f, 0.66f, 0.7f));
            sorter.AddInput(ItemType.FullPot, 4, new Vector3(-0.5f, 0f, 0f));
            sorter.AddOutput(ItemType.Crab, 8, new Vector3(0.45f, 0f, -0.15f));
            sorter.AddOutput(ItemType.EmptyPot, 4, new Vector3(0.45f, 0f, 0.28f));

            bander = AddStation("Banding bench", StationKind.BandingBench, new RectInt(6, 2, 1, 1), new Vector2Int(6, 1), 1.2f, 0.5f, new Color(0.35f, 0.62f, 0.4f));
            bander.AddInput(ItemType.Crab, 8, new Vector3(-0.2f, 0f, 0.1f));
            bander.AddOutput(ItemType.BandedCrab, 6, new Vector3(0.22f, 0f, 0.1f));

            tank = AddStation("Live tank", StationKind.LiveTank, new RectInt(5, 4, 2, 1), new Vector2Int(6, 3), 0f, 0.7f, new Color(0.22f, 0.45f, 0.75f));
            tank.AddInput(ItemType.BandedCrab, 30, null);
            tankWater = Visuals.Box("Water", tank.Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.7f, 0f), new Vector3(1.7f, 0.02f, 0.7f), new Color(0.35f, 0.75f, 0.9f)).transform;

            rack = AddStation("Pot rack", StationKind.PotRack, new RectInt(1, 4, 2, 1), new Vector2Int(1, 3), 1.5f, 0.2f, new Color(0.5f, 0.38f, 0.25f));
            rack.AddInput(ItemType.EmptyPot, 6, new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0f));

            hauler.Routes[ItemType.FullPot] = sorter;
            sorter.Routes[ItemType.Crab] = bander;
            sorter.Routes[ItemType.EmptyPot] = rack;
            bander.Routes[ItemType.BandedCrab] = tank;
        }

        void BuildCrew()
        {
            var captain = AddWorker("Captain", true, new Color(0.15f, 0.2f, 0.4f), Color.white);
            captain.WorkSpeed = 1.3f;
            AddWorker("Sam", false, new Color(0.98f, 0.8f, 0.2f), new Color(0.95f, 0.45f, 0.15f)).Assigned = hauler;
            AddWorker("Jo", false, new Color(0.95f, 0.5f, 0.15f), new Color(0.2f, 0.4f, 0.75f)).Assigned = sorter;
            Select(captain);
        }

        Worker AddWorker(string name, bool captain, Color suit, Color hat)
        {
            int index = Workers.Count;
            var w = new Worker(name, captain, index);
            w.BuildVisual(Boat, RestTiles[index % RestTiles.Length], suit, hat);
            Workers.Add(w);
            return w;
        }

        // ---- simulation -----------------------------------------------------------

        public void Tick(float dt)
        {
            if (!built) return;
            // Fixed small steps keep 4x speed stable.
            while (dt > 0f)
            {
                float step = Mathf.Min(dt, 0.05f);
                dt -= step;
                SimTime += step;
                foreach (var w in Workers) w.Tick(this, step);
                var face = Cam.transform.rotation;
                foreach (var s in Stations) s.Tick(this, step, face);
            }

            Boat.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(Mathf.Sin(SimTime * 0.53f) * 0.5f, 0f, Mathf.Sin(SimTime * 0.8f) * 0.8f);
            for (int i = 0; i < buoys.Count; i++)
            {
                buoys[i].SetActive(i < PotsInWater);
                var p = buoys[i].transform.localPosition;
                buoys[i].transform.localPosition = new Vector3(p.x, -0.5f + Mathf.Sin(SimTime * 1.7f + i) * 0.04f, p.z);
            }
            var t = tank.Inputs[ItemType.BandedCrab];
            float level = Mathf.Max(0.02f, 0.6f * t.Fill);
            tankWater.localScale = new Vector3(1.7f, level, 0.7f);
            tankWater.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, 0.1f + level / 2f, 0f);
            while (deliveryTimes.Count > 0 && deliveryTimes.Peek() < SimTime - 60f) deliveryTimes.Dequeue();
        }

        public int RollKeepers()
        {
            // Shore crab: plenty undersized, so 0-3 keepers per pot, 1.5 on average.
            int r = rng.Next(100);
            return r < 15 ? 0 : r < 50 ? 1 : r < 85 ? 2 : 3;
        }

        public void OnDelivered(Station dest, ItemType item)
        {
            if (dest != tank) return;
            CrabsDelivered++;
            deliveryTimes.Enqueue(SimTime);
        }

        public float CrabsPerMinute => deliveryTimes.Count * 60f / Mathf.Clamp(SimTime, 1f, 60f);

        // Picks the next job for an idle worker.
        public void AssignJob(Worker w)
        {
            if (w.Assigned != null)
            {
                var s = w.Assigned;
                if (s.HasWork && s.Operator == null && s.CanStartWork(this))
                {
                    w.StartWork(this, s);
                    return;
                }
                var c = BestCarry(s);
                if (c.from != null)
                {
                    w.StartCarry(this, c.from, c.to, c.type);
                    return;
                }
                if (w.Tile != s.Access) w.StartIdle(this, s.Access);
                else w.Wait(0.3f);
                return;
            }

            // Free deckhand: carry the fullest pile, or run a station nobody is assigned to.
            var carry = BestCarry(null);
            Station bestWork = null;
            float workScore = -1f;
            foreach (var s in Stations)
            {
                if (!s.HasWork || s.Operator != null || !s.CanStartWork(this) || HasAssignee(s)) continue;
                float score = s.WorkScore(this);
                if (score > workScore)
                {
                    workScore = score;
                    bestWork = s;
                }
            }

            if (carry.from != null && (bestWork == null || carry.score - 0.15f >= 0.5f && carry.score >= workScore)) w.StartCarry(this, carry.from, carry.to, carry.type);
            else if (bestWork != null) w.StartWork(this, bestWork);
            else
            {
                var rest = RestTiles[Workers.IndexOf(w) % RestTiles.Length];
                if (w.Tile != rest) w.StartIdle(this, rest);
                else w.Wait(0.4f);
            }
        }

        bool HasAssignee(Station s)
        {
            foreach (var o in Workers)
                if (o.Assigned == s) return true;
            return false;
        }

        (Station from, Station to, ItemType type, float score) BestCarry(Station onlyFrom)
        {
            (Station, Station, ItemType, float) best = (null, null, default, -1f);
            foreach (var s in Stations)
            {
                if (onlyFrom != null && s != onlyFrom) continue;
                foreach (var route in s.Routes)
                {
                    var src = s.Outputs[route.Key];
                    var dst = route.Value.Inputs[route.Key];
                    if (src.Available <= 0 || dst.Space <= 0) continue;
                    float score = src.Fill + 0.15f;
                    if (score > best.Item4) best = (s, route.Value, route.Key, score);
                }
            }
            return best;
        }

        // ---- player actions ---------------------------------------------------------

        void Select(Worker w)
        {
            if (selected != null) selected.SetSelected(false);
            selected = w;
            if (selected != null) selected.SetSelected(true);
        }

        void Assign(Worker w, Station s)
        {
            if (s != null && !s.HasWork)
            {
                Toast("The tank has no job of its own: free deckhands carry crabs into it.");
                return;
            }
            w.Assigned = s;
            Toast(s == null ? $"{w.Name} is now a free deckhand." : $"{w.Name} now runs the {s.Name.ToLower()}.");
        }

        void SellCatch()
        {
            var t = tank.Inputs[ItemType.BandedCrab];
            if (t.Count == 0)
            {
                Toast("The tank is empty.");
                return;
            }
            int earned = t.Count * CrabPrice;
            Money += earned;
            Toast($"Sold {t.Count} crabs for €{earned}.");
            t.Clear();
        }

        void Hire()
        {
            if (Workers.Count >= MaxCrew) { Toast("The deck is full."); return; }
            if (Money < HireCost) { Toast($"Hiring costs €{HireCost}."); return; }
            Money -= HireCost;
            int i = Workers.Count - 3;
            var w = AddWorker(HireNames[i], false, HireSuits[i], new Color(0.9f, 0.2f, 0.25f));
            Select(w);
            Toast($"{w.Name} joined as a free deckhand. Tap a station to give them a job.");
        }

        // Test hook: staffs the banding bench and pot rack without paying.
        public void StaffFully()
        {
            AddWorker(HireNames[0], false, HireSuits[0], new Color(0.9f, 0.2f, 0.25f)).Assigned = bander;
            AddWorker(HireNames[1], false, HireSuits[1], new Color(0.9f, 0.2f, 0.25f)).Assigned = rack;
        }

        void Toast(string message)
        {
            toast = message;
            toastUntil = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 3.5f;
        }

        // ---- input -------------------------------------------------------------------

        readonly List<Rect> hudRects = new();
        float GuiScale => Screen.height / 720f;

        void HandleInput()
        {
            var mouse = Mouse.current;
            if (mouse != null)
            {
                float scroll = mouse.scroll.ReadValue().y;
                if (Mathf.Abs(scroll) > 0.01f) Cam.orthographicSize = Mathf.Clamp(Cam.orthographicSize - Mathf.Sign(scroll) * 0.4f, 2.5f, 8f);
            }

            var pointer = Pointer.current;
            if (pointer == null || !pointer.press.wasReleasedThisFrame) return;
            var screen = pointer.position.ReadValue();
            var gui = new Vector2(screen.x / GuiScale, (Screen.height - screen.y) / GuiScale);
            foreach (var r in hudRects)
                if (r.Contains(gui)) return;

            if (!Physics.Raycast(Cam.ScreenPointToRay(screen), out var hit, 200f)) return;
            var c = hit.collider.GetComponent<Clickable>();
            if (c == null) return;
            if (c.Worker != null) Select(c.Worker);
            else if (c.Station != null && selected != null) Assign(selected, c.Station);
            else if (c.Deck && selected != null) Assign(selected, null);
        }

        // ---- HUD (IMGUI, prototype only) ------------------------------------------

        GUIStyle box, big, small, button;

        void OnGUI()
        {
            if (!built) return;
            if (box == null)
            {
                box = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.box) { alignment = TextAnchor.UpperLeft, padding = new RectOffset(12, 12, 10, 10) };
                big = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label) { fontSize = 22, fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold };
                small = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label) { fontSize = 15, wordWrap = true };
                button = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.button) { fontSize = 15 };
            }
            GUI.matrix = Matrix4x4.Scale(new Vector3(GuiScale, GuiScale, 1f));
            hudRects.Clear();

            var stats = new Rect(12, 12, 300, 238);
            hudRects.Add(stats);
            GUI.Box(stats, GUIContent.none, box);
            GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(stats.x + 12, stats.y + 8, stats.width - 24, stats.height - 16));
            GUILayout.Label($"€{Money}", big);
            var t = tank.Inputs[ItemType.BandedCrab];
            GUILayout.Label($"Live tank {t.Count}/{t.Capacity}   Pots in water {PotsInWater}/{TotalPots}", small);
            GUILayout.Label($"Crabs per minute {CrabsPerMinute:0.0}   Total {CrabsDelivered}", small);
            GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
            foreach (var sp in new[] { 1f, 2f, 4f })
                if (GUILayout.Toggle(Mathf.Approximately(Speed, sp), $"{sp}x", button)) Speed = sp;
            GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            if (GUILayout.Button($"Sell catch (€{CrabPrice} per crab)", button)) SellCatch();
            if (GUILayout.Button($"Hire deckhand (€{HireCost})", button)) Hire();
            GUILayout.EndArea();

            float vw = Screen.width / GuiScale;
            var crew = new Rect(vw - 312, 12, 300, 30 + Workers.Count * 40);
            hudRects.Add(crew);
            GUI.Box(crew, GUIContent.none, box);
            GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(crew.x + 12, crew.y + 8, crew.width - 24, crew.height - 16));
            foreach (var w in Workers)
            {
                string job = w.Assigned != null ? w.Assigned.Name : "free deckhand";
                if (GUILayout.Button($"{(w == selected ? "> " : "")}{w.Name}: {job}\n{w.StateText}", small)) Select(w);
            }
            GUILayout.EndArea();

            var help = new Rect(12, 720 - 74, 620, 62);
            hudRects.Add(help);
            GUI.Box(help, GUIContent.none, box);
            GUI.Label(new Rect(help.x + 12, help.y + 6, help.width - 24, help.height - 12),
                "Tap a person to select them, then tap a station to give them that job. Tap the bare deck to make them a free deckhand. Scroll to zoom.", small);

            if (toast != null && Time.realtimeSinceStartup < toastUntil)
            {
                var r = new Rect(vw / 2 - 260, 14, 520, 40);
                GUI.Box(r, GUIContent.none, box);
                GUI.Label(new Rect(r.x + 12, r.y + 9, r.width - 24, 26), toast, small);
            }
        }

        // ---- diagnostics -------------------------------------------------------------

        public string Report()
        {
            var sb = new StringBuilder();
            sb.AppendLine($"t={SimTime:0}s  crabs delivered={CrabsDelivered}  per-min(last 60s)={CrabsPerMinute:0.0}  pots in water={PotsInWater}  money=€{Money}");
            foreach (var s in Stations) sb.AppendLine("  " + s.Report(SimTime));
            foreach (var w in Workers) sb.AppendLine($"  {w.Name}: {(w.Assigned != null ? w.Assigned.Name : "free")} - {w.StateText}");
            return sb.ToString();
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Prototype/ItemBuffer.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Prototype
{
    // A pile of one item type at a station. Reserved and Incoming are promises made
    // by workers on their way, so two workers never grab the same crab or the last free slot.
    public class ItemBuffer
    {
        public readonly ItemType Type;
        public readonly int Capacity;
        public int Count { get; private set; }
        public int Reserved;
        public int Incoming;

        public int Available => Count - Reserved;
        public int Space => Capacity - Count - Incoming;
        public float Fill => (float)Count / Capacity;

        readonly Transform anchor;
        readonly List<GameObject> pile = new();

        public ItemBuffer(ItemType type, int capacity, Transform anchor)
        {
            Type = type;
            Capacity = capacity;
            this.anchor = anchor;
        }

        // A worker picks up an item they reserved.
        public void Take() { Reserved--; Count--; Refresh(); }
        // A worker drops an item into a slot they reserved.
        public void Deliver() { Incoming--; Count++; Refresh(); }
        // Station work consumes a reserved input.
        public void Consume() { Reserved--; Count--; Refresh(); }
        // Station work produces n items into space it reserved for `promised`.
        public void Produce(int n, int promised) { Incoming -= promised; Count = Mathf.Min(Capacity, Count + n); Refresh(); }

        public void Clear() { Count = 0; Reserved = 0; Refresh(); }

        void Refresh()
        {
            if (anchor == null) return;
            while (pile.Count < Count) pile.Add(Spawn(pile.Count));
            while (pile.Count > Count)
            {
                Visuals.Kill(pile[^1]);
                pile.RemoveAt(pile.Count - 1);
            }
        }

        GameObject Spawn(int i)
        {
            var size = Items.SizeOf(Type);
            int per = Items.PerLayer(Type);
            int layer = i / per, slot = i % per;
            var pos = new Vector3(0f, layer * (size.y + 0.01f) + size.y / 2f, 0f);
            if (per == 4)
            {
                pos.x = ((slot % 2) - 0.5f) * (size.x + 0.03f);
                pos.z = ((slot / 2) - 0.5f) * (size.z + 0.03f);
            }
            return Visuals.Box(Type.ToString(), anchor, pos, size, Items.ColorOf(Type));
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Prototype/Items.cs
========================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Prototype
{
    public enum ItemType { FullPot, EmptyPot, Crab, BandedCrab }

    // Look of each item when it sits in a pile or is carried.
    public static class Items
    {
        public static Color ColorOf(ItemType t) => t switch
        {
            ItemType.FullPot => new Color(0.42f, 0.28f, 0.16f),
            ItemType.EmptyPot => new Color(0.66f, 0.54f, 0.38f),
            ItemType.Crab => new Color(0.86f, 0.28f, 0.18f),
            _ => new Color(0.98f, 0.62f, 0.12f),
        };

        public static Vector3 SizeOf(ItemType t) => t switch
        {
            ItemType.FullPot or ItemType.EmptyPot => new Vector3(0.34f, 0.24f, 0.34f),
            _ => new Vector3(0.2f, 0.09f, 0.16f),
        };

        // Items per layer in a pile: pots stack straight up, crabs spread 2x2.
        public static int PerLayer(ItemType t) => t is ItemType.FullPot or ItemType.EmptyPot ? 1 : 4;
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Prototype/Station.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Prototype
{
    public enum StationKind { Hauler, SortTable, BandingBench, LiveTank, PotRack }

    // A machine or work spot on deck. One worker at a time can run it; items arrive in
    // input piles and leave from output piles, carried by crew along Routes.
    public class Station
    {
        public readonly string Name;
        public readonly StationKind Kind;
        public readonly RectInt Footprint;
        public readonly Vector2Int Access;
        public readonly float WorkTime;
        public readonly float Height;

        public readonly Dictionary<ItemType, ItemBuffer> Inputs = new();
        public readonly Dictionary<ItemType, ItemBuffer> Outputs = new();
        public readonly Dictionary<ItemType, Station> Routes = new();

        public Worker Operator;
        public float Progress;
        public float BusyTime, BlockedTime, StarvedTime;

        public Transform Root { get; private set; }
        TextMesh label;
        Renderer statusLight;
        Transform progressFill;

        // The sorting table reserves room for the biggest possible catch from one pot.
        const int MaxKeepersPerPot = 3;

        public Station(string name, StationKind kind, RectInt footprint, Vector2Int access, float workTime, float height)
        {
            Name = name;
            Kind = kind;
            Footprint = footprint;
            Access = access;
            WorkTime = workTime;
            Height = height;
        }

        public Vector3 Center => new(Footprint.x + (Footprint.width - 1) / 2f, 0f, Footprint.y + (Footprint.height - 1) / 2f);

        public void BuildVisual(Transform boat, Color color)
        {
            Root = new GameObject(Name).transform;
            Root.SetParent(boat, false);
            Root.localPosition = Center;

            var size = new Vector3(Footprint.width - 0.12f, Height, Footprint.height - 0.12f);
            var body = Visuals.Box("Body", Root, new Vector3(0f, Height / 2f, 0f), size, color, keepCollider: true);
            body.AddComponent<Clickable>().Station = this;

            statusLight = Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "Status", Root,
                new Vector3(size.x / 2f - 0.08f, Height + 0.06f, -size.z / 2f + 0.08f), Vector3.one * 0.12f, Color.gray).GetComponent<Renderer>();

            if (Kind != StationKind.LiveTank)
            {
                Visuals.Box("ProgressBack", Root, new Vector3(0f, Height + 0.55f, -0.45f), new Vector3(0.8f, 0.06f, 0.06f), new Color(0.15f, 0.15f, 0.18f));
                progressFill = Visuals.Box("ProgressFill", Root, new Vector3(-0.4f, Height + 0.55f, -0.47f), new Vector3(0f, 0.07f, 0.07f), new Color(0.3f, 0.9f, 0.4f)).transform;
            }

            label = Visuals.Label("Label", Root, new Vector3(0f, Height + 0.7f, 0f), 0.034f);
        }

        public Transform AddAnchor(Vector3 offset)
        {
            var a = new GameObject("Pile").transform;
            a.SetParent(Root, false);
            a.localPosition = new Vector3(offset.x, Height, offset.z);
            return a;
        }

        public void AddInput(ItemType t, int capacity, Vector3? pileOffset)
            => Inputs[t] = new ItemBuffer(t, capacity, pileOffset.HasValue ? AddAnchor(pileOffset.Value) : null);

        public void AddOutput(ItemType t, int capacity, Vector3 pileOffset)
            => Outputs[t] = new ItemBuffer(t, capacity, AddAnchor(pileOffset));

        // ---- work rules -------------------------------------------------------

        public bool HasWork => Kind != StationKind.LiveTank;

        public bool CanStartWork(DeckPrototype deck) => Kind switch
        {
            StationKind.Hauler => deck.PotsInWater - deck.PotsBeingHauled > 0 && Outputs[ItemType.FullPot].Space >= 1,
            StationKind.SortTable => Inputs[ItemType.FullPot].Available >= 1
                                     && Outputs[ItemType.Crab].Space >= MaxKeepersPerPot
                                     && Outputs[ItemType.EmptyPot].Space >= 1,
            StationKind.BandingBench => Inputs[ItemType.Crab].Available >= 1 && Outputs[ItemType.BandedCrab].Space >= 1,
            StationKind.PotRack => Inputs[ItemType.EmptyPot].Available >= 1,
            _ => false,
        };

        public void ReserveWork(DeckPrototype deck)
        {
            switch (Kind)
            {
                case StationKind.Hauler:
                    deck.PotsBeingHauled++;
                    Outputs[ItemType.FullPot].Incoming++;
                    break;
                case StationKind.SortTable:
                    Inputs[ItemType.FullPot].Reserved++;
                    Outputs[ItemType.Crab].Incoming += MaxKeepersPerPot;
                    Outputs[ItemType.EmptyPot].Incoming++;
                    break;
                case StationKind.BandingBench:
                    Inputs[ItemType.Crab].Reserved++;
                    Outputs[ItemType.BandedCrab].Incoming++;
                    break;
                case StationKind.PotRack:
                    Inputs[ItemType.EmptyPot].Reserved++;
                    break;
            }
        }

        public void CompleteWork(DeckPrototype deck)
        {
            switch (Kind)
            {
                case StationKind.Hauler:
                    deck.PotsBeingHauled--;
                    deck.PotsInWater--;
                    Outputs[ItemType.FullPot].Produce(1, 1);
                    break;
                case StationKind.SortTable:
                    Inputs[ItemType.FullPot].Consume();
                    Outputs[ItemType.Crab].Produce(deck.RollKeepers(), MaxKeepersPerPot);
                    Outputs[ItemType.EmptyPot].Produce(1, 1);
                    break;
                case StationKind.BandingBench:
                    Inputs[ItemType.Crab].Consume();
                    Outputs[ItemType.BandedCrab].Produce(1, 1);
                    break;
                case StationKind.PotRack:
                    // Restacked pots are rebaited and go straight back over the side.
                    Inputs[ItemType.EmptyPot].Consume();
                    deck.PotsInWater++;
                    break;
            }
            Progress = 0f;
            Operator = null;
        }

        // Base priority for a free worker choosing between stations.
        public float WorkScore(DeckPrototype deck)
        {
            if (Kind == StationKind.Hauler) return 0.35f + 0.3f * deck.PotsInWater / DeckPrototype.TotalPots;
            float fill = 0f;
            foreach (var b in Inputs.Values) fill = Mathf.Max(fill, b.Fill);
            return 0.3f + fill;
        }

        // Free room in an output pile, ignoring space a worker has already promised.
        int Room(ItemType t) => Outputs[t].Capacity - Outputs[t].Count;

        bool OutputBlocked()
        {
            return Kind switch
            {
                StationKind.Hauler => Room(ItemType.FullPot) < 1,
                StationKind.SortTable => Room(ItemType.Crab) < MaxKeepersPerPot || Room(ItemType.EmptyPot) < 1,
                StationKind.BandingBench => Room(ItemType.BandedCrab) < 1,
                StationKind.LiveTank => Inputs[ItemType.BandedCrab].Count >= Inputs[ItemType.BandedCrab].Capacity,
                _ => false,
            };
        }

        // ---- per-frame state, stats and visuals -------------------------------

        public void Tick(DeckPrototype deck, float dt, Quaternion faceCamera)
        {
            bool working = Operator != null && Operator.IsWorkingAt(this);
            bool blocked = OutputBlocked();

            if (working) BusyTime += dt;
            else if (blocked) BlockedTime += dt;
            else StarvedTime += dt;

            string state;
            Color light, text;
            if (Kind == StationKind.LiveTank)
            {
                var tank = Inputs[ItemType.BandedCrab];
                state = blocked ? "FULL - sell catch" : $"{tank.Count}/{tank.Capacity} crabs";
                light = blocked ? new Color(0.95f, 0.25f, 0.2f) : new Color(0.3f, 0.75f, 0.95f);
                text = blocked ? new Color(1f, 0.45f, 0.4f) : Color.white;
            }
            else if (working)
            {
                state = "working";
                light = new Color(0.3f, 0.95f, 0.4f);
                text = Color.white;
            }
            else if (Operator != null)
            {
                state = "crew on the way";
                light = new Color(0.95f, 0.85f, 0.3f);
                text = Color.white;
            }
            else if (blocked)
            {
                state = "OUTPUT FULL";
                light = new Color(0.95f, 0.25f, 0.2f);
                text = new Color(1f, 0.45f, 0.4f);
            }
            else if (Kind == StationKind.Hauler && deck.PotsInWater == 0)
            {
                state = "no pots in the water";
                light = Color.gray;
                text = new Color(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.8f);
            }
            else if (CanStartWork(deck))
            {
                state = "needs a worker";
