                light = new Color(0.95f, 0.65f, 0.2f);
                text = new Color(1f, 0.8f, 0.4f);
            }
            else
            {
                state = "waiting for input";
                light = Color.gray;
                text = new Color(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.8f);
            }

            int queue = 0;
            if (Kind != StationKind.LiveTank)
                foreach (var b in Inputs.Values) queue += b.Count;
            string queueText = queue >= 2 ? $"  queue {queue}" : "";

            label.text = $"{Name}\n{state}{queueText}";
            label.color = text;
            label.transform.rotation = faceCamera;
            statusLight.sharedMaterial = Visuals.Mat(light);

            if (progressFill != null)
            {
                float p = Mathf.Clamp01(Progress);
                progressFill.localScale = new Vector3(0.8f * p, 0.07f, 0.07f);
                progressFill.localPosition = new Vector3(-0.4f + 0.4f * p, Height + 0.55f, -0.47f);
            }
        }

        public string Report(float simTime)
        {
            float t = Mathf.Max(0.001f, simTime);
            string piles = "";
            foreach (var b in Inputs.Values) piles += $" in:{b.Type}={b.Count}/{b.Capacity}";
            foreach (var b in Outputs.Values) piles += $" out:{b.Type}={b.Count}/{b.Capacity}";
            return $"{Name,-14} busy {BusyTime / t:P0}  blocked {BlockedTime / t:P0}  idle {StarvedTime / t:P0} |{piles}";
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Prototype/Visuals.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Prototype
{
    // Placeholder art: every object is a coloured primitive.
    public static class Visuals
    {
        static readonly Dictionary<Color, Material> cache = new();
        static Material baseMaterial;
        static Font font;

        public static Material Mat(Color c)
        {
            if (cache.TryGetValue(c, out var m) && m != null) return m;
            if (baseMaterial == null)
            {
                baseMaterial = new Material(Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit"));
                baseMaterial.SetFloat("_Smoothness", 0.2f);
            }
            m = new Material(baseMaterial) { color = c };
            cache[c] = m;
            return m;
        }

        public static GameObject Prim(PrimitiveType type, string name, Transform parent, Vector3 localPos, Vector3 scale, Color c, bool keepCollider = false)
        {
            var go = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(type);
            go.name = name;
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            go.transform.localPosition = localPos;
            go.transform.localScale = scale;
            go.GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial = Mat(c);
            if (!keepCollider) Kill(go.GetComponent<Collider>());
            return go;
        }

        public static GameObject Box(string name, Transform parent, Vector3 localPos, Vector3 size, Color c, bool keepCollider = false)
            => Prim(PrimitiveType.Cube, name, parent, localPos, size, c, keepCollider);

        public static TextMesh Label(string name, Transform parent, Vector3 localPos, float size)
        {
            if (font == null) font = Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
            var go = new GameObject(name);
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            go.transform.localPosition = localPos;
            var tm = go.AddComponent<TextMesh>();
            tm.font = font;
            tm.fontSize = 64;
            tm.characterSize = size;
            tm.anchor = TextAnchor.LowerCenter;
            tm.alignment = TextAlignment.Center;
            tm.color = Color.white;
            go.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>().sharedMaterial = font.material;
            return tm;
        }

        public static void Kill(Object o)
        {
            if (o == null) return;
            if (Application.isPlaying) Object.Destroy(o);
            else Object.DestroyImmediate(o);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Prototype/Worker.cs
========================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.Prototype
{
    public enum JobKind { None, Work, Carry, Idle }

    // A person on deck. The deck hands out jobs; the worker walks the grid and does them.
    public class Worker
    {
        public readonly string Name;
        public readonly bool IsCaptain;
        public Station Assigned;
        public float WalkSpeed = 2.6f;
        public float WorkSpeed = 1f;

        public Transform Root { get; private set; }
        Vector3 pos; // boat-local, on the deck surface
        readonly Vector3 standOffset;
        GameObject ring, carried;

        JobKind job;
        Station target, dest;
        ItemType item;
        int phase;
        float thinkDelay;
        float animTime;
        bool moving;
        readonly List<Vector2Int> path = new();

        public Worker(string name, bool isCaptain, int index)
        {
            Name = name;
            IsCaptain = isCaptain;
            // Small per-worker offset so two people at the same spot don't merge into one.
            standOffset = new Vector3(((index % 3) - 1) * 0.16f, 0f, ((index / 3) % 2) * 0.16f - 0.08f);
        }

        public Vector2Int Tile => DeckGrid.ToTile(pos);
        public bool IsWorkingAt(Station s) => job == JobKind.Work && phase == 1 && target == s;

        public string StateText => job switch
        {
            JobKind.Work => phase == 0 ? $"walking to {target.Name}" : $"working at {target.Name}",
            JobKind.Carry => phase == 0 ? $"fetching {item} from {target.Name}" : $"carrying {item} to {dest.Name}",
            JobKind.Idle => "moving",
            _ => "waiting",
        };

        public void BuildVisual(Transform boat, Vector2Int startTile, Color suit, Color hat)
        {
            Root = new GameObject(Name).transform;
            Root.SetParent(boat, false);
            pos = DeckGrid.ToLocal(startTile) + standOffset;
            Root.localPosition = pos;

            var col = Root.gameObject.AddComponent<CapsuleCollider>();
            col.center = new Vector3(0f, 0.5f, 0f);
            col.radius = 0.32f;
            col.height = 1.1f;
            Root.gameObject.AddComponent<Clickable>().Worker = this;

            var skin = new Color(0.96f, 0.78f, 0.62f);
            Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Capsule, "Body", Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.32f, 0f), new Vector3(0.36f, 0.32f, 0.3f), suit);
            Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "Head", Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.78f, 0f), Vector3.one * 0.3f, skin);
            if (IsCaptain)
            {
                Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "Cap", Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.92f, 0f), new Vector3(0.3f, 0.05f, 0.3f), hat);
                Visuals.Box("Brim", Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.89f, 0.15f), new Vector3(0.22f, 0.02f, 0.12f), new Color(0.1f, 0.12f, 0.2f));
            }
            else
            {
                Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "Beanie", Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.88f, 0f), new Vector3(0.31f, 0.2f, 0.31f), hat);
            }
            // Facing marker so you can read which way they're walking.
            Visuals.Box("Nose", Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.78f, 0.15f), new Vector3(0.05f, 0.05f, 0.04f), skin * 0.9f);

            ring = Visuals.Prim(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "Selected", Root, new Vector3(0f, 0.01f, 0f), new Vector3(0.75f, 0.005f, 0.75f), new Color(1f, 0.85f, 0.2f));
            ring.SetActive(false);
        }

        public void SetSelected(bool on) => ring.SetActive(on);

        // ---- jobs (started by the deck) --------------------------------------

        public void StartWork(DeckPrototype deck, Station s)
        {
            job = JobKind.Work;
            target = s;
            phase = 0;
            s.Operator = this;
            s.ReserveWork(deck);
            SetPath(deck, s.Access);
        }

        public void StartCarry(DeckPrototype deck, Station from, Station to, ItemType t)
        {
            job = JobKind.Carry;
            target = from;
            dest = to;
            item = t;
            phase = 0;
            from.Outputs[t].Reserved++;
            to.Inputs[t].Incoming++;
            SetPath(deck, from.Access);
        }

        public void StartIdle(DeckPrototype deck, Vector2Int tile)
        {
            job = JobKind.Idle;
            SetPath(deck, tile);
        }

        public void Wait(float seconds)
        {
            job = JobKind.None;
            thinkDelay = seconds;
        }

        void SetPath(DeckPrototype deck, Vector2Int goal)
        {
            path.Clear();
            var p = deck.Grid.FindPath(Tile, goal);
            if (p != null) path.AddRange(p);
        }

        // ---- per-frame --------------------------------------------------------

        public void Tick(DeckPrototype deck, float dt)
        {
            moving = false;
            switch (job)
            {
                case JobKind.None:
                    thinkDelay -= dt;
                    if (thinkDelay <= 0f) deck.AssignJob(this);
                    break;

                case JobKind.Work:
                    if (phase == 0)
                    {
                        if (Walk(dt)) phase = 1;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Face(target.Center);
                        target.Progress += dt * WorkSpeed / target.WorkTime;
                        if (target.Progress >= 1f)
                        {
                            target.CompleteWork(deck);
                            Wait(0.1f);
                        }
                    }
                    break;

                case JobKind.Carry:
                    if (phase == 0)
                    {
                        if (Walk(dt))
                        {
                            target.Outputs[item].Take();
                            ShowCarried(item);
                            phase = 1;
                            SetPath(deck, dest.Access);
                        }
                    }
                    else if (Walk(dt))
                    {
                        dest.Inputs[item].Deliver();
                        deck.OnDelivered(dest, item);
                        HideCarried();
                        Wait(0.1f);
                    }
                    break;

                case JobKind.Idle:
                    if (Walk(dt)) Wait(0.5f);
                    break;
            }
            Animate(dt);
        }

        // Moves along the path; true once the last tile is reached.
        bool Walk(float dt)
        {
            float budget = WalkSpeed * dt;
            while (path.Count > 0 && budget > 0f)
            {
                var goal = DeckGrid.ToLocal(path[0]) + standOffset;
                var delta = goal - pos;
                float dist = delta.magnitude;
                if (dist > 0.001f) Face(goal);
                moving = true;
                if (dist <= budget)
                {
                    pos = goal;
                    budget -= dist;
                    path.RemoveAt(0);
                }
                else
                {
                    pos += delta / dist * budget;
                    budget = 0f;
                }
            }
            return path.Count == 0;
        }

        void Face(Vector3 localTarget)
        {
            var d = localTarget - pos;
            d.y = 0f;
            if (d.sqrMagnitude > 0.0001f) Root.localRotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(d);
        }

        void Animate(float dt)
        {
            animTime += dt;
            float bob = 0f;
            if (moving) bob = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.Sin(animTime * 13f)) * 0.07f;
            else if (job == JobKind.Work && phase == 1) bob = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.Sin(animTime * 8f)) * 0.04f;
            Root.localPosition = pos + Vector3.up * bob;
        }

        void ShowCarried(ItemType t)
        {
            HideCarried();
            carried = Visuals.Box("Carried", Root, new Vector3(0f, 1.12f, 0f), Items.SizeOf(t) * 1.1f, Items.ColorOf(t));
        }

        void HideCarried()
        {
            if (carried != null) Visuals.Kill(carried);
            carried = null;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/ArtImport.cs
========================================================================
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Import rules for Codex's art, and the ArtLibrary asset the game loads at runtime.
    public class ArtImport : AssetPostprocessor
    {
        const string UiFolder = "Assets/Art/UI/";
        static bool rebuildQueued;

        static void OnPostprocessAllAssets(string[] imported, string[] deleted, string[] moved, string[] movedFrom)
        {
            if (rebuildQueued || !imported.Any(p => p.StartsWith("Assets/Art/Models/") && p.EndsWith(".fbx")
                || p.StartsWith("Assets/Art/Water/") && (p.EndsWith(".png") || p.EndsWith(".tif")) || p.EndsWith("/CoastalWater.shader") || p.EndsWith("/PtsWater.shader"))) return;
            rebuildQueued = true;
            EditorApplication.delayCall += () =>
            {
                rebuildQueued = false;
                BuildLibrary();
            };
        }

        // UI sprites are exported at 2x. Import them as IMGUI textures at 1x so 9-slice borders match the HUD sizes.
        void OnPreprocessTexture()
        {
            if (assetPath.StartsWith("Assets/Art/Water/PTS/"))
            {
                var water = (TextureImporter)assetImporter;
                water.textureType = TextureImporterType.Default;
                water.sRGBTexture = false;
                water.mipmapEnabled = true;
                water.wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Repeat;
                water.filterMode = FilterMode.Trilinear;
                water.maxTextureSize = 2048;
                return;
            }
            if (assetPath.StartsWith("Assets/Art/Textures/"))
            {
                var water = (TextureImporter)assetImporter;
                water.textureType = TextureImporterType.Default;
                water.textureShape = TextureImporterShape.Texture2D;
                water.wrapMode = Path.GetFileName(assetPath).StartsWith("water-tile") || assetPath.Contains("deck-planks") ? TextureWrapMode.Repeat : TextureWrapMode.Clamp;
                water.mipmapEnabled = true;
                water.alphaIsTransparency = true;
                water.filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear;
                water.maxTextureSize = 1024;
                return;
            }
            if (!assetPath.StartsWith(UiFolder)) return;
            var ti = (TextureImporter)assetImporter;
            ti.textureType = TextureImporterType.GUI;
            ti.textureShape = TextureImporterShape.Texture2D;
            ti.mipmapEnabled = false;
            ti.wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp;
            ti.filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear;
            ti.alphaIsTransparency = true;
            ti.npotScale = TextureImporterNPOTScale.None;
            ti.textureCompression = TextureImporterCompression.Uncompressed;
            string name = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(assetPath);
            ti.maxTextureSize = name == "title-backdrop" || name.StartsWith("header-") || name == "sea-chart" ? 2048
                : name == "logo-catchline" || name == "strike-water" ? 1024
                : name == "daily-gift" ? 512
                : name.StartsWith("fish-") && name != "fish-shadow" || name.StartsWith("portrait-") ? 256
                : name.StartsWith("bar-") ? 64 : name.EndsWith("-tail") ? 32 : 128;
        }

        // Models keep their materials inside the FBX (no loose Materials folders); the game remaps them
        // to its own URP materials by palette name at spawn time anyway.
        void OnPreprocessModel()
        {
            if (!assetPath.StartsWith("Assets/Art/Models/")) return;
            var mi = (ModelImporter)assetImporter;
            mi.materialImportMode = ModelImporterMaterialImportMode.ImportViaMaterialDescription;
            mi.materialLocation = ModelImporterMaterialLocation.InPrefab;
            mi.importAnimation = assetPath.EndsWith("/CrewRig.fbx");
            if (mi.importAnimation) mi.animationType = ModelImporterAnimationType.Legacy;
            if (assetPath.EndsWith("/PrepFisherV1.fbx")) mi.isReadable = true; // Physical deck slices also run in the player.
            mi.importCameras = false;
            mi.importLights = false;
        }

        const string LibraryPath = "Assets/Resources/ArtLibrary.asset";

        static GameObject Model(string path) => AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>("Assets/Art/Models/" + path + ".fbx");

        [MenuItem("Catchline/Rebuild Art Library")]
        public static void BuildLibrary()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Assets/Resources");
            var lib = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<ArtLibrary>(LibraryPath);
            if (lib == null)
            {
                lib = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<ArtLibrary>();
                AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(lib, LibraryPath);
            }
            lib.Rowboat = Model("Boats/Rowboat");
            lib.FishingRod = Model("Fishing/FishingRod");
            lib.Bobber = Model("Fishing/Bobber");
            lib.Cooler = Model("DeckProps/Cooler");
            lib.IceBox = Model("DeckProps/IceBox");
            lib.BaitBucket = Model("DeckProps/BaitBucket");
            lib.LunchTin = Model("DeckProps/LunchTin");
            lib.Quay = Model("Harbour/Quay4m");
            lib.QuayLadder = Model("Harbour/Quay4mLadder");
            lib.Bollard = Model("Harbour/Bollard");
            lib.LampPost = Model("Harbour/LampPost");
            lib.HouseA = Model("Harbour/HouseA");
            lib.HouseB = Model("Harbour/HouseB");
            lib.Lighthouse = Model("Harbour/Lighthouse");
            lib.RockA = Model("Harbour/BreakwaterRockA");
            lib.RockB = Model("Harbour/BreakwaterRockB");
            lib.RockC = Model("Harbour/BreakwaterRockC");
            lib.Kelp = Model("Harbour/KelpPatch");
            lib.Fish = new[] { Model("Fish/Herring"), Model("Fish/Mackerel"), Model("Fish/Pollock"), Model("Fish/SeaBass"), Model("Fish/CongerEel"), Model("Fish/Plaice"), Model("Fish/Cod") };
            lib.Models = Directory.GetFiles(Path.GetFullPath("Assets/Art/Models"), "*.fbx", SearchOption.AllDirectories)
                .Select(p => "Assets/Art/Models/" + Path.GetRelativePath(Path.GetFullPath("Assets/Art/Models"), p).Replace('\\', '/'))
                .Select(p => AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(p))
                .Where(g => g != null)
                .ToArray();
            lib.CrewClips = AssetDatabase.LoadAllAssetsAtPath("Assets/Art/Models/Crew/CrewRig.fbx")
                .OfType<AnimationClip>().Where(c => !c.name.StartsWith("__preview__")).ToArray();
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Art library: {lib.Models.Length} models");
            var pngs = Directory.GetFiles(Path.GetFullPath(UiFolder), "*.png");
            lib.Ui = pngs
                .Select(p => UiFolder + Path.GetFileName(p))
                .Select(p => AssetDatabase.LoadMainAssetAtPath(p) as Texture2D)
                .Where(t => t != null)
                .ToArray();
            if (lib.Ui.Length == 0 && pngs.Length > 0)
                Debug.LogWarning($"[Catchline] {pngs.Length} UI pngs on disk but none loaded; first: {UiFolder + Path.GetFileName(pngs[0])} -> {AssetDatabase.LoadMainAssetAtPath(UiFolder + Path.GetFileName(pngs[0]))?.GetType().Name ?? "null"}");
            lib.UiFont = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Font>(UiFolder + "Fonts/Fredoka-Medium.ttf");
            lib.WaterShader = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Shader>("Assets/Art/Shaders/PtsWater.shader")
                ?? AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Shader>("Assets/Art/Shaders/CoastalWater.shader");
            lib.PtsWaterNormals = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Texture2D>("Assets/Art/Water/PTS/WaterNormals.tif");
            lib.PtsWaterDither = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Texture2D>("Assets/Art/Water/PTS/normalNoise.png");
            lib.WaterRipples = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Texture2D>("Assets/Art/Water/normal-ripples.png");
            lib.WaterCrossing = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Texture2D>("Assets/Art/Water/normal-crossing.png");
            var waterPaths = Directory.GetFiles(Path.GetFullPath("Assets/Art/Textures"), "*.png")
                .Select(p => "Assets/Art/Textures/" + Path.GetFileName(p)).ToArray();
            foreach (var path in waterPaths)
                if (AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Texture2D>(path) == null)
                    AssetDatabase.ImportAsset(path, ImportAssetOptions.ForceUpdate | ImportAssetOptions.ForceSynchronousImport);
            lib.WaterTextures = waterPaths.Select(p => AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Texture2D>(p)).Where(t => t != null).ToArray();
            if (lib.WaterTextures.Length != waterPaths.Length)
                throw new System.InvalidOperationException("[Catchline] Water texture import incomplete: " + lib.WaterTextures.Length + "/" + waterPaths.Length);
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(lib);
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            var mats = lib.Rowboat != null ? lib.Rowboat.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>().SelectMany(r => r.sharedMaterials).Select(m => m != null ? m.name : "null").Distinct() : Enumerable.Empty<string>();
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Art library: rowboat {(lib.Rowboat != null)} [{string.Join(", ", mats)}], {lib.Ui.Length} UI textures, font {(lib.UiFont != null)}");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/CatchRewardChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class CatchRewardChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static object Call(VoyageGame g, string name, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(name, Hidden).Invoke(g, args);
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Catch reward check failed: " + why); }
        static void Until(VoyageGame g, Func<bool> done, string why, float seconds = 30)
        {
            for (float t = 0; t < seconds && !done(); t += .05f) g.Tick(.05f);
            Check(done(), why);
        }
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("Isolated catch reward fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build(); g.State.Hull = 2; g.State.Fuel = g.State.FuelCapacity;
            Call(g, "RebuildBoat", false);
            typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("showTitle", Hidden).SetValue(g, false);
            g.State.FoodMinutes = g.State.FoodCapacity; g.Showcase(0, 0);
            foreach (var rod in g.Rods) rod.State = RodState.Ready;
            return g;
        }
        static void Basket(VoyageGame g, int count)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                var f = new DeckFish { Species = i % 2 == 0 ? Species.Herring : Species.Mackerel,
                    Kg = i % 2 == 0 ? .2f : .4f, Age = 1, CaughtAt = g.SimTime, FromNetBasket = true, Tile = g.State.HullDef.NetAccess };
                f.RestPos = new Vector3(4.2f, .07f, 1.15f);
                f.Root = DeckFish.BuildModel(f.Species, f.Kg, g.Boat); f.Root.localPosition = f.RestPos;
                g.OnDeck.Add(f);
            }
            Call(g, "BeginNetUnload", g.Captain);
        }
        static Rect UiRect(VoyageGame g, string name)
        {
            var r = g.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Single(t => t.name == name);
            return new Rect(r.anchoredPosition.x, -r.anchoredPosition.y, r.rect.width, r.rect.height);
        }
        static void Shot(VoyageGame g, string name, int w = 1440, int h = 900, float type = 1)
            => typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic)
                .Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../catch018/" + name, w, h, type });
        public static void Run()
        {
            Check(CatchPresentation.Tier(Species.Herring, .2f) == CatchTier.Common, "white common");
            Check(CatchPresentation.Tier(Species.Herring, .29f) == CatchTier.BigCommon, "green big common relative to species");
            Check(CatchPresentation.Tier(Species.Turbot, 2) == CatchTier.Rare, "blue rare");
            Check(CatchPresentation.Tier(Species.Turbot, 4.9f) == CatchTier.BigRare, "purple big rare");
            foreach (var fish in GameData.Fish)
            {
                float cutoff = Mathf.Lerp(fish.MinKg, fish.MaxKg, .75f);
                Check(!CatchPresentation.IsBig(fish.Species, cutoff - .0001f) && CatchPresentation.IsBig(fish.Species, cutoff), "size boundary " + fish.Name);
                Check((int)CatchPresentation.Tier(fish.Species, fish.MaxKg) < (int)CatchTier.Legendary, "no current legendary " + fish.Name);
            }
            Check(Enum.GetValues(typeof(CatchTier)).Cast<CatchTier>().Select(CatchPresentation.Accent).Distinct().Count() == 6, "six distinct future-ready styles");
            var g = Fixture();
            Check(g.SetNet(), "actual captain sets net"); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(g);
            Call(g, "TickNet", 20f); Check(g.HaulNet(), "actual captain hauls"); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainHaul(g);
            var actual = g.OnDeck.Where(f => f.FromNetBasket).ToArray();
            Check(actual.Length > 0 && g.LastNetReceipt == null && g.Cooler.Count == 0, "no storage receipt at landing");
            Until(g, () => g.Captain.BusyLabel == "Gathering net catch" && g.Captain.Busy, "real gathering");
            Check(g.LastNetReceipt == null, "no receipt during gathering");
            Until(g, () => g.Captain.BusyLabel == "Stowing net catch" && g.Captain.Busy, "real stowing");
            Check(g.LastNetReceipt == null, "no receipt before stow completes");
            Until(g, () => g.LastNetReceipt != null, "receipt follows actual successful storage");
            var receipt = g.LastNetReceipt;
            Check(receipt.Count == actual.Length && g.Cooler.Count == actual.Length, "net list exact inserted count");
            Check(Mathf.Abs(receipt.Kg - actual.Sum(f => f.Kg)) < .0001f, "net list exact total weight");
            Check(receipt.Value == g.Cooler.Sum(f => g.State.ValueOf(f, g.SimTime)), "receipt value uses actual current market and freshness");
            Check(receipt.Items.Select(f => f.Species).OrderBy(x => x).SequenceEqual(actual.Select(f => f.Species).OrderBy(x => x)), "net list exact species multiset");
            int coins = g.State.Coins;
            g.Tick(.4f);
            foreach (var size in new[] { new Vector2Int(1440, 900), new Vector2Int(844, 390), new Vector2Int(2560, 1440) })
            {
                g.PrepareInterface(size.x, size.y, 1);
                var panel = UiRect(g, "catch/net"); var ui = g.GetComponentInChildren<CoastalCanvas>();
                Check(panel.xMin >= 0 && panel.yMin >= 0 && panel.xMax <= ui.Width && panel.yMax <= ui.Height, "receipt fits native viewport");
                Check(!panel.Overlaps(UiRect(g, "hold/freshness")), "receipt clear of hold status");
                Check(g.GetComponentsInChildren<Text>().Single(t => t.name == "catch/net/total").text.StartsWith(receipt.Count + " fish"), "visible count honest");
            }
            g.Tick(12); g.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("catch/net/review"), "last haul can be reopened"); g.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            Check(ReferenceEquals(g.LastNetReceipt, receipt) && g.State.Coins == coins && g.Cooler.Count == actual.Length, "review never pays or duplicates fish");
            g.State.Rng = new System.Random(92);
            for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) g.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            Check(g.State.Rng.Next() == new System.Random(92).Next(), "presentation consumes no catch RNG");
            g.Cooler[0].Kg = 100;
            Check(receipt.Kg < 100, "receipt snapshot independent of later live mutation");
            Call(g, "ResetFeedback"); Check(g.LastNetReceipt == null, "new voyage clears review snapshot");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);

            g = Fixture(); Basket(g, 3); g.Tick(.2f); g.Captain.Stop();
            Check(g.Cooler.Count == 0 && g.LastNetReceipt == null && !g.NetUnloadActive && g.OnDeck.All(f => f.ClaimedBy == null), "cancel gather releases claims with no false reward");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            g = Fixture();
            g.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = g.State.CoolerCapacity - .01f });
            Basket(g, 3); Until(g, () => g.LastNetReceipt != null, "partial hold stores available fish");
            Check(g.LastNetReceipt.Count == 1 && g.OnDeck.Count(f => f.FromNetBasket) == 2, "partial receipt excludes unstored backlog");
            int count = g.Cooler.Count;
            g.Tick(10); Check(g.Cooler.Count == count && g.LastNetReceipt.Count == 1, "full hold never adds ghost receipt entries");
            Call(g, "ResetCatchReceipts");
            var carried = g.OnDeck.First(); g.OnDeck.Remove(carried); carried.Root.SetParent(g.Captain.Root, false); g.Captain.Carrying = carried;
            Check(!(bool)Call(g, "TryStow", g.Captain) && g.LastNetReceipt == null, "full manual storage creates no reward");
            g.Cooler.RemoveAt(0);
            Check((bool)Call(g, "TryStow", g.Captain) && g.LastNetReceipt.Count == 1, "manual leftover net fish gets actual storage receipt");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] CATCH REWARD CHECKS PASSED: tiers/size boundaries, no current legendary, actual net-storage species/count/kg/value, cancellation/full/partial/manual safety, review no mutation, viewport/status clearance, snapshot/RNG isolation");
        }
        public static void Capture()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetFullPath("Logs/catch018"));
            var g = Fixture(); g.StartNetBoatTrial();
            var examples = new[] { (Species.Mackerel, .42f, "white"), (Species.Mackerel, .68f, "green"), (Species.Turbot, 2.4f, "blue"), (Species.Turbot, 4.9f, "purple") };
            foreach (var example in examples)
            {
                g.LandFish(g.Rods[0], example.Item1, example.Item2); g.Tick(.45f);
                Shot(g, example.Item3 + "-desktop"); Shot(g, example.Item3 + "-phone", 844, 390);
                g.PrepareInterface(844, 390);
                var panel = UiRect(g, "catch");
                Check(!panel.Overlaps(UiRect(g, "net/orders")) && !panel.Overlaps(UiRect(g, "home")), "reward does not cover crew or home controls");
            }
            // Art fixture only: real supported species/weights, passed through the exact
            // production storage-snapshot/UI methods. Actual net distribution tested in Run.
            var sample = new[] {
                new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .2f, StowedAt = g.SimTime },
                new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .24f, StowedAt = g.SimTime },
                new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Mackerel, Kg = .4f, StowedAt = g.SimTime },
                new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Whiting, Kg = .7f, StowedAt = g.SimTime },
                new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Pollock, Kg = 1.3f, StowedAt = g.SimTime } };
            g.Cooler.AddRange(sample); Call(g, "RecordStoredNet", new object[] { sample }); g.Tick(.45f);
            Shot(g, "net-list-desktop"); Shot(g, "net-list-phone", 844, 390); Shot(g, "net-list-laptop", 2560, 1440);
            Shot(g, "large-type-phone", 844, 390, 1.15f);
            g.Tick(12); Shot(g, "review-phone", 844, 390);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/catch018 (native world+Canvas, four active tiers and net receipt)");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary(); FirstVoyageChecks.Regression(); NetHaulChecks.Run(); NetEconomyChecks.Run(); CrewPolishChecks.Run(); Run(); Capture(); BuildPreview();
        }
        public static void ReviewAndBuildPreview() { ArtImport.BuildLibrary(); Run(); Capture(); BuildPreview(); }
        public static void BuildPreview()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Catchline catch presentation preview").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); game.SessionOnly = true;
            const string scene = "Assets/Scenes/Catch018Preview.unity";
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), scene);
            var output = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Catch018/Catchline.exe");
            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions { scenes = new[] { scene }, locationPathName = output,
                target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64, options = BuildOptions.None });
            Check(report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded, "separate reward preview built");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + output);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/ChartChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class ChartChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string reason) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Chart check failed: " + reason); }
        static void Field(VoyageGame g, string name, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(name, Hidden).SetValue(g, value);
        static Text Text(VoyageGame g, string id) => g.GetComponentsInChildren<Text>().Single(t => t.name == id);
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            var g = new GameObject("Isolated illustrated coast").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build();
            Field(g, "showTitle", false); Field(g, "paused", false); Field(g, "panelOpen", true);
            Field(g, "hudTab", VoyageGame.HudTab.HarbourMaster); Field(g, "toastUntil", 0f);
            typeof(VoyageGame).GetProperty("State").GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(g, new object[] { new GameState(11) });
            g.State.Charts.Clear(); g.SelectedSpot = 0; g.State.Coins = 0; return g;
        }
        static void Render(VoyageGame g, int w = 1920, int h = 1080) { g.PrepareInterface(w, h, 1); Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases(); }
        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary(); EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = Fixture(); Render(g);
            var plate = g.GetComponentsInChildren<Image>().Single(i => i.name == "chart/illustrated/field");
            Check(plate.sprite != null && plate.sprite.texture.width == 1672 && plate.sprite.texture.height == 941, "original-resolution illustrated resource is used without power-of-two resampling");
            Check(g.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().All(r => r.name != "shop/shell"), "map replaces shop shell across entire screen");
            for (int i = 0; i < GameData.Spots.Length; i++)
            {
                Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/nautical/pin/" + i), "live landmark target"); Render(g);
                Check(Text(g, "chart/focus/title").text == GameData.Spots[i].Name, "inspect real destination");
                Check(g.SelectedSpot == 0, "inspection does not change sailing destination");
            }
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/nautical/pin/2"), "inspect locked sandbar");
            int price = GameData.Spots[2].ChartCost; g.State.Coins = price - 1; Render(g);
            Check(!g.InvokeInterface("chart/select") && !g.State.Known(2) && g.SelectedSpot == 0, "no money cannot unlock");
            g.State.Coins = price; Render(g); Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/select"), "real buy action"); Render(g);
            Check(g.State.Known(2) && g.SelectedSpot == 2 && g.State.Coins == 0, "one purchase selects correct ground");
            g.InvokeInterface("chart/nautical/pin/2"); Render(g); Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/select") && g.State.Coins == 0, "owned chart never charges again");
            g.InvokeInterface("chart/nautical/pin/1"); Render(g); Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/select") && g.SelectedSpot == 1, "known grounds need no coins");
            g.State.Active.Clear(); g.State.Offers.Clear();
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) g.State.Offers.Add(new Contract { Species = (Species)i, Count = 3 + i, Reward = 15 + i, VoyagesLeft = 3 });
            g.InvokeInterface("chart/contracts"); Render(g); var contract = g.State.Offers[0];
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("offer/0/take"), "contracts reachable in map modal");
            Check(g.State.Active.Contains(contract) && !g.State.Offers.Contains(contract), "actual contract added once");
            g.InvokeInterface("offer/0/take"); Check(g.State.Active.Count == 1, "stale callback cannot duplicate");
            Render(g); g.InvokeInterface("offer/0/take"); Render(g);
            Check(!g.InvokeInterface("offer/0/take") && g.State.Active.Count == GameData.MaxActiveContracts, "contract cap preserved");
            g.InvokeInterface("chart/contracts/close");
            foreach (var size in new[] { new Vector2Int(1920, 1080), new Vector2Int(1440, 900), new Vector2Int(844, 390) })
            {
                Render(g, size.x, size.y);
                var field = (RectTransform)g.GetComponentsInChildren<Image>().Single(i => i.name == "chart/illustrated/field").transform;
                var root = g.GetComponentInChildren<CoastalCanvas>();
                Check(Mathf.Abs(field.rect.width - root.Width) < 1 && Mathf.Abs(field.rect.height - root.Height) < 1, "edge-to-edge map at " + size);
                for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
                {
                    var hit = g.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Single(t => t.name == "chart/nautical/pin/" + i);
                    Check(hit.rect.width * size.y / root.Height >= 44 && hit.rect.height * size.y / root.Height >= 44, "44pixel landmark target");
                    Vector2 p = hit.anchoredPosition;
                    Check(p.x >= 0 && p.x + hit.rect.width <= root.Width + 1 && -p.y >= 0 && -p.y + hit.rect.height <= root.Height + 1, "landmark target within screen");
                }
                Check(g.GetComponentsInChildren<IllustratedRoutesGraphic>().Length == 1, "one retained route overlay");
                Check(g.GetComponentsInChildren<ChartPinGraphic>().Length == 11, "six pins and five working navigation icons");
                foreach (var text in g.GetComponentsInChildren<Text>().Where(t => t.name.StartsWith("chart/"))) Check(text.font != null, "real supplied live fonts");
            }
            Render(g); g.State.Coins = 24850; Render(g); Check(Text(g, "chart/status/coins").text == "24,850", "wallet uses current amount");
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/nav/Crew"), "crew destination works"); Render(g);
            Check(g.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Any(t => t.name == "shop/shell"), "real crew shop opened");
            Field(g, "hudTab", VoyageGame.HudTab.HarbourMaster); Render(g);
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/nav/Gear"), "gear destination works");
            Field(g, "hudTab", VoyageGame.HudTab.HarbourMaster); Render(g);
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("chart/nav/Boat"), "boat destination returns to physical harbour"); Render(g);
            Check(g.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().All(t => t.name != "chart/illustrated/field"), "closed chart hit targets/art are inactive");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] CHART CHECKS PASSED: illustrated resource/fullscreen, real focus/unlock/contracts, live status, touch bounds and working navigation");
        }
        public static void Capture()
        {
            Run(); var g = Fixture(); g.State.Coins = 200;
            System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(System.IO.Path.GetFullPath("Logs/chart017"));
            var shot = typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            void Shot(string n, int w, int h) => shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../chart017/" + n, w, h, 1f });
            Shot("map-laptop", 2560, 1440); Shot("map-desktop", 1440, 900); Shot("map-phone", 844, 390);
            g.InvokeInterface("chart/nautical/pin/2"); Shot("map-sandbar", 1440, 900);
            g.InvokeInterface("chart/contracts"); Shot("map-contracts", 1440, 900);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/chart017");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/ChartPresentationChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class ChartPresentationChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Chart presentation check failed: " + why); }
        static Bounds BoundsIn(Transform source, Transform frame)
        {
            Bounds result = new(); bool first = true;
            foreach (var mf in source.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
            {
                var bounds = mf.sharedMesh.bounds;
                for (int corner = 0; corner < 8; corner++)
                {
                    var p = frame.InverseTransformPoint(mf.transform.TransformPoint(bounds.center + Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents,
                        new Vector3((corner & 1) == 0 ? -1 : 1, (corner & 2) == 0 ? -1 : 1, (corner & 4) == 0 ? -1 : 1))));
                    if (first) { result = new Bounds(p, Vector3.zero); first = false; } else result.Encapsulate(p);
                }
            }
            return result;
        }
        public static void Capture()
        {
            var texture = (TextureImporter)AssetImporter.GetAtPath("Assets/Resources/Charts/gull-coast.png");
            texture.mipmapEnabled = false; texture.maxTextureSize = 4096; texture.textureCompression = TextureImporterCompression.Uncompressed;
            texture.sRGBTexture = true; texture.alphaIsTransparency = false; texture.SaveAndReimport();
            texture.npotScale = TextureImporterNPOTScale.None; texture.SaveAndReimport();
            ChartChecks.Capture();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Stern trailing net fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); game.SessionOnly = true; game.Build(); game.StartNetBoatTrial();
            Check(game.SetNet(), "begin net deployment"); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(game);
            var floats = (Transform)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("netFloats", Hidden).GetValue(game);
            var model = (Transform)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("netModelRoot", Hidden).GetValue(game);
            var stern = BoundsIn(model, game.Boat).max.x;
            for (int frame = 0; frame < 100; frame++)
            {
                game.Tick(.05f); var gear = BoundsIn(floats, game.Boat);
                Check(gear.min.x > stern + .5f, "all net panels stay behind physical stern while water bobs");
                Check(Mathf.Abs(gear.center.z - game.State.HullDef.Center.z) < .2f, "net centred behind boat, not alongside");
                var ropes = floats.GetComponentsInChildren<LineRenderer>(); Check(ropes.Length == 2, "two stern ropes visible");
                foreach (var rope in ropes) Check(rope.positionCount == 2 && Vector3.Distance(rope.GetPosition(0), rope.GetPosition(1)) < 3.5f, "ropes connect stern to nearby floating gear");
            }
            var shot = typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { game, "../chart017/net-behind-stern", 1440, 900, 1f });
            shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { game, "../chart017/net-behind-stern-phone", 844, 390, 1f });
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] CHART PRESENTATION CHECKS PASSED: illustrated map, stern clearance/centre/ropes through moving water");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/chart017");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            Capture(); NetHaulChecks.Run(); CrewPolishChecks.Run(); BuildPreview();
        }
        public static void ReviewAndBuildPreview() { Capture(); BuildPreview(); }
        public static void BuildPreview()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Illustrated chart preview").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); game.SessionOnly = true;
            const string scene = "Assets/Scenes/Chart017Preview.unity";
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), scene);
            string output = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Chart017/Catchline.exe");
            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions { scenes = new[] { scene }, locationPathName = output,
                target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64, options = BuildOptions.None });
            Check(report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded, "separate illustrated chart preview built");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + output);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/CoastalPolishChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class CoastalPolishChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool pass, string label) { if (!pass) throw new Exception("Coastal polish check failed: " + label); }
        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                var root = new GameObject("Coastal route check");
                var game = root.AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
                game.SessionOnly = true;
                game.Build();
                for (int i = 0; i < GameData.Spots.Length; i++) game.State.Charts.Add(i);
                game.State.FoodMinutes = 100;
                game.SelectedSpot = spot;
                var start = game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center);
                game.SetSail();
                float duration = GameData.Spots[spot].RowSeconds / game.WorkMult;
                game.Tick(duration * .17f);
                Check(game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center).z < start.z - 1.25f, "clears quay sideways before turn " + spot);
                Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.DeltaAngle(game.Boat.eulerAngles.y, 0)) < 1, "parallel to quay during clearance");
                game.Tick(duration * .27f);
                var bow = game.Boat.TransformDirection(Vector3.left); bow.y = 0;
                Check(Vector3.Dot(bow.normalized, VoyageGame.RouteDirection(spot)) > .99f, "bow follows chart bearing " + spot);
                game.Tick(duration);
                Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "outbound arrives " + spot);
                var arrived = game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center);
                Check(Mathf.Abs(arrived.x - game.State.HullDef.Center.x) < .0001f && Mathf.Abs(arrived.z - game.State.HullDef.Center.z) < .0001f, "fishing stage restored about hull centre");
                game.HeadHome();
                game.Tick(duration * .84f);
                Check(game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center).z < game.State.HullDef.Center.z - 1.20f, "approaches clear of quay " + spot);
                game.Tick(duration);
                Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour, "home arrives " + spot);
                Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.DeltaAngle(game.Boat.eulerAngles.y, 0)) < 1
                    && Mathf.Abs(game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center).z - game.State.HullDef.Center.z) < .0001f, "parked alongside " + spot);
                UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(root);
            }
            var environment = ArtLibrary.Get().M("GullHarbourMouth");
            if (environment != null)
            {
                bool beacon = false, quay = false;
                foreach (var mesh in environment.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
                {
                    var center = mesh.transform.TransformPoint(mesh.sharedMesh.bounds.center);
                    if (mesh.name.Contains("Beacon")) { Check(center.x > 0, "beacon correct handedness"); beacon = true; }
                    if (mesh.name.Contains("WorkingQuay") || mesh.name.Contains("Working_Quay")) { Check(center.x > 0, "quay correct handedness"); quay = true; }
                }
                Check(beacon && quay, "authored landmarks imported");
            }
            foreach (string name in new[] { "GullKelpBank", "GullSandbar", "GullRockyPoint", "GullDeepChannel" })
            {
                var prefab = ArtLibrary.Get().M(name);
                if (prefab == null) continue; // Artist deliveries can arrive separately.
                bool terrain = false;
                foreach (var mesh in prefab.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
                {
                    if (mesh.name != "Terrain_ConnectedShoalsAndSeabed") continue;
                    var center = mesh.transform.TransformPoint(mesh.sharedMesh.bounds.center);
                    // All four use the same exact-camera terrain rectangle; this catches
                    // the X reflection that previously passed Blender-only round trips.
                    Check(Mathf.Abs(center.x - 1.533545f) < .002f && Mathf.Abs(center.z - .462129f) < .002f, name + " Unity terrain handedness");
                    terrain = true;
                }
                Check(terrain, name + " connected opaque depth support");
                int triangles = 0;
                foreach (var mesh in prefab.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>()) triangles += mesh.sharedMesh.triangles.Length / 3;
                Check(triangles <= 24000, name + " bounded triangle budget");
                Debug.Log($"[Catchline] ENVIRONMENT IMPORT PASSED: {name}, {triangles} triangles; terrain bounds match source axes");
            }
            Check(!ShaderUtil.ShaderHasError(ArtLibrary.Get().WaterShader), "coastal shader compiles");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] COASTAL POLISH CHECKS PASSED");
        }

        public static void RenderMaps()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Camera-only background review").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            game.Build();
            string directory = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/polish004-maps");
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                game.Showcase(spot, 0);
                game.Tick(.4f);
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(directory, $"map-{spot}-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(directory, $"map-{spot}-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            }
            game.Showcase(0, 0);
            for (int frame = 0; frame < 12; frame++)
            {
                game.Tick(.12f);
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(directory, $"water-{frame:00}.png"), 1440, 900);
            }
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to " + directory + " (camera-only; IMGUI absent)");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/CompactFisherChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class CompactFisherChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Private = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool pass, string why) { if (!pass) throw new Exception("Compact fisher check failed: " + why); }
        static object Call(VoyageGame game, string name, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(name, Private).Invoke(game, args);
        static void Field(VoyageGame game, string name, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(name, Private).SetValue(game, value);
        static void Property(VoyageGame game, string name, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetProperty(name).GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(game, new[] { value });
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            var game = new GameObject("Isolated compact fisher").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true; game.Build(); Field(game, "showTitle", false);
            Check(game.TrialCompactFisher(), "trial accessible from harbour");
            return game;
        }
        static void Crew(VoyageGame game, params CrewRole[] roles)
        {
            game.State.Deckhand = null; game.State.ExtraCrew.Clear(); game.SyncCrew();
            game.State.Coins = 1000;
            foreach (var role in roles)
            {
                game.State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
                Check(game.State.Hire(game.State.Board.First(c => c.Role == role)), "hire " + role);
            }
            game.SyncCrew();
        }
        static void Fishing(VoyageGame game)
        {
            Property(game, "CurrentMode", VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing);
            Property(game, "ActiveSpot", 0);
            Field(game, "panelOpen", false);
            game.State.Bait = 200; game.State.FoodMinutes = 24;
        }
        static void Work(Person p)
        { for (int i = 0; i < 1000 && !p.Idle; i++) p.Tick(.05f, 1, Quaternion.identity); }

        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = Fixture();
            var hull = game.State.HullDef;
            Check(GameData.Hulls[0].Model == "Rowboat" && GameData.Hulls[1].Model == "Skiff", "old saved hull IDs preserved");
            Check(JsonUtility.FromJson<CrewMember>("{\"Role\":0}").WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Fish, "old crew saves default to Fish duty");
            Check(game.Grid.CellSize == .1f && game.Rods.Count == 3 && hull.CrewCap == 3, "physical grid/three rods/three hired berths");
            var model = game.Boat.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>().First(t => t.name == "CompactFisherV1");
            var mesh = model.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>().First(m => m.name == "Hull");
            var points = mesh.sharedMesh.vertices.Select(v => game.Boat.InverseTransformPoint(mesh.transform.TransformPoint(v))).ToArray();
            var size = new Vector3(points.Max(v => v.x) - points.Min(v => v.x), points.Max(v => v.y) - points.Min(v => v.y), points.Max(v => v.z) - points.Min(v => v.z));
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Compact native hull metres {size}; walkable {game.CompactWalkableTiles}");
            Check(Mathf.Abs(size.x - 5.4f) < .1f && Mathf.Abs(size.z - 2.8f) < .1f && size.y < 1.5f, "real model upright at approved physical size");
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                var socket = model.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>().First(t => t.name == "RodSocket_" + (i + 1));
                var socketPos = game.Boat.InverseTransformPoint(socket.position);
                Check(Vector3.Distance(socketPos, hull.RodBases[i]) < .06f, "actual rod socket " + i + " " + socketPos);
            }
            var jobs = new[] { hull.Seat, hull.HoldAccess, hull.NetAccess }.Concat(hull.CrewRest).Concat(hull.RodTiles).ToArray();
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetFullPath("Logs/net010"));
            File.WriteAllLines("Logs/net010/deck-grid.txt", Enumerable.Range(0, game.Grid.Height).Reverse().Select(y => string.Concat(Enumerable.Range(0, game.Grid.Width).Select(x => jobs.Contains(new Vector2Int(x, y)) ? 'J' : game.Grid.IsBlocked(new Vector2Int(x, y)) ? '#' : '.'))));
            foreach (var a in jobs)
            {
                Check(!game.Grid.IsBlocked(a), "job tile walkable: " + a);
                foreach (var b in jobs) Check(game.Grid.FindPath(a, b) != null, "actual deck connects " + a + " to " + b);
            }
            Check(game.Boat.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>().Count(t => t.name.StartsWith("RodVisual_")) == 0, "display-only duplicate rods hidden");
            var lid = model.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>().First(t => t.name == "HoldLid");
            var lidMesh = lid.GetComponent<MeshFilter>();
            var lidRest = lid.localRotation;
            float closedY = lidMesh.sharedMesh.vertices.Average(v => lidMesh.transform.TransformPoint(v).y);
            lid.localRotation = lidRest * Quaternion.Euler(90, 0, 0);
            Check(lidMesh.sharedMesh.vertices.Average(v => lidMesh.transform.TransformPoint(v).y) > closedY + .2f, "native hinged lid opens upward");
            lid.localRotation = lidRest;
            var propeller = model.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>().First(t => t.name == "Propeller");
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(propeller.TransformDirection(Vector3.up), game.Boat.right)) > .99f, "source-Y propeller shaft matches native stern axis");
            Crew(game, CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Fisherman);
            Fishing(game);
            game.CommandRod(game.Rods[0]);
            int activeHandSteps = 0, maxDeck = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 2400; i++)
            {
                if (game.MinigameRod?.State == RodState.Bite) game.StrikeTap();
                if (game.AwaitingCast) game.ThrowAgain();
                game.Tick(.05f);
                if (game.Hand.Walking || game.Hand.Busy && game.Hand.BusyLabel != "Resting") activeHandSteps++;
                maxDeck = Mathf.Max(maxDeck, game.OnDeck.Count);
                foreach (var p in new[] { game.Captain, game.Hand }.Concat(game.ExtraPeople))
                    Check(!game.Grid.IsBlocked(p.Tile), "crew stays on clear deck: " + p.Name + " at " + p.Tile);
            }
            Check(game.Rods.Count(r => r.Fisher != null) == 2, "two hired fishers run distinct rods");
            Check(game.Stats.Landed > 8 && game.Cooler.Count > 4, "four real actors fish and carry");
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Compact 120s crew trace landed={game.Stats.Landed} held={game.Cooler.Count} deck={game.OnDeck.Count} peakDeck={maxDeck} lost={game.Stats.Lost} handActive={activeHandSteps / 24f:0}%");
            // Save through an explicit scratch filename, never the player's persistent path.
            var hand = game.State.Deckhand;
            Check(game.SetDeckhandWork(hand, DeckhandWork.Nets), "net work preference accepted");
            string save = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/net010/preference-save.json");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(save)); game.State.Save(save);
            var loaded = GameState.Load(save);
            Check(loaded.Hull == 2 && loaded.Deckhand.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Nets, "new hull and duty round-trip save");
            // Isolate the work-allocation checks from any rod/reel left in the timed trace.
            foreach (var p in new[] { game.Captain, game.Hand }.Concat(game.ExtraPeople)) p.Stop();
            Property(game, "Manning", null);
            game.Cooler.Clear();
            game.Hand.Stop();
            if (game.Hand.Carrying != null) Call(game, "TryStow", game.Hand);
            Call(game, "ResetNet");
            Call(game, "HandThink", game.Hand);
            Work(game.Hand);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking, "net-priority hand sets autonomously");
            Call(game, "TickNet", 20f);
            // Isolate this success-path allocation check from the separate20% tear regression.
            // Seed0's first roll(.726...) is inside the safe band for a completed cycle.
            var safeRoll = new System.Random(0).NextDouble();
            Check(safeRoll >= .2 && safeRoll < .8, "known deterministic safe worker seed");
            game.State.Rng = new System.Random(0);
            Call(game, "HandThink", game.Hand); Work(game.Hand);
            Check(game.NetBasketCount > 0, "net-priority hand hauls the weight-budget catch into basket on safe cycle");
            var basket = game.OnDeck.Where(f => f.FromNetBasket).ToArray();
            Call(game, "TickDeckFish", 120f);
            Check(basket.All(f => game.OnDeck.Contains(f)), "basket cannot flop away");
            foreach (var f in basket) Check(game.CanCarry(f), "captain can collect while hired hand is available");
            Call(game, "RequestHeadHome");
            Check(!(bool)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("confirmHeadHome", Private).GetValue(game), "stowed net and fitting catch can return directly");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            game = Fixture(); Fishing(game);
            Property(game, "Manning", game.Rods[0]);
            for (int i = 0; i < game.State.CoolerCapacity; i++) game.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Kg = 1f });
            Check(!game.CaptainNetAction() && game.Manning == game.Rods[0], "failed full-hold action preserves captain rod");
            game.Cooler.Clear(); Property(game, "Manning", null);
            Check(game.SetNet(), "set for departure protection"); Work(game.Captain); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(game);
            Call(game, "RequestHeadHome");
            Check((bool)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("confirmHeadHome", Private).GetValue(game), "deployed net prompts before abandoning");
            Call(game, "CancelHeadHome");
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking && game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "cancel preserves deployed net");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            foreach (var role in new[] { CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Deckhand })
            {
                game = Fixture(); Crew(game, role, role, role); Fishing(game);
                for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
                Check(game.State.CrewCount == 3 && game.ExtraPeople.Count + (game.Hand != null ? 1 : 0) == 3, "three-" + role + " composition");
                if (role == CrewRole.Fisherman) Check(game.Rods.All(r => r.Fisher != null), "three fishers use all rods; captain can do logistics");
                game.HeadHome();
                Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.SailingHome, "compact boat begins return with full crew");
                for (int i = 0; i < 400 && game.CurrentMode != VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
                Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour, "compact boat docks successfully");
                Check(game.SailBlocker() == null, "compact boat ready to launch with included fuel");
                game.SetSail();
                for (int i = 0; i < 400 && game.CurrentMode != VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
                Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "compact boat reaches fishing ground again");
                UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            }
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] COMPACT FISHER CHECKS PASSED");
        }

        public static void ValidateCaptureAndBuildPreview()
        {
            Capture();
            ReferenceHudChecks.Capture();
            FirstVoyageChecks.BuildPreview();
        }

        public static void Capture()
        {
            Run(); NetChoiceChecks.Run();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = Fixture(); Crew(game, CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Fisherman);
            game.Showcase(0, 0); Fishing(game); game.Tick(.1f);
            var shot = typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            string folder = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/net010");
            void Shot(string name, int width, int height) => shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { game, "../net010/" + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(name), width, height, 1f });
            Shot("compact-desktop.png", 1440, 900); Shot("compact-phone.png", 844, 390);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("net/orders"), "native crew orders button bound");
            Shot("duties-desktop.png", 1440, 900); Shot("duties-phone.png", 844, 390);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("net/orders/0/nets"), "native Nets choice bound");
            Check(game.State.Deckhand.WorkPreference == DeckhandWork.Nets, "native choice changes real scheduler");
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("net/orders/close"), "close duties");
            game.SetDeckhandWork(game.State.Deckhand, DeckhandWork.Fish);
            game.Captain.Stop();
            Field(game, "toastUntil", 0f);
            game.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("net"), "captain net action bound"); Work(game.Captain); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(game);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking, "native set starts real soak");
            Shot("net-soaking-desktop.png", 1440, 900);
            Call(game, "TickNet", 20f);
            Shot("net-ready-phone.png", 844, 390);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("net"), "captain haul action bound"); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainHaul(game);
            Call(game, "TickDeckFish", 1f);
            Shot("net-basket-desktop.png", 1440, 900); Shot("net-basket-phone.png", 844, 390);
            int secured = game.Cooler.Count;
            var fish = game.OnDeck.First(f => f.FromNetBasket);
            game.CommandCarry(fish); Work(game.Captain);
            Check(game.Cooler.Count == secured + 1 && !game.OnDeck.Contains(fish), "captain carries basket catch into hold");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to " + folder);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/CrewPolishChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class CrewPolishChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static object Call(VoyageGame g, string name, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(name, Hidden).Invoke(g, args);
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Crew polish check failed: " + why); }
        static void Until(VoyageGame g, Func<bool> done, string why, float seconds = 30)
        {
            for (float t = 0; t < seconds && !done(); t += .05f) g.Tick(.05f);
            Check(done(), why);
        }
        static VoyageGame Fixture(int hull)
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("Save-isolated crew polish").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build();
            g.State.Hull = hull; g.State.Fuel = g.State.FuelCapacity; g.State.Coins = 1000; g.State.FoodMinutes = g.State.FoodCapacity;
            g.State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
            Check(g.State.Hire(g.State.Board.First(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand)), "hand hired");
            Call(g, "RebuildBoat", false);
            typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("showTitle", Hidden).SetValue(g, false);
            g.Showcase(0, 0);
            foreach (var rod in g.Rods) rod.State = RodState.Ready;
            return g;
        }
        static DeckFish Fish(VoyageGame g)
        {
            var f = new DeckFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .3f, Tile = g.Rods[0].Tile, Age = 1, CaughtAt = g.SimTime };
            f.RestPos = g.Grid.GetLocal(f.Tile) + new Vector3(.1f, .07f, .15f);
            f.Root = DeckFish.BuildModel(f.Species, f.Kg, g.Boat); f.Root.localPosition = f.RestPos;
            g.OnDeck.Add(f); return f;
        }
        static void Facing(Person p, Vector3 target, string why)
        {
            target.y = 0;
            Check(Vector3.Dot(p.Root.localRotation * Vector3.forward, target.normalized) > .97f, why);
        }
        static void Shot(VoyageGame g, string name, int w = 1440, int h = 900) => typeof(ReferenceHudChecks)
            .GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic).Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../crew016/" + name, w, h, 1f });
        public static void Run()
        {
            for (int hull = 0; hull < 3; hull++)
            {
                var g = Fixture(hull); var hand = g.Hand;
                Check(hand.Root.Find("Progress") == null && g.Captain.Root.Find("Progress") == null, "no overhead progress geometry");
                g.State.Rng = new System.Random(123);
                var start = hand.Root.localPosition; bool walking = false, looking = false;
                float greatestTravel = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 600; i++)
                {
                    g.Tick(.05f);
                    Check(!g.Grid.IsBlocked(hand.Tile), "patrol remains on walkable deck hull " + hull);
                    Check(!hand.Busy, "idle routine never creates a fake timed work job");
                    greatestTravel = Mathf.Max(greatestTravel, Vector3.Distance(start, hand.Root.localPosition));
                    walking |= hand.Loitering && hand.Walking;
                    looking |= hand.Loitering && !hand.Walking;
                    if (hand.Loitering && !hand.Walking)
                        Check(Mathf.Abs(hand.Root.localPosition.y - start.y) < .0001f, "lookout has no root jitter");
                }
                Check(walking && looking && greatestTravel >= .6f && hand.PatrolLeg >= 2, "walk, dwell, then continue on hull " + hull);
                Check(g.State.Rng.Next() == new System.Random(123).Next(), "cosmetic patrol does not consume catch RNG");
                var frozen = hand.Root.localPosition; var rotation = hand.Root.localRotation;
                g.Tick(0);
                Check(hand.Root.localPosition == frozen && Quaternion.Angle(rotation, hand.Root.localRotation) < .001f, "pause freezes patrol");
                Until(g, () => hand.Loitering && hand.Walking, "reach an active patrol walk");
                var fish = Fish(g); g.Tick(.05f);
                Check(fish.ClaimedBy == hand && !hand.Loitering, "new catch interrupts patrol on next simulation step");
                Until(g, () => hand.BusyLabel == "Picking up" && hand.Busy, "actual pickup pose");
                hand.Tick(.5f, 1, Quaternion.identity);
                Facing(hand, fish.RestPos - hand.Root.localPosition, "pickup faces fish");
                Until(g, () => hand.Busy && hand.BusyLabel == "Stowing", "actual stow pose");
                hand.Tick(.5f, 1, Quaternion.identity);
                Facing(hand, g.Grid.GetLocal(g.State.HullDef.Hold) - hand.Root.localPosition, "delivery faces hold");
                Until(g, () => g.Cooler.Count == 1, "patrol interruption delivers fish exactly once");
                Until(g, () => hand.Loitering && hand.Walking, "resumes patrol after delivery");
                var rod = g.Rods[0]; rod.State = RodState.Unbaited; g.Tick(.05f);
                Check(rod.BaitingBy == hand && !hand.Loitering, "baiting interrupts stroll");
                Until(g, () => hand.Busy && hand.BusyLabel == "Baiting", "bait action starts");
                hand.Tick(.5f, 1, Quaternion.identity); Facing(hand, rod.Outward, "bait faces actual rod side");
                float deckY = hand.Root.localPosition.y; hand.Tick(.05f, 1, Quaternion.identity);
                Check(Mathf.Abs(hand.Root.localPosition.y - deckY) < .0001f, "rigged work has no second root bob");
                Debug.Log("[Catchline] CREW016 hull " + hull + ": patrol legs=" + hand.PatrolLeg + ", travel=" + greatestTravel + "; walk/look/resume, interrupt pickup/bait, facing, pause, no RNG passed");
                UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            }
            var net = Fixture(2); var worker = net.Hand;
            net.State.Rng = new System.Random(0);
            net.Tick(.05f); Check(worker.Loitering, "net fixture initially strolls");
            worker.Contract.WorkPreference = DeckhandWork.Nets; net.Tick(.05f);
            Check(net.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Setting && !worker.Loitering, "net duty interrupts active patrol immediately");
            Until(net, () => worker.Busy && worker.BusyLabel == "Setting net", "net worker reaches winch");
            worker.Tick(.5f, 1, Quaternion.identity); Facing(worker, Vector3.right, "net worker faces stern equipment");
            Until(net, () => net.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking, "worker sets net");
            net.Tick(.05f); Check(worker.Loitering, "soaking frees worker to patrol");
            worker.Contract.WorkPreference = DeckhandWork.Fish;
            net.Captain.Stop(); net.Captain.Place(net.Rods[1].Tile); net.CommandRod(net.Rods[1]);
            net.Tick(.5f); Facing(net.Captain, net.Rods[1].Outward, "already-at-rod captain faces rod rather than old heading");
            net.PrepareInterface(1672, 941, 1);
            foreach (string role in new[] { "captain", "deckhand", "fisherman" })
            {
                var texture = Resources.Load<Texture2D>("FishReference/portrait-" + role);
                Check(texture != null && texture.width == 57 && texture.height == 60, "actual reference portrait imported " + role);
            }
            var images = net.GetComponentsInChildren<Image>();
            Check(images.Single(i => i.name == "reference/crew/0/portrait").sprite.name == "reference/portrait-captain", "captain uses reference pixels");
            Check(images.Single(i => i.name == "reference/crew/1/portrait").sprite.name == "reference/portrait-deckhand", "deckhand uses reference pixels");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(net.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] CREW POLISH CHECKS PASSED");
        }
        public static void Capture()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetFullPath("Logs/crew016"));
            var g = Fixture(2); g.State.Coins = 1000; g.State.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
            g.State.Hire(g.State.Board.First(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman)); g.SyncCrew();
            for (int i = 0; i < 500 && !(g.Hand.Loitering && !g.Hand.Walking); i++) g.Tick(.05f);
            Shot(g, "lookout-desktop"); Shot(g, "lookout-reference", 1672, 941); Shot(g, "lookout-phone", 844, 390);
            for (int i = 0; i < 500 && !(g.Hand.Loitering && g.Hand.Walking); i++) g.Tick(.05f);
            Shot(g, "strolling-desktop");
            Fish(g);
            Until(g, () => g.Hand.Busy && g.Hand.BusyLabel == "Stowing", "stow capture");
            g.Tick(.5f); Shot(g, "stowing-desktop"); Shot(g, "stowing-phone", 844, 390);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/crew016");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            FirstVoyageChecks.Regression(); NetHaulChecks.Run(); NetEconomyChecks.Run(); Run(); Capture(); BuildPreview();
        }
        public static void ReviewAndBuildPreview()
        {
            Capture(); BuildPreview();
        }
        public static void BuildPreview()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("Catchline crew polish preview").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); g.SessionOnly = true;
            const string scene = "Assets/Scenes/Crew016Preview.unity";
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), scene);
            string output = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Crew016/Catchline.exe");
            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions { scenes = new[] { scene }, locationPathName = output,
                target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64, options = BuildOptions.None });
            Check(report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded, "separate crew preview built");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + output);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/FirstVoyageChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class FirstVoyageChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool condition, string message)
        {
            if (!condition) throw new Exception("[Catchline] First voyage check failed: " + message);
        }

        static void Until(VoyageGame game, Func<bool> done, string label, float timeout = 45f)
        {
            for (float t = 0f; t < timeout && !done(); t += .05f) game.Tick(.05f);
            Check(done(), label);
        }

        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
