            var game = new GameObject("First voyage check").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            game.Build();
            Check(game.FirstVoyage && game.LearningFishing && game.StrikeWindow == 1.5f, "gentle first catches");
            game.SetSail();
            Until(game, () => game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "arrives at sea");
            Check(game.VoyageHint().Contains("Tap Fish"), "first cast prompt");
            var rod = game.Rods[0];
            // Fail once on purpose: the normal early-strike cost and recovery still work.
            game.CommandRod(rod);
            Until(game, () => rod.State == RodState.Waiting, "first cast");
            Check(game.VoyageHint().Contains("bobber"), "waiting never says tap Fish");
            game.StrikeTap();
            Check(game.Stats.Spooked == 1 && rod.State == RodState.Unbaited, "early strike still costs bait");

            for (int n = 0; n < 100 && !game.HoldFull; n++)
            {
                // Manning recasts after an early strike; following catches need the real Fish command.
                if (game.CanCommandRod(rod)) game.CommandRod(rod);
                Until(game, () => rod.State == RodState.Bite, "bite");
                Check(game.VoyageHint().Contains("now"), "strike instruction at bite");
                game.StrikeTap();
                Until(game, () => game.OnDeck.Count > 0 && game.CanCarry(game.OnDeck[0]), "catch lands and can be carried");
                Check(game.VoyageHint().Contains("Carry"), "carry prompt");
                game.CommandCarry(game.OnDeck[0]);
                int target = n + 1;
                Until(game, () => game.Cooler.Count == target, "real pickup and stow path");
                if (n == 2) Check(!game.LearningFishing && Mathf.Approximately(game.StrikeWindow, game.State.BiteWindow), "normal timing after third landed fish");
            }
            Check(game.VoyageHint().Contains("Cooler full"), "return prompt");
            int securedCount = game.Cooler.Count;
            game.HeadHome();
            Until(game, () => game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour, "sale at harbour");
            Check(game.State.UnsoldCatch.Count == securedCount && game.State.LastSale == null, "arrival keeps the haul unsold");
            Check(game.SellHaul(), "captain explicitly sells haul");
            Check(game.State.LogCount[0] + game.State.LogCount[1] == securedCount && game.State.LastSale.Coins > 0, "sale and fish log");
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] First trip: {game.State.LastSale.Minutes:0.00} min, {game.State.LastSale.Coins} earned, {game.State.Coins} total; cooler costs {GameData.Upgrade(UpgradeId.BigCooler).Cost}");
            Check(!game.FirstVoyage && !game.LearningFishing, "tutorial stays completed after sale");
            int coins = game.State.Coins;
            Check(coins >= GameData.Upgrade(UpgradeId.BigCooler).Cost + GameData.BaitPackCost, "first full trip funds cooler plus replacement bait");
            Check(game.State.TryBuy(UpgradeId.BigCooler) && game.State.CoolerCapacity == 6, "first upgrade unlocks6kg");
            Check(!game.State.TryBuy(UpgradeId.BigCooler), "upgrade spends once");
            string save = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/first-voyage-test-save.json");
            try
            {
                game.State.Save(save);
                var loaded = GameState.Load(save);
                Check(loaded != null && loaded.LogCount[0] + loaded.LogCount[1] == securedCount, "learning progress survives save");
                Check(ArtLibrary.Get().UiTex("fish-herring") != null && ArtLibrary.Get().UiTex("fish-mackerel") != null, "catch portraits available");
            }
            finally { if (File.Exists(save)) File.Delete(save); }
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] FIRST VOYAGE CHECKS PASSED");
        }

        public static void Regression()
        {
            Run();
            SkiffChecks.Run();
            SkiffChecks.Automation();
            UrgentCrewChecks.Run();
            SalePayoutChecks.Run();
            FisherFeedbackChecks.Run();
            CoastalPolishChecks.Run();
            PtsWaterChecks.Run();
            MenuSystemChecks.Run();
            InterfaceChecks.Run();
            CompactFisherChecks.Run();
            NetChoiceChecks.Run();
            ReferenceHudChecks.Run();
            Check(ArtLibrary.Get().WaterShader != null && !ShaderUtil.ShaderHasError(ArtLibrary.Get().WaterShader), "water shader imports and compiles");
            Check(ArtLibrary.Get().WaterTextures.Length == 7 && ArtLibrary.Get().WaterTex("water-tile").wrapMode == TextureWrapMode.Repeat, "seven water/effect textures import with repeating sea tile");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] FIRST VOYAGE REGRESSION PASSED");
        }

        // Call on Catchline-sim only. This preview has a separate scene and never loads/saves real progress.
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            Regression();
            BuildPreview();
        }

        public static void BuildPreview()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Catchline preview").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            const string scenePath = "Assets/Scenes/FirstVoyagePreview.unity";
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, scenePath);
            string output = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/FirstVoyage/Catchline.exe");
            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions
            {
                scenes = new[] { scenePath }, locationPathName = output,
                target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64, options = BuildOptions.None,
            });
            if (report.summary.result != BuildResult.Succeeded) throw new Exception("Preview build failed: " + report.summary.result);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + output);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/FishArtChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Run only in Windows-owned scratch after water checks release it. No scene/player capture.
    public static class FishArtChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool ok, string message)
        { if (!ok) throw new Exception("[Catchline] Fish art check failed: " + message); }
        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            var parent = new GameObject("Isolated fish art check");
            try
            {
                foreach (var fish in GameData.Fish)
                {
                    string model = fish.Species == Species.Conger ? "CongerEel" : fish.Species.ToString();
                    var asset = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>("Assets/Art/Models/Fish/" + model + ".fbx");
                    Check(asset != null, model + " imported FBX");
                    int tris = asset.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>(true).Sum(m => m.sharedMesh.triangles.Length / 3);
                    Check(tris > 1000 && tris < 4000, model + " bounded triangle count");
                    string portrait = "fish-" + fish.Species.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();
                    var texture = ArtLibrary.Get().UiTex(portrait);
                    Check(texture != null && texture.width >= 256 && texture.width == texture.height * 2, portrait + " library/landscape import");
                    var instance = DeckFish.BuildModel(fish.Species, (fish.MinKg + fish.MaxKg) * .5f, parent.transform);
                    var renderers = instance.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>();
                    Check(renderers.Length > 0 && renderers.Length <= 2, model + " resolves authored mesh, not fallback");
                    var bounds = renderers[0].bounds;
                    foreach (var r in renderers) { bounds.Encapsulate(r.bounds); Check(r.sharedMaterials.All(m => m != null && m.shader != null), model + " material slots"); }
                    Check(Mathf.Max(bounds.size.x, bounds.size.z) > bounds.size.y * 1.7f, model + " lies on deck, not vertical");
                    Check(bounds.size.magnitude < 2f, model + " metre-scale bounds");
                    UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(instance.gameObject);
                }
            }
            finally { UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(parent); }
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] FISH ART IMPORT CHECKS PASSED; native HUD screenshots/listening/Mo acceptance still pending");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/FishContentChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Pure state checks. Run on Windows after water checks release scratch; never uses Mo's save.
    public static class FishContentChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool ok, string message)
        { if (!ok) throw new Exception("[Catchline] Fish content check failed: " + message); }

        public static void Run()
        {
            Check((int)Species.Cod == 6 && (int)Species.Whiting == 7, "legacy species IDs retained");
            Check(GameData.Fish.Length == 10, "ten species have definitions");
            for (int i = 0; i < GameData.Fish.Length; i++)
                Check((int)GameData.Fish[i].Species == i, "definition/enum index " + i);
            var all = GameData.Spots.SelectMany(s => s.Table).Select(e => e.species).Distinct().ToArray();
            Check(all.Length == GameData.Fish.Length, "every species is catchable in existing locations");
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                var worms = new GameState(4700 + spot); var squid = new GameState(4700 + spot);
                double wormValue = 0, squidValue = 0; int wormSmall = 0, squidSmall = 0;
                const int samples = 12000;
                for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++)
                {
                    var (a, ak) = worms.RollFish(spot, .65f);
                    var (b, bk) = squid.RollFish(spot, .65f + GameData.SquidPowerBonus, false, true);
                    Check(GameData.Spots[spot].Table.Any(e => e.species == a) && GameData.Spots[spot].Table.Any(e => e.species == b), "bait respects location");
                    var def = GameData.Get(b);
                    Check(bk >= def.MinKg + .35f * (def.MaxKg - def.MinKg) - .00001f && bk <= def.MaxKg, "squid specimen bounds");
                    wormValue += worms.ValueOf(new CaughtFish { Species = a, Kg = ak }, 0);
                    squidValue += squid.ValueOf(new CaughtFish { Species = b, Kg = bk }, 0);
                    if (a is Species.Herring or Species.Mackerel) wormSmall++;
                    if (b is Species.Herring or Species.Mackerel) squidSmall++;
                    var (poor, pk) = worms.RollFish(spot, 99, true, true);
                    var pd = GameData.Get(poor);
                    Check((poor is Species.Herring or Species.Mackerel) && pk <= pd.MinKg + .2f * (pd.MaxKg - pd.MinKg) + .00001f, "scraps stay small regardless of squid/power flags");
                }
                Check(squidValue / samples - 3 > wormValue / samples - 1, "squid improves expected landed net value at " + spot);
                if (wormSmall > 0) Check(squidSmall < wormSmall * .65, "squid reduces small-school catches at " + spot);
                if (spot == 0) Check(squidSmall < samples * .20, "harbour squid favours substantial whiting over small schools");
                Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Bait sample {GameData.Spots[spot].Name}: worms {wormValue / samples:0.00}, squid {squidValue / samples:0.00} gross/catch; small {wormSmall}/{squidSmall} of {samples}");
            }
            var s = new GameState(31) { Coins = 23, Bait = 0, Squid = 0 };
            Check(!s.BuyBait(true) && s.Coins == 23 && s.Bait == 0, "bulk shortfall changes nothing");
            s.Coins = 24;
            Check(s.BuyBait(true) && s.Coins == 0 && s.Bait == 30, "bulk worms exact payment");
            s.Coins = 36;
            Check(s.BuySquid(true) && s.Coins == 0 && s.Squid == 15, "bulk squid exact payment");
            s.UseSquid = false;
            Check(!s.WillUseSquid && s.TakeBait(out bool isSquid) && !isSquid && s.Bait == 29, "worm preference matches next-bait UI");
            s.Bait = 0;
            Check(s.WillUseSquid && s.TakeBait(out isSquid) && isSquid && s.Squid == 14, "squid fallback matches next-bait UI");
            s.Squid = 0;
            Check(s.TakeBait(out isSquid) && !isSquid && s.Coins == 0, "unlimited free fallback");
            Check(GameData.Get(Species.Herring).PricePerKg == 12 && GameData.Get(Species.Mackerel).PricePerKg == 13, "small-fish baseline stable");
            var market = new GameState(17) { Coins = 0 };
            var catchList = new[] { Species.Whiting, Species.RedMullet, Species.Turbot }
                .Select(f => new CaughtFish { Species = f, Kg = GameData.Get(f).MaxKg, StowedAt = 0 }).ToList();
            var quote = market.QuoteSale(catchList, 0);
            Check(market.Coins == 0 && market.LogCount.All(n => n == 0) && market.Market.All(m => m == 1), "new fish quote never changes live state");
            var sold = market.Sell(catchList, 0);
            Check(sold.FishSold == 3 && sold.Coins == quote.Coins && market.Coins == quote.Coins && market.Coins > 0, "new fish earn the quoted coins");
            Check(catchList.All(f => market.LogCount[(int)f.Species] == 1 && market.LogBest[(int)f.Species] == f.Kg), "new fish update their own log slots");
            string path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "catchline-fish-check-" + Guid.NewGuid() + ".json");
            try
            {
                File.WriteAllText(path, "{\"Version\":1,\"Coins\":123,\"Bait\":7,\"Squid\":4,\"Voyage\":5,\"UseSquid\":true,\"Owned\":[],\"Charts\":[],\"Board\":[],\"Offers\":[],\"Active\":[],\"Market\":[0.8,1,1,1,1,1,1],\"LogCount\":[2,0,0,0,0,0,1],\"LogBest\":[0.25,0,0,0,0,0,3]}");
                var loaded = GameState.Load(path);
                Check(loaded != null && loaded.Coins == 123 && loaded.LogCount[6] == 1 && loaded.LogCount[7] == 0 && loaded.Market[9] == 1, "seven-species save grows without erasure");
                loaded.UnsoldCatch.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Turbot, Kg = 4, StowedAt = -30 });
                loaded.Save(path); loaded = GameState.Load(path);
                Check(loaded != null && loaded.UnsoldCatch.Single().Species == Species.Turbot && loaded.UnsoldCatch.Single().Kg == 4, "new-species unsold haul roundtrips");
            }
            finally { if (File.Exists(path)) File.Delete(path); }
            RunBalance();
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] FISH CONTENT STATE CHECKS PASSED; native art/audio/UI review remains required");
        }

        // Expected per-cast value at the real basic fisher's strike rate. This excludes
        // crew fees/food/fuel and deliberately tests poor prices/old fish too.
        public static void RunBalance()
        {
            float hit = GameData.StrikeChance(Grade.Hand);
            foreach (var scenario in new[] { (name: "fresh", market: 1f, age: 0f),
                (name: "depressed", market: .75f, age: 180f), (name: "floor", market: .5f, age: 600f) })
            {
                for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
                {
                    var s = new GameState(8700 + spot);
                    for (int i = 0; i < s.Market.Length; i++) s.Market[i] = scenario.market;
                    double worms = 0, squid = 0;
                    const int samples = 10000;
                    for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++)
                    {
                        var (a, ak) = s.RollFish(spot, .65f);
                        var (b, bk) = s.RollFish(spot, .65f + GameData.SquidPowerBonus, false, true);
                        worms += s.ValueOf(new CaughtFish { Species = a, Kg = ak }, scenario.age);
                        squid += s.ValueOf(new CaughtFish { Species = b, Kg = bk }, scenario.age);
                    }
                    double w = hit * worms / samples - GameData.BaitPackCost / (double)GameData.BaitPackSize;
                    double q = hit * squid / samples - GameData.SquidPackCost / (double)GameData.SquidPackSize;
                    if (scenario.name == "fresh") Check(q > w && w > 0, "premium bait improves expected per-cast value after misses/cost at " + spot);
                    Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Cast EV {scenario.name}/{GameData.Spots[spot].Name}: worms {w:0.00}, squid {q:0.00} coins after bait; {hit * 100:0}% strikes");
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/FisherFeedbackChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class FisherFeedbackChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool ok,string message)
        {
            if(!ok)throw new Exception("[Catchline] First voyage check failed: fisherman feedback "+message);
        }
        static T Read<T>(VoyageGame game,string name)=>(T)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(name,BindingFlags.Instance|BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(game);
        public static void Run()
        {
            Check(Mathf.Approximately(GameData.StrikeChance(Grade.Hand),.8f),"basic fisherman strikes 80 percent");
            Check(GameData.StrikeChance(Grade.Experienced)>.8f && GameData.StrikeChance(Grade.Veteran)>GameData.StrikeChance(Grade.Experienced),"higher grades retain advantage");
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene,NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game=new GameObject("Quiet fisherman check").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly=true;game.Build();game.State.Coins=1000;
            game.State.Hire(game.State.Board.First(c=>c.Role==CrewRole.Fisherman));game.SyncCrew();game.SyncUpgrades();
            game.State.FoodMinutes=20;game.SetSail();
            for(int i=0;i<1000 && game.CurrentMode!=VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing;i++)game.Tick(.05f);
            game.Tick(2f);
            var captain=game.Rods[0];var crew=game.Rods[1];
            Check(crew.Fisher!=null,"fisherman owns separate rod");
            game.LandFish(captain,Species.Herring,.25f);
            float until=Read<float>(game,"catchUntil");
            game.Toast("Captain feedback stays");
            game.LandFish(crew,Species.Mackerel,.3f);
            Check(Read<float>(game,"catchUntil")==until && Read<float>(game,"lastCatchKg")==.25f,"crew catch never replaces captain catch card");
            Check(Read<string>(game,"toast")=="Captain feedback stays","crew catch never clears captain toast");
            Check(game.OnDeck.Last().CaughtByCrew && game.CurrentHaul().Count>=2,"crew fish still land and count in haul");
            crew.State=RodState.Bite;crew.FisherAttempted=true;
            int missed=game.Stats.Missed;
            game.Tick(2f);
            Check(game.Stats.Missed>missed,"forced missed crew bite simulated");
            Check(Read<string>(game,"toast")=="Captain feedback stays","missed crew bite never overwrites captain toast");
            game.LandFish(captain,Species.Pollock,1.1f);
            Check(Read<float>(game,"lastCatchKg")==1.1f,"captain catches still show rewards");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] FISHER FEEDBACK CHECKS PASSED: 80 percent baseline; crew catch/miss silent; captain feedback preserved; haul counts");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/FuelDepartureChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class FuelDepartureChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags H = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string reason) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Fuel/departure: " + reason); }
        static void Field(VoyageGame g, string n, object v) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(n, H).SetValue(g, v);
        static object Call(VoyageGame g, string n, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(n, H).Invoke(g, args);
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("Fuel021 isolated fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build(); g.StartNetBoatTrial(); return g;
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs/fuel021");
            var s = new GameState(12) { Hull = 2, Fuel = 0, Coins = 10 };
            Check(s.FuelCapacity == 10 && s.FuelFillCost == 10 && s.BuyFuel() && s.Fuel == 10 && s.Coins == 0, "ten minutes at former1coin/min provision cost");
            Check(!s.BuyFuel(), "full tank never charges");
            s.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.LunchTin); s.Coins = 10;
            Check(s.FuelCapacity == 20 && s.BuyFuel() && s.Fuel == 20 && s.Coins == 0, "owned old tin doubles tank without refilling for free");
            s.Fuel = 12.345f; s.Save("Logs/fuel021/save.json"); var loaded = GameState.Load("Logs/fuel021/save.json");
            Check(loaded.Fuel == s.Fuel && loaded.FuelCapacity == 20, "fractional fuel and tank persist exactly");
            s.Fuel = 0; s.Save("Logs/fuel021/save.json"); Check(GameState.Load("Logs/fuel021/save.json").Fuel == 0, "empty saved fuel stays empty");
            s.Fuel = 19; s.Coins = 0; Check(!s.BuyFuel() && s.Fuel == 19, "unaffordable fill atomic");
            var g = Fixture(); g.State.FoodMinutes = 0; Check(g.WorkMult == .7f, "empty food standard crew pace");
            g.State.FoodMinutes = 100; Check(g.WorkMult == .7f, "legacy food grants no speed bonus");
            g.State.Fuel = 10; g.Tick(60); Check(Mathf.Abs(g.State.Fuel - 9) < .003f, "one minute elapsed burns one minute independent of crew");
            g.State.Fuel = 1.001f; g.Tick(.2f); Check(g.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.SailingHome, "base tank automatically returns at10percent");
            for (int i = 0; i < 1000 && g.CurrentMode != VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour; i++) g.Tick(.05f);
            Check(g.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour && g.State.Fuel > 0 && g.State.Fuel < 1, "reserve completes home leg and dock never refills");
            Check(g.SailBlocker() != null, "reserve cannot relaunch");
            g.State.UnsoldCatch.Clear(); g.RequestDeparture(); g.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            Check(!g.InvokeInterface("depart/go") && g.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour, "go disabled until fuel restored");
            int coins = g.State.Coins, cost = g.State.FuelFillCost;
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("depart/refuel") && g.State.Coins == coins - cost && g.State.Fuel == 10, "manifest refill exact inventory and cost");
            g.PrepareInterface(1440, 900); int bait = g.State.Bait;
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("depart/worms/buy") && g.State.Bait == bait + GameData.BaitPackSize, "quick bait purchase real inventory");
            g.PrepareInterface(844, 390);
            Check(!g.InvokeInterface("supplies/deep-net/buy") && !g.InvokeInterface("port/sail"), "manifest excludes real shop/stale departure controls");
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("depart/go") && g.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.SailingOut, "explicit go launches once");
            g.State.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.LunchTin); g.Tick(30); g.State.Fuel = 2.001f; g.Tick(.2f);
            Check(g.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.SailingHome, "upgraded return threshold is10percent/two minutes");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] FUEL DEPARTURE CHECKS PASSED");
        }
        static void Capture()
        {
            var g = Fixture(); g.Showcase(-1, 0); g.State.DeepNetUses.AddRange(new[] { 2, 3 }); g.State.SelectedNet = NetKind.Deep;
            g.State.Fuel = 4.6f; g.State.Squid = 18; g.RequestDeparture();
            var shot = typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            void Shot(string n, int w, int h, float scale = 1) => shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../fuel021/" + n, w, h, scale });
            Shot("departure-desktop", 1440, 900); Shot("departure-phone", 844, 390); Shot("departure-phone-larger", 844, 390, 1.15f);
            g.State.Fuel = .8f; g.State.Coins = 0; Shot("departure-shortfall", 1440, 900);
            Field(g, "departureReady", false); g.StartNetBoatTrial(); Shot("fishing-fuel", 1440, 900);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary(); Run(); Capture(); WeightNetChecks.Regression(); BuildPreview();
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] FUEL021 COMBINED UPDATE VERIFIED");
        }
        public static void ReviewAndBuildPreview() { ArtImport.BuildLibrary(); Run(); WeightNetChecks.Review(); Capture(); BuildPreview(); }

        {

            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions { scenes = new[] { scene }, locationPathName = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Fuel021/Catchline.exe"), target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64 });
            Check(report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded, "combined Windows build");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Fuel021/Catchline.exe"));
        }

========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/HandlingPaceChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class HandlingPaceChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags H = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("PACE036: " + why); }
        static void Call(VoyageGame g, string n, params object[] a) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(n,H).Invoke(g,a);
        static float Until(Person p, Func<bool> done)
        {
            float t = 0;
            for (; t < 30 && !done(); t += .002f) p.Tick(.002f,1,Quaternion.identity);
            Check(done(), "action completes"); return t;
        }
        static void Work(Person p) => Until(p, () => p.Idle);
        static void Still(Person h, Vector3 pos)
        {
            var now = h.Root.localPosition;
            Check(!h.Walking && !h.Loitering && Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(now.x,now.z),new Vector2(pos.x,pos.z)) < .001f,
                "prep hand stays planted during work / empty input / refill");
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs/pace036"); ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene,NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("PACE036 isolated check").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();g.SessionOnly=true;g.Build();
            Check(g.StartPrepBoatTrial(), "trial starts");
            var hands = new[]{g.Hand}.Concat(g.ExtraPeople).Where(p=>p.Contract.Role==CrewRole.Deckhand).ToArray();
            var carrier=hands[0]; carrier.Stop();carrier.Speed=1;
            g.LandFish(g.Rods[0],Species.Herring,.2f);
            var fish=g.OnDeck.Single();carrier.Place(fish.Tile);Call(g,"Fetch",carrier,fish);
            float pickup=Until(carrier,()=>carrier.Carrying!=null);
            Check(pickup<=GameData.CompactPickupTime/3f+.01f,"actual delivery hand pickup at least3xfaster");
            carrier.Place(g.State.HullDef.HoldAccess);Call(g,"Stow",carrier);
            float release=Until(carrier,()=>carrier.Carrying==null);
            Check(release<=GameData.CompactStowTime/3f+.01f && g.Cooler.Count==1,"actual release3xfaster and fish safely stored");
            Check(VoyageGame.FishHandlingSeconds(g.Captain,GameData.CompactStowTime)==GameData.CompactStowTime,"captain timing preserved");
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] PACE036 measured grade1 actions: pickup={pickup:0.000}s release={release:0.000}s (previous1.4s/3s, same pace multiplier)");
            foreach(var h in hands) { h.Stop();h.Speed=1;g.SetDeckhandWork(h.Contract,DeckhandWork.Prep); }
            g.Cooler.Clear();g.State.Chum=0;
            // Empty input: walk once to distinct assigned positions, then stay there.
            foreach(var h in hands) { Call(g,"HandThink",h); Work(h);h.Tick(0,1,Quaternion.identity); }
            Check(hands[0].Tile!=hands[1].Tile,"distinct permanent work positions");
            var positions=hands.Select(h=>h.Root.localPosition).ToArray();
            for(int n=0;n<400;n++) for(int i=0;i<2;i++) { Call(g,"HandThink",hands[i]);hands[i].Tick(.02f,1,Quaternion.identity);Still(hands[i],positions[i]); }
            for(int n=0;n<24;n++) g.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish{Species=Species.Herring,Kg=.2f,StowedAt=g.SimTime});
            bool take=false,cut=false,put=false;
            for(int n=0;n<2400 && (g.Cooler.Any(f=>!f.Prepared)||g.PrepWorkersActive>0);n++)
                for(int i=0;i<2;i++)
                {
                    var h=hands[i]; if(h.Idle)Call(g,"HandThink",h);
                    h.Tick(.02f,1,Quaternion.identity);Still(h,positions[i]);
                    take |= h.BusyLabel=="Taking fish";cut |= h.BusyLabel is "Making chum" or "Preparing fish";put |= h.BusyLabel=="Putting fish away";
                }
            Check(take&&cut&&put,"real take / cut / place phases run without a walk");
            Check(g.State.Chum==10 && g.Cooler.Count==14 && g.Cooler.All(f=>f.Prepared),"stationary crew fill ten then prepare remainder");
            g.State.TakeBait(out _,out _);g.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish{Species=Species.Herring,Kg=.2f,StowedAt=g.SimTime});
            Call(g,"HandThink",hands[1]);
            Check(!hands[1].Walking && hands[1].Busy,"refill starts immediately at same standing position");
            while(!hands[1].Idle){hands[1].Tick(.02f,1,Quaternion.identity);Still(hands[1],positions[1]);}
            Check(g.State.Chum==10,"stationary refill completes");
            // Releasing the other station must not move the remaining worker.
            g.SetDeckhandWork(hands[0].Contract,DeckhandWork.Fish);
            g.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish{Species=Species.Cod,Kg=5,StowedAt=g.SimTime});
            Call(g,"HandThink",hands[1]);Check(!hands[1].Walking,"remaining prep hand keeps own slot when other leaves");Work(hands[1]);Still(hands[1],positions[1]);
            Check(g.Cooler.All(f=>f.Prepared) && g.Cooler.All(f=>g.State.Freshness(f,999999)==1),"prepared no-spoil preserved");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            PrepOrdersChecks.Run();
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PACE036 CHECKS PASSED");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/pace036");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuild(){Run();PrepFisherChecks.BuildPreview();}
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/InterfaceChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class InterfaceChecks
    {
        static readonly BindingFlags Private = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Set(VoyageGame game, string field, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(field, Private).SetValue(game, value);
        static void Check(bool pass, string message) { if (!pass) throw new Exception("Coastal interface check failed: " + message); }
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            var game = new GameObject("Isolated coastal UI fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true; game.Build(); Set(game, "showTitle", false);
            game.Tick(.01f);
            return game;
        }
        static void Shot(VoyageGame game, string path, int width, int height, float textScale = 0)
        {
            var rt = new RenderTexture(width, height, 24, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
            game.Cam.targetTexture = rt;
            game.Cam.aspect = (float)width / height;
            game.PrepareInterface(width, height, textScale);
            Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();
            var request = new RenderPipeline.StandardRequest { destination = rt };
            if (RenderPipeline.SupportsRenderRequest(game.Cam, request)) RenderPipeline.SubmitRenderRequest(game.Cam, request);
            else game.Cam.Render();
            var uiTarget = new RenderTexture(width, height, 24, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
            game.GetComponentInChildren<CoastalCanvas>().RenderCapture(uiTarget);
            var composite = new Material(Shader.Find("Hidden/Catchline/Coastal UI Composite"));
            Graphics.Blit(uiTarget, rt, composite);
            var previous = RenderTexture.active; RenderTexture.active = rt;
            var texture = new Texture2D(width, height, TextureFormat.RGB24, false);
            texture.ReadPixels(new Rect(0, 0, width, height), 0, 0); texture.Apply();
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(path)); File.WriteAllBytes(path, texture.EncodeToPNG());
            // A completed scene-only render must never be reported as HUD proof.
            int bright = 0;
            for (int y = 0; y < 16; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 16; x++)
            {
                float px = game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour ? .35f + x * .035f : .04f + x * .009f;
                float py = game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour ? .12f + y * .045f : .77f + y * .010f;
                Color sample = texture.GetPixel(Mathf.RoundToInt(width * px), Mathf.RoundToInt(height * py));
                if (sample.r > .8f && sample.g > .75f && sample.b > .6f) bright++;
            }
            Check(bright > 85, "native interface surface is present in capture");
            RenderTexture.active = previous; game.Cam.targetTexture = null;
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(texture); UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(rt);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(uiTarget); UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(composite);
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            var game = Fixture();
            game.State.Coins = 100;
            game.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("shop/tab/Supplies"), "supplies tab is a real retained button"); game.Tick(0);
            int bait = game.State.Bait, coins = game.State.Coins;
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("supplies/worms/buy"), "worm purchase is reachable");
            Check(game.State.Bait == bait + GameData.BaitPackSize && game.State.Coins == coins - GameData.BaitPackCost, "UI purchase charges exactly once");
            game.State.Coins = 0; game.Tick(0);
            Check(!game.InvokeInterface("supplies/squid/buy") && game.State.Coins == 0, "unaffordable control disabled");
            game.State.Coins = 100; game.InvokeInterface("shop/tab/Chandlery"); game.Tick(0);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("upgrade/BigCooler/buy"), "upgrade button bound"); game.Tick(0);
            Check(game.State.Has(UpgradeId.BigCooler) && !game.InvokeInterface("upgrade/BigCooler/buy"), "owned upgrade cannot be bought twice");
            Check(!game.InvokeInterface("refit/buy"), "locked refit disabled");
            game.InvokeInterface("shop/tab/Market"); game.State.UnsoldCatch.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .3f, StowedAt = 0 }); game.Tick(0);
            coins = game.State.Coins;
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("market/sell") && game.State.Coins > coins, "sell control pays the saved haul"); game.Tick(0);
            Check(!game.InvokeInterface("market/sell"), "sold haul cannot be sold twice");
            game.Showcase(0, 0); game.Tick(.01f);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("rod/0"), "world fishing control bound");
            game.Tick(.1f);
            Check(game.Manning != null, "rod command reaches gameplay");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] COASTAL INTERFACE CHECKS PASSED (native Canvas controls/state; no physical pointer claim)");
        }
        public static void Capture()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            FishArtChecks.Run();
            Run();
            var game = Fixture();
            game.State.Coins = 380;
            string folder = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/hud008");
            foreach (VoyageGame.HudTab tab in Enum.GetValues(typeof(VoyageGame.HudTab)))
            {
                Set(game, "hudTab", tab); game.Tick(0);
                Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "shop-" + tab + "-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
                Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "shop-" + tab + "-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) game.State.UnsoldCatch.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Mackerel, Kg = .4f, StowedAt = 0 });
            Set(game, "hudTab", VoyageGame.HudTab.Market); game.Tick(0);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "market-haul-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
            game.Showcase(0, 0); game.Tick(.1f);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "fishing-idle-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "fishing-idle-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            game.CommandRod(game.Rods[0]);
            for (int i = 0; i < 500 && game.MinigameRod?.State != RodState.Bite; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
            Check(game.MinigameRod?.State == RodState.Bite, "capture reaches actual bite");
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "fishing-bite-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "fishing-bite-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            game.StrikeTap();
            for (int i = 0; i < 300 && game.Stats.Landed == 0; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
            game.Tick(.5f);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "fishing-catch-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "fishing-catch-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            game = Fixture(); game.State.Coins = 5000; game.State.BestVoyageFish = GameData.SkiffFishGoal;
            Check(game.RefitSkiff(), "crew capture refit"); game.Tick(2);
            for (int i = 0; i < 3 && game.State.Board.Count > 0; i++) game.State.Hire(game.State.Board[0]);
            game.SyncCrew(); game.Showcase(1, 0); game.Tick(.1f);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "skiff-crew-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            Shot(game, Path.Combine(folder, "skiff-crew-large-phone.png"), 844, 390, 1.15f);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to " + folder + " (actual native Canvas and world, isolated fixtures)");
        }
        public static void CaptureSingle()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = Fixture();
            Shot(game, Path.GetFullPath("Logs/hud008/render-diagnostic.png"), 1440, 900);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to render-diagnostic");
        }
    }

========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/MenuSystemChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class MenuSystemChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Private = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool pass, string label) { if (!pass) throw new Exception("Menu/system check failed: " + label); }
        static T Read<T>(VoyageGame game, string field) => (T)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(field, Private).GetValue(game);
        static object Call(VoyageGame game, string method, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(method, Private).Invoke(game, args);

        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Isolated menu/system check").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            game.Build();
            typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("showTitle", Private).SetValue(game, false);
            var state = game.State;
            state.Coins = 1000;
            Check(state.Hire(state.Board.First(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand)), "hire fixture deckhand");
            game.SyncCrew();
            state.FoodMinutes = 100;
            game.SetSail();
            for (int i = 0; i < 500 && game.CurrentMode != VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
            Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "fixture reaches sea through normal route");
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) game.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .45f, StowedAt = game.SimTime });
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) game.LandFish(game.Rods[0], Species.Herring, .25f);
            game.Captain.Carrying = game.OnDeck[0]; game.OnDeck.RemoveAt(0);
            game.Hand.Carrying = game.OnDeck[0]; game.OnDeck.RemoveAt(0);
            Check(game.CurrentHaul().Count == 13 && game.UnstowedHaul().Count == 5 && game.DepartureOverflow() == 3, "carried/deck fish counted once; exact three-fish loss");
            state.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.BigCooler);
            Check(game.DepartureOverflow() == 0 && state.CoolerCapacity == 6, "larger cooler prevents overflow");
            state.Hull = 1;
            Check(game.DepartureOverflow() == 0 && state.CoolerCapacity == 27, "skiff capacity used");
            state.Hull = 0; state.Owned.Remove(UpgradeId.BigCooler);
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) game.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .25f, StowedAt = game.SimTime });
            Check(game.DepartureOverflow() == 5, "full cooler exposes all unstowed fish");
            game.Cooler.RemoveRange(8, 2);

            state.Active.Clear();
            state.Active.Add(new Contract { Species = Species.Herring, Count = 9, VoyagesLeft = 3, Reward = 50 });
            int coins = state.Coins;
            float[] market = (float[])state.Market.Clone();
            var secured = state.QuoteSale(game.Cooler, game.SimTime);
            var total = state.QuoteSale(game.CurrentHaul(), game.SimTime);
            Check(secured.ContractCoins == 0 && total.ContractCoins == 50, "pending contract bonus is separate from secured value");
            Check(state.Coins == coins && state.Active[0].Delivered == 0 && market.SequenceEqual(state.Market), "quotation never sells or changes the live market/contracts");
            Call(game, "RequestHeadHome");
            Check(Read<bool>(game, "confirmHeadHome") && Read<bool>(game, "paused") && game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "player overflow request waits for explicit choice");
            float clock = game.SimTime, food = state.FoodMinutes;
            for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) Call(game, "Update");
            Check(game.SimTime == clock && state.FoodMinutes == food && game.CurrentHaul().Count == 13, "real Update respects the modal pause without losing fish");
            Call(game, "CancelHeadHome");
            Check(!Read<bool>(game, "paused") && !Read<bool>(game, "confirmHeadHome") && game.CurrentHaul().Count == 13, "cancel preserves every fish and resumes");
            Call(game, "RequestHeadHome"); Call(game, "ConfirmHeadHome");
            Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.SailingHome && game.Cooler.Count == 10 && game.OnDeck.Count == 0 && game.Captain.Carrying == null && game.Hand.Carrying == null && game.ExtraPeople.All(p => p.Carrying == null), "confirmation keeps capacity and normal carried-first disposal");
            for (int i = 0; i < 500 && game.CurrentMode != VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
            Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour && state.UnsoldCatch.Count == 10, "return secures ten fish for explicit sale");
            Check((bool)Call(game, "ShopHasNews", VoyageGame.HudTab.Market), "unsold haul attracts attention to Market");
            Check(game.SellHaul(), "explicit sale succeeds");
            int paid = state.Coins;
            Check(paid > coins && !game.SellHaul() && state.Coins == paid, "sale pays once");

            var fish = new System.Collections.Generic.List<CaughtFish> { new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .25f, StowedAt = 0 } };
            Check(game.FreshnessStatus(fish, 59).Contains("1s"), "freshness countdown before grace boundary");
            Check(game.FreshnessStatus(fish, 60).Contains("loses 7%"), "depreciation explanation at grace boundary");
            Check(Mathf.Approximately(state.Freshness(fish[0], 120), .93f) && game.FreshnessStatus(fish, 9999).Contains("minimum"), "freshness rate and minimum preserved");
            state.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.IceBox);
            Check(game.FreshnessStatus(fish, 9999).Contains("Iced"), "ice preserves full value");
            state.Coins = state.Bait = state.Squid = 0;
            Check(!(bool)Call(game, "ShopHasNews", VoyageGame.HudTab.Supplies) && state.TakeBait(out _), "free scraps work without false unaffordable Supplies badge");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] MENU AND HARBOUR SYSTEM CHECKS PASSED (native state/input methods; visual controls still need player review)");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/NetChoiceChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Isolated scratch runner; not executed in the main project or against a player's save.
    public static class NetChoiceChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Private = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string message)
        { if (!ok) throw new Exception("[Catchline] Net choice check failed: " + message); }
        static object Call(VoyageGame game, string method, params object[] args)
            => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(method, Private).Invoke(game, args);
        static void Set(VoyageGame game, string property, object value)
            => typeof(VoyageGame).GetProperty(property).GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(game, new[] { value });
        static void Work(Person person)
        { for (int i = 0; i < 800 && !person.Idle; i++) person.Tick(.05f, 1f, Quaternion.identity); }

        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Net choice regression").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            game.AutoPlay = true;
            game.Build();
            game.State.Hull = 2;
            Call(game, "RebuildBoat", true);
            Set(game, "CurrentMode", VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing);
            Set(game, "ActiveSpot", 0);
            var a = game.Captain;
            a.Stop();
            Check(game.SetNet(), "captain starts setting");
            Check(!game.SetNet() && !game.HaulNet(), "one actor claims the net");
            var other = new Person("Other hand", false, Vector3.zero) { Contract = new CrewMember { Role = CrewRole.Deckhand } };
            Check(!game.TryNetWork(other), "second hand cannot claim occupied winch");
            a.Stop();
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Stowed, "cancel walking set rolls back");
            Check(game.SetNet(), "can retry canceled set");
            Work(a);
            NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(game);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking && a.Idle, "soak releases worker");
            float soak = game.NetRemaining;
            Check(soak > 19.8f && soak <= 20f, "compact soak remains about20 seconds after final set tap");
            Call(game, "TickNet", soak - 1f);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking && game.NetCatch == 0, "no early yield");
            Call(game, "TickNet", 0f);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(game.NetRemaining - 1f) < .001f, "zero time freezes soak");
            Call(game, "TickNet", 1f);
            int count = game.NetCatch;
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Ready && count > 0 && game.NetCatchKg >= game.NetWeightRange(game.ActiveSpot, false).x, "bounded yield at 20 seconds");
            Call(game, "TickNet", 600f);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Ready && game.NetCatch == count, "ready waits without automatic haul or extra catch");
            for (int i = 0; i < game.State.CoolerCapacity; i++) game.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Kg = 1f });
            Check(game.HaulNet(), "ready net can be recovered with full hold");
            a.Stop();
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Ready && game.NetCatch == count, "canceled haul retains catch");
            Check(game.HaulNet(), "retry haul");
            Work(a);
            NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainHaul(game);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Stowed && game.NetCatch == 0, "haul consumed once");
            Check(game.NetBasketCount == count && game.OnDeck.All(f => f.FromNetBasket), "haul lands in contained backlog");
            Check(game.HoldFull && !game.SetNet(), "haul does not bypass full hold and basket blocks next set");
            game.Cooler.Clear();
            int landed = game.Stats.Landed;
            Call(game, "TickNet", 1000f);
            Check(game.Stats.Landed == landed && !game.HaulNet(), "completed haul cannot repeat");
            foreach (var fish in game.OnDeck) UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(fish.Root.gameObject);
            game.OnDeck.Clear();
            Check(game.SetNet(), "empty basket releases next cycle");
            // Exercise cancellation after arrival, while the active setting game owns the winch.
            for (int i = 0; i < 400 && a.Walking; i++) a.Tick(.01f, 1f, Quaternion.identity);
            Check(a.Busy && game.NetGameActive && game.NetGameSetting, "active setting game reached");
            a.Stop();
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Stowed && game.NetCatch == 0, "cancel active set releases ownership");
            // Yield uses the location table, never paid-bait power or nonlocal trophies.
            for (int seed = 0; seed < 100; seed++)
            {
                var state = new GameState(seed) { Hull = 2 };
                Set(game, "State", state);
                for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
                {
                    Set(game, "ActiveSpot", spot);
                    Call(game, "RollCompactCatch");
                    var fish = (System.Collections.Generic.List<(Species species, float kg)>)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("netFish", Private).GetValue(game);
                    Check(fish.Count > 0 && fish.Sum(f => f.kg) > 0, "all seeded locations yield 3-5");
                    Check(fish.All(f => GameData.Spots[spot].Table.Any(e => e.species == f.species)), "catch remains local");
                    Check(fish.All(f => f.species == Species.Herring || f.species == Species.Mackerel || f.species == Species.Whiting || f.species == Species.Pollock), "schooling species only");
                }
            }
            Call(game, "ResetNet");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] NET CHOICE CHECKS PASSED: claim, cancellation, soak, bounded catch, ready waiting and safe basket");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/NetEconomyChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Run only in isolated Catchline-sim. Temporary saves never target the player path.
    public static class NetEconomyChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Net economy check failed: " + why); }
        static void Field(VoyageGame game, string key, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(key, Hidden).SetValue(game, value);
        static void Property(VoyageGame game, string key, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetProperty(key).GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(game, new[] { value });
        public static void Run()
        {
            Check(GameData.NetReplacementCost == 30 && GameData.NetInventoryCapacity == 4, "approved provisional replacement price and capacity");
            var state = new GameState(51);
            Check(state.NetInventory == 3, "new game has one fitted net and two reserves");
            state.Coins = 29;
            Check(!state.BuyNet() && state.Coins == 29 && state.NetInventory == 3, "insufficient funds change neither resource");
            state.Coins = 30;
            Check(state.BuyNet() && state.Coins == 0 && state.NetInventory == 4, "exact funds buy one usable net once");
            state.Coins = 1000;
            Check(!state.BuyNet() && state.Coins == 1000 && state.NetInventory == 4, "full rack rejects purchase without spending");
            state.NetInventory = 0; state.Coins = 30;
            Check(state.BuyNet() && state.NetInventory == 1 && state.Coins == 0, "exhausted stock can recover with replacement");
            state.NetInventory = 0; state.Coins = 150;
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) Check(state.BuyNet(), "purchase up to rack capacity");
            Check(state.NetInventory == 4 && state.Coins == 30 && !state.BuyNet() && state.Coins == 30, "repeated purchases charge exactly four times");
            state.NetInventory = -1; state.Coins = 30;
            Check(!state.BuyNet() && state.NetInventory == -1 && state.Coins == 30, "invalid runtime stock cannot become a paid negative inventory");
            state.NetInventory = 0; state.Coins = -1;
            Check(!state.BuyNet() && state.NetInventory == 0 && state.Coins == -1, "negative funds cannot purchase");
            state.Hull = 0; state.Coins = 30;
            Check(state.BuyNet() && state.Coins == 0 && state.NetInventory == 1, "domain follows other supplies without embedding harbour/hull mode");

            string path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "catchline-net-economy-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".json");
            try
            {
                for (int inventory = 0; inventory <= GameData.NetInventoryCapacity; inventory++)
                {
                    state.Hull = 2; state.Coins = 123; state.NetInventory = inventory;
                    state.Save(path); var loaded = GameState.Load(path);
                    Check(loaded != null && loaded.NetInventory == inventory && loaded.Coins == 123 && loaded.Hull == 2, "each saved stock including exhausted zero round trips");
                }
                string json = File.ReadAllText(path);
                File.WriteAllText(path, Regex.Replace(json, "\"NetInventory\"\\s*:\\s*-?\\d+", "\"NetInventory\":999"));
                Check(GameState.Load(path)?.NetInventory == 4, "oversize saved stock clamps to capacity");
                File.WriteAllText(path, Regex.Replace(json, "\"NetInventory\"\\s*:\\s*-?\\d+", "\"NetInventory\":-2"));
                Check(GameState.Load(path)?.NetInventory == 0, "negative saved stock clamps to zero");
                // Existing Version1 saves predate net stock; this must not treat missing as exhausted.
                File.WriteAllText(path, "{\"Version\":1,\"Coins\":400,\"Bait\":15,\"Voyage\":3,\"FoodMinutes\":4,\"Owned\":[],\"Charts\":[],\"Board\":[],\"Offers\":[],\"Active\":[]}");
                var legacy = GameState.Load(path);
                Check(legacy != null && legacy.Hull == 0 && legacy.Coins == 400 && legacy.NetInventory == 3, "legacy save receives default stock without migrating hull or coins");
                legacy.NetInventory = 0; legacy.Save(path);
                Check(GameState.Load(path)?.NetInventory == 0, "legacy migration then exhausted save cannot replenish itself");
            }
            finally { if (File.Exists(path)) File.Delete(path); }

            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Isolated replacement-net supplies").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true; game.AutoPlay = false; game.Build();
            Property(game, "State", new GameState(18) { Hull = 2, NetInventory = 3, Coins = 29 });
            typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod("RebuildBoat", Hidden).Invoke(game, new object[] { true });
            Property(game, "CurrentMode", VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour);
            Field(game, "showTitle", false); Field(game, "paused", false); Field(game, "panelOpen", true);
            Field(game, "hudTab", VoyageGame.HudTab.Supplies); Field(game, "toastUntil", 0f);
            void Render() { game.PrepareInterface(1440, 900, 1); Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases(); }
            Render();
            Check(!game.InvokeInterface("supplies/net/buy") && game.State.NetInventory == 3 && game.State.Coins == 29, "native supplies control disabled without money");
            game.State.Coins = 30; Render();
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("supplies/net/buy") && game.State.NetInventory == 4 && game.State.Coins == 0, "native supplies action buys exact replacement");
            game.State.Coins = 1000; Render();
            Check(!game.InvokeInterface("supplies/net/buy") && game.State.Coins == 1000, "native full-rack control disabled");
            var catalog = game.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Single(t => t.name == "catalog/Supplies/content");
            var footer = game.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Single(t => t.name == "supplies/preference");
            float footerBottom = -footer.anchoredPosition.y + footer.rect.height;
            Check(catalog.rect.height >= footerBottom, "exact product count still includes scrollable footer");
            Property(game, "CurrentMode", VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing); Render();
            Check(!game.InvokeInterface("supplies/net/buy"), "shop purchase not available through sea UI");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] NET ECONOMY CHECKS PASSED: price/default/cap, exact purchase, zero recovery, migration/save bounds and native harbour-only supplies");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/NetHaulChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class NetHaulChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Net tap check failed: " + why); }
        static object Call(VoyageGame g, string key, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(key, Hidden).Invoke(g, args);
        static void Set(VoyageGame g, string key, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetProperty(key).GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(g, new[] { value });
        static void Tick(VoyageGame g, float dt) { Call(g, "TickNet", dt); g.Captain.Tick(dt, 1, Quaternion.identity); }
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            var g = new GameObject("Isolated tap net").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build();
            Set(g, "State", new GameState(37) { Hull = 2, FoodMinutes = 24 }); Call(g, "RebuildBoat", true);
            Set(g, "CurrentMode", VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing); Set(g, "ActiveSpot", 0);
            typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("showTitle", Hidden).SetValue(g, false);
            return g;
        }
        public static void ReachCaptainWinch(VoyageGame g)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < 800 && g.Captain.Walking; i++) g.Captain.Tick(.05f, 1, Quaternion.identity);
            Check(g.NetGameActive && g.Captain.Busy, "actual cancellable captain activity reached");
        }
        public static void FinishCaptainSet(VoyageGame g)
        {
            if (!g.NetGameActive) ReachCaptainWinch(g);
            Check(g.NetGameSetting, "setting uses its own timing game");
            for (int i = 0; i < 700 && g.NetGameActive; i++)
            {
                if (g.NetGamePhase >= .43f && g.NetGamePhase <= .55f) g.TapNetGame();
                Tick(g, .025f);
            }
            Check(!g.NetGameActive && g.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking, "three clean timed casts deploy net");
        }
        public static float FinishCaptainHaul(VoyageGame g)
        {
            if (!g.NetGameActive) ReachCaptainWinch(g);
            Check(!g.NetGameSetting, "haul uses tension game"); float elapsed = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 800 && g.NetGameActive; i++)
            {
                if (g.NetGameTension < .64f) g.TapNetGame();
                Tick(g, .025f); elapsed += .025f;
            }
            Check(!g.NetGameActive && g.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Stowed, "balanced tap rhythm recovers net");
            return elapsed;
        }
        static void Ready(VoyageGame g) { Call(g, "RollCompactCatch"); Set(g, "Net", VoyageGame.NetState.Ready); }
        sealed class FixedRoll : System.Random
        {
            readonly double value; public FixedRoll(double value) { this.value = value; }
            public override double NextDouble() => value;
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary(); EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = Fixture(); int nets = g.State.NetInventory;
            Check(g.SetNet(), "captain claims setting"); ReachCaptainWinch(g);
            Tick(g, 3); Check(g.NetGameProgress == 0 && g.NetGameSetting, "setting never completes without taps");
            g.CancelNetHaulGame(); Check(g.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Stowed && g.State.NetInventory == nets, "cancel preserves reusable net");
            Check(g.SetNet(), "retry setting"); FinishCaptainSet(g);
            Check(g.State.NetInventory == nets && g.NetRemaining >= 19.9f, "clean setting reuses fitted net and starts20s soak");
            Call(g, "TickNet", 20f); int fish = g.NetCatch; Check(fish > 0 && g.NetCatchKg >= g.NetWeightRange(g.ActiveSpot, false).x, "schooling mass yield");
            Check(g.HaulNet(), "claim haul"); ReachCaptainWinch(g); Tick(g, 4);
            Check(g.NetGameProgress == 0 && g.NetCatch == fish, "idle game cannot win");
            Check(g.TapNetGame(), "one tap accepted"); Tick(g, 0);
            Check(g.NetGameProgress == 0, "zero-dt pause does not consume queued input");
            Tick(g, .025f); float one = g.NetGameProgress; Tick(g, 2);
            Check(g.NetGameActive && g.NetGameProgress < one, "one press never repeats; cold progress slips away");
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) { g.TapNetGame(); Tick(g, 2); }
            Check(g.NetGameProgress < .1f && g.NetGameDamage == 0, "lazy isolated taps cannot win without nearing red");
            Set(g, "NetGameProgress", .7f); Set(g, "NetGameTension", .77f); Tick(g, .2f);
            float warmLoss = .7f - g.NetGameProgress;
            Set(g, "NetGameProgress", .7f); Set(g, "NetGameTension", .3f); Tick(g, .2f);
            Check(.7f - g.NetGameProgress > warmLoss * 3, "near-red tension loses far less progress than slack line");
            Set(g, "NetGameProgress", .7f); Set(g, "NetGameTension", .99f); Tick(g, .2f);
            Check(.7f - g.NetGameProgress > warmLoss * 10, "overload rapidly loses progress before tear");
            g.CancelNetHaulGame(); g.CancelNetHaulGame();
            Check(g.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Ready && g.NetCatch == fish && g.State.NetInventory == nets, "repeat cancel preserves exact catch/inventory");
            g.State.Rng = new System.Random(771); var untouched = new System.Random(771);
            Check(g.HaulNet(), "retry careful haul"); float elapsed = FinishCaptainHaul(g);
            Check(elapsed > 3 && elapsed < 15, "careful active haul takes seconds: " + elapsed);
            Check(g.NetBasketCount == fish && g.State.NetInventory == nets && g.Stats.Landed == fish, "whole batch once, successful net remains reusable");
            Check(g.State.Rng.Next() == untouched.Next(), "captain skill consumes no chance roll");
            Check(!g.TapNetGame(), "stale tap cannot duplicate catch");
            foreach (var f in g.OnDeck) UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(f.Root.gameObject); g.OnDeck.Clear();
            Call(g, "ResetNet"); Ready(g); Check(g.HaulNet(), "begin tear test"); ReachCaptainWinch(g);
            // Two deliberately overloaded pulls, with enough separation for real input.
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) { Set(g, "NetGameTension", .95f); Check(g.TapNetGame(), "overload press accepted after cooldown"); Tick(g, .25f); }
            Check(!g.NetGameActive && g.State.NetInventory == nets - 1 && g.NetCatch == 0 && g.NetBasketCount == 0, $"two overloaded taps destroy exactly one net and lose batch (active={g.NetGameActive}, stock={g.State.NetInventory}, catch={g.NetCatch}, basket={g.NetBasketCount}, mistakes={g.NetGameDamage})");
            Tick(g, 10); g.CancelNetHaulGame(); Check(g.State.NetInventory == nets - 1, "tear callback cannot consume another reserve");
            Check(g.SetNet(), "spare permits setting"); ReachCaptainWinch(g);
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
            {
                while (g.NetGamePhase > .15f || g.NetGamePhase < .02f) Tick(g, .025f);
                g.TapNetGame(); Tick(g, .5f);
            }
            Check(!g.NetGameActive && g.State.NetInventory == nets - 2, "two missed setting casts tear one reserve");
            g.State.NetInventory = 0; Check(!g.SetNet(), "no spare cannot deploy phantom net");
            // Exact worker probability boundary, exercised through actual claim/completion.
            foreach (double roll in new[] { .799999, .8 })
            {
                Call(g, "ResetNet"); g.State.NetInventory = 3; Ready(g); fish = g.NetCatch;
                g.State.Rng = new FixedRoll(roll);
                var hand = new Person("Net hand", false, Vector3.zero) { Grid = g.Grid, Contract = new CrewMember { Role = CrewRole.Deckhand } };
                hand.Build(g.Boat, g.State.HullDef.NetAccess, Palette.Oilskin, Palette.Accent);
                Check(g.TryNetWork(hand), "hand claims recovery"); hand.Tick(.01f, 1, Quaternion.identity);
                hand.Tick(5, 1, Quaternion.identity);
                Check(g.State.NetInventory == (roll < .8 ? 3 : 2), "worker80/20 boundary consumes net only on tear");
                Check(g.NetBasketCount == (roll < .8 ? fish : 0), "worker safe batch or lost batch matches risk");
                foreach (var f in g.OnDeck) UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(f.Root.gameObject); g.OnDeck.Clear();
                hand.Stop(); UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(hand.Root.gameObject);
            }
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] NET HAUL CHECKS PASSED: tap-only set/haul, skill band, cold/idle/overload decay, tears/reserves, cancellation/exact batch/no captain RNG, worker80/20 boundary");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/NetPlayChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class NetPlayChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static object Call(VoyageGame g, string name, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(name, Hidden).Invoke(g, args);
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Net play check failed: " + why); }
        static void Work(Person p) { for (int i = 0; i < 1000 && !p.Idle; i++) p.Tick(.05f, 1, Quaternion.identity); }
        static void Shot(VoyageGame g, string name, int w = 1440, int h = 900) => typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic)
            .Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../net015/" + name, w, h, 1f });
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            FirstVoyageChecks.Regression();
            NetEconomyChecks.Run();
            SeaClearanceChecks.Run();
            ChartChecks.Capture();
            Capture();
            BuildPreview();
        }
        public static void ReviewAndBuildPreview()
        {
            Capture();
            BuildPreview();
        }
        public static void BuildPreview()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("Catchline feedback preview").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            g.SessionOnly = true;
            const string scene = "Assets/Scenes/Net015Preview.unity";
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), scene);
            string output = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Net015/Catchline.exe");
            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions { scenes = new[] { scene }, locationPathName = output,
                target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64, options = BuildOptions.None });
            Check(report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded, "separate feedback preview built");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + output);
        }
        public static void Capture()
        {
            PreviewSetupChecks.Run();
            NetHaulChecks.Run();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Player feedback net/scene fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true; game.Build(); game.StartNetBoatTrial();
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetFullPath("Logs/net015"));
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                game.Showcase(spot, 0); game.Tick(.01f);
                SeaClearanceChecks.Verify(game, spot);
                Shot(game, "map-" + spot);
            }
            game.Showcase(0, 0);
            Shot(game, "stern-icon-laptop", 2560, 1440); Shot(game, "stern-icon-phone", 844, 390);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("net"), "stern net icon uses real set action"); Work(game.Captain);
            Shot(game, "setting-taps"); Shot(game, "setting-phone", 844, 390);
            NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(game);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking && game.NetRemaining == 20, "short20second soak");
            Call(game, "TickNet", 20f); game.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("net"), "stern icon starts ready haul");
            NetHaulChecks.ReachCaptainWinch(game);
            Shot(game, "winch-start"); Shot(game, "winch-phone", 844, 390);
            for (int i = 0; i < 500 && game.NetGameActive; i++)
            {
                if (game.NetGameTension < .64f) game.TapNetGame();
                game.Tick(.05f);
                if (i == 40) Shot(game, "winch-mid");
            }
            Check(!game.NetGameActive && game.NetUnloadActive && game.NetBasketCount >= 2, "haul starts automatic batch unloading");
            int netCount = game.NetBasketCount, before = game.Cooler.Count;
            for (int i = 0; i < 400 && game.NetUnloadActive; i++)
            {
                game.Tick(.05f);
                if (i == 32) Shot(game, "automatic-unloading");
            }
            Check(!game.NetUnloadActive && game.NetBasketCount == 0 && game.Cooler.Count >= before + netCount, "whole net batch automatically enters storage without a single Carry command");
            Shot(game, "unloaded");
            // Two simultaneous deliveries deliberately share the same hold-access tile.
            game.Captain.Stop(); game.Hand.Stop();
            foreach (var p in new[] { game.Captain, game.Hand })
            {
                p.Place(game.State.HullDef.HoldAccess);
                p.Carrying = new DeckFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .3f, CaughtAt = game.SimTime, Root = new GameObject("Concurrent delivery").transform };
                p.Carrying.Root.SetParent(p.Root, false);
                Call(game, "Stow", p); p.Tick(.01f, 1, Quaternion.identity);
            }
            Check(game.Captain.Busy && game.Hand.Busy && game.Captain.BusyLabel == "Stowing" && game.Hand.BusyLabel == "Stowing", "both actors stow at once without a hold lock");
            before = game.Cooler.Count; Work(game.Captain); Work(game.Hand);
            Check(game.Cooler.Count == before + 2, "concurrent deliveries each count exactly once");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] NET PLAY CHECKS PASSED: clear hull-growth corridor, stern icon,20s soak,actual winch,automatic batch storage,concurrent deliveries");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/net015");
        }
    }
}

========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/PrepFisherChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class PrepFisherChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags H = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("TIER033: " + why); }
        static void Field(VoyageGame g, string n, object v) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(n, H).SetValue(g, v);
        static void Call(VoyageGame g, string n, params object[] args) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(n, H).Invoke(g, args);
        static void Work(Person p) { for (int n = 0; n < 1600 && !p.Idle; n++) p.Tick(.05f, 1, Quaternion.identity); }
        static Transform Find(VoyageGame g, string name) => g.Boat.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true).Single(t => t.name == name);
        static void Deck(VoyageGame g, string variant)
        {
            var h = g.State.HullDef;
            var mesh = Find(g, "Hull").GetComponent<MeshFilter>();
            Check(mesh.sharedMesh.isReadable, "player can read geometry for routing");
            var points = mesh.sharedMesh.vertices.Select(v => g.Boat.InverseTransformPoint(mesh.transform.TransformPoint(v))).ToArray();
            var size = new Vector3(points.Max(v=>v.x)-points.Min(v=>v.x),points.Max(v=>v.y)-points.Min(v=>v.y),points.Max(v=>v.z)-points.Min(v=>v.z));
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] TIER033 {variant} hull={size}, walkable={g.CompactWalkableTiles}; prep={string.Join(";",g.PrepWorkerTiles)}");
            Check(Mathf.Abs(size.x - 6.8f)<.01f && Mathf.Abs(size.z - 3.5f)<.01f && size.y<1.2f,"approved dimensions upright at native metre scale");
            for (int i=0;i<3;i++)
            {
                var actual=g.Boat.InverseTransformPoint(Find(g,"RodSocket_"+(i+1)).position);
                Debug.Log($"[Catchline] TIER033 socket{i} {actual}");
                Check(Vector3.Distance(actual,h.RodBases[i])<.006f,"rod attached to exact socket"+i);
            }
            Check(!g.Boat.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true).Any(t=>t.name.StartsWith("T3 ") || t.name.StartsWith("T4 prep hand")),"no comparison statues in game");
            var jobs=new[]{h.Seat,h.HoldAccess,h.NetAccess}.Concat(h.CrewRest).Concat(h.RodTiles).Concat(g.PrepWorkerTiles).Distinct().ToArray();
            File.WriteAllLines("Logs/tier033/deck-"+variant+".txt",Enumerable.Range(0,g.Grid.Height).Reverse().Select(y=>string.Concat(Enumerable.Range(0,g.Grid.Width).Select(x=>jobs.Contains(new Vector2Int(x,y)) ? (g.Grid.IsBlocked(new Vector2Int(x,y))?'X':'J') : g.Grid.IsBlocked(new Vector2Int(x,y))?'#':'.'))));
            foreach(var a in jobs)
            {
                Check(!g.Grid.IsBlocked(a),"job must be physically clear "+variant+" "+a);
                foreach(var b in jobs) Check(g.Grid.FindPath(a,b)!=null,"connected jobs "+a+" to "+b);
            }
            Check(Find(g,"RawBuckets").gameObject.activeSelf==!g.State.Has(UpgradeId.IceBox),"bucket variant follows upgrade");
            Check(Find(g,"RawIceCoolers").gameObject.activeSelf==g.State.Has(UpgradeId.IceBox),"cooler variant follows upgrade");
            Check(Find(g,"PreparedHold").gameObject.activeInHierarchy,"rear prepared boxes always present");
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs/tier033");
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene,NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g=new GameObject("TIER033 isolated check").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();g.SessionOnly=true;g.Build();
            Check(g.StartPrepBoatTrial(),"isolated trial can start");
            Check(g.IsPrepFisher && g.IsNetFisher && g.State.CrewCount==4 && g.ExtraPeople.Count==3,"four real temporary workers plus captain");
            Check(g.Rods.Count==3 && g.State.CoolerCapacity==100,"retain existing capacity and rod count");
