            Deck(g,"buckets");
            g.Showcase(-1,0);g.State.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.IceBox);g.SyncUpgrades();
            // Storage footprints differ; rebuild the physical grid on a port upgrade.
            Deck(g,"ice-coolers");
            g.State.Owned.Remove(UpgradeId.IceBox);Call(g,"RebuildBoat",true);
            g.Showcase(0,0);Field(g,"toastUntil",0f);
            for(int i=0;i<2400;i++)
            {
                g.Tick(.05f);
                foreach(var p in new[]{g.Captain,g.Hand}.Concat(g.ExtraPeople)) Check(!g.Grid.IsBlocked(p.Tile),"live worker remains on clear deck "+p.Name+" "+p.Tile);
            }
            Check(g.Rods.Count(r=>r.Fisher!=null)==2 && g.Stats.Landed>3 && g.Cooler.Count>0,"real fishermen and deckhands fish and deliver on the new deck");
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] TIER033120s trace: landed={g.Stats.Landed}, stored={g.Cooler.Count}, kg={g.HoldKg}");
            foreach(var p in new[]{g.Captain,g.Hand}.Concat(g.ExtraPeople))p.Stop();
            // The timed trace may finish with a fish in hand. Finish that delivery
            // before testing a separate net allocation; Stop intentionally preserves cargo.
            foreach(var p in new[]{g.Captain,g.Hand}.Concat(g.ExtraPeople))
                if(p.Carrying!=null) Call(g,"TryStow",p);
            Call(g,"ResetNet");g.Cooler.Clear();
            Check(g.SetDeckhandWork(g.State.Deckhand,DeckhandWork.Nets),"tier4 net duty selectable");
            Call(g,"HandThink",g.Hand);Work(g.Hand);Check(g.Net==VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking,"hand reaches new stern and sets net");
            Call(g,"TickNet",20f);g.State.Rng=new System.Random(0);Call(g,"HandThink",g.Hand);Work(g.Hand);
            Check(g.NetBasketCount>0,"hand can haul into relocated basket");
            Check(g.OnDeck.Where(f=>f.FromNetBasket).All(f=>f.RestPos.x>5.3f),"net catch stays behind prepared boxes");
            // Clear net work so normal return can run without a modal confirmation.
            g.HeadHome();for(int n=0;n<1200 && g.CurrentMode!=VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour;n++)g.Tick(.05f);
            Check(g.CurrentMode==VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour,"trial docks");
            g.State.Save("Logs/tier033/isolated-state.json");var loaded=GameState.Load("Logs/tier033/isolated-state.json");
            Check(loaded.Hull==3 && loaded.CrewCount==4 && loaded.HullDef.Model=="PrepFisherV1","additive hull/four crew save roundtrip");
            Check(GameData.Hulls[0].Model=="Rowboat" && GameData.Hulls[1].Model=="Skiff" && GameData.Hulls[2].Model=="CompactFisherV1","old saved hull IDs unchanged");
            g.State.Coins=3000;g.PrepareInterface(844,390);Field(g,"hudTab",VoyageGame.HudTab.Chandlery);Field(g,"panelOpen",true);Field(g,"toastUntil",0f);g.PrepareInterface(844,390);
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("trial/netboat/buy") && g.State.Hull==2 && g.State.CrewCount==3,"tier3 trial downgrade respects three-crew cap");
            g.PrepareInterface(844,390);Check(g.InvokeInterface("trial/prepboat/buy") && g.IsPrepFisher,"native shipyard card selects tier4");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            CompactFisherChecks.Run();
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] TIER033 CHECKS PASSED");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/tier033");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuild()
        {
            Run();
            BuildPreview();
        }
        public static void BuildPreview()
        {
            var scene=EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene,NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g=new GameObject("Tier4 save-isolated preview").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();g.SessionOnly=true;
            const string path="Assets/Scenes/Tier033Preview.unity";EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene,path);
            var output=Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Tier033/Catchline.exe");
            var report=BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions{scenes=new[]{path},locationPathName=output,target=BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64,options=BuildOptions.None});
            Check(report.summary.result==BuildResult.Succeeded,"standalone player build "+report.summary.result);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] TIER033 PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: "+output);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/PrepOrdersChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class PrepOrdersChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags H = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("PREP035: " + why); }
        static void Call(VoyageGame g, string n, params object[] a) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod(n, H).Invoke(g, a);
        static void Work(Person p) { for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && !p.Idle; i++) p.Tick(.05f, 1, Quaternion.identity); }
        static CaughtFish Small(VoyageGame g) { var f = new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .2f, StowedAt = g.SimTime }; g.Cooler.Add(f); return f; }

        public static void Run()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs/prep035");
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("PREP035 isolated check").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build();
            Check(g.StartPrepBoatTrial(), "trial starts");
            var hands = new[] { g.Hand }.Concat(g.ExtraPeople).Where(p => p.Contract.Role == CrewRole.Deckhand).ToArray();
            foreach (var h in hands) { h.Stop(); Check(g.SetDeckhandWork(h.Contract, DeckhandWork.Prep), "two prep duties allowed"); }
            g.State.Chum = 9; var f1 = Small(g); var f2 = Small(g); var f3 = Small(g);
            var before = g.HoldKg;
            Check(g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]) && g.TryPrepWork(hands[1]), "both start");
            Check(g.ChumBeingMade == 1 && g.PrepWorkersActive == 2 && g.HoldKg == before, "one chum slot left reserved atomically; cargo mass retained");
            Work(hands[0]); Work(hands[1]);
            Check(g.State.Chum == 10 && g.Cooler.Count == 2 && g.Cooler.Count(f => f.Prepared) == 1, "stop exactly at ten; other hand prepares");
            Check(g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]), "at target falls back to prep"); Work(hands[0]);
            Check(g.Cooler.All(f => f.Prepared), "already prepared fish not processed twice");
            Check(!g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]), "no endless premium stacking");
            var prepared = g.Cooler[0];
            Check(g.State.Freshness(prepared, g.SimTime + 36000) == 1, "prepared never spoils without ice");
            int premium = g.State.ValueOf(prepared, g.SimTime + 36000);
            var raw = new CaughtFish { Species = prepared.Species, Kg = prepared.Kg, StowedAt = g.SimTime };
            Check(premium == Mathf.Max(1, Mathf.RoundToInt(raw.Kg * g.State.SpecimenPricePerKg(raw.Species, raw.Kg) * 1.25f)), "single 25 percent premium");
            Check(g.State.Freshness(raw, g.SimTime + 36000) == GameData.MinFreshness, "raw bucket fish still spoils");
            g.State.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.IceBox); Check(g.State.Freshness(raw, g.SimTime + 36000) == 1, "ice protects raw"); g.State.Owned.Remove(UpgradeId.IceBox);
            int worms = g.State.Bait, squidCount = g.State.Squid;
            Check(g.State.TakeBait(out bool squid, out bool chum) && chum && !squid && g.State.Chum == 9 && g.State.Bait == worms && g.State.Squid == squidCount, "premium chum really used before paid bait");
            var refill = Small(g); Check(g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]) && g.ChumBeingMade == 1, "consuming chum reactivates priority"); Work(hands[0]);
            Check(g.State.Chum == 10 && !g.Cooler.Contains(refill), "refill consumes one raw fish");
            g.State.Chum = 0; var cancelled = Small(g); Check(g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]), "reserve cancellation test");
            g.SetDeckhandWork(hands[0].Contract, DeckhandWork.Fish);
            Check(g.PrepWorkersActive == 0 && g.Cooler.Contains(cancelled) && !cancelled.Prepared && g.State.Chum == 0, "duty change releases reservation without consuming");
            g.SetDeckhandWork(hands[0].Contract, DeckhandWork.Prep);
            g.State.PrepOrders.ChumMaxKg = .1f;
            Check(g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]) && g.ChumBeingMade == 0, "ineligible chum input falls back to preparation"); Work(hands[0]);
            g.State.PrepOrders.ChumMaxKg = 1;
            g.ChangePrepPriority(); Small(g);
            Check(g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]) && g.ChumBeingMade == 0, "changed priority used by next job"); Work(hands[0]);
            // Preparation preserves existing deterioration; it never refreshes old fish.
            var old = Small(g); old.StowedAt = g.SimTime - 240;
            Check(g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]), "old fish can be prepared"); Work(hands[0]);
            float frozen = g.State.Freshness(old, g.SimTime);
            Check(old.Prepared && frozen < 1 && g.State.Freshness(old, 999999) == frozen, "preparation freezes existing quality rather than healing it");
            // Four temporary deckhands: third must be rejected by duty setter and job allocator.
            var spare = g.ExtraPeople.First(p => p.Contract.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman);
            spare.Contract.Role = CrewRole.Deckhand;
            Check(!g.SetDeckhandWork(spare.Contract, DeckhandWork.Prep), "third prep duty rejected");
            g.PrepareInterface(844,390); Check(g.InvokeInterface("prep/open"), "table opens in native UI");
            g.PrepareInterface(844,390); Check(g.InvokeInterface("prep/priority"), "native priority changes");
            g.State.Chum = 10; Small(g); g.TryPrepWork(hands[0]);
            g.HeadHome();
            Check(g.PrepWorkersActive == 0 && g.Cooler.Any(f => !f.Prepared), "return cancels unfinished job and keeps raw cargo");
            for (int i = 0; i < 1500 && g.CurrentMode != VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour; i++) g.Tick(.05f);
            Check(g.State.Chum == 0 && g.State.UnsoldCatch.Any(f => f.Prepared), "chum expires on return; prepared status survives docking");
            var path = "Logs/prep035/isolated-state.json"; g.State.Save(path); var loaded = GameState.Load(path);
            Check(loaded.Chum == 0 && loaded.UnsoldCatch.Any(f => f.Prepared) && loaded.PrepOrders.ChumTarget == 10, "prepared fish and orders save; chum does not");
            var preserved = loaded.UnsoldCatch.First(f => f.Prepared);
            Check(loaded.Freshness(preserved, 999999) == 1 && loaded.ValueOf(preserved,0) == loaded.ValueOf(preserved,999999), "saved prepared fish never loses value");
            var quote = loaded.QuoteSale(loaded.UnsoldCatch, 0); var sale = loaded.Sell(loaded.UnsoldCatch,0);
            Check(quote.Coins == sale.Coins, "premium quote equals actual payout");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            PrepFisherChecks.Run();
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PREP035 CHECKS PASSED");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/prep035");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuild() { Run(); PrepFisherChecks.BuildPreview(); }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/PreviewSetupChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class PreviewSetupChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string reason) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Preview setup check failed: " + reason); }
        static T Field<T>(VoyageGame game, string name) => (T)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(name, Hidden).GetValue(game);
        static void Set(VoyageGame game, string name, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(name, Hidden).SetValue(game, value);
        static void Route(VoyageGame game, bool home, int spot)
        {
            float duration = GameData.Spots[spot].RowSeconds * game.State.HullDef.TravelMultiplier;
            var incoming = Field<Transform>(game, "scenery");
            var outgoing = Field<Transform>(game, "oldScenery");
            game.Tick(duration * .4f);
            var startIn = incoming.localPosition; var startOut = outgoing.localPosition;
            game.Tick(duration * .02f);
            var bow = game.Boat.TransformDirection(Vector3.left); bow.y = 0;
            var direction = VoyageGame.RouteDirection(spot) * (home ? -1 : 1);
            Check(Vector3.Dot(bow.normalized, direction) > .99f, "physical bow faces voyage direction");
            Check(Vector3.Dot((incoming.localPosition - startIn).normalized, bow.normalized) < -.99f, "incoming scenery passes sternward " + spot + " home=" + home);
            Check(Vector3.Dot((outgoing.localPosition - startOut).normalized, bow.normalized) < -.99f, "outgoing scenery passes sternward " + spot + " home=" + home);
            game.Tick(duration);
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Native laptop trial fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true; game.Build();
            Check(game.StartNetBoatTrial(), "isolated setup runs");
            Check(game.IsNetFisher && game.State.Coins == 200 && game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing && !game.MenuOpen, "direct playable200coin net boat");
            Check(game.State.CrewCount == 3 && game.State.Crew.Count(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman) == 2 && game.Hand != null, "two fishers and one hand pre-hired");
            Check(game.State.Bait == 60 && game.State.FoodMinutes == game.State.FoodCapacity, "ready supplies");
            Check(game.SetNet(), "net immediately usable"); game.Captain.Stop();
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                game.Showcase(-1, 0); game.State.Charts.Add(spot); game.State.Fuel = 100;
                game.SelectedSpot = spot; game.SetSail(); Route(game, false, spot);
                Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "outbound arrives");
                game.HeadHome(); Route(game, true, spot);
                Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Harbour, "return arrives");
            }
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PREVIEW SETUP CHECKS PASSED: direct200coin net boat,3crew,supplies,forward cruise both directions across5maps");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            Run();
            FirstVoyageChecks.BuildPreview();
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/ProgressionLaunchChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class ProgressionLaunchChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags H = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string why) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("NET023: " + why); }
        static void Field(VoyageGame g, string n, object v) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(n, H).SetValue(g, v);
        static void State(VoyageGame g, GameState s) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetProperty("State").GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(g, new object[] { s });
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("NET023 isolated fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build(); g.StartNetBoatTrial(); return g;
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs/crew026");
            var s = new GameState(9) { Hull = 2, Coins = 4999 };
            Check(s.CoolerCapacity == 100 && s.NetCapacityKg == 10, "base tier3 hold100/net10");
            Check(!s.TryBuy(UpgradeId.NetSystem) && s.Coins == 4999 && s.NetCapacityKg == 10, "net upgrade shortfall atomic");
            s.Coins = 5000; Check(s.TryBuy(UpgradeId.NetSystem) && s.Coins == 0 && s.NetCapacityKg == 15, "exact5000 upgrade changes both net capacities");
            Check(!s.TryBuy(UpgradeId.NetSystem), "upgrade cannot charge twice");
            s.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.BigCooler); s.Save("Logs/crew026/state.json"); var loaded = GameState.Load("Logs/crew026/state.json");
            Check(loaded.CoolerCapacity == 150 && loaded.NetCapacityKg == 15 && loaded.Coins == 0, "owned upgrades persist without repurchase");
            Check(s.UpgradeCost(UpgradeId.BigCooler) == 3000 && s.UpgradeCost(UpgradeId.RodCarbon) == 3500 && s.UpgradeCost(UpgradeId.LunchTin) == 1500, "tier3 authoritative upgrade prices");
            var early = new GameState(9) { Coins = 10000 }; Check(early.UpgradeCost(UpgradeId.BigCooler) == 60 && !early.TryBuy(UpgradeId.NetSystem), "starter economy preserved and net system unavailable early");
            var refit = new GameState(2) { Hull = 1, Coins = 3999 };
            Check(!refit.RefitCompact() && refit.Coins == 3999, "real tier3 refit shortfall atomic");
            refit.Coins = 4000; refit.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.BigCooler); refit.UnsoldCatch.Add(new CaughtFish { Kg = 3 });
            Check(refit.RefitCompact() && refit.Coins == 0 && refit.Hull == 2 && refit.CoolerCapacity == 150 && refit.UnsoldCatch.Count == 1 && refit.Fuel == refit.FuelCapacity, "real progression to tier3 preserves owned upgrades/cargo");
            var a = new GameState(1) { Hull = 2 }; var b = new GameState(1) { Hull = 2 };
            for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) a.OnCatch(0, .1f); b.OnCatch(0, 10);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(a.Richness[0] - b.Richness[0]) < .0001f, "equal mass depletes equally regardless fish count");
            var g = Fixture();
            var report = new System.Text.StringBuilder("ground,capacity,method,meanFish,meanKg,meanFreshQuote\n");
            foreach (int spot in Enumerable.Range(0, GameData.Spots.Length))
            foreach (bool upgraded in new[] { false, true })
            {
                double[] values = new double[2], counts = new double[2];
                foreach (bool deep in new[] { false, true })
                {
                    double kg = 0, val = 0, count = 0;
                    for (int seed = 0; seed < 200; seed++)
                    {
                        var run = new GameState(seed) { Hull = 2, SelectedNet = deep ? NetKind.Deep : NetKind.Shallow };
                        if (upgraded) run.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.NetSystem); State(g, run);
                        typeof(VoyageGame).GetProperty("ActiveSpot").GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(g, new object[] { spot });
                        typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod("RollCompactCatch", H).Invoke(g, null);
                        var batch = (List<(Species species, float kg)>)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("netFish", H).GetValue(g);
                        Check(g.NetCatchKg <= run.NetCapacityKg + .0001f && g.NetCatchKg >= run.NetCapacityKg * .8f, "hard shared kg budget on every ground");
                        Check(batch.All(f => GameData.Spots[spot].Table.Any(e => e.species == f.species) && f.kg >= GameData.Get(f.species).MinKg && f.kg <= GameData.Get(f.species).MaxKg), "only honest local specimens");
                        var cargo = batch.Select(f => new CaughtFish { Species = f.species, Kg = f.kg }).ToList();
                        int quote = run.QuoteSale(cargo, 0).Coins; int sale = run.Sell(cargo, 0).Coins; Check(quote == sale, "hull-specific quote equals actual payout");
                        count += batch.Count; kg += g.NetCatchKg; val += quote;
                        if (spot == 0 && !deep) Check(batch.Count > 5, "school net genuinely exceeds old five-fish impression");
                    }
                    int index = deep ? 1 : 0; values[index] = val / 200; counts[index] = count / 200;
                    report.AppendLine($"{GameData.Spots[spot].Name},{(upgraded ? 15 : 10)},{(deep ? "deep" : "shallow")},{count/200:0.00},{kg/200:0.00},{val/200:0.00}");
                }
                Check(values[1] > values[0] && counts[0] > counts[1], "same net mass: deep earns more, shallow has more fish");
            }
            File.WriteAllText("Logs/crew026/net-balance.csv", report.ToString()); Debug.Log("[Catchline] CREW026 ACTUAL NET BALANCE:\n" + report);
            TripEconomy(); UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            Ui(); Debug.Log("[Catchline] NET023 PROGRESSION CHECKS PASSED");
        }
        static void TripEconomy()
        {
            var report = new System.Text.StringBuilder("composition,freshGross,agedGross,netAfterExampleCosts\n");
            foreach (bool mixed in new[] { false, true })
            {
                var cargo = new List<CaughtFish>();
                for (int i = 0; i < (mixed ? 125 : 250); i++) cargo.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .2f });
                for (int i = 0; i < (mixed ? 50 : 100); i++) cargo.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Mackerel, Kg = .5f });
                if (mixed) { for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) cargo.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Cod, Kg = 6 }); for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) cargo.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Conger, Kg = 7 }); }
                var s = new GameState(1) { Hull = 2 }; int fresh = s.QuoteSale(cargo, 0).Coins;
                int aged = s.QuoteSale(cargo, 180).Coins;
                // Illustrative expenses: fuel/crew/bait and10shallow or5shallow+5deep net cycles.
                report.AppendLine($"{(mixed ? "50/50 by kg" : "small")},{fresh},{aged},{aged-(mixed ? 332 : 245)}");
            }
            File.WriteAllText("Logs/crew026/trip-economy.csv", report.ToString()); Debug.Log("[Catchline] CREW026 ILLUSTRATIVE100KG TRIP ECONOMY (not measured play):\n" + report);
        }
        static void Ui()
        {
            var g = Fixture(); g.Showcase(-1, 0); g.State.Coins = 4999;
            Field(g, "hudTab", VoyageGame.HudTab.Chandlery); Field(g, "panelOpen", true); Field(g, "toastUntil", 0f);
            g.PrepareInterface(844, 390);
            Check(!g.InvokeInterface("upgrade/NetSystem/buy") && g.State.Coins == 4999, "real shop refuses4999");
            g.State.Coins = 5000; g.PrepareInterface(844, 390);
            Check(g.InvokeInterface("upgrade/NetSystem/buy") && g.State.Coins == 0 && g.State.NetCapacityKg == 15, "real shop charges5000 and upgrades both nets");
            g.State.Owned.Remove(UpgradeId.NetSystem); g.State.Coins = 6000; Field(g, "toastUntil", 0f);
            var shot = typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            void Shot(string n, int w, int h) => shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../crew026/" + n, w, h, 1f });
            foreach (var size in new[] { new Vector2Int(1440,900), new Vector2Int(844,390) })
            {
                g.PrepareInterface(size.x, size.y); var scroll = g.GetComponentsInChildren<UnityEngine.UI.ScrollRect>().Single(); Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();
                var card = g.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Single(t => t.name == "upgrade/NetSystem");
                scroll.verticalNormalizedPosition = 1 - Mathf.Clamp01((-card.anchoredPosition.y - 6) / (scroll.content.rect.height - scroll.viewport.rect.height));
                Shot("net-upgrade-" + size.x, size.x, size.y);
            }
            g.State.Coins = 200; g.RequestDeparture(); Shot("departure-100kg", 1440, 900);
            Field(g, "departureReady", false); g.StartNetBoatTrial();
            g.State.Deckhand = null; g.State.ExtraCrew.Clear(); g.SyncCrew(); foreach (var rod in g.Rods) rod.State = RodState.Ready;
            g.State.SelectedNet = NetKind.Shallow; g.State.Rng = new System.Random(12);
            Check(g.SetNet(), "manyfish real capture starts net"); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(g);
            typeof(VoyageGame).GetMethod("TickNet", H).Invoke(g, new object[] { 20f });
            Check(g.HaulNet(), "manyfish real capture hauls"); NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainHaul(g);
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && g.LastNetReceipt == null; i++) g.Tick(.05f);
            Check(g.LastNetReceipt != null && g.LastNetReceipt.Count > 5 && g.HoldKg <= 10.001f, "manyfish actual storage/capture");
            Shot("school-net-stowed", 1440, 900); Shot("school-net-stowed-phone", 844, 390);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuild()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary(); Run(); FuelDepartureChecks.Run(); WeightNetChecks.Regression(); BuildBoth();
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] CREW026 COMBINED REGRESSION PASSED");
        }
        public static void BuildBoth()
        {
            // Every release, including stat-only rebuilds, resolves and validates live water.
            PtsWaterChecks.Run();
            foreach (bool test in new[] { false, true })
            {
                EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
                var g = new GameObject(test ? "Tier3 isolated test" : "Persistent Catchline").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); g.SessionOnly = test;
                string scene = test ? "Assets/Scenes/Crew026Test.unity" : "Assets/Scenes/Crew026Real.unity";
                EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), scene);
                string output = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Crew026/" + (test ? "Test" : "Real") + "/Catchline.exe");
                var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions { scenes = new[] { scene }, locationPathName = output, target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64 });
                Check(report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded, "build " + (test ? "test" : "real"));
                Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + output);
            }
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/PrototypeTools.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Game;
using Catchline.Prototype;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class PrototypeTools
    {
        const string ScenePath = "Assets/Scenes/DeckPrototype.unity";

        // Creates the prototype scene (one GameObject with DeckPrototype) and makes it the first build scene.
        [MenuItem("Catchline/Create Deck Prototype Scene")]
        public static void CreateScene()
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            new GameObject("DeckPrototype").AddComponent<DeckPrototype>();
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, ScenePath);
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Created {ScenePath}");
        }

        // Opens the prototype scene and enters Play mode. Also usable as a launch argument:
        //   Unity -projectPath . -executeMethod Catchline.EditorTools.PrototypeTools.OpenAndPlay
        [MenuItem("Catchline/Play Deck Prototype (old)")]
        public static void OpenAndPlay()
        {
            if (!File.Exists(ScenePath)) CreateScene();
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo()) return;
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(ScenePath);
            EditorApplication.delayCall += () => EditorApplication.isPlaying = true;
        }

        // Batchmode check: builds the deck, runs the simulation headless, logs a report
        // and renders a PNG.
        //   Unity -batchmode -quit -projectPath . -executeMethod Catchline.EditorTools.PrototypeTools.Snapshot
        //         -snapshotPath out.png -simSeconds 90 [-fullCrew yes]
        public static void Snapshot()
        {
            string path = Arg("-snapshotPath", Path.GetFullPath("Logs/deck-snapshot.png"));
            float seconds = float.Parse(Arg("-simSeconds", "90"), System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

            if (!File.Exists(ScenePath)) CreateScene();
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(ScenePath);
            var deck = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<DeckPrototype>();
            deck.Build();
            if (Arg("-fullCrew", "no") == "yes") deck.StaffFully();

            float reportEvery = 30f, nextReport = reportEvery;
            for (float t = 0f; t < seconds; t += 0.05f)
            {
                deck.Tick(0.05f);
                if (deck.SimTime >= nextReport)
                {
                    Debug.Log("[Catchline] " + deck.Report());
                    nextReport += reportEvery;
                }
            }

            Render(deck.Cam, path, 1600, 900);
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Snapshot written to {path}");
        }

        const string VoyagePath = "Assets/Scenes/Voyage.unity";

        // Build 1 scene: one GameObject with VoyageGame. It becomes the first build scene.
        [MenuItem("Catchline/Create Voyage Scene")]
        public static void CreateVoyageScene()
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            new GameObject("VoyageGame").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, VoyagePath);
            EditorBuildSettings.scenes = new[] { new EditorBuildSettingsScene(VoyagePath, true), new EditorBuildSettingsScene(ScenePath, true) };
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Created {VoyagePath}");
        }

        [MenuItem("Catchline/Play Voyage %#p")]
        public static void PlayVoyage()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            if (!File.Exists(VoyagePath)) CreateVoyageScene();
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo()) return;
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(VoyagePath);
            EditorApplication.delayCall += () => EditorApplication.isPlaying = true;
        }

        // Balance check: a bot plays N voyages headless, logs each voyage and renders a PNG mid-fishing.
        //   Unity -batchmode -quit -projectPath . -executeMethod Catchline.EditorTools.PrototypeTools.VoyageSim
        //         -voyages 15 [-seed 7] [-snapshotPath out.png]
        public static void VoyageSim()
        {
            int voyages = int.Parse(Arg("-voyages", "15"));
            string path = Arg("-snapshotPath", Path.GetFullPath("Logs/voyage-snapshot.png"));
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            if (!File.Exists(VoyagePath)) CreateVoyageScene();
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(VoyagePath);
            var game = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<VoyageGame>();
            game.Seed = int.Parse(Arg("-seed", "7"));
            game.AutoPlay = true;
            game.Build();
            var shaders = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<Renderer>(FindObjectsSortMode.None)
                .SelectMany(r => r.sharedMaterials.Select(m => m == null ? $"{r.name}:null" : $"{m.name}:{m.shader.name}"))
                .Distinct().Take(25);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Materials: " + string.Join(" | ", shaders));
            // A shot of the harbour before the bot sails.
            game.Tick(0.2f);
            Render(game.Cam, path.Replace(".png", "-harbour.png"), 1600, 900);
            if (Arg("-showcase", "0") == "1")
            {
                // One shot per sea at the fishing camera, then the harbour wide and the deck close.
                for (int k = 0; k < GameData.Spots.Length; k++)
                {
                    game.Showcase(k, 0f);
                    Render(game.Cam, path.Replace(".png", $"-sea{k}.png"), 1600, 900);
                }
                game.Showcase(-1, 3f);
                Render(game.Cam, path.Replace(".png", "-harbour-wide.png"), 1600, 900);
                game.Showcase(-2, -1.4f);
                Render(game.Cam, path.Replace(".png", "-deck.png"), 1600, 900);
                game.Showcase(-1, 0f);
            }
            game.Tick(0.3f);

            int logged = 0;
            while (game.State.Voyage <= voyages && game.SimTime < voyages * 900f)
            {
                game.Tick(0.05f);
                while (logged < game.Log.Count) Debug.Log("[Catchline] " + game.Log[logged++]);
            }
            float until = game.SimTime + 600f;
            while (game.CurrentMode != VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing && game.SimTime < until) game.Tick(0.05f);
            float shotAt = game.SimTime + 35f;
            while (game.SimTime < shotAt) game.Tick(0.05f);
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Sim done at {game.SimTime / 60f:0.0} min, coins {game.State.Coins}");
            Render(game.Cam, path, 1600, 900);
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] Snapshot written to {path}");
        }

        public static void Render(Camera cam, string path, int width, int height)
        {
            var rt = new RenderTexture(width, height, 24, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
            cam.aspect = (float)width / height;
            var request = new RenderPipeline.StandardRequest { destination = rt };
            if (RenderPipeline.SupportsRenderRequest(cam, request)) RenderPipeline.SubmitRenderRequest(cam, request);
            else
            {
                cam.targetTexture = rt;
                cam.Render();
                cam.targetTexture = null;
            }

            var prev = RenderTexture.active;
            RenderTexture.active = rt;
            var tex = new Texture2D(width, height, TextureFormat.RGB24, false);
            tex.ReadPixels(new Rect(0, 0, width, height), 0, 0);
            tex.Apply();
            RenderTexture.active = prev;
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(path));
            File.WriteAllBytes(path, tex.EncodeToPNG());
        }

        static string Arg(string name, string fallback)
        {
            var args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
            int i = Array.IndexOf(args, name);
            return i >= 0 && i + 1 < args.Length ? args[i + 1] : fallback;
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/PtsWaterChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class PtsWaterChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool pass, string label)
        { if (!pass) throw new Exception("PTS water check failed: " + label); }

        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            var lib = ArtLibrary.Get();
            Check(lib.WaterShader != null && lib.WaterShader.name == "Catchline/PTS Water" && !ShaderUtil.ShaderHasError(lib.WaterShader), "real PTS shading adapter imports");
            Check(lib.PtsWaterNormals != null && lib.PtsWaterDither != null, "original PTS surface/shadow textures referenced");
            Check(lib.PtsWaterNormals.mipmapCount > 1, "surface normals have mipmaps to avoid fine shimmer");
            var root = new GameObject("PTS water integration check");
            var game = root.AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            game.Build();
            var sea = root.GetComponentsInChildren<CoastalWater>().Single();
            var material = sea.GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial;
            Check(material.shader == lib.WaterShader && material.GetTexture("_SurfaceNormals") == lib.PtsWaterNormals, "live stage uses PTS material and packed normals");
            Check(material.GetFloat("_BoatAttack_water_FoamIntensity") == 0, "foam disabled");
            Check(sea.GetComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh.vertexCount <= 65535, "dense water stays within mobile 16-bit index range");
            game.Showcase(0, 0);
            game.Tick(.5f);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(material.GetFloat("_Clock") - PtsWater.Clock(game.SimTime)) < .001f, "waves follow slowed voyage simulation clock");
            Vector3 center = game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center);
            Quaternion rotation = game.Boat.localRotation;
            game.Tick(0);
            Check(Vector3.Distance(center, game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center)) < .00001f
                && Quaternion.Angle(rotation, game.Boat.localRotation) < .001f, "paused water and hull pose stay still");
            float minHeight = center.y, maxHeight = center.y;
            for (int frame = 0; frame < 80; frame++)
            {
                game.Tick(.1f);
                Vector3 moved = game.Boat.TransformPoint(game.State.HullDef.Center);
                Check(Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(center.x, center.z), new Vector2(moved.x, moved.z)) < .0001f,
                    "rocking stays centred on hull without horizontal deck drift");
                Check(Mathf.Abs(moved.y - game.State.HullDef.Center.y) < .055f, "heave stays subtle");
                minHeight = Mathf.Min(minHeight, moved.y); maxHeight = Mathf.Max(maxHeight, moved.y);
            }
            Check(maxHeight - minHeight > .001f, "boat follows changing surface height");
            float harbourVisibility = material.GetFloat("_MaxDepth");
            game.Showcase(4, 0);
            Check(material.GetFloat("_MaxDepth") < harbourVisibility, "deep channel obscures finite seabed edge");
            game.Showcase(2, 0);
            foreach (var terrain in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>().Where(r => r.name == "Terrain_ConnectedShoalsAndSeabed"))
                Check(!terrain.sharedMaterials.Any(m => m.color == Palette.Hex("BEA77D")), "sand wetness comes from water depth rather than square material bands");
            var camera = game.Cam;
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(root);
            Check(sea == null && camera == null, "disposing a voyage removes its water and camera");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PTS WATER INTEGRATION CHECKS PASSED");
        }

        public static void RestoreAndBuild()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs/water025");
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var g = new GameObject("Restored water025 native view").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            g.SessionOnly = true; g.Build(); g.StartNetBoatTrial(); g.Tick(.5f);
            var material = g.GetComponentInChildren<CoastalWater>().GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial;
            Check(material.shader.name == "Catchline/PTS Water", "restored actual runtime shader before capture");
            var shot = typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../water025/restored-desktop", 1440, 900, 1f });
            shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { g, "../water025/restored-phone", 844, 390, 1f });
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(g.gameObject);
            ProgressionLaunchChecks.BuildBoth();
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] WATER025 RESTORE AND BUILDS PASSED");
        }

        public static void Capture()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            Run();
            var game = new GameObject("PTS background map capture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            game.Build();
            string folder = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/pts-water-integrated");
            for (int map = 0; map < 5; map++)
            {
                game.Showcase(map, 0);
                game.Tick(.1f);
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, $"map-{map}-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, $"map-{map}-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            }
            game.Showcase(0, 0);
            game.Tick(.1f);
            var material = game.GetComponentInChildren<CoastalWater>().GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial;
            // Freeze every scene object; only the water's simulation clock changes.
            for (int frame = 0; frame < 20; frame++)
            {
                material.SetFloat("_Clock", PtsWater.Clock(frame * .16f));
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, $"water-{frame:00}.png"), 1080, 675);
            }
            material.SetFloat("_Clock", PtsWater.Clock(.64f));
            float detailScale = material.GetFloat("_DetailScale");
            foreach (float scale in new[] { .8f, 1.6f, 3.2f })
            {
                material.SetFloat("_DetailScale", scale);
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, $"ripple-scale-{scale:F1}.png"), 1440, 900);
            }
            material.SetFloat("_DetailScale", detailScale);
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, "sun-reflection-on.png"), 1080, 675);
            float specularStrength = material.GetFloat("_SpecularStrength");
            material.SetFloat("_SpecularStrength", 0);
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, "sun-reflection-off.png"), 1080, 675);
            material.SetFloat("_SpecularStrength", specularStrength);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to " + folder + " (scene-only; runtime HUD not captured)");
        }

        public static void CaptureBuoyancy()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            Run();
            var game = new GameObject("Coupled boat/water background capture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true;
            game.Build();
            game.Showcase(0, 0);
            game.Tick(.1f);
            Vector3 cameraPosition = game.Cam.transform.position;
            float cameraSize = game.Cam.orthographicSize;
            string folder = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/water007-coupled");
            for (int frame = 0; frame < 40; frame++)
            {
                game.Tick(.16f);
                // Hold the camera steady so the actual hull bob/rock is visible.
                game.Cam.transform.position = cameraPosition;
                game.Cam.orthographicSize = cameraSize;
                PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, $"motion-{frame:00}.png"), 1080, 675);
            }
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.Combine(folder, "phone.png"), 844, 390);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to " + folder + " (coupled boat/water, fixed camera; no HUD)");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/ReferenceHudChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Run in a released scratch project only. SessionOnly fixtures never touch Mo's save.
    public static class ReferenceHudChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        static void Check(bool ok, string reason) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Reference HUD check failed: " + reason); }
        static void Set(VoyageGame game, string key, object value) => typeof(VoyageGame).GetField(key, Hidden).SetValue(game, value);
        static VoyageGame Fixture()
        {
            var game = new GameObject("Isolated fish reference HUD").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true; game.Build(); Set(game, "showTitle", false);
            game.Showcase(0, 0); game.Tick(.01f); game.PrepareInterface(1672, 941, 1);
            return game;
        }
        static Text Text(VoyageGame game, string id) => game.GetComponentsInChildren<Text>().Single(t => t.name == id);
        static RectTransform Rect(VoyageGame game, string id) => game.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Single(t => t.name == id);
        static void Inside(VoyageGame game, string child, string parent)
        {
            var c = Rect(game, child); var p = Rect(game, parent);
            var corners = new Vector3[4]; c.GetWorldCorners(corners);
            foreach (var corner in corners)
            {
                var q = p.InverseTransformPoint(corner);
                Check(q.x >= p.rect.xMin - 1 && q.x <= p.rect.xMax + 1 && q.y >= p.rect.yMin - 1 && q.y <= p.rect.yMax + 1,
                    child + " remains in its containing panel");
            }
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            foreach (var asset in new[] { "panel-status", "panel-goal", "panel-crew", "panel-row", "panel-toolbar", "bar-track", "bar-fill", "icon-Sun", "icon-Coin" })
                Check(Resources.Load<Texture2D>("FishReference/" + asset) != null, "required original reference artwork " + asset);
            Check(Resources.Load<Font>("FishReference/ReferenceRegular") != null && Resources.Load<Font>("FishReference/ReferenceBold") != null, "shared live text fonts imported");
            var game = Fixture();
            game.State.Coins = 2845; game.Tick(0); game.PrepareInterface(1672, 941, 1);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/status/coins").text == "2,845", "wallet uses real state at reference value");
            game.State.Coins += 29; game.Tick(0);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/status/coins").text == "2,874", "wallet updates after a state change");
            game.State.Coins = int.MaxValue; game.Tick(0);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/status/coins").text.Length <= 7, "huge balance remains bounded");
            game.State.Active.Clear();
            game.State.Active.Add(new Contract { Species = Species.Herring, Count = 3, Delivered = 0, VoyagesLeft = 2, Reward = 10 });
            game.Cooler.Add(new CaughtFish { Species = Species.Herring, Kg = .2f, StowedAt = game.SimTime }); game.Tick(0);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/goal/count").text == "0/3", "unsold fish cannot falsely complete delivered goal");
            game.State.Active[0].Delivered = 2; game.Tick(0);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/goal/count").text == "2/3", "actual delivered progress updates");
            game.State.Active.Clear(); game.State.BestVoyageFish = 0; game.Tick(0);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/goal/count").text == "0/" + GameData.SkiffFishGoal, "no-contract record requirement preserved");
            game.State.BestVoyageFish = GameData.SkiffFishGoal; game.State.Coins = GameData.SkiffCost; game.Tick(0);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/goal/objective").text == "Skiff refit ready", "existing ready state is exposed without executing refit");
            Check(game.State.Hull == 0, "HUD does not buy or mutate hull");
            foreach (var size in new[] { new Vector2Int(1672, 941), new Vector2Int(1440, 900), new Vector2Int(844, 390) })
            {
                game.PrepareInterface(size.x, size.y, 1); Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();
                Inside(game, "reference/status/coins", "reference/status");
                Inside(game, "reference/goal/count", "reference/goal");
                Inside(game, "reference/crew/count", "reference/crew/toolbar");
                Inside(game, "reference/crew/0/row", "reference/crew/panel");
                Check(game.GetComponentsInChildren<Text>().Where(t => t.name.StartsWith("reference/")).All(t => t.font != null), "all live labels use a real imported font");
            }
            game.PrepareInterface(844, 390, 1.15f);
            Check(Text(game, "reference/goal/title").gameObject.activeInHierarchy, "larger type uses live scalable heading");
            game.PrepareInterface(844, 390, 1);
            Check(!game.GetComponentsInChildren<Text>().Any(t => t.name == "reference/goal/title"), "switching back hides stale scalable heading");
            Check(Rect(game, "reference/goal/title/raster").gameObject.activeInHierarchy, "source caption restored after type toggle");
            var canvas = game.GetComponentInChildren<CoastalCanvas>().GetComponent<Canvas>();
            Check(Rect(game, "pause").rect.width * canvas.scaleFactor >= 43.9f && Rect(game, "pause").rect.height * canvas.scaleFactor >= 43.9f,
                "phone pause target remains44physical pixels without stretching icon");
            Check(game.InvokeInterface("pause"), "source hamburger invokes existing pause action");
            Check((bool)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("paused", Hidden).GetValue(game), "pause control actually pauses game");
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] REFERENCE HUD CHECKS PASSED (live state/assets/containment/retained pause; physical input and visual match separate)");
        }
        static void Shot(VoyageGame game, string filename, int width, int height, float type = 1)
        {
            game.PrepareInterface(width, height, type); game.Cam.aspect = width / (float)height;
            Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();
            var rt = new RenderTexture(width, height, 24, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
            var ui = new RenderTexture(width, height, 24, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
            var oldTarget = game.Cam.targetTexture; var oldActive = RenderTexture.active;
            var composite = new Material(Shader.Find("Hidden/Catchline/Coastal UI Composite"));
            var texture = new Texture2D(width, height, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false);
            try
            {
                game.Cam.targetTexture = rt;
                var request = new RenderPipeline.StandardRequest { destination = rt };
                RenderPipeline.SubmitRenderRequest(game.Cam, request);
                game.GetComponentInChildren<CoastalCanvas>().RenderCapture(ui);
                Graphics.Blit(ui, rt, composite);
                RenderTexture.active = rt;
                texture.ReadPixels(new Rect(0, 0, width, height), 0, 0); texture.Apply();
                string folder = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/ui024"); Directory.CreateDirectory(folder);
                File.WriteAllBytes(Path.Combine(folder, filename + ".png"), texture.EncodeToPNG());
                // Capture the real production Canvas separately for overlay comparisons.
                RenderTexture.active = ui; texture.ReadPixels(new Rect(0, 0, width, height), 0, 0); texture.Apply();
                File.WriteAllBytes(Path.Combine(folder, filename + "-ui.png"), texture.EncodeToPNG());
            }
            finally
            {
                game.Cam.targetTexture = oldTarget; RenderTexture.active = oldActive;
                UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(rt); UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(ui);
                UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(composite); UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(texture);
            }
        }
        public static void Capture()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            Run();
            var game = Fixture(); game.State.Coins = 2845;
            Shot(game, "reference-1672", 1672, 941);
            Shot(game, "desktop-1440", 1440, 900);
            Shot(game, "phone-844", 844, 390);
            Shot(game, "phone-large", 844, 390, 1.15f);
            game.CommandRod(game.Rods[0]);
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && game.MinigameRod?.State != RodState.Bite; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
            Check(game.MinigameRod?.State == RodState.Bite, "native bite fixture reaches real fishing state");
            Shot(game, "bite-phone", 844, 390);
            game.StrikeTap();
            for (int i = 0; i < 300 && game.Stats.Landed == 0; i++) game.Tick(.05f);
            Shot(game, "catch-phone", 844, 390);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/ui024 (real Canvas+world and transparent UI pairs; Mo visual acceptance pending)");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/SalePayoutChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEngine;
namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class SalePayoutChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool ok,string message)
        {
            if(!ok)throw new Exception("[Catchline] First voyage check failed: sale payout "+message);
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            var state=new GameState(9);
            state.Coins=5;
            var pollock=new Contract {Species=Species.Pollock,Count=5,Delivered=0,VoyagesLeft=2,Reward=125};
            state.Active.Add(pollock);
            var haul=new List<CaughtFish>();
            for(int i=0;i<3;i++)haul.Add(new CaughtFish {Species=Species.Pollock,Kg=1.2f});
            int originalCoins=state.Coins;
            float originalPrice=state.Market[(int)Species.Pollock];
            var quote=state.QuoteSale(haul,0);
            Check(quote.Coins>0 && quote.ToContracts==3 && quote.ContractCoins==0,"partial contract haul still earns market coins");
            Check(state.Coins==originalCoins && pollock.Delivered==0 && state.Market[(int)Species.Pollock]==originalPrice,"counter quote does not consume progress or money");
            var sale=state.Sell(haul,0);
            Check(sale.Coins==quote.Coins && state.Coins==originalCoins+quote.Coins && sale.FishSold==3,"every fish paid, including contract fish");
            state.EndVoyage(sale);
            Check(pollock.Delivered==3 && pollock.VoyagesLeft==1,"partial contract persists alongside real payout");
            haul.RemoveAt(0);
            quote=state.QuoteSale(haul,0);
            originalCoins=state.Coins;
            sale=state.Sell(haul,0);
            Check(sale.Coins>0 && sale.ContractCoins==125 && sale.Coins+sale.ContractCoins==quote.Coins+quote.ContractCoins,"completion pays market plus bonus, exact counter");
            Check(state.Coins==originalCoins+sale.Coins+125 && !state.Active.Contains(pollock),"completion bonus paid once");
            sale=state.Sell(new List<CaughtFish>(),0);
            Check(sale.Coins==0 && sale.ContractCoins==0,"empty sale cannot pay completion twice");
            state.Active.Add(new Contract {Species=Species.Mackerel,Count=8,VoyagesLeft=1,Reward=100});
            haul.Clear();haul.Add(new CaughtFish {Species=Species.Mackerel,Kg=.3f});
            sale=state.Sell(haul,10000);state.EndVoyage(sale);
            Check(sale.Coins>=1 && state.Active.Count==0,"even degrading fish on expiring partial contract earn coins");
            // A live state made before Mo's credit must preserve that credit on its next save.
            string path=Path.GetFullPath("Logs/sale-payout-credit-test.json");
            try
            {
                var live=new GameState(7);live.Coins=5;live.Save(path);
                var credited=GameState.Load(path);credited.Coins+=100;
                credited.CoinCredits.Add(new GameState.CoinCredit {Id="test-credit-once",Amount=100});credited.Save(path);
                live.Save(path);
                Check(live.Coins==105 && live.CoinCredits.Count==1,"stale live save preserves authorized credit");
                live.Save(path);
                var reloaded=GameState.Load(path);
                Check(reloaded.Coins==105 && reloaded.CoinCredits.Count==1,"credit does not duplicate on save/reload");
            }
            finally {if(File.Exists(path))File.Delete(path);}
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] SALE PAYOUT CHECKS PASSED: unfinished/finished/expired contracts pay every fish; quote exact; bonus/credit once");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/SeaClearanceChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    public static class SeaClearanceChecks
    {
        const BindingFlags Hidden = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
        const float Surface = -.52f;
        static void Check(bool ok, string reason) { if (!ok) throw new Exception("Sea clearance check failed: " + reason); }
        public static void Verify(VoyageGame game, int spot)
        {
            var scene = (Transform)typeof(VoyageGame).GetField("scenery", Hidden).GetValue(game);
            float edge = game.State.HullDef.Center.z + Mathf.Max(VoyageGame.FishingWaterHalfWidth,
                game.State.HullDef.Height * game.State.HullDef.CellSize * .5f + 2f);
            int tested = 0, meshes = 0;
            foreach (var mesh in scene.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
            {
                if (!mesh.sharedMesh) continue;
                meshes++;
                var vertices = mesh.sharedMesh.vertices;
                for (int i = 0; i < vertices.Length; i++) vertices[i] = scene.InverseTransformPoint(mesh.transform.TransformPoint(vertices[i]));
                var triangles = mesh.sharedMesh.triangles;
                for (int i = 0; i < triangles.Length; i += 3)
                    for (int e = 0; e < 3; e++)
                    {
                        Vector3 a = vertices[triangles[i + e]], b = vertices[triangles[i + (e + 1) % 3]];
                        if (a.y >= Surface)
                        {
                            Check(a.z >= edge - .002f, $"map{spot} hull{game.State.Hull} exposed {mesh.name} inside reserve ({a.z:F3} < {edge:F3})");
                            tested++;
                        }
                        if ((a.y < Surface) == (b.y < Surface)) continue;
                        float z = a.z + (b.z - a.z) * (Surface - a.y) / (b.y - a.y);
                        Check(z >= edge - .002f, $"map{spot} {mesh.name} surface-crossing edge intrudes");
                    }
            }
            Check(meshes > 0, "actual scene meshes checked (deep channel may be entirely submerged)");
            Debug.Log($"[Catchline] CLEAR WATER map{spot} hull{game.State.Hull}: {tested} exposed vertices/edges; minimum bank edge {edge:F2}m; fore/aft corridor unbounded");
        }
        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            foreach (string name in new[] { "GullHarbourMouth", "GullKelpBank", "GullSandbar", "GullRockyPoint", "GullDeepChannel" })
                Check(((ModelImporter)AssetImporter.GetAtPath("Assets/Art/Models/Environment/" + name + ".fbx")).isReadable,
                    name + " retains clearance geometry in built player");
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Whole-scenery clearance fixture").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.SessionOnly = true; game.Build(); game.StartNetBoatTrial();
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetFullPath("Logs/sea014"));
            for (int hull = 0; hull < GameData.Hulls.Length; hull++)
            {
                game.State.Hull = hull;
                for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++) { game.Showcase(spot, 0); Verify(game, spot); }
            }
            // Future growth test: much wider and longer than any delivered hull.
            var future = GameData.Hulls[2]; int width = future.Width, height = future.Height;
            try
            {
                future.Width = 141; future.Height = 81; game.State.Hull = 2;
                for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++) { game.Showcase(spot, 0); Verify(game, spot); }
            }
            finally { future.Width = width; future.Height = height; }
            game.State.Hull = 2;
            for (int spot = 0; spot < GameData.Spots.Length; spot++)
            {
                game.Showcase(spot, 0); game.Tick(.01f); Verify(game, spot);
                typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic)
                    .Invoke(null, new object[] { game, "../sea014/map-" + spot, 1440, 900, 1f });
            }
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(game.gameObject);
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] SEA CLEARANCE CHECKS PASSED: all exposed meshes including docks/props;5maps x3hulls +14m future hull/8m beam");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] Snapshot written to Logs/sea014");
        }
        public static void ValidateAndBuildPreview()
        {
            Run();
            PreviewSetupChecks.Run();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Clear fishing water preview").AddComponent<VoyageGame>(); game.SessionOnly = true;
            const string scene = "Assets/Scenes/Sea014Preview.unity";
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), scene);
            string output = Path.GetFullPath("Builds/Sea014/Catchline.exe");
            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions { scenes = new[] { scene }, locationPathName = output,
                target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64, options = BuildOptions.None });
            Check(report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded, "separate preview built");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] PLAYABLE PREVIEW BUILT: " + output);
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/SkiffChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Scratch-project regression check; never uses the player's actual save.
    public static class SkiffChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool ok, string message)
        {
            if (!ok) throw new Exception("[Catchline] Skiff check failed: " + message);
        }

        public static void Run()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Skiff regression").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.AutoPlay = true;
            game.Build();
            var state = game.State;
            state.Coins = 1500;
            Check(!game.RefitSkiff() && state.Coins == 1500, "goal blocks refit without spending");
            state.BestVoyageFish = 16;
            state.Coins = 1499;
            Check(!game.RefitSkiff() && state.Hull == 0, "fee blocks refit");
            state.Coins = 1500;
            game.Rods[0].State = RodState.Ready;
            game.Rods[0].Squid = true;
            state.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.BigCooler);
            state.Owned.Add(UpgradeId.RodCarbon);
            game.SyncUpgrades();
            Check(game.Rods[0].State == RodState.Ready && game.Rods[0].Squid, "visual upgrades preserve baited rods");
            Check(ArtLibrary.Find(game.Boat.gameObject, "BigCooler") != null && ArtLibrary.Find(game.Rods[0].Root.gameObject, "FishingRod_Carbon") != null, "purchased cooler and rod variants visible");
            Check(game.RefitSkiff() && state.Coins == 0 && state.Hull == 1, "refit spends once");
            Check(game.Rods[0].State == RodState.Ready && game.Rods[0].Squid, "refit preserves baited rods");
            Check(!game.RefitSkiff() && state.Coins == 0, "second refit rejected");
            Check(game.Grid.Width == 5 && game.Grid.Height == 3 && state.CoolerCapacity == 27, "skiff dimensions and hold");
            Check(game.Rods.Count == 3 && game.Rods.TrueForAll(r => r.Unlocked), "three working rods");
            foreach (var rod in game.Rods)
                Check(game.Grid.FindPath(state.HullDef.Seat, rod.Tile) != null, "console-to-rod route");
            Check(game.Grid.FindPath(state.HullDef.Rest, state.HullDef.HoldAccess) != null, "deckhand-to-hold route");
            Check(game.Grid.IsBlocked(new Vector2Int(4, 2)) && game.Grid.IsBlocked(new Vector2Int(1, 1)), "console and hold blocked");
            var model = ArtLibrary.Get()?.M("Skiff");
            Check(model != null && ArtLibrary.Find(game.Boat.gameObject, "HoldLid") != null, "skiff asset and lid integrated");
            var library = ArtLibrary.Get();
            Check(library.CrewClips.Length == 11, "eleven crew clips imported");
            Check(ArtLibrary.Find(game.Captain.Root.gameObject, "Outfit_Captain").gameObject.activeSelf, "captain outfit visible");
            Check(!ArtLibrary.Find(game.Captain.Root.gameObject, "Outfit_Deckhand").gameObject.activeSelf, "other outfits hidden");
            var rig = ArtLibrary.Find(game.Captain.Root.gameObject, "CrewRig");
            var leg = ArtLibrary.Find(rig.gameObject, "LeftUpLeg");
            var walk = library.CrewClips.Single(c => c.name.Split('|').Last() == "Walk");
            walk.SampleAnimation(rig.gameObject, .1f);
            var before = leg.localRotation;
            walk.SampleAnimation(rig.gameObject, .6f);
            Check(Quaternion.Angle(before, leg.localRotation) > 5f, "walk animation moves skinned rig in Unity");
            game.Tick(.2f);
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.GetFullPath("Logs/skiff-harbour.png"), 1440, 900);

            string save = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/skiff-check-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".json");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(save));
            try
            {
                state.Save(save);
                var loaded = GameState.Load(save);
                Check(loaded != null && loaded.Hull == 1 && loaded.Fuel == state.Fuel && loaded.BestVoyageFish == 16, "skiff save round trip");
                File.WriteAllText(save, "{\"Version\":1,\"Coins\":400,\"Bait\":15,\"Voyage\":3,\"FoodMinutes\":4,\"Owned\":[],\"Charts\":[],\"Board\":[],\"Offers\":[],\"Active\":[]}");
                loaded = GameState.Load(save);
                Check(loaded != null && loaded.Hull == 0 && loaded.Fuel == 0 && loaded.Coins == 400, "old rowboat save loads");
            }
            finally { if (File.Exists(save)) File.Delete(save); }

            game.SelectedSpot = 4;
            state.Fuel = 0;
            Check(game.SailBlocker() == "Buy fuel at Supplies", "empty tank blocks departure");
            state.Coins = GameData.FuelPackCost;
            Check(state.BuyFuel() && state.Coins == 0, "fuel purchase");
            float fuel = state.Fuel;
            game.SetSail();
            Check(state.Fuel == fuel, "fuel burns with elapsed voyage time");
            // Prevent the autoplay bot from interfering during this targeted voyage.
            game.AutoPlay = false;
            game.Tick(11f);
            Check(game.CurrentMode == VoyageGame.Mode.Fishing, "outboard reaches channel in half row time");
            game.Tick(2f);
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.GetFullPath("Logs/skiff-sea-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.GetFullPath("Logs/skiff-sea-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            Check(!game.RefitSkiff(), "refit rejected at sea");
            game.HeadHome();
            Check(state.Fuel < fuel && state.Fuel > fuel - .3f, "elapsed fuel remains for return");
            // Do not complete arrival with AutoPlay false: that would save the test state.
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] SKIFF CHECKS PASSED: refit, models, routes, save migration, fuel and travel");
        }

        public static void Automation()
        {
            ArtImport.BuildLibrary();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var game = new GameObject("Automation regression").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            game.AutoPlay = true;
            game.Build();
            var s = game.State;
            s.Coins = 10000; s.BestVoyageFish = 16;
            Check(game.RefitSkiff(), "automation refit");
            s.Board.Clear();
            var deckhand = new CrewMember { Name = "Test hand", Role = CrewRole.Deckhand, Grade = Grade.Hand, VoyagesLeft = 2 };
            var fisher = new CrewMember { Name = "Test fisherman", Role = CrewRole.Fisherman, Grade = Grade.Veteran, VoyagesLeft = 5 };
            var secondHand = new CrewMember { Name = "Test second hand", Role = CrewRole.Deckhand, Grade = Grade.Experienced, VoyagesLeft = 3 };
            s.Board.AddRange(new[] { deckhand, fisher, secondHand });
            Check(s.Hire(deckhand) && s.Hire(fisher) && s.Hire(secondHand), "three role-aware hires");
            var fourth = new CrewMember { Name = "Fourth", Grade = Grade.Hand, VoyagesLeft = 2 };
            s.Board.Add(fourth);
            Check(!s.Hire(fourth) && s.CrewCount == 3, "crew cap enforced");
            game.SyncCrew();
            Check(game.ExtraPeople.Count == 2 && game.Hand != null, "all hired crew built");
            string save = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/crew-check-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".json");
            try
            {
                s.Save(save);
                var loaded = GameState.Load(save);
                Check(loaded.CrewCount == 3 && loaded.Crew.Count(c => c.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman) == 1, "crew roles save round trip");
            }
            finally { if (File.Exists(save)) File.Delete(save); }
            s.FoodMinutes = 48f; s.Bait = 200;
            game.SelectedSpot = 4;
            game.SetSail();
            game.AutoPlay = false;
            game.Tick(11f);
            Check(game.SetNet(), "captain can set net");
            NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainSet(game);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Soaking, "set action reaches soaking");
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.GetFullPath("Logs/skiff-net-soaking.png"), 1440, 900);
            game.Tick(90f);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Ready && game.NetCatch > 0, "net soaks and catches");
            Check(game.Stats.Landed > 0 && game.Rods.Any(r => r.Fisher != null), "fisherman lands fish without captain taps");
            int landed = game.Stats.Landed, net = game.NetCatch;
            Check(game.HaulNet(), "one tap starts haul");
            NetHaulChecks.FinishCaptainHaul(game);
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Stowed && game.Stats.Landed >= landed + net, "haul deposits net fish on deck");
            Check(game.OnDeck.Count > 0, "haul creates carrying backlog");
            Check(game.ExtraPeople.Where(p => p.Contract.Role == CrewRole.Fisherman).All(p => p.Carrying == null), "fishermen leave carrying to deckhands");
            game.Tick(2f);
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.GetFullPath("Logs/skiff-net-desktop.png"), 1440, 900);
            PrototypeTools.Render(game.Cam, Path.GetFullPath("Logs/skiff-net-phone.png"), 844, 390);
            game.HeadHome();
            Check(game.Net == VoyageGame.NetState.Stowed && game.NetCatch == 0, "return clears net state");
            var sale = new SaleReport();
            s.EndVoyage(sale); s.EndVoyage(sale);
            Check(!s.Crew.Contains(deckhand) && s.Crew.Contains(fisher) && fisher.VoyagesLeft == 3, "contracts expire by voyage including extra crew");
            Debug.Log("[Catchline] SKIFF CHECKS PASSED: role hiring, cap, save, fisherman, net, backlog and contracts");
        }
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: Assets/Editor/UrgentCrewChecks.cs
========================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using Catchline.Game;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Catchline.EditorTools
{
    // Save-isolated regression for Mo's urgent rowboat automation / recovery request.
    public static class UrgentCrewChecks
    {
        static void Check(bool ok, string message)
        {
            if (!ok) throw new Exception("[Catchline] First voyage check failed: urgent " + message);
        }
        static void Until(VoyageGame game, Func<bool> done, string message, float timeout = 90f)
        {
            for (float t = 0; t < timeout && !done(); t += .05f) game.Tick(.05f);
            Check(done(), message);
        }
        static int Offers(GameState state, CrewRole role) => state.Board.Count(c => c.Role == role);
        static int Hired(GameState state, CrewRole role) => state.Crew.Count(c => c.Role == role);
        static void CheckComposition(CrewRole[] roles, int seed, bool saveReload)
        {
            var state = new GameState(seed) { Hull = 2, Coins = 10000 };
            state.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
            Check(Offers(state, CrewRole.Deckhand) >= 3 && Offers(state, CrewRole.Fisherman) >= 3, "tier3 always offers three candidates of each role");
            foreach (var role in roles)
            {
                var offer = state.Board.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Role == role);
                Check(offer != null && state.Hire(offer), "hire contract " + role);
                Check(Offers(state, CrewRole.Deckhand) >= 3 && Offers(state, CrewRole.Fisherman) >= 3, "both role offers replenish after each hire");
            }
            Check(state.CrewCount == 3, "fill all three berths");
            foreach (CrewRole role in new[] { CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman })
                Check(Hired(state, role) == roles.Count(r => r == role), "requested composition remains aboard: " + role);
            Check(state.Crew.All(c => c.VoyagesLeft == GameData.ContractVoyages(c.Grade)), "new hires retain grade-based contract duration");
            if (saveReload)
            {
                string path = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/crew026/crew-mix-save.json");
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(path));
                try
                {
                    state.Save(path);
                    var loaded = GameState.Load(path);
                    Check(loaded.CrewCount == 3 && loaded.Crew.All(c => c.VoyagesLeft == GameData.ContractVoyages(c.Grade)), "contract timers survive save reload");
                    Check(Offers(loaded, CrewRole.Deckhand) >= 3 && Offers(loaded, CrewRole.Fisherman) >= 3, "both offers survive save reload");
                    state = loaded;
                    loaded.Board.Clear(); loaded.Save(path); loaded = GameState.Load(path);
                    Check(Offers(loaded, CrewRole.Deckhand) >= 3 && Offers(loaded, CrewRole.Fisherman) >= 3, "older empty board saves restore all role choices");
                    state = loaded;
                }
                finally { if (File.Exists(path)) File.Delete(path); }
            }

            var hiredCrew = state.Crew.ToArray();
            var contractLengths = hiredCrew.ToDictionary(c => c.Name, c => c.VoyagesLeft);
            for (int voyage = 1; voyage <= contractLengths.Values.Max(); voyage++)
            {
                state.EndVoyage(new SaleReport());
                foreach (var crew in hiredCrew)
                {
                    int remaining = contractLengths[crew.Name] - voyage;
                    Check(state.Crew.Contains(crew) == (remaining > 0), "crew contract expires on its stated voyage");
                    if (remaining > 0) Check(crew.VoyagesLeft == remaining, "crew contract countdown advances by one voyage");
                }
                Check(Offers(state, CrewRole.Deckhand) >= 3 && Offers(state, CrewRole.Fisherman) >= 3, "both roles remain available after contract expiry and board roll");
            }
            Check(state.CrewCount == 0, "all hired contracts expire on schedule");
        }
        static void CheckCrewMixes()
        {
            foreach (int hull in Enumerable.Range(0, GameData.Hulls.Length))
                for (int seed = 0; seed < 30; seed++)
                {
                    var state = new GameState(seed) { Hull = hull };
                    state.EnsureCrewRoleOffers();
                    Check(Offers(state, CrewRole.Deckhand) >= state.HullDef.CrewCap && Offers(state, CrewRole.Fisherman) >= state.HullDef.CrewCap,
                        $"both roles fill all {state.HullDef.CrewCap} berths on hull {hull}, seed {seed}");
                }
            CheckComposition(new[] { CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Fisherman }, 9101, true);
            CheckComposition(new[] { CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Deckhand }, 9102, false);
            CheckComposition(new[] { CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman }, 9103, false);
        }
        static void CheckCrewMixTrial()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var trial = new GameObject("Save-isolated crew mix test").AddComponent<VoyageGame>();
            trial.SessionOnly = true; trial.Build();
            Check(trial.StartCrewMixTrial(), "isolated crew trial starts");
            Check(trial.State.Hull == 2 && trial.State.CrewCount == 0 && trial.State.Coins == 1000, "test starts at tier3 harbour with empty berths and hiring funds");
            Check(Offers(trial.State, CrewRole.Deckhand) >= 3 && Offers(trial.State, CrewRole.Fisherman) >= 3, "test offers all role choices");
            Check(trial.State.Board[0].Role != trial.State.Board[1].Role, "first Crew Office row exposes both worker roles");
            trial.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
            var controls = trial.GetComponentsInChildren<RectTransform>().Select(t => t.name).ToHashSet();
            Check(Enumerable.Range(0, 6).All(i => controls.Contains("hire/" + i + "/buy")), "Crew Office exposes six candidate purchase controls");
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs/crew026");
            var shot = typeof(ReferenceHudChecks).GetMethod("Shot", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { trial, "../crew026/crew-office-desktop", 1440, 900, 1f });
            shot.Invoke(null, new object[] { trial, "../crew026/crew-office-phone", 844, 390, 1f });
            foreach (var role in new[] { CrewRole.Fisherman, CrewRole.Deckhand, CrewRole.Fisherman })
            {
                int index = trial.State.Board.FindIndex(c => c.Role == role);
                trial.PrepareInterface(1440, 900);
                Check(index >= 0 && trial.InvokeInterface("hire/" + index + "/buy"), "Crew Office buys selected " + role + " through its real control");
